Selected blocks are evaluated through exact token-level computation, while the remaining blocks contribute through their centroids under a shared softmax normalization. On five vDiTs, NOVA achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS among sparse attention methods, improving PSNR by up to 1.55\,dB over the strongest baseline, while delivering up to 1.82$\times$ end-to-end speedup on an H100 GPU.

</details>


### [35] [Is It the Agent or the Retry? Separating Selection from Agency in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28116)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出单一工具拒绝重试基线SRR，审计四个agentic文生图系统的真实增益，证明其大部分性能来自验证-重试而非复杂agentic流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic文生图系统（规划、路由、验证、精炼）在组合性基准上宣称大幅提升，但这些提升几乎总是相对于单次采样比较，缺少与"最强单生成器+现成验证器+按系统自有预算重试"这一强基线的公平对照。

Method: 定义SRR基线：取系统池中最强生成器、现成验证器、在系统自身预算内重试并在首个通过验证的图像处停止。用官方评测器审计OctoT2I、GEMS、GenPilot等四个系统，并在T2I-CompBench++及非基准调优生成器上复现；通过闭式分析建模不同预算下SRR的准确率并推导验证器似然比上界；推导跨生成器路由的准确率上限；开展离线蒸馏实验（选中样本vs随机样本）。

Result: GenEval上SRR与OctoT2I持平（0.962 vs 0.96），回收GEMS 63%–90%的增益（取决于验证器），在每图2.3次生成下超GenPilot 12分；效果可迁移。闭式分析精确预测各预算下SRR准确率，增益上限由验证器似然比决定。GenEval 2上，五样本中评测器选优仍比GEMS低15分（95%区间上端差12分），属真实agentic增益。路由带来的准确率上限为1–3分，其真正价值是算力：等准确率下约为SRR的1/2.5。蒸馏中选中样本与随机样本对生成器的教学效果无差别。

Conclusion: agentic文生图系统的多数增益可由简单验证-重试基线复现，路由主要节省算力而非提升准确率，选中样本蒸馏无额外信息收益；建议论文采用统一的报告清单（强基线对照、预算一致、验证器增益上限等）以更严谨地评估agentic增益。

Abstract: Agentic text-to-image systems wrap generators in loops of planning, routing, verification and refinement, and report large gains on compositional benchmarks, almost always relative to a single sample. We ask how much of the gain survives against Single-tool Rejection Retry (\ours): the strongest generator already in the system's pool, an off-the-shelf verifier, and retries that stop at the first accepted image under the system's own budget. Auditing four recent systems with official evaluators, we find that on GenEval SRR matches the accuracy of OctoT2I (0.962 vs.\ 0.96), recovers 63 to 90% of the gain of GEMS depending on the verifier, and exceeds GenPilot by 12 points with 2.3 generations per image; the effect persists with generators not tuned to the benchmark and on T2I-CompBench++. A closed-form analysis predicts the accuracy of SRR at every budget and shows that its gain is capped by the verifier's likelihood ratio. The same analysis also exposes genuine agentic gains: on GenEval 2, even the best of five samples chosen by the evaluator stays 15 points below the score GEMS reports (12 at the upper end of its 95% interval). We prove a ceiling on the accuracy that routing across generators can add, bracket it between one and three points on OctoT2I's own pool, and show that what OctoT2I's routing buys is compute (about 2.5
  less than SRR at equal accuracy), not accuracy. In our offline distillation experiments selected samples teach the generator no more than random ones, which a short information argument explains. We end with a reporting checklist for agentic generation papers.

</details>


### [36] [PhoneQC: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs for Order-Level Smartphone Trade-in Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28117)
*Zihan Deng,Xinpei Lin,Du Tiantian,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhoneQC基准：335笔以iPhone为主的回收订单，按统一协议（纯图像/图像+检测器旁挂）以0–100表单得分Score100评测系统；并以field-only LoRA验证"表单字段"监督可提升分数，但人类估价仍不可替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型已进入手机以旧换新的远程度量场景（6视图+诊断截图），易被误认为可替代人工报价。但报价是员工表单，外观填充率仅约0.25，闭源模型仅在愿意作答的订单上略高，人工鉴定仍必要。现有单视图缺陷集与属性基准无法在固定输入契约下评测订单级表单，检测框或泄露字段会被误当作完成的报价；且表单中未被阅读的"员工字段"无法通过生成框或描述来学习。

Method: 构建PhoneQC：335个iPhone为主的订单切片；定义Score100（0–100）表单评分，仅在匹配协议内排名（仅图像；图像+检测器sidecar），并将声明上界单列为非同级参考。案例研究采用field-only LoRA：冻结骨干、适配注意力投影、仅监督员工字段并令缺陷列表为空，插入器与零样本模型看到相同图像。

Result: 在image-only T1协议下，3B模型从26.6提升至33.3（覆盖率0.928）；剔除"空阻断项"后，开放排名仍偏向该适配器；但在此切片上与人评估的差距依然很大。

Conclusion: 订单级报价需在固定输入契约与匹配协议下按Score100评测，避免以框或字段泄露冒充完成报价；field-only LoRA证明了对员工表单字段的定向监督有效，但仅靠适配器远不能达到人工水平，人工鉴定仍不可或缺。

Abstract: As vision-language models enter remote smartphone trade-in, sellers already upload six views and a diagnostic screen, which makes it easy to treat the human quote as a step a model can take over. A model may mark a crack or read the screen, yet the quote is a staff form: appearance fill stays near 0.25, and the closed models that score a little higher do so only on the orders they answer, so human appraisal remains necessary. Two obstacles stand in the way. (1) Single-view defect sets and attribute benchmarks do not score an order-level form under a fixed input contract, so a box or a leaked field can be mistaken for a finished quote. (2) The unread half of the form is the staff fields, and a model left to emit boxes or captions does not learn them. We present PhoneQC, a 335-order iPhone-heavy cut that ranks systems by a 0 to 100 form score (Score100) only inside matched protocols (images alone; images plus a detector sidecar), with a declared upper bound that is not ranked as a peer. As a case study on that board, field-only LoRA freezes the backbone, adapts attention projections, and supervises staff fields while leaving the defect list empty; the adapter sees the same images as the zero-shot model. On image-only T1 the 3B model rises from 26.6 to 33.3 at coverage 0.928; without the empty-blocker item the open ranking still favors the adapter, while the remaining gap to a person stays large on this cut.

</details>


### [37] [StateBind: Exposing the Symbol-to-State Binding Gap in Visual Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28118)
*Qing Mu,Jiani Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出StateBind-200基准与免训练验证器Factorial StateBind，指出实例级“符号—物理状态绑定”是视觉数值测量的独立可靠性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠视觉系统应把数值主张与可见证据对应起来，即把候选数值映射到图像特定的物理状态；现有评测缺乏对这种state binding能力的受控检验。

Method: 构建StateBind-200：200张真实图像配人工精修局部几何，并提供Numeric、Calibrated、Physicalized三种匹配接口；在128张留出集上做6,696次受控调用。另提出Factorial StateBind：两个VLM分别提议目标与晶格组件，独立VLM验证证据，有效组合确定性求解；在69张开发图上选定精度模式（组件有效性、四方一致性、状态分离）。

Result: physicalizing相同候选使三模型多数投票准确率由75.54%升至91.13%，源图像均衡增益+15.30点；校准与额外推理未能消除差距；目标定位正确但数值晶格绑定错误可共存，双端点标尺跨度负担更大。40图几何审计中位目标偏差0.08 tick，主要效应在分歧敏感性下保持。留出集上高精度证据门在36/128图（28.12%）与9.68%有序单元达91.67%候选精度。

Conclusion: 实例级符号到状态绑定应被视为独立的可靠性瓶颈，所提可解释证据门为数值视觉主张提供了精度可控的验证途径。

Abstract: Reliable vision systems should connect numerical claims to visible evidence. In visual measurement, this requires mapping candidate values to image-specific physical states. StateBind-200 augments 200 real images with human-refined local geometry and matched Numeric, Calibrated, and Physicalized interfaces. Across a 128-image held-out evaluation and 6,696 controlled calls, physicalizing identical candidates raises three-model majority accuracy from 75.54% to 91.13%, a source-image-balanced gain of +15.30 points. Calibration and additional reasoning leave most of the gap intact; target localization can succeed despite incorrect value-lattice binding, with a larger burden for two-endpoint ruler spans. An independent 40-image geometry audit yields a 0.08-tick median target discrepancy and preserves the main effect under disagreement sensitivity.
  We further propose Factorial StateBind, a training-free selective verifier. Two VLMs propose target and lattice components, an independent VLM validates their evidence, and valid combinations are resolved deterministically. A precision mode selected on 69 development images combines component validity, four-way agreement, and state separation. Applied unchanged to held-out evaluation, this high-precision evidence gate reaches 91.67% candidate precision across 36/128 images (28.12%) and 9.68% of ordered cells. The results identify instance-specific symbol-to-state binding as a distinct reliability bottleneck and provide an interpretable evidence gate for numerical visual claims.

</details>


### [38] [Seeing Beyond Pixels: Radar Feature Generation from Images and Sparse Returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28119)
*Hongyu Zhou,Yubo Peng,Luping Xiang,Kun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VRTG方法，利用视觉信息引导生成雷达token，仅用每帧4个雷达点即可接近完整雷达模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机-雷达融合依赖完整雷达表示导致计算开销大，而视觉信息能在多大程度上补偿有限雷达测量尚未充分研究。

Method: VRTG先用图像目标线索筛选少量有信息量的雷达点；再建立视觉目标与雷达回波的一对一标定关联，用匹配测量精化目标距离、对未匹配目标保留视觉估计；最后将关联目标特征与视觉上下文转换为与现有融合网络兼容的雷达token。

Result: 在RADIal数据集上，每帧仅用4个雷达点即达到90.76%车辆检测F1和86.68%自由空间mIoU，接近完整雷达模型性能。

Conclusion: 稀疏雷达测量可由视觉信息有效补偿，VRTG在显著降低雷达表示需求的同时保持接近完整雷达的融合性能。

Abstract: Camera-radar fusion benefits from complementary visual and geometric information but often relies on complete radar representations, leading to substantial computing overhead. However, the extent to which visual information can compensate for limited radar measurements remains insufficiently explored. Therefore, we propose Vision-informed Radar Token Generation (VRTG), which exploits cross-modal information to generate task-relevant radar features from camera images and sparse radar measurements. Specifically, VRTG first leverages image-derived object cues to select a limited number of informative radar points. It then establishes calibrated one-to-one associations between visual objects and radar returns, using matched measurements to refine object ranges while retaining visual estimates for unmatched objects. Finally, the associated object features and visual context are transformed into radar tokens compatible with existing camera-radar fusion networks. Experiments on RADIal demonstrate that VRTG achieves 90.76\% vehicle-detection F1 and 86.68\% free-space mIoU using only four radar points per frame, approaching the performance of the full-radar model.

</details>


### [39] [Separating Module Gain From Transport Plan Contribution in Audio-Visual Dynamic Facial Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28120)
*Vu Nhat Minh Pham,Cong Khanh Nguyen,Minh Tuan Le,Van Thong Huynh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 跨模态路由模块相对骨干的提升成立，但增益不能归因于传输计划：计划扰动有影响仅说明推理依赖，非模块增益来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验“跨模态路由模块的提升是否来自其传输计划（transport plan）”这一常见但未经验证的归因假设，因为模块对骨干的对比无法区分计划与参数、结构等混杂因素。

Method: 在 MMA-DFER、ABVnet 上于 DFEW、MAFW，以及 MMA-DFER 于 CREMA-D 上评测；每配置单次训练，采用配对折区间。设计两类模块对骨干测试无法提供的对比：仅改变计划规则的参数匹配变体，以及不重训练即替换计划（均匀计划、行置换、保留双边缘但去除帧特定混合的自积计划）的干预实验。1.5 折依赖加宽下做稳健性检验。

Result: SCR 较重训基线 UAR +0.87（已解析）；较参数匹配池化变体 +0.17（未解析，不构成等价）。均匀替换 −0.72 UAR，行置换 −0.20（未解析），置换较均匀少损 0.51（已解析）；自积计划替换 −0.70。1.5 折依赖加宽后，基线增益仍解析，替换效应与配对对比转为未解析。

Conclusion: 模块可靠性成立，但“增益源于传输计划”的归因未被证实；计划替换造成的下降只反映推理期依赖，非因果归因证据。作者建议以参数匹配变体与免重训练干预作为此类模块超出模块对骨干比较的标准评估手段。

Abstract: A cross-modal routing module can improve over its backbone while its transport plan accounts for no demonstrable part of that improvement. In ABVnet with DFEW, Sinkhorn cross-modal routing (SCR) exceeds the retrained baseline by 0.87 unweighted average recall (UAR) points, a resolved difference, yet sits 0.17 points above an individually trained, parameter-matched pooled variant, an unresolved difference that establishes no equivalence; under the development protocol the comparison with the baseline reverses. Separating the two needs comparisons a module-against-backbone test cannot supply: parameter-matched variants changing only the plan rule, and interventions replacing the plan without retraining. The evaluation covers MMA-DFER and ABVnet on DFEW and MAFW, and MMA-DFER on CREMA-D, with a single training run per configuration and paired fold intervals. ABVnet with DFEW motivated the follow-up after the broader results were available. Replacing the plan everywhere with a uniform one reduces UAR by 0.72, whereas permuting its rows gives an unresolved difference of -0.20 UAR points; permutation is less damaging by 0.51 UAR points, a resolved contrast. Replacing the plan with the product of its own marginals, preserving both marginals and removing only frame-specific mixtures, reduces UAR by 0.70. These reductions concern reliance at inference; attribution of the module gain to the transport plan remains unestablished. Under the 1.5 fold-dependence widening, the baseline gain remains resolved, whereas the replacement reductions and the paired contrast are unresolved.

</details>


### [40] [RALC: Risk-Controlled Local Adoption of Segmentation Updates with Few Calibration Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28122)
*Shize Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RALC在固定模型更新下以局部单元选择性地采纳变化，利用候选相关的误差上界与校准损失进行风险控制，在BDD100K上以相同风险预算将恶劣场景mIoU提升1.89–3.19个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为雨天与夜间驾驶场景微调道路分割模型，可能损害正常白天图像的表现；而可用的正常标注校准图像极少，整图级风险检查往往会拒绝那些若局部采纳本可通过的更新。

Method: RALC针对固定模型更新选择局部变更：以置信度差异与图像得分构造嵌套候选；在每个正常校准图像上统计候选相对原模型多出的错误数，错误不变或减少时损失记为零；测试期每个替换至多引入一个错误，从而用替换数量界定无标签条件下的净误差增量，并结合候选相关上界与校准损失，选出通过风险检查的最大候选集。理论保证在模型与采纳规则预先固定、校准与测试图像可交换时成立。

Result: 在留出BDD100K上评估三个权重插值更新，每次校准抽样加入10张正常图像：相同固定预算下RALC的恶劣场景mIoU比整图采纳高1.89–3.19个百分点，但实测正常损失也更高；事后将正常损失匹配至每图1000像素后，RALC仍领先1.135–2.428个百分点；以8×8像素单元选择变化可保留93–98%的优势。

Conclusion: 局部化风险控制的采纳规则在小样本正常校准下比整图规则更有效，即使在校准后对齐正常图像损失仍具优势，且细粒度单元实现几乎无损；当校准图像增至250张时，两种规则均在原始预算下完整采纳全部三个更新，说明校准规模是关键限制因素。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a road-scene segmenter for rain and nighttime driving can introduce errors on normal daytime images. With few labeled normal images for calibration, a whole-image risk check may reject changes that would pass if adopted locally. Risk-Controlled Adoption of Label Changes (RALC) selects local changes from a fixed model update, using confidence differences and an image score to form nested candidates. On each normal calibration image, RALC measures how many more errors each candidate makes than the original model, assigning zero loss when errors stay the same or fall. At test time, each replacement can add at most one error, so the replacement count bounds the net increase without test labels. RALC combines this candidate-dependent bound with recorded calibration losses to select the largest set that passes the risk check. If the models and adoption rules are fixed before calibration and normal calibration and test images are exchangeable, the expected per-image loss stays within budget. On held-out BDD100K, we evaluated three weight-interpolated updates with 10 additional normal images per calibration draw. At a common fixed budget, adverse-scene mIoU was 1.89--3.19 percentage points higher with RALC than with whole-image adoption, while measured mean normal loss was also higher. After matching this loss post hoc at 1,000 pixels per image, RALC still led by 1.135--2.428 points. Selecting changes in $8\times8$-pixel cells recovered 93--98% of this advantage. With 250 calibration images at the original budget, both rules adopted all three updates in full.

</details>


### [41] [Seeing the Invisible: How Vision-Language Models Read, Predict and Trace Radio Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28123)
*Yiyang Zhu,Xin Li,Guangyuan Liu,Ziheng Liu,Jiayi Zhang,Chau Yuen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RadioVISTA基准与数据引擎：将任意地表坐标转为可射线追踪城市并生成精确单因素反事实，揭示VLM存在"场-路径解离"现象——能判断场强弱却无法判断单条直射/一次反射路径是否存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模型能否从可见建筑街道结构推断不可见无线电场，属真实物理推理问题；但此类研究缺乏真实城市的精确场标签与受控单因素干预数据。

Method: 构建无标注引擎：把地球上任意坐标转换为可射线追踪的城市，模拟匹配的单因素反事实并精确计算所有答案，形成RadioVISTA基准与训练数据集；在六大洲评估前沿及开源VLM，并开展监督与强化微调实验。

Result: 前沿VLM能较准确判断场强弱，但无法判断单条直射或一次反射路径是否存在；开源模型在读取场本身阶段即失败，系统性地低于随机猜测（即使按四次尝试任一正确计分）。在未见城市上，微调使9B开源模型在场预测上接近前沿闭源模型，但监督与强化微调均未改善路径追踪。

Conclusion: 仅监督最终答案使模型学会近似场分布，却无法掌握单路径几何；VLM对不可见场的"结果级"推理与"机制级"推理存在根本解离。

Abstract: Operating in the physical world requires reasoning beyond what cameras directly capture. In a city, radio signals are invisible to the lens, yet their strength is governed by the buildings and streets an image does show. Given only these visible structures, can vision-language models (VLMs) infer the invisible radio field they shape? Probing true physical reasoning requires exact fields in real cities, alongside controlled interventions where a single physical factor changes. We build a label-free engine that turns any coordinate on Earth into a ray-traceable city, simulates matched single-factor counterfactuals, and computes every answer exactly. This yields RadioVISTA, a benchmark and training dataset for visual reasoning about invisible fields. Physically, a radio field is the sum of its propagation paths, yet across six continents we uncover a striking field-path dissociation: frontier VLMs accurately judge where a field is strong, but fail to tell whether a single direct or one-wall path exists. Open models fail even earlier at reading the field itself, and are systematically wrong, scoring below random guessing even when credited for any of four attempts. On unseen cities, fine-tuning brings a 9B open model close to frontier proprietary models on fields, yet neither supervised nor reinforcement fine-tuning improves path tracing. Supervising only final answers teaches models to approximate the field, yet leaves single-path geometry out of reach.

</details>


### [42] [ReMiC: Representation-Driven Token Pruning for 3D VLMs via Minimax Hierarchical Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28124)
*Bangfeng Yan,Chaoning Zhang,Yuanhao Liu,Pengcheng Zheng,Mengyuan Li,Wei Dong,Sheng Zheng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无训练、表示驱动的3D视觉token剪枝框架ReMiC，将剪枝建模为极小极大层次聚类，仅保留10% token仍保持93.2%性能并提速2.04倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D VLM依赖多视角输入导致视觉token序列过长、推理开销大；现有剪枝方法以token显著性/特征多样性作为代表性代理，未显式建模原始token集合与保留子集之间的表示差距，导致场景表示次优。

Method: 将保留token视为原始观测的原型，把剪枝表述为固定token预算下最小化最大表示误差的极小极大层次聚类；包含局部原型初始化（按区域表示需求分配token）与全局原型精炼（利用跨区域信息更新原型以进一步降低最大表示误差）；整体无需训练。

Result: 在5个3D场景理解基准、3个代表性3D VLM上验证有效性与泛化性；仅保留10%视觉token时，ReMiC保持Video-3D LLM 93.2%的性能，推理速度提升2.04倍。

Conclusion: 以显式表示误差为目标的表示驱动剪枝优于基于显著性/多样性的启发式方法，可在无训练条件下实现高效且可泛化的3D VLM推理加速。

Abstract: 3D vision-language models (3D VLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in 3D scene understanding and spatial reasoning. However, their reliance on multi-view inputs produces long visual token sequences, imposing substantial computational overhead during inference. Existing pruning methods often rely on token salience or feature diversity as proxies for representativeness, without explicitly modeling the representation gap between the original token set and the retained subset, thereby leading to suboptimal representation of the original scene. In this paper, we propose ReMiC, a training-free, representation-driven token pruning framework based on minimax hierarchical clustering. ReMiC treats retained tokens as prototypes for the original observations, formulating pruning as minimizing the maximum representation error under a fixed token budget. Local prototype initialization allocates tokens according to regional representation needs, while global prototype refinement updates the selected prototypes using cross-region information to further reduce the maximum representation error. Extensive experiments conducted on three representative 3D VLMs across five 3D scene understanding benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization of our method. Notably, by retaining only 10% of visual tokens, ReMiC preserves 93.2% of the performance of Video-3D LLM and achieves a $2.04\times$ faster inference speed.

</details>


### [43] [DINO-KNN: Training-Free Multiclass In-Context Segmentation via Nearest Neighbors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28127)
*Hussain Alasmawi,Numan Saeed,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 DINO-KNN：用冻结 DINOv3 特征构建单一参考记忆库，以距离加权 kNN 投票一次性完成多类多参考的上下文分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于冻结基础模型的免训练上下文分割方法逐类分割，且增加参考图像收益很小；作者希望考察在多类、多参考设定下，简单联合决策规则能达到的上限。

Method: 将冻结 DINOv3 编码器提取的所有有标注参考图像 patch 存入单一记忆库；目标图像每个 patch 通过其 k 个最近邻的距离加权投票确定标签；单次前向即输出所有类别、每像素一个标签，无需训练与掩码解码器。

Result: 在 8 个数据集（4 自然、4 医学）、R=1~15、3 个参考随机种子下，DINO-KNN 在所有 R 下平均 mIoU 最优；R≥3 时在 6/8 数据集最优，R≥7 时 7/8 最优；mIoU 由 R=1 的 37.8 提升至 R=15 的 56.2，而最强基线 INSID3 仅从 36.1 到 42.9；速度比 INSID3 快 2.1 倍。

Conclusion: 简单的联合 kNN 决策规则即可显著超越逐类训练的免训练方法，且能有效利用更多参考图像，表明多参考扩展性是当前方法被忽视的关键维度。

Abstract: In-context segmentation (ICS) segments a target image given a few annotated reference images. Recent training-free methods built on frozen foundation models segment one class at a time and gain little from additional references. We study the multiclass, multi-reference setting and ask how far a simple joint decision rule can go. We introduce DINO-KNN, which stores every labeled reference patch of a frozen DINOv3 encoder in a single memory and labels each target patch by a distance-weighted vote of its k nearest neighbors. All classes are predicted in a single pass, with one label per pixel, without training or a mask decoder. We evaluate on eight datasets, four natural and four medical, with R=1 to 15 reference images and three reference seeds. DINO-KNN achieves the best average mIoU at every R; it is the best method on six of the eight datasets from R=3 and on seven of the eight from R=7. Its accuracy continues to grow with the number of references, from 37.8 mIoU at R=1 to 56.2 at R=15, while the strongest baseline, INSID3, rises from 36.1 to 42.9. DINO-KNN is also 2.1x faster than INSID3. The code will be made public.

</details>


### [44] [Robust Trajectory Prediction Under Sensing and V2X Noise: Evaluation and Mitigation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28128)
*Shahab Salehi,Luca Lusvarghi,Miguel Sepulcre,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer交互感知轨迹预测模型对真实噪声目标状态估计的敏感性，发现预测精度最多下降6倍，并证明在噪声增强数据集上重训练比输入端去噪模块更有效，可将精度退化控制在1.5倍以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA轨迹预测模型依赖经过大量离线后处理、高度筛选的大规模数据集进行训练与评估，而实际部署中检测目标的位置、朝向、速度估计受感知与定位不确定性影响，V2X场景下噪声更大。训练与部署噪声条件不匹配，安全性存疑。

Method: 首先评估五个SOTA基于Transformer的交互感知轨迹预测模型对现实噪声目标状态估计的敏感性；随后对比两种缓解策略：在输入端使用去噪模块，以及在噪声增强数据集上重训练模型。

Result: 随噪声水平上升，预测精度最多退化至6倍，凸显模型对训练与部署噪声条件失配的敏感性；在噪声增强数据上重训练可将精度退化限制在1.5倍以内，优于输入端去噪模块。噪声增强数据集已开源发布。

Conclusion: 稳健的轨迹预测应通过在贴近真实噪声分布的数据上重训练来实现，而非依赖输入端去噪；开源噪声增强数据集可支撑后续研究。

Abstract: Anticipating the future behavior of surrounding objects is key for the safe and efficient navigation of autonomous vehicles. State-of-the-art Transformer-based interaction-aware trajectory prediction models are typically trained and evaluated on large-scale, highly curated datasets obtained by filtering any object detection and tracking noise through extensive offline post-processing. However, in real-world deployments, the detected objects' state (position, heading, and speed) estimates are inherently affected by sensing and localization uncertainties. These uncertainties can be even higher for connected and automated vehicles receiving information from other road users through Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communications. In this paper, we first investigate the sensitivity of five state-of-the-art Transformer-based interaction-aware trajectory prediction models to realistic noisy object state estimates. Numerical results show that prediction accuracy degrades by up to a 6x factor as the noise level increases, highlighting the models' sensitivity to the mismatch between the noise conditions encountered during training and those experienced at deployment. We then show that retraining models on a more realistic noise-augmented dataset is a more effective strategy to mitigate the impact of noisy object state estimates on trajectory prediction accuracy than using denoising modules at the input. Retrained models limit the prediction accuracy degradation below a 1.5x factor. The noise-augmented dataset is openly released to support further research.

</details>


### [45] [Urban State Representation for Autonomous Mobility: A Survey of Multimodal Perception, Architectures, Datasets, and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28129)
*Binay Kumar Singh,Niels Da Vitoria Lobo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述以"城市状态表征（USR）"为统一视角，梳理当前多平台、多城市、多任务割裂的城市感知表征研究，提出USR形式化定义与五项期望属性，构建从传感器到决策的完整分类体系，汇编26个城市数据集/模拟器并给出基准快照与差距分析，最后提出USR-5五测协议与六大开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市被车载相机、LiDAR、雷达、路侧单元、无人机、卫星及GNSS轨迹等多平台同时感知，但现有学习表征大多局限于单一平台、单一城市、单一任务，缺乏可跨平台共享与复用的统一状态表示，导致重复建设、难以迁移与系统级评估缺失。

Method: 以USR（地理参照、分层、时变、不确定性感知的状态）为单一透镜组织综述：(i) 形式化USR及五项期望属性，并与BEV特征、占用栅格、矢量化地图、神经场与3D高斯、场景图、路网图、语言token、世界模型潜变量建立关联；(ii) 将传感平台、跨视角（地-空）匹配、融合算子、3D几何基础模型、端到端与视觉-语言-动作模型、生成式世界模型与数据引擎纳入统一分类；(iii) 汇编26个城市数据集与模拟器（自车、协同V2X、空中、轨迹、语言场景），附指标术语表、跨基准结果快照、趋势分析与平台覆盖差距分析；(iv) 综述应用并提出USR-5五测协议（含显式参考与单一聚合分数）及六大开放问题研究议程。

Result: 形成一套跨传感器至决策层的统一分类体系；完成26个数据集的系统性汇编与跨基准结果快照、趋势及平台覆盖缺口分析；提出可量化的USR-5评估协议与聚合分数；给出六项开放问题（跨城市与跨传感器迁移、不确定性、效率、仿真到现实评估、治理等）；并公开论文列表、数据集与USR-5代码。

Conclusion: USR可作为连接计算机视觉、自动驾驶、交通工程与城市计算研究的统一中间表示与评估框架；后续需在跨城市/跨传感器迁移、不确定性建模、效率、sim-to-real评估与治理方面推进，以支撑可共享的城市状态表征。

Abstract: Cities are sensed by many platforms at once: on-board cameras, LiDAR and radar on autonomous vehicles, roadside units at intersections, drones and satellites overhead, and the GNSS traces of millions of trips. Yet most learned representations are built for one platform, one city, and one task. We survey this fragmented landscape through a single lens, the Urban State Representation (USR): a georeferenced, layered, time-varying, uncertainty-aware state that any sensing platform can write to and any downstream task can read from. We (i) formalize USR and five desiderata for it, and relate it to bird's-eye-view (BEV) features, occupancy, vectorized maps, neural fields and 3D Gaussians, scene graphs, road-network graphs, language tokens, and world-model latents; (ii) organize sensing platforms, cross-view (ground-aerial) matching, fusion operators, 3D geometric foundation models, end-to-end and vision-language-action models, and generative world models and data engines in one taxonomy that spans sensors to decisions; (iii) compile 26 urban datasets and simulators across ego, cooperative (V2X), aerial, trajectory, and language settings, with a metric glossary, a cross-benchmark results snapshot, a trend analysis, and a platform-coverage gap analysis; and (iv) review applications and propose, as an outlook, USR-5, a five-test protocol with explicit references and one aggregate score for measuring whether a state is shared, together with a research agenda of six open problems covering cross-city and cross-sensor transfer, uncertainty, efficiency, sim-to-real evaluation, and governance. This survey is an entry point for computer vision, autonomous driving, transportation, and urban computing researchers; the paper list, datasets, and USR-5 code are at https://github.com/binayksingh/Awesome-Urban-State-Representation.

</details>


### [46] [SceneTether: Activity-Gated Value Anchoring for Long-Horizon Autoregressive Image Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28134)
*Matiur Rahman Minar,Seunghun Oh,Ganghyeon Jeong,Unsang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneTether：一种免训练的注意力读取策略，通过保持读取位置不变而改变返回值（低活动区KV向源图混合），解决长时程图像动画中的外观漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程图像动画需保持场景稳定而自然材料（水、烟、火）持续运动。自回归生成器依赖近期帧提供时序连续性，但历史帧累积外观误差；稀疏化历史又会破坏运动。

Method: 免训练注意力读取策略：保留最新latent的全部key；基于首批生成latent估计并冻结的活动图决定混合位置；将低活动value向同位置源图value混合，高活动value保持不变以允许运动演化；不增加去噪评估次数，不改变缓存更新规则。

Result: 在Causal Forcing++上，两seed下source-support PSNR较逐帧Echo提升5.15 dB（后期活动量为其1.5倍）；预选四分钟子集上较空间Echo-Forcing提升7.31 dB；同等近期记忆覆盖下仅value锚定即提升8.14 dB；使用相同活动图的后处理像素合成在静态保真度相当但运动区颜色漂移约为其两倍；盲测中被偏好。

Conclusion: 通过解耦"读取何处"与"返回何值"，SceneTether在不训练、不增加计算的前提下有效抑制长时程外观误差累积，同时保留运动自然性，优于像素级后处理合成。

Abstract: Long-horizon image animation must keep the scene fixed while natural material (e.g., water, smoke, or fire) continues to move. Autoregressive video generators read recent frames for temporal continuity, but this history also carries accumulated appearance errors, and sparsifying it disrupts motion. We introduce SceneTether, a training-free attention-read policy that keeps where attention reads recent memory but changes what appearance that memory returns. SceneTether keeps every key of the newest latent, blends its low-activity values toward co-located source-image values, and leaves high-activity values untouched so motion can evolve; an activity map estimated from the first generated latents and then frozen decides where to blend. It adds no denoising evaluations and leaves the cache-update rule unchanged. On a Causal Forcing++ generator, SceneTether raises source-support PSNR by 5.15 dB over its framewise Echo host across two seeds with 1.5x its late activity, and by 7.31 dB over spatial Echo-Forcing on a preselected four-minute subset. At equal recent-memory coverage, value anchoring alone adds 8.14 dB. A post-hoc pixel composite with the same map matches static fidelity but retains nearly twice the moving-region color drift, and viewers in a small blinded study preferred SceneTether for its natural scene and motion preservation. Project page: https://minar09.github.io/scenetether/

</details>


### [47] [Beyond Output Suppression: Mitigating Privacy Risks in LVLMs via Visual Intrinsic Privacy Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28135)
*Xiangkui Cao,Jie Zhang,Meina Kan,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有对齐式微调/模型编辑只能改变输出分布，无法阻止句补全场景下的隐私泄露；VIPE 通过擦除隐私相关的视觉表征，从感知源头阻断泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型可从图像中提取个人身份信息，现有定向微调与模型编辑仅让模型对齐到"安全回答"，属于输出层防护；作者发现这种保护是"虚假"的——正常解码下模型拒答，但在句补全场景中仍泄露隐私，说明模型仍保有对隐私信息的感知能力，敏感信息仍驻留于模型内部。

Method: 提出 Visual Intrinsic Privacy Erasure（VIPE）：构造隐私敏感图像与隐私掩蔽图像配对，利用二者内部特征残差定位隐私相关的视觉成分，并在其被模型感知之前进行抑制（擦除）。为保持良性表征，擦除目标将特征差投影到松弛的良性子空间，并自适应控制擦除强度。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL 与 LLaVA-OneVision 上，VIPE 显著缓解前缀触发（prefix-triggered）的隐私泄露，同时在通用视觉-语言基准上保持有竞争力的实用性。

Conclusion: 隐私保护应从输出分布层面转向视觉表征层面：擦除模型对敏感信息的感知能力，比仅修改输出分布更根本地封堵泄露来源，且不必牺牲通用能力。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can extract personally identifiable information from images, making privacy protection essential for their deployment in perception-oriented tasks. Existing targeted fine-tuning and model-editing methods usually suppress sensitive answers by aligning the model toward safe responses. In this paper, we show that such protection can be illusory: although the fine-tuned model refuses to disclose private information under normal decoding, privacy leakage still occurs in sentence completion scenarios. This indicates that existing algorithms only change the model's output distribution, but the model still retains the ability to perceive private information. Private information therefore still remains inside the model. To address this problem, we propose Visual Intrinsic Privacy Erasure (VIPE), which targets privacy-related visual representations to weaken the model's ability to perceive sensitive information. VIPE constructs paired privacy-sensitive and privacy-masked images, uses their internal feature residuals to identify privacy-related visual components, and suppresses these components before they are perceived by the model. To preserve benign visual representations, the erasure objective projects feature differences onto a relaxed benign subspace and adaptively controls the erasure strength. VIPE compels the model to ignore the extraction of sensitive information in the visual representation space, thereby blocking the source of privacy leakage more fundamentally rather than merely altering the model's output distribution. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL and LLaVA-OneVision show that VIPE substantially alleviates prefix-triggered privacy leakage while maintaining competitive utility on general visual-language benchmarks.

</details>


### [48] [ENGRAM: Reliability-Weighted Latent Fusion and Occlusion-Aware Layered Readout for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28136)
*Chenhao Wu,Zhaolong Su,Yang Liu,Tianrui Su,Yingguang Yang,Shang Luo,Yancheng Chen,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Kefu Xu,Congjing Ran,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ENGRAM 是一种用于相机条件视频世界模型的潜在空间记忆框架，通过可靠性加权潜在融合与遮挡感知分层读取，替换集合式写入与最前表面读取，实现 3D 一致的场景记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在空间记忆存在两点缺陷：写入端采用集合并集更新，导致重复观测未被合并；读取端仅返回最前表面，无法同时访问不同深度的已存证据。这限制了多表面信息在遮挡变化时的可用性。

Method: 1) 递归融合：在通道归一化潜在空间中按几何与视觉兼容性加权整合观测，将重复证据合并为共享场景表示；2) 分层读取：在每个目标格点输出多个按深度排序的潜在候选，并携带其可靠性与可用性；3) 以显式相机射线提供与记忆覆盖无关的视角信息，配合几何一致性约束指导记忆更新；4) 检索仅在潜在分辨率上做单次投影，避免中间 RGB 渲染与 VAE 重编码；5) 仅用 256 个训练场景微调预训练视频生成器。

Result: 在 RealEstate10K 的 100 个测试场景上，新视角合成与闭环 PSNR 相比 Spatia 分别提升 0.82 dB 与 4.48 dB；在 200 例 WorldScore 子集上平均得分 67.78，超过 Spatia 3.31 分。

Conclusion: 融合式写入与分层读取的潜在记忆设计能有效提升相机条件视频世界模型的 3D 一致性与闭环生成质量，且数据效率高、无需 RGB 中间渲染。

Abstract: Camera-conditioned video world models aim to generate 3D-consistent videos from a single image along specified camera trajectories. However, set-union updates in latent spatial memory leave repeated observations unconsolidated, while frontmost-only readout limits simultaneous access to stored evidence at different depths. To address these limitations, we present ENGRAM, a latent spatial memory framework combining reliability-weighted latent fusion and occlusion-aware layered readout. Specifically, recursive fusion integrates geometrically and visually compatible observations in a channel-normalized latent space, consolidating repeated evidence into a shared scene representation. Layered readout exposes multiple depth-ordered latent candidates with their reliability and availability at each target cell. This gives the generator access to information from multiple surfaces as visibility changes. Explicit camera rays provide viewpoint information independently of memory coverage, while geometric consistency constraints guide memory updates. Memory retrieval uses a single projection at latent resolution, avoiding intermediate RGB rendering and VAE re-encoding. We adapt a pretrained video generator using only 256 training scenes. On 100 RealEstate10K test scenes, ENGRAM improves novel-view-synthesis and closed-loop PSNRs over Spatia by 0.82 and 4.48 dB, respectively. On a 200-case WorldScore evaluation subset, it achieves an average score of 67.78, exceeding Spatia by 3.31 points. Project page: https://wchkoutian.github.io/engram-website/.

</details>


### [49] [Choose What Returns: Object-Aware KV Memory for Selective Recall in Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28137)
*Hyunwook Kang,Junoh Park,Hyunggyu Lim,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出对象感知KV记忆框架，实现交互式长视频生成中的精确选择性记忆召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆方法虽能保留历史内容，但在精确选择性召回上表现不佳：返回错误的person外观、混淆不同人物、或保留未被请求的背景。作者通过分析记忆形式与检索机制来针对性解决这一问题。

Method: 面向冻结的自回归扩散生成器构建对象感知KV记忆：保留被跟踪对象区域的原始KV；对区域外背景按attention-score选取KV；为每条记忆关联可读的源场景与外观记录；由LLM选择所需记录，记忆整合器在当前prompt下注入其KV。

Result: 提出六场景评测，涵盖选择性属性修改、特定时刻外观召回、多对象组合，且每个人的目标历史外观随请求变化。实验表明该框架在显式场景引用与自然语言线索两种条件下，比现有记忆方法更可靠地保持人物身份并复现所请求的历史外观。

Conclusion: 对象级KV记忆结合LLM检索可显著提升长视频生成中的人物身份保持与选择性外观复现能力，并提供了更贴近真实需求的评测设定。

Abstract: Interactive long-video generation lets users build a story through successive prompts and reuse earlier characters and settings under new instructions. Existing memory methods retain past content, yet they often fail at precise selective recall, returning the wrong appearance of a person, mixing different people, or keeping an unrequested background. Based on our analysis of memory form and retrieval, we introduce an object-aware key--value (KV) memory framework for a frozen autoregressive diffusion generator. It keeps the original KV of tracked object regions, selects background KV outside them by attention-scores, and links each memory with a readable record of its source scene and observed appearance. An LLM selects the requested records, and a memory integrator supplies their KV for generation under the current prompt. We also introduce a six-scene evaluation that tests selective attribute modification, time-specific appearance recall, and multi-object composition, in which each person's requested past appearance changes across requests. Experiments show that our framework more reliably preserves each person's identity while reproducing their requested past appearance than existing memory methods, under both explicit scene references and natural-language cues.

</details>


### [50] [Dynamics-Guided Conditional MeanFlow from Regional Interaction to Internal Revision and Its Application to CT Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28139)
*Md Shifatul Ahsan Apurba,Md Selim,Jin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 IRCMF——动力学引导的条件均值流（Conditional MeanFlow）框架，将区域交互与循环内部修正结合，用预测的速度动力学指导瓶颈特征修订，并以 actor-critic 策略按区域自适应控制解码次数，在 CT 域迁移任务上兼顾质量与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件图像增强需要空间变化的校正，但固定深度模型无论何处需要额外细化都分配相同计算预算；一步流模型虽降低推理成本、迭代式复原方法虽引入后续校正，但"如何用预测的变换动力学指导内部特征细化"仍鲜有探索。

Method: 解码器预测带符号的区间速度与可学习的速度变化线索；软区域图聚合上下文以估计速度，动力学条件图建立区域关系以引导循环瓶颈更新；修订后特征经共享解码器重算速度，形成动力学与内部表示间的反馈；由 actor-critic 策略控制区域更新与进一步修订次数，平衡质量与计算开销。

Result: 在 NLST（STANDARD→LUNG 核）与 LDCT-and-Projection-data（模拟低剂量→常规剂量）上，9 次解码器评估时切片级 PSNR 分别达 36.24±3.49 dB / SSIM 0.97±0.01，以及 32.45±1.97 dB / SSIM 0.84±0.05；学习策略将平均解码评估降至 4.42 与 3.45 次，单切片时延由 97.00→51.82 ms、97.12→41.95 ms，PSNR 仅下降 0.20 与 0.06 dB。

Conclusion: 以变换动力学为反馈信号驱动特征级循环修正，并配合强化学习式区域策略，可在显著降低推理延迟的同时基本保持增强质量，为条件图像增强提供自适应计算分配范式。

Abstract: Conditional image enhancement requires spatially varying corrections, yet fixed-depth models allocate similar computational budgets regardless of where additional refinement is needed. Although one-step flow models reduce inference costs and iterative restoration methods introduce subsequent corrections, how predicted transformation dynamics can guide internal feature refinement remains underexplored. We introduce IRCMF, a dynamics-guided Conditional MeanFlow framework that combines regional interaction with recurrent internal revision. The decoder predicts signed interval velocity and a learned velocity-change cue. A soft-region graph aggregates contextual information for velocity estimation, while a dynamics-conditioned graph establishes regional relationships to guide recurrent bottleneck updates. The revised features are processed by the shared decoder to recompute velocity, establishing feedback between transformation dynamics and internal representations. An actor-critic policy controls regional updates and further revisions to balance enhancement quality and computational cost. We evaluate IRCMF on two computed tomography (CT) datasets, NLST for STANDARD-to-LUNG kernel enhancement and LDCT-and-Projection-data for simulated low-to-routine-dose enhancement. With nine decoder evaluations, IRCMF achieves slice-level PSNR of $36.24 \pm 3.49$ dB and SSIM of $0.97 \pm 0.01$ on NLST, and PSNR of $32.45 \pm 1.97$ dB and SSIM of $0.84 \pm 0.05$ on LDCT-and-Projection-data. The learned policy reduces average decoder evaluations to 4.42 and 3.45, respectively, lowering per-slice latency from 97.00 to 51.82 ms and from 97.12 to 41.95 ms, with PSNR reductions of only 0.20 and 0.06 dB.

</details>


### [51] [Close the Loop on the Retrievability Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28146)
*Guangyuan Dong,Wenhao Li,Zhen Tian,Jiaqi Zhang,Haoyu Wang,Junhao Xu,Mingzhe Liu,Pan Wang,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"可检索性差距"：18个全色锐化系统中，$Q2^n$与HQNR评测选出的系统与语言编码器可检索性排序不一致（分别约1/3与1/2概率），根源是位置平均损失的面积加权；提出LEDGER训练框架，以冻结视觉-语言编码器的概念集合软Jaccard覆盖损失作无标签目标，在保真度、检索、分辨率差距、提取与迁移性上全面改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言编码器既用于引导图像复原、又用于索引其输出，但现有保真度指标（有参考的$Q2^n$、无参考的HQNR）与编码器可检索性排序存在系统性不一致，尤其小目标被低可检索性拖累；作者将这一问题归因于位置平均损失的面积加权，使小面积概念梯度占比过低。

Method: LEDGER闭环围绕冻结编码器：将输入索引为命名概念集合；生成输出后仅用同一冻结编码器重新编码；对概念集合覆盖率最小化集合级软Jaccard损失。该目标无需标签、测试时可得，使每个尺度上每个出现概念的梯度份额与其面积无关。评估在环外进行，使用标签锚定探针、留出检索器、无语言评测器、检测器与人工评分。

Result: 在四个传感器上保真度匹配或超过最强基线；词汇内检索提升7–9点、词汇外2–5点；缩小三分之二的降分辨率与参考差距；在四个城市的原始SpaceNet影像上提取效果均超过厂商产品；可作为仅训练损失迁移至现有生成器，无推理开销。

Conclusion: 保真度指标与语义可检索性之间存在可度量的差距，且源于损失面积加权；以概念集合覆盖为训练目标可有效弥合该差距而不依赖标签或在环评估，具备跨传感器、跨生成器的通用性与零推理成本的实用性。

Abstract: A frozen vision-language encoder now guides image restoration and later indexes what it produced. Over a fixed set of eighteen pansharpening systems on one WorldView-III test set, the fidelity index that ranks them, $Q2^n$ with a reference and HQNR without one, picks the less retrievable of two systems one time in three and one in two; the disagreement lies between the pixel-supervised and the language-conditioned family, and even the best system falls short of the reference, small objects first. We call this the retrievability gap and trace it to the area weighting of position-averaged losses. LEDGER closes the loop around the encoder: it indexes the input into a set of named concepts, generates, re-encodes the output alone with the same frozen encoder, and minimises a set-level soft-Jaccard loss on the coverage of that set, a label-free target available at test time, so that every present concept of a scale receives a gradient share independent of its area. Measured outside the loop by label-grounded probes, held-out retrievers, language-free evaluators, detectors and human raters, LEDGER matches or exceeds the strongest baselines in fidelity on four sensors, raises retrieval by seven to nine points within its vocabulary and two to five outside it, closes two thirds of the reduced-resolution gap to the reference, lifts extraction on raw SpaceNet imagery above the vendor product in each of four cities, and transfers to existing generators as a training-only loss at no inference cost.

</details>


### [52] [VideoJSON: A Minimal Harness Beats Purpose-Built Memory for Ultra-Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28167)
*Wenqi Marshall Guo,Kuan-Hao Huang,Junda Chen,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VideoJSON：将超长视频逐分钟转为 JSON 记录，让编码智能体在沙箱中自写 Python/Bash 程序完成多步检索与推理，在四个超长视频基准上显著超越 MAGIC-Video 等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨天/周的超长视频问答需要沿长时序搜寻分散线索，现有视频智能体依赖复杂记忆图和固定检索流水线，限制了智能体的搜索自由度。

Method: 极简 harness：索引阶段用 VLM 将每分钟视频编码为一条 JSON 记录（稠密字幕＋事件、实体、地点、语音转写等结构化字段）；查询阶段智能体在沙箱容器中编写 Python/Bash 程序，逐步完成过滤、关联与读取；无需任务特定训练，也不直接访问原始视频。

Result: DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 加持下，在 EgoLifeQA、Ego-R1-Bench、MM-Lifelong Week 上分别领先 MAGIC-Video 7.0、20.0、24.75 分，并超最佳 EgoMemReason 12.6 分；四种能力递增策略模型中，平均分提升最多 11.58 分（MAGIC-Video 至多 5.50 分）。消融显示智能体代码搜索与结构化 JSON 记录均为增益来源，而增加小时级摘要、额外工具或原始视频访问对均分影响小于 1.5 分。另用 100 小时无关直播的 64 条轨迹微调 Qwen-3.5-Flash，MM-Lifelong 最高提升 8.25 分。

Conclusion: 简单的结构化记忆＋智能体自写代码搜索是超长视频理解的有效范式，且对策略模型能力提升具有良好可扩展性与跨域迁移潜力。

Abstract: Answering questions over days or weeks of video requires searching for scattered clues across long timelines. Existing video agents rely on complex memory graphs and fixed retrieval pipelines, which restrict how the agent can search. We propose VideoJSON, a minimal harness for ultra-long video understanding that keeps the memory simple and lets a coding agent write its own search programs. During indexing, a vision-language model turns each minute of video into a single JSON record containing a dense caption and structured fields such as events, entities, places, and speech transcripts. At query time, the agent writes Python or Bash programs in a sandboxed container to filter, link, and read these records over multiple steps, without task-specific training or raw video access. On four ultra-long video benchmarks, VideoJSON with DeepSeek V4.1 Flash outperforms MAGIC-Video by 7.0, 20.0, and 24.75 points on EgoLifeQA, Ego-R1-Bench, and MM-Lifelong Week, and beats the best prior EgoMemReason score by 12.6 points. Across four policy models of increasing capability, VideoJSON's average score rises by up to 11.58 points over the weakest model, compared with at most 5.50 points for MAGIC-Video. Ablations show that both agentic code search and the structured JSON records drive these gains, while adding hourly summaries, extra tools, or video access changes the average score by less than 1.5 points. In addition, fine-tuning Qwen-3.5-Flash on 64 VideoJSON trajectories from an unrelated 100-hour livestream improves its MM-Lifelong scores by up to 8.25 points. Code will be open-sourced after publication.

</details>


### [53] [Med-Agentic: Distilling Agentic Medical Reasoning with Internalized Meta-Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28176)
*Yucheng Zhou,Junwei Sheng,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"内化元能力"(internalized meta-capabilities)：将原子化、参数化内化、可组合的推理单元在单条思维链(CoT)中由LLM自行选择与组合，并以Med-Agentic在医学视觉诊断上实例化——一次前向传播输出带能力标签的结构化CoT，把"调用工具"变为"生成标签token"；训练用Med-OPD多教师在线策略蒸馏，按图像与当前能力标签做token级双因素教师路由，证明其为同一教师KL梯度期望的无偏估计。在9个解剖部位、8种模态的公开医学影像数据上，分布内与分布外诊断准确率均稳定优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家领域LLM存在两难：agentic工具使用把专业知识外置到脆弱的多轮运行时；端到端专业化则只在任务级粒度上隐式内化知识。二者分别损失鲁棒性与细粒度可控性。

Method: 提出内化元能力概念：原子、参数化内化、可组合的推理单元，可在单条CoT内选择与组合。实例化为Med-Agentic：单次前向输出带能力标签的结构化CoT，将能力调用转为标签token生成而非外部工具调用。训练采用Med-OPD多教师在线策略蒸馏，以图像+当前能力标签为条件的token级双因素教师路由，严格推广了按prompt路由，并给出其为同一期望教师-KL梯度无偏估计的证明。

Result: 在覆盖9个解剖部位、8种模态的公开医学影像数据集上，Med-Agentic在分布内与分布外诊断准确率上均一致优于强对比方法。

Conclusion: 以标签token生成替代外部工具调用、结合token级双因素多教师蒸馏，可在单次前向内化并组合专家能力，兼顾可控性与鲁棒性，为专家领域LLM提供无需多轮运行时的专业化范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) in expert domains face a trade-off between agentic tool use, which externalizes expertise behind brittle multi-turn runtimes, and end-to-end specialization, which internalizes expertise only implicitly at task-level granularity. We propose internalized meta-capabilities: atomic, parametrically internalized, and composable reasoning units that an LLM can select and combine within a single chain-of-thought (CoT). We instantiate this idea as Med-Agentic for medical visual diagnosis, where the model emits a capability-tagged structured CoT in one forward pass, turning capability invocation into tagged token generation rather than external tool calls. For training, Med-OPD performs multi-teacher on-policy distillation with token-level dual-factor teacher routing conditioned on both the image and the active capability tag, strictly generalizing per-prompt teacher routing. We show that this routing gives an unbiased estimator of the same expected teacher-KL gradient. Across public medical imaging datasets spanning nine anatomies and eight modalities, Med-Agentic consistently improves in-distribution and out-of-distribution diagnostic accuracy over the strong competitors.

</details>


### [54] [Don't Test Where You Trained: Geographic Overlap Inflates End-to-End Driving Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28177)
*Willi Poh,Kevin Rösch,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NAVSIMv1 测试集中 89.6% 场景位于训练场景 4m 范围内，这种地理重叠抬高了报告分数；作者提出地理不相交划分，发现感知模型在未见场景上 PDMS 下降 3.4–6.1 分（无传感器基线仅 1.5 分），差距集中于训练轨迹曾经过的位置，且模型能预测相机不可见的路面几何，说明其记忆了地点而非泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶基准的评测分数可能被测试场景与训练场景的地理重叠所污染，需要量化这种重叠对指标的影响，并提供能衡量"对新地点泛化"的评测协议。

Method: 构建 NAVSIMv1 的地理不相交划分（geographic split），并在同一批双方均未见过的新场景上比较"原始划分训练"与"地理划分训练"的模型；对 DiffusionDrive、Latent TransFuser、DrivoR 及无传感器基线对比 PDMS，并结合地图损失消融与 nuScenes 上 SparseDrive 的建图/规划实验。

Result: 基于传感器的模型 PDMS 下降 3.4–6.1 分（主要为驶出道路、逼近其他物体），无传感器基线仅 1.5 分；性能损失集中于原划分训练轨迹曾经过之处，其余地点两模型无差异；模型可预测相机不可见的路面几何，移除辅助地图损失后差距仍存在；nuScenes 上地理划分使 SparseDrive 建图精度下降 59% 并恶化规划。

Conclusion: 基于日志场景的驾驶分数部分度量的是模型对测试地点的记忆而非泛化能力；作者发布该地理划分，用于评测模型对新地点的泛化性能。

Abstract: In NAVSIMv1, the standard benchmark for end-to-end driving, $89.6\%$ of the test scenes lie within 4m of a training scene. We show that this geographic overlap inflates the reported scores. We propose a geographically disjoint split of NAVSIMv1 and an evaluation that compares models trained on either split on the same scenes, which neither has seen in training. All sensor-based models we test, DiffusionDrive, Latent TransFuser, and DrivoR, lose 3.4 to 6.1 PDMS points on these scenes, mainly by leaving the road and coming close to other objects, against 1.5 points for a baseline without sensors. The loss concentrates where a training drive of the original split passed the same place. At all other places, models trained on the original split are no better than those trained on the geographic split. Models trained on the original split predict road geometry that no camera can see. The gap persists when the auxiliary map loss is removed, so the models memorize locations even without being supervised to. On nuScenes, the geographic split cuts SparseDrive's mapping accuracy by $59\%$ and also degrades its planning. Driving scores on logged scenes thus partly measure how well a model remembers the test locations. We release the split to evaluate generalization to new places.

</details>


### [55] [BReD: BEV Residual Diffusion for Feed-Forward Road Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28191)
*Baizhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BReD是一个前馈式框架，无需测试时优化即可从六相机序列重建路面高程、外观与语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路面重建对自动驾驶建图与仿真至关重要，但现有方法在重建质量与跨场景推理之间存在权衡：优化类方法需逐场景昂贵优化，前馈类方法缺乏道路特定约束、高程误差大。

Method: 先用LiDAR监督的深度学习与BEV提升获得粗高程（推理仅用相机）；再用轻量BEV残差扩散模型，以相机导出的深度—高程差异和含高程信息的图像特征为条件细化高程；训练时用扩散索引扰动模拟不同程度差异损坏，推理时从干净差异做一次确定性细化；细化后高程时序聚合以锚定道路对齐高斯，其属性由几何先验引导解码器预测，无需测试时优化。

Result: 在四个留出的nuScenes场景上，BReD达到7.34 cm高程RMSE和90.92%语义mIoU；对比RoGS无测试时LiDAR为14.43 cm，其测试时LiDAR辅助变体为8.88 cm，且两者均需逐场景优化。

Conclusion: BReD以纯相机前馈推理实现了优于需逐场景优化方法的路面高程与语义重建精度，兼顾了重建质量与跨场景泛化。

Abstract: Accurate road surface reconstruction is essential for autonomous-driving mapping and simulation, but existing methods trade reconstruction quality for cross-scene inference. Optimization-based methods recover detailed surfaces through costly per-scene optimization, while feed-forward methods lack road-specific constraints and often produce large elevation errors. We propose \textbf{BReD}, a feed-forward framework for reconstructing road elevation, appearance, and semantics from calibrated six-camera sequences. BReD first estimates coarse elevation through LiDAR-supervised depth learning and BEV lifting, using only cameras at inference. A lightweight BEV residual diffusion model then conditions elevation refinement on a camera-derived depth--elevation discrepancy and elevation-aware image features. Diffusion-indexed perturbations expose the model to different discrepancy corruption levels during training, while inference performs one deterministic refinement from the clean discrepancy. The refined elevations are temporally aggregated to anchor road-aligned Gaussians, whose attributes are predicted by a geometry-prior-guided decoder without test-time optimization. On four held-out nuScenes scenes, BReD achieves 7.34 cm elevation RMSE and 90.92\% semantic mIoU, compared with 14.43 cm for RoGS without test-time LiDAR and 8.88 cm for its test-time-LiDAR-assisted variant; both RoGS variants require per-scene optimization.

</details>


### [56] [FastD4RT: Training-Free Acceleration of Dynamic 4D Reconstruction and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28193)
*Meng Bi,Yili Jin,Kaiyuan Hu,Xize Duan,Jiaxin Huang,Hong Huang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastD4RT：一种免训练框架，通过稀疏锚点编码解码+插值恢复、段内共享token集合，将D4RT推理加速约5倍且精度有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D4RT类基于查询的4D重建模型推理开销大：稠密输出需为每帧每像素各做一次解码查询，长视频被编码为大量重叠分段，且作者发现其中大量计算是冗余的（后续解码块仅关注所有查询共享的少量token）。

Method: 训练-free加速框架：①利用输出在时间与像素维度上的平滑性，仅在稀疏锚点上进行编码与解码，其余输出通过插值恢复；②每段只选择一次共享token集合，其余查询统一对该集合解码；③对两个组件引入的误差进行理论刻画。

Result: 在所有任务上平均加速约5倍，精度具竞争力；在长视频上精度进一步提升。

Conclusion: D4RT的推理成本存在显著冗余，稀疏锚点+插值及共享token集合可在无需训练的情况下高效复用其编码-解码解耦结构，实现高效4D重建。

Abstract: Query-based 4D reconstruction models such as D4RT encode a video once into a global scene representation and obtain outputs by decoding independent point queries. However, inference is often influenced by dense outputs and long videos. Although D4RT separates encoding from decoding, a dense output still requires one decoder query for every pixel of every frame, and a long video is still encoded as many overlapping segments. In this work, we analyze the inference cost of D4RT and show that a large part of it is redundant. In particular, we find that the later decoder blocks attend to a small set of tokens shared by all queries. Based on these findings, we propose FastD4RT, a training-free framework that accelerates D4RT. By exploiting the smoothness of the outputs over time and across pixels, FastD4RT encodes and decodes only at sparse anchors and recovers the remaining outputs via interpolation. It decodes the remaining queries against a shared token set selected once per segment. We further characterize the errors introduced by both components. Experimental results show that FastD4RT accelerates D4RT by about 5 times on average across all its tasks with competitive accuracy, and further improves accuracy on long videos. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lycheemeng/fastd4rt.

</details>


### [57] [Video-as-Weights: Compiling a Video Once into Parametric and Token Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28221)
*Wentao Zhang,Ian Miao,Chen Qu,Bill Bi,Donna Schut,Yuntian Deng,Yale Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Video-as-Weights (VaW)：用一个编译器 VLM 将视频一次性"编译"为冻结解释器 VLM 的 LoRA 权重与可选前缀 token，实现同模型编译与强到弱迁移，在六个视频 QA 基准上以极少显存超越全长上下文读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 将视频表示为数千视觉 token，KV cache 随视频长度线性增长，显存开销大；同时可部署在有限硬件上的小 VLM 的视频理解能力远逊于大模型。现有 token 压缩方法仅把视觉 token 压缩为短提示，但小模型仍以固定权重处理，无法弥合能力差距。

Method: VaW 在未见任何问题的情况下将视频一次性编译进冻结解释器 VLM 的参数与 token 记忆：编译器 VLM 观看视频，单一权重解码器联合地从编译器每一层生成解释器的 LoRA 权重及可选前缀 token，支持同模型编译及跨模型规模与深度的强到弱迁移。

Result: 六个视频 QA 基准上，0.8B 解释器自编译较全长上下文读取提升 1.7 分，使用 9B 编译器时提升 4.3 分；上下文由最多 8,192 视觉 token 压缩至 64 前缀 token，显存占用比视频 KV cache 少 5.5 倍。生成权重相较纯 token 压缩额外带来 3.9 分增益；全层解码优于逐层生成 2.9 分。

Conclusion: 将视频编译为权重是缓解长视频 KV cache 开销并实现强到弱能力迁移的有效范式；机制分析表明：有前缀 token 时权重主要改变注意力 query 与 MLP down projection，用于引导检索并存储基线摘要；无 token 时权重转向注意力输出投影，将视频内容直接写入残差流。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) represent videos as thousands of visual tokens, creating a large key-value cache whose memory grows with video length. At the same time, small VLMs that fit on limited hardware understand video far worse than large models. Existing compression methods shorten visual tokens into a brief prompt to save memory, yet the small model still processes them through fixed weights and cannot close this capability gap.
  To address both challenges, we introduce Video-as-Weights (VaW), which compiles a video once without seeing any question into the parametric and token memory of a frozen interpreter VLM. Specifically, a compiler VLM watches the video, and a single weight decoder jointly generates the interpreter's LoRA weights and optional prefix tokens from every compiler layer, enabling both same-model compilation and strong-to-weak transfer across model sizes and depths.
  Across six video QA benchmarks, a 0.8B interpreter beats full in-context reading by 1.7 points when compiled by itself and by 4.3 points with a 9B compiler, reducing context from up to 8,192 visual tokens to 64 prefix tokens while using 5.5x less memory than the video's key-value cache. Generated weights add 3.9 points over token compression alone, and all-layer decoding outperforms layer-wise generation by 2.9 points. Mechanistic analysis shows that with prefix tokens, the weights mainly alter attention queries and MLP down projections to guide retrieval and store a baseline summary, whereas without tokens, they shift to attention output projections to write video content directly into the residual stream.

</details>


### [58] [A Closed-Form Temporal Readout for Surgical Triplet Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28240)
*Lemar Abdi,Amaan Valiuddin,Fida Mohammad Thoker,Karen Sanchez,Bernard Ghanem,Fons van der Sommen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 STRIDE：一种后处理式时间读出模块，为任意已训练的手术三元组识别器添加时间上下文，通过固定时间窗对特征与logits做平均并用岭回归（CPU闭式解）与当前帧融合，在9种方法、13种编码器上普遍提升 AP_IVT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术三元组识别需同时判定"哪个器械对哪个解剖目标执行哪个动作"，并将其随时间演化进行跟踪。难点在于：多个三元组同时发生、各组件持续时间差异极大、单帧证据往往不完整。现有识别器多为逐帧决策，缺乏时间上下文聚合。

Method: STRIDE 为与模型无关的后处理读出：对识别器的特征与logits在若干固定时间窗内取平均，再与当前帧表示通过岭回归融合，回归系数在 CPU 上以闭式解求得；作为即插即用模块接在已有识别器输出之后。此外提出"适配的视觉基础模型编码器 + 线性分类器 + STRIDE"的简洁流水线。

Result: 作为即插即用读出应用于9种现有方法，27个方法-数据集组合中24个的 AP_IVT 提升，最高 +7.4；在13种编码器上，39个编码器-数据集组合中37个提升，最高 +7.2。误报率降低41%，代价是交互边界处响应变慢。

Conclusion: 简单的后处理时间聚合即可显著提升手术三元组识别性能，且具良好通用性；主要权衡是边界时间响应延迟，适合作为低成本通用增强手段。

Abstract: Surgical triplet recognition requires identifying which instrument performs which action on which anatomical target, and following these interactions as they unfold over time. The task is challenging because several triplets can occur at once, their components span very different durations, and the evidence for an interaction is rarely complete in a single frame. We introduce STRIDE, a post-hoc temporal readout that adds temporal context to any trained recogniser. It averages the recogniser's features and logits over a few fixed temporal windows and combines them with the current frame through ridge regression, solved in closed form on a CPU. Applied as a plug-and-play readout to nine existing methods, STRIDE improves $AP_{IVT}$ in 24 of 27 method-dataset pairs, by up to 7.4 points. Moreover, we propose a simple pipeline consisting of an adapted foundation vision encoder with a linear classifier and STRIDE. Across thirteen encoders, STRIDE improves 37 of 39 encoder-dataset pairs by up to 7.2 points. STRIDE reduces false alarms by 41%, at the cost of a slower response at interaction boundaries. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [59] [DeFlowCritic: Dense Latent Reward Alignment for Text-to-Image Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28265)
*Zeeshan Khan,Xin Yu,Shizhe Chen,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeFlowCritic：基于稠密潜在奖励对齐的在线强化学习框架，用扩散模型内部特征构建critic对噪声潜变量做早期监督，在提示词忠实度与视觉质量间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线RL方法仅用最终解码图像的稀疏奖励，导致去噪轨迹上的信用分配滞后、训练代价高；且多奖励简单加权难以获得可靠的Pareto权衡（构图准确性与美学质量此消彼长）。

Method: 提出Dense Latent Reward Alignment，在中间潜变量上提供稠密监督，实现去噪轨迹的早期引导。引入内部critic，利用直接从扩散模型提取的特征评估带噪潜变量（比标准图像编码器更适合潜空间奖励估计）。构建带连续分数的高质量奖励建模数据集用于critic训练，捕捉高质量参考图上的构图正确性；该稠密奖励同时支持高效在线RL训练与推理时扩展。

Result: 在GenEval2与T2I-CompBench++上，提示词忠实度与视觉质量之间的平衡优于对比方法；方法可泛化至SD3.5-Medium、SD3.5-Large与FLUX.1-dev等文生图流匹配模型。

Conclusion: 稠密潜在空间奖励监督配合模型内部critic，可有效缓解稀疏奖励的信用分配与多奖励权衡问题，为流匹配文生图模型的在线RL对齐提供高效且可泛化的方案。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for aligning text-to-image flow matching models with complex user intent. However, existing methods typically rely on sparse rewards computed only from final decoded images. This delays credit assignment across the denoising trajectory and makes training computationally expensive. Moreover, simply combining multiple rewards does not reliably produce a Pareto trade-off, where gains in compositional accuracy often degrade aesthetic quality. In this work, we propose DeFlowCritic, an efficient online RL framework based on Dense Latent Reward Alignment. Unlike prior work, our method provides dense supervision on intermediate latents, enabling early-stage guidance for the denoising trajectory. To achieve this, we introduce an internal critic that evaluates noisy latents using features extracted directly from the diffusion model, a representation inherently better suited for latent-space reward estimation than standard image encoders. We construct a high-quality reward-modeling dataset with continuous scores for critic training, capturing compositional correctness on high-quality reference images. The resulting dense reward enables efficient online RL training and inference-time scaling. Experiments show that our method achieves a superior balance between prompt faithfulness and visual quality on GenEval2 and T2I-CompBench++ benchmarks. The approach also generalizes across text-to-image flow matching models, including SD3.5-Medium, SD3.5-Large, and FLUX.1-dev.

</details>


### [60] [eSPG: The Unreasonable Effectiveness of Event Speckle Plethysmography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28275)
*Aniket Dashpute,Anirudh Bindiganavale Harish,Bhargav Ghanekar,Alankar Kotwal,Andrew Negrut,Syna Gupta,Pablo A. Valdes,Ashok Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出事件散斑血流容积描记术（eSPG），用低功率激光照射身体部位并以事件相机捕获动态散斑，实现1 km外非接触心率测量（MAE 5 bpm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非接触生命体征（心率）测量方法受环境光照与运动干扰，且仅在数十米距离下得到验证，远距离鲁棒监测能力不足。

Method: 以0.5–5 mW低功率激光照射身体部位产生动态散斑，用事件相机（高动态范围、高时间分辨率、对强度变化敏感）记录散斑快速波动，从中解码生理信号并估计心率；同时通过理论分析与实验表征其对运动和环境光照的鲁棒性。

Result: 在1 km距离实现心率估计，平均绝对误差5 bpm，90%的估计满足AAMI心率估计标准；测距较既往远程血流容积描记提升一个数量级，较既往散斑血流容积描记提升三个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 解析动态散斑波动可在公里级距离实现准确心率估计，为鲁棒、非接触生理监测开辟新方向。

Abstract: Non-contact measurement of vital signs such as heart rate (HR) is valuable for patient monitoring and rescue operations. However, existing methods struggle with ambient illumination and motion, and have been demonstrated only at distances of tens of meters. Here we introduce event speckle plethysmography (eSPG), which illuminates a body part with a low-power (0.5-5 mW) laser and captures the resulting dynamic speckle pattern using an event camera. Event cameras, with their high dynamic range, temporal resolution, and sensitivity to intensity changes, are well-suited to capturing rapid speckle fluctuations that encode the physiological signal, even under challenging illumination and imaging conditions. We demonstrate HR estimation at 1 km with a mean absolute error of 5 beats per minute, and 90% of estimates satisfy an AAMI-defined HR estimation criterion. This distance exceeds previous remote plethysmography demonstrations by an order of magnitude and previous speckle plethysmography demonstrations by over three orders of magnitude. We also study and characterize robustness to motion and ambient illumination. Our theoretical analysis and experimental results demonstrate that resolving dynamic speckle fluctuations enables accurate heart rate estimation even at kilometer-scale distances, establishing a new direction toward robust, non-contact physiological monitoring.

</details>


### [61] [Learning a Rule Bank to Revise 3D CT Reports with Independent Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28281)
*Renjie Liang,Mengxian Lyu,Zijian Xu,Boxin Zhao,Yang Yang,Jinqian Pan,Zhengkang Fan,Mei Liu,Yonghui Wu,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种智能体系统，为每个报告模型学习以可执行规则库表示的修订策略，利用独立影像证据对3D胸部CT报告进行局部修订，在CT-RATE上CE-F1提升11.5分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型生成的放射学报告可能遗漏异常或错误陈述发现；引入独立图像分析模型可提供额外观察，但盲目采纳不确定证据会引入新错误，因此需要可控的修订机制。

Method: 构建智能体系统，借鉴经验库思想，在开发集上通过修订结果反馈提出并精炼规则，形成每个报告模型专属的可执行规则库；推理时用保留规则结合独立分类器预测与定量影像测量指导局部编辑，不重训报告模型，未编辑句子保持不变，每处修改可溯源到触发规则。

Result: CT-RATE上，CE-F1对SelfCue-KD提升11.5分、对NV-Reason-CT提升0.9分，且在五个草稿采样种子下均有增益；SelfCue-KD的CT-FineBench分数提升15.2分。仅基于草稿的规则学习增益有限，两类影像证据源在异常检测上互补，测量值还补充了草稿缺失的数值细节。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学习模型专属规则可有效控制独立影像证据如何参与报告修订，兼顾修正遗漏与避免引入新错误，并具备可解释性。

Abstract: Vision--language models can generate fluent radiology reports from 3D chest CT scans, yet they may still omit abnormalities or misstate findings. Independent image analysis using models separate from the report model provides additional observations, but acting on uncertain evidence may introduce new errors. We propose an agent system that learns a revision policy represented as an executable rule bank for each report model. Inspired by experience banks, the agent proposes and refines rules using revision outcomes across development cases. During inference, the retained rules guide local edits using independent classifier predictions and quantitative image measurements without retraining the report model. Unedited sentences are preserved, and each change is linked to the rule that triggered it. On CT-RATE, revision increases CE-F1 by 11.5 points for SelfCue-KD and 0.9 points for NV-Reason-CT, with gains across five draft-sampling seeds. SelfCue-KD's CT-FineBench score also increases by 15.2 points. Draft-only rule learning yields limited gains, while the two image-evidence sources provide complementary benefits for abnormality detection. Measurements also supply numerical details missing from the drafts. These results support learning model-specific rules to control how independent image evidence informs report revision. Code is available at https://github.com/renjie-liang/CT-RuleBank.

</details>


### [62] [HandXFM: Semantic-Structural Distillation for Hand Radiograph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28283)
*Yuxi Long,Ganlin Feng,Lianghong Chen,Liam J. O'Neil,Carol A. Hitchon,Pingzhao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HandXfm——手部X光专用基础模型，通过多专家蒸馏融合语义与结构先验，统一支持骨龄评估、评分预测、异常检测、关节定位、骨分割与VQA等多任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部X光可支撑骨成熟度评估、类风湿关节炎评分、异常筛查与解剖定位等多种临床任务，但现有模型通常针对单一任务与单一数据集分别训练，缺乏可迁移的通用表示，且知识来源孤立。

Method: 构建领域专用基础模型：从BiomedCLIP蒸馏语义先验，从胸部X光ViT蒸馏结构线索，通过统一对齐框架将两种互补医学知识以可解释方式整合进单一预训练模型；预训练后可适配骨龄估计、SvH评分预测、异常检测、关节定位、骨分割及视觉问答等任务。

Result: 在多项任务上一致优于任务专用与单专家基线：骨龄预测Pearson相关系数最高提升0.11，异常分类准确率提升12%；可泛化到未见数据集，并生成将临床术语与解剖区域对应的交叉注意力图。

Conclusion: 多专家蒸馏能够将语义监督与结构监督有效统一于单一预训练模型，使HandXfm成为可解释、可泛化的手部X光分析基础模型。

Abstract: Hand radiographs support diverse clinical tasks, including skeletal maturity assessment, rheumatoid arthritis scoring, abnormality screening, and anatomical localization, yet existing models are typically trained separately for each task and dataset. We present HandXFM, a domain-specific foundation model that leverages cross-modality knowledge for comprehensive hand X-ray understanding. HandXFM distills semantic priors from BiomedCLIP and structural cues from a chest X-ray Vision Transformer (ViT) through a unified alignment framework, integrating complementary medical knowledge in an interpretable way. After pretraining, HandXFM can be adapted to a range of musculoskeletal tasks, including bone age estimation, SvH score prediction, abnormality detection, joint localization, bone segmentation, and visual question answering. It consistently outperforms task-specific and single-expert baselines, achieving up to 0.11 improvement in Pearson correlation coefficient (PCC) for bone age prediction and a 12\% accuracy gain in abnormality classification. Moreover, HandXFM generalizes to unseen datasets and yields cross-attention maps linking clinical terms to anatomical regions. These results demonstrate that multi-expert distillation effectively unifies semantic and structural supervision within a single pretrained model, establishing HandXFM as an interpretable and generalizable foundation model for hand radiograph analysis.

</details>


### [63] [When Video Verifier Scores Mislead: Capability Assessment and Verifier Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28287)
*Charles Weng,Zixi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频验证器的评测分数高度依赖"负样本类型"与"判断接口"两个测试选择，足以反转关于模型能力与验证器选型的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频验证器以单一分数被筛选与部署，但该分数未区分测试设计中的关键变量，导致对模型能力与验证器优劣的判断可能失真。

Method: 采用具有精确真值条件的受控构造：端点匹配负样本（仅内部状态错误）、真值切换测试（同一对候选在两段视频间交换正确性）、分阶段输入定位失败步骤；并在人工验证的真实视频上，以统一协议比较直接判断与先写推理再作答两种接口。

Result: 直接判断下，能拒斥明显错误起点的模型在端点匹配负样本上接近随机；帧中明确展示事件时，先写推理使四个模型中三个在真值切换测试上从<10%升至70–100%；分阶段输入将失败定位到"把事件组合为状态"这一步；但在需先定位事件的真实视频上，同一改动无显著增益，且当候选项否认事件发生时可降低准确率。验证器选择亦随负样本类型变化：基于事件否认负样本选出的验证器，在"保留事件、改动细节"负样本上低8.9分，重排序增益约为一半。

Conclusion: 验证器应在其实际会面对的误差类型上评估；低直接分数或推理带来的增益都不能被视为模型本身的固有属性。

Abstract: Video verifiers judge whether a candidate answer or trajectory agrees with a video, and are themselves chosen by a score. We show that this score depends on two choices in the test, the type of negative and the judgment interface, strongly enough to reverse conclusions about what a model can do and which verifier to deploy. We separate the two with controlled constructions that have exact truth conditions: endpoint-matched negatives, wrong only in their internal states, and a truth-switch test, in which the same two candidates swap correctness between two videos. Under direct judgment, where the verdict is read from answer scores, models that reject a visibly wrong start fall to near chance against endpoint-matched negatives. When the frames disclose each event, writing out reasoning before the answer lifts three of four models from under 10% to 70-100% on the truth-switch test, and staged inputs trace the direct failure to one step, composing events into states. But when the event must first be found, on human-validated natural video, the same change brings no significant gain under a protocol common to all models and can lower accuracy if one candidate denies that the event occurs. The negative type also changes which verifier is selected: one chosen on event-denial negatives is 8.9 points less accurate against negatives that keep the event and change a detail, the errors a generator mostly makes, and recovers about half as much accuracy when it reranks that generator's answers. A verifier should be evaluated on the errors it will face, and neither a low direct score nor a gain from reasoning should be read as a property of the model alone.

</details>


### [64] [Task-Agnostic Incremental Vision-Language Object Detection via Prompt Augmentation and Distribution-Aware Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28291)
*Yonghan Jiang,Zhengyuan Xie,Wenchu Liu,Linlan Huang,Fei Yang,Xialei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出任务无关增量视觉-语言目标检测（TA-IVLOD）设定，并设计模块化框架TADA，通过随机提示增强与测试时分布感知融合解决跨任务语义干扰与参数冲突，在ODinW-13上显著优于基线且保持MS COCO零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增量视觉-语言目标检测（IVLOD）方法在推理时依赖已知的任务身份（oracle task identities），严重限制其开放世界实用性；而移除该先验后，跨任务语义干扰与参数冲突会导致性能严重下降。

Method: 提出模块化框架TADA：1）Stochastic Prompt Augmentation，通过训练期噪声注入缓解跨任务语义干扰；2）Test-Time Distribution-Aware Fusion，在测试时动态加权类别特定的LoRA专家，以解决参数冲突。

Result: 在标准IVLOD设定下显著优于基线；在更严格的TA-IVLOD设定下于ODinW-13基准取得大幅提升，同时在MS COCO上保持有效的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将IVLOD推广至无需任务先验的TA-IVLOD现实设定，TADA以噪声注入式提示增强与分布感知的LoRA专家融合有效应对语义干扰和参数冲突，兼顾增量学习与零样本泛化。

Abstract: Incremental Vision-Language Object Detection (IVLOD) empowers pre-trained open-vocabulary detectors to continuously learn new visual concepts from sequential tasks without forgetting foundational knowledge. However, existing methods predominantly rely on oracle task identities during inference, severely limiting their open-world practicality. In this paper, we formalize Task-Agnostic IVLOD (TA-IVLOD), a more realistic setting where such priors are unavailable, requiring the model to simultaneously recognize classes from all learned tasks. This unconstrained setting exposes severe performance degradation due to cross-task semantic interference and parameter conflicts. To tackle these challenges, we propose TADA, a novel modular framework. Specifically, TADA introduces Stochastic Prompt Augmentation to mitigate semantic interference via training-time noise injection, and Test-Time Distribution-Aware Fusion to dynamically weight class-specific LoRA experts, effectively resolving parameter conflicts. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms baselines in standard IVLOD and yields substantial improvements in the rigorous TA-IVLOD setting on the ODinW-13 benchmark, while effectively preserving zero-shot generalization on the MS COCO dataset. The code is available at https://github.com/yonghanjiang/TADA.

</details>


### [65] [Click and Count: Multimodal Prototype Adaptation for Class-Agnostic Object Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28321)
*Amani Aifi,Wassim Bouachir,Ahmed Zgaren,Nizar Bouguila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态、类别无关、基于密度的目标计数框架，用点击生成掩码作视觉示例并可融合文本描述，在迭代原型精化中逐步融合两模态，达到最优计数精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计数方法分两类：融合手绘框与文本两模态的方法，以及仅依赖框示例或仅文本的单模态方法；近期少数方法用点/点击替代框，但仍缺乏将点击精确示例化并与文本在精化全过程中融合的统一框架。

Method: 将单次点击经冻结的视觉提示分割模型扩展为精确目标掩码，输入迭代式原型精化过程；提出点击派生示例提取模块（去除框标注需求）与逐步文本融合机制（每个精化步融合视觉与文本提示）。

Result: 结合文本引导后计数精度达SOTA，在所有指标上优于先前基于点击的计数方法，并在平均绝对误差上超过框示例基线架构。

Conclusion: 验证了点击可替代边界框作为精确视觉示例，且在整个迭代精化中持续融合文本能显著提升类别无关计数性能。

Abstract: We propose a multimodal, class-agnostic, density-based object counting framework that specifies the object of interest through visual exemplars, a text description, or both, within a single iterative refinement pipeline. Existing counters typically fall into two groups: methods that fuse two modalities, a hand-drawn bounding box and a text description, to specify the target object, and single-modality methods that rely on either a box exemplar or text alone. A few recent methods replace the box with a point or click. We expand a single click into a precise object mask via a frozen visual-prompt segmentation model, feeding an iterative prototype-refinement process that also fuses a text description at every refinement step. We make two contributions: first, a click-derived exemplar extraction module that removes the bounding-box requirement of prior exemplar-based counters; second, a per-step text-fusion mechanism that combines visual and textual prompts throughout refinement. Combined with text guidance, our model achieves state-of-the-art counting accuracy, outperforming prior click-based counting methods across all metrics and surpassing the box-exemplar base architecture in mean absolute error. The code is available at github.com/ameniifi/Click-and-Count.

</details>


### [66] [GlaucomaMarker - Biomarker-Grounded Glaucoma Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28332)
*Ahammed Sakir Nabil,Sadia Siraz,Nikita Chincholkar,Atalie C. Thompson,Jason Xiao,Theodore Leng,Jennifer I. Lim,Minhaj Nur Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GlaucomaMarker 框架，将公开分割掩膜转换为描述11种生物标志物的句子，用于训练视网膜基础模型的视觉-语言能力，从而提升青光眼诊断与标志物识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型（VLM）用于青光眼时多依赖固定模板或AI生成文本，缺乏描述生物标志物的公开文本，导致对临床关键征象的语义对齐不足。

Method: 将公开分割掩膜转换为描述11种视网膜结构生物标志物的自然语言句子，构建9,778张图像、45,190个图文对的训练集，并分别应用于RETFound（GlaucomaMarker-R）和FLAIR（GlaucomaMarker-F）。

Result: 在真实临床队列上，GlaucomaMarker-F 检测青光眼的 AUROC 达 0.905，优于 FLAIR 的 0.848；两个模型均能正确读取生物标志物，而基线模型接近随机猜测。

Conclusion: 基于分割掩膜生成的生物标志物文本可有效为青光眼VLM提供结构接地，且无额外标注成本，具有临床可行性。

Abstract: Glaucoma is a leading cause of irreversible blindness. In the current clinical setting, glaucoma diagnosis is generally conducted by assessing optic disc cupping and asymmetry. Vision-language models (VLMs) have been utilized in recent years for glaucoma, mostly trained on fixed templates or AI-generated captions, as no public text describes the biomarkers. Our proposed framework, GlaucomaMarker, hypothesizes that retinal structural biomarkers can ground glaucoma-based VLMs. It converts public segmentation masks into sentences describing eleven biomarkers, to teach retinal foundation models to read them. We applied this framework to RETFound (GlaucomaMarker-R) and FLAIR (GlaucomaMarker-F), training on 9,778 images that yielded 45,190 image-text pairs. On a real-world clinical cohort, GlaucomaMarker-F detects glaucoma with an AUROC of 0.905, against 0.848 for FLAIR, and both read the biomarkers where baselines stay near random guessing. Since the biomarker text is computed from existing masks, it comes at no extra cost.

</details>


### [67] [Alignment under Hard Negative Re-Pairing: Cross-Modal Similarity, Scale and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28336)
*Charles Weng,Alexander Martin,Sandra Arcos-Holzinger,Andrew Yates,Dixin Zheng,Yue Zhang,Landi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CKA等跨模态相似性诊断是"多近邻池化"的尺度统计量，不能回答"模型是否给图像自身caption高分"，做模型选择时应改用验证集recall，并用hard negative re-pairing检验诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文指出"视觉与语言模型是否表征相似的世界"实为三个问题：各模态自身结构、两模态是否以相同方式组织图文、以及模型是否给图像自有caption高分。CKA与邻域一致性只回答第二个问题，但现有工作在图文匹配与模型选择中把它们当作能识别个体配对并回答第三个问题来用。作者要检验这一误读。

Method: 提出hard negative re-pairing（硬负样本重配对）：给每张图像换上相似图像的caption，其余不变；再用random re-pairing（保留80%原始配对）作为对照。在22个图文模型上比较CKA、邻域一致性与top-1检索的表现，并对CKA做精确的尺度分解，考察近邻对与远端对的贡献；同时在COCO与Flickr30k上比较"诊断值最高的模型"与"验证集recall选出的模型"在留出数据上的检索表现。

Result: hard negative重配对后没有任何图像保留自有caption、多数替换caption被标注者判为错误，但CKA仍高于保留80%原始配对的random重配对；22个模型中邻域一致性下降远小于top-1检索（后者崩溃）。精确分解显示近邻对CKA贡献几乎为零。作者测试的匹配问题中，CKA的精确最大化解不返回原始配对。COCO与Flickr30k上，诊断值最高的模型留出检索不如按验证recall选出的模型，即便二者正相关。被重配对去掉的"informative part"（信息性成分）占比在数据集上与检索表现同步，并能在对齐训练前排序编码器对。

Conclusion: CKA与邻域一致性是聚合量，无法识别个体配对，不能替代检索/匹配指标做模型选择；诊断指标应连同其问题与尺度一并报告，并以hard negative re-pairing作压力测试。

Abstract: Do vision and language models represent the world alike? That question hides three: what structure each modality has, whether the two modalities organize images and their captions alike, and whether a model ranks an image's own caption highly. Centered kernel alignment (CKA) and neighborhood agreement answer the second, yet matching and model selection use them as if they identified individual pairs and answered the third. To test this reading, we introduce hard negative re-pairing: every image receives the caption of a similar image, and nothing else changes. Although no image keeps its own caption and annotators judge a majority of the substituted captions wrong, CKA stays higher than under a random re-pairing that keeps 80% of the original pairs. In all 22 image-text models we test, neighborhood agreement loses far less than top-one retrieval, which collapses. The reason is scale: these diagnostics pool over many neighbors, matching and retrieval are decided among the nearest ones, and an exact decomposition shows that near-neighbor pairs contribute almost nothing to CKA. In the matching problems we test, an exact maximizer of CKA would not return the original pairing. On COCO and Flickr30k, the model with the highest diagnostic value retrieves worse on held-out data than the model chosen by validation recall, even where the two correlate positively. What re-pairing removes is the informative part: that share tracks retrieval on both datasets and ranks encoder pairs before alignment training. A diagnostic should be reported with its question and its scale, and tested against hard negative re-pairing.

</details>


### [68] [Two Actions from One State: Fork Futures Teach JEPA World Models to Compare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28350)
*Yihao Wu,Runjia Zeng,Tongyan Wang,Renzhong Huang,Chongzhou Fang,Qifan Wang,Fan Heng,Dongfang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JEPA世界模型用行为日志训练、却在单状态下比较反事实动作，存在可辨识性缺陷；作者用"分叉未来"(fork futures)补充同一状态下替代动作的实测结果，提出Fork-JEPA，显著提升动作效应可读性与规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA世界模型以预测日志转移为目标训练，但使用时需要在同一状态下比较不同(反事实)动作的结果。日志行为数据每个状态仅含一个动作，作者证明：在局部线性近似下，若策略在某状态内沿某方向从不改变动作，则该方向上的动作响应不可辨识。因此仅用行为数据无法学到可靠的动作效应。

Method: 提出Fork-JEPA：从同一恢复状态出发执行两个动作，构成"分叉未来"，其结局之差消去状态项，从而在采样张成的子空间上辨识动作响应。训练目标包括：使预测差异与实测差异匹配；用行为预测锚定日志后继状态；增加回看(lookback)头以保持前驱状态可恢复。所有改动仅在训练期，规划流程不变。

Result: 在三个视觉控制任务上，纯行为训练下预测的动作效应与常数猜测无异；Fork-JEPA使其可读，规划成功率较基座模型LeWM最高提升14%。在动作响应随隐藏区域变化的设定下，跨区域成功率近乎翻倍。1,024个分叉对的效果优于额外120万步行为数据。

Conclusion: 反事实动作比较必须以同状态下的替代动作实测数据为支撑，而非仅依赖单动作日志转移；分叉未来提供了高效、可辨识的动作响应监督信号，在不改变规划接口的前提下显著提升世界模型的动作敏感性与规划性能。

Abstract: JEPA world models are trained to predict logged transitions but used to compare counterfactual actions from one state. The logged behavior data show one action per state, and under a local linear approximation we prove the action response is unidentified along any direction the policy never varies within a state. We therefore supplement behavior data with measured outcomes of alternative actions from the same state. Our fork futures execute two actions from one restored state, and their outcome difference cancels the state term, identifying the action response on the sampled span. The resulting model, Fork-JEPA, matches predicted to measured differences, anchors the logged successor with behavior prediction, and adds a lookback head that keeps the predecessor recoverable, all at training time, leaving planning unchanged. On three visual control tasks, behavior-only training leaves the predicted action effect no better than a constant guess. Fork-JEPA makes it readable and raises planning success by up to 14\% over LeWM, its base model. Where the action response changes across hidden zones, it nearly doubles cross-zone success, and 1,024 fork pairs beat 1.2M extra behavior steps. Our project is available at https://fork-jepa.github.io/.

</details>


### [69] [TOGO: Take One Image Editor, Get One Video Editor for Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28397)
*Yixuan Luo,Jose Caballero,Yanjing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TOGO在推理阶段把图像编辑器的"编辑速度"注入共享潜空间的视频采样器，无需训练即可实现指令式视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像编辑模型基于海量编辑对训练，指令遵循能力远强于当前视频编辑器。作者发现越来越多的图像编辑器与视频生成器共享同一潜空间，因此希望将图像编辑能力零训练地迁移到视频编辑。
