Method: 将指令下与中性提示下图像编辑器速度场之差定义为"编辑速度"，在采样时叠加到视频模型采样器中。分析表明该速度仅在粗尺度、高噪声水平下小幅且可靠，未集中于编辑对象，且帧间不相关。据此设计引导算子，对编辑速度在噪声水平、空间与时间三个维度上放大并约束，使视频模型把帧间不连贯信号转为时序一致的编辑。方法组合Qwen-Image-Edit与Wan2.1，无需训练或微调。

Result: 在FiVE-Bench上，编辑成功率由47.0%（无编辑器的同款视频模型）提升至65.6%，超过最佳已发表方法的59.2%；同时保持背景PSNR，并取得所有方法中最优的LPIPS与SSIM。

Conclusion: 共享潜空间的图像编辑器可通过推理时组合实现高质量视频编辑；由于编辑器在推理时装配，可零成本升级为任意共享潜空间的新版图像编辑器。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editors are trained on millions of edit pairs and follow instructions far more reliably than current video editors. We show that this ability can be transferred to video editing without any training. Our key observation is that a growing number of image editors and video generators share the same latent space. Their velocity fields can therefore be combined at sampling time: the difference between the editor's velocity under the instruction and under a neutral prompt, which we call the \emph{edit velocity}, can be added to the sampler of a video model. Added naively, however, it blurs the video, alters the background and flickers. An analysis of the edit velocity explains why. It is small and reliable only at coarse scales and high noise levels, not concentrated on the edited object, and uncorrelated across frames. From these properties, we derive a simple guidance operator that amplifies the edit velocity and restricts it in noise level, space, and time. The video model then turns the incoherent per-frame signal into a temporally coherent edit. Our method, TOGO, combines Qwen-Image-Edit with the Wan2.1 video model without training or fine-tuning. On FiVE-Bench, it raises the edit success rate from 47.0\% (the same video model without the editor) to 65.6\%, above the best published method (59.2\%). At the same time, it maintains the background PSNR and obtains the best LPIPS and SSIM of all methods. Because the video editor is assembled at inference time, it can be upgraded to any newer image editor that shares the latent space with no cost.

</details>


### [70] [Plan Big, Paint Small: Semantic-Aware Computation Allocation for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28402)
*Yixuan Luo,Yanjing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出训练无关的"规划-渲染"采样框架BigSmall：14B大模型仅生成首块并素描内容变化块，1.3B小模型渲染其余部分，配合近似窗口化惰性跨模型KV缓存，实现约3倍加速且语义不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散逐块生成，但大AR模型过慢难以实时；核心疑问是AR rollout中"哪里真正需要大模型"。作者发现模型顺序至关重要：大模型先出首块、小模型续接可保留95%的14B语义优势，反之仅剩16%却耗时约10倍——即大模型决定"视频显示什么"，小模型只需"承接推进"。

Method: 配对共享潜空间与去噪调度的大模型(Krea-Realtime-14B)与小模型(Self-Forcing-1.3B)。BigSmall：14B规划开篇块并对内容发生变化的块做素描(保运动)，1.3B渲染其余块；更快的BigSmall(变体)只规划开篇块。引入近似、窗口化、惰性的跨模型KV缓存，使规划器能以低成本退出与重入rollout。

Result: VBench上BigSmall比14B快3.02×，语义分数与运动不变；比SDVG(小模型先起草)快1.55×；BigSmall变体达3.77×，语义不变但运动损失10%。LoCoT2V-Bench分钟级长视频中，14B因读取自身长历史而退化，BigSmall快3.14×且得分更高(+1.78)。

Conclusion: AR视频扩散中大模型只需在"决定内容"处介入，首块与内容变化块由大模型规划、其余由小模型承接，即可在几乎不损语义的前提下获得显著加速，并在长视频中规避大模型长历史退化问题。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion streams a video chunk by chunk, but large AR models are too slow for real-time use. We ask where in an AR rollout a large model is actually needed. Pairing Krea-Realtime-14B with Self-Forcing-1.3B, which share their latent space and denoising schedule, we find that the order of the two models matters: when the 14B model generates only the first chunk and the 1.3B model continues, the rollout keeps 95\% of the 14B model's semantic advantage, whereas the reverse allocation keeps 16\% at about ten times the cost. The large model decides what the video shows; the small model only has to carry it forward. We turn this into BigSmall, a training-free planner--renderer sampler. The 14B model plans the opening chunk and sketches the chunks in which content changes, which preserves motion, and the 1.3B model renders the rest; a faster variant, BigSmall, plans only the opening chunk. A cross-model KV cache that is approximate, windowed and lazy lets the planner leave and re-enter the rollout at low cost. On VBench, BigSmall is $3.02\times$ faster than the 14B model at an unchanged semantic score and motion, and $1.55\times$ faster than SDVG, which lets the small model draft first; BigSmall reaches $3.77\times$ at the same semantic score, trading away 10\% of the motion. On minute-long videos (LoCoT2V-Bench), where the 14B model degrades when it reads its own long history, BigSmall is $3.14\times$ faster than the 14B model and scores higher ($+1.78$).`

</details>


### [71] [Center-Aware Ordinal Learning for Prostate Cancer Grading from Histopathology Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28419)
*Mohammad Mahdi Abootorabi,Parvin Mousavi,Purang Abolmaesumi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出中心感知序数学习框架，结合EMD损失、序数监督对比与课程学习，提升多中心前列腺癌ISUP分级及跨中心迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前列腺癌WSI分级面临ISUP等级的有序性、类别不平衡以及染色/制片/扫描等中心差异问题；多数弱监督方法将等级组视为独立类别，学到的表征难以跨机构可靠迁移。

Method: 在预训练病理编码器+注意力多示例学习基础上，引入：EMD损失建模序数误差、序数监督对比目标（对齐跨中心同级切片并按疾病严重度区隔等级）、课程学习以缓解良性病例主导。

Result: 在两中心PANDA约9,100张活检WSI上评估：留出测试集准确率0.842、二次加权kappa 0.958；跨中心迁移显著提升——Karolinska训练线性探针在Radboud评估，准确率0.643→0.823，QWK 0.899→0.930，反向稳定；UMAP显示中心间更好混合且保留序数等级轨迹。

Conclusion: 序数目标与中心感知目标的结合可产生更具迁移性的切片表征，适用于多中心前列腺癌分级。

Abstract: Prostate cancer grading from histopathology whole-slide images (WSIs) is complicated by the ordered nature of ISUP grades, class imbalance, and center-dependent variations in staining, tissue preparation, and scanning. Most weakly supervised methods treat grade groups as independent categories and may therefore learn representations that do not transfer reliably across institutions. We propose a center-aware ordinal learning framework that combines a pretrained pathology encoder with attention-based multiple instance learning. The framework uses an Earth Mover's Distance loss to account for ordinal errors, an ordinal supervised contrastive objective to align same-grade slides across centers while separating grades according to disease severity, and curriculum learning to reduce the dominance of benign cases. We evaluate the framework on approximately 9,100 biopsy WSIs from the two-center PANDA dataset. The proposed framework achieves an accuracy of 0.842 and a quadratic weighted kappa of 0.958 on the held-out test set. Its main benefit is observed in cross-center transfer: when a linear probe is trained on Karolinska representations and evaluated on Radboud, accuracy increases from 0.643 to 0.823 and quadratic weighted kappa from 0.899 to 0.930. Performance remains stable in the reverse direction. UMAP projections further show improved mixing between centers while preserving an ordinal grade trajectory. These results suggest that combining ordinal and center-aware objectives produces more transferable slide representations for multi-center prostate cancer grading.

</details>


### [72] [Decoding Frozen Vision Representations for Training-Free Few-Shot Pineapple Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28442)
*Manfred Gonzalez-Hernandez,Danny Xie-Li,Fabian Fallas-Moya,Camile Lendering,Joaquín Figueira,Erkut Akdag,Giacomo D'Amicantonio,Egor Bondarev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无训练检测器，将冻结视觉基础模型的稠密特征直接解码为检测结果，用于农业图像小样本目标检测，并验证预测深度可直接输入RGB预训练编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业航拍图像中小样本目标检测受密集植被、重复纹理模式与标注稀缺所制约，现有方法依赖微调或学习式检测头，代价高且数据需求大。

Method: 无需微调、学习头或外部候选框：从带标注的support图像估计白化的前景-背景方向，对所有query图像块打分，得到得分场；用自适应阈值与由support推导的几何先验解码。另测试将预测相对深度直接输入RGB预训练的冻结编码器，并考察RGB-深度融合。

Result: 在航拍菠萝图像上，各support规模（K值）下均优于PET-DINO与GeCo2；K=40时，仅用预测深度即可达到DINOv2下RGB性能的92%；RGB-深度融合对两种编码器均降低计数误差。发布Pineapple Plantation数据集（4,908帧航拍图，1,995帧含17,609个框，含预测深度与防泄漏划分），并分析向另外两个农业数据集的迁移性。

Conclusion: 冻结基础模型的稠密特征经简单的判别方向投影即可支撑有效的免训练少样本农业检测，预测深度可作为RGB预训练编码器的有效输入，数据集与迁移分析为该方向提供基准。

Abstract: Few-shot object detection in agricultural imagery is hampered by dense vegetation, repeated patterns, and scarce annotations. We present a training-free detector that turns dense features from frozen visual foundation models directly into detections, without fine-tuning, a learned head, or external proposals: a whitened foreground-background direction estimated from annotated support images scores every query patch, and the score field is decoded with an adaptive threshold and support-derived geometry. We also test whether predicted relative depth can be fed directly to RGB-pretrained frozen encoders. On aerial pineapple imagery, the detector outperforms PET-DINO and GeCo2 at every evaluated support size; at K=40, predicted depth alone reaches 92% of the RGB performance with DINOv2, and RGB-depth fusion lowers counting error for both encoders. We release the Pineapple Plantation dataset (4,908 aerial frames, 1,995 annotated with 17,609 boxes, predicted depth, leakage-aware splits) and analyse transfer to two further agricultural datasets.

</details>


### [73] [CAMA: Calibrated Anti-Collapse Marginal Alignment for Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28461)
*Jinho Kang,Yunhui Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出图像损坏会导致视觉语言模型（VLM）出现特征压缩，使熵最小化这一主流测试时适应目标偏向少数类别并引发预测崩溃；为此提出CAMA目标，通过最大化批互信息并引入参考类分布对齐来防止崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作发现VLM在分布偏移下产生特征压缩，熵最小化会放大该偏差导致性能退化，但缺乏对失败机理的细致分析，也缺少有效的防止策略。

Method: 提出CAMA测试时适应目标：最大化批互信息，既鼓励单张图像预测置信，又保持批内预测多样性；同时在测试流早期估计参考类分布，并在适应过程中使每批平均预测与之对齐。此外给出理论证明，说明特征压缩如何偏置预测、熵最小化如何将偏置放大为崩溃，而CAMA可保证保留每个类别。

Result: 在标准损坏基准上，几乎所有评估的VLM骨干都出现崩溃；CAMA在所有观察到的情形中均防止了崩溃，并在每个骨干上取得最佳准确率。定量与定性分析表明CAMA逆转了导致崩溃的特征压缩，消融实验验证了各组件均有贡献。

Conclusion: 特征压缩是VLM测试时熵最小化失效的根源，CAMA通过批互信息最大化与参考分布对齐有效避免类别崩溃，是一种稳健有效的测试时适应方法。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) updates a model using unlabeled test data to help it handle distribution shifts. However, prior work has found that image corruption causes a vision-language model (VLM) to produce more similar features for different images, a phenomenon known as feature compression. Under compression, entropy minimization, the most common TTA objective, can cause the model to favor a few classes and perform worse than before adaptation. Despite these observations, a detailed analysis of how these failures are connected and an effective strategy for preventing them are still lacking.
  In this paper, we first analyze why entropy minimization fails for VLMs. We then propose CAMA, a new test-time adaptation objective that maximizes batch mutual information to encourage confident predictions for individual images while maintaining diverse predictions across the batch. CAMA also estimates a reference class distribution early in the test stream and aligns each batch's average prediction with this reference during adaptation. We prove that feature compression biases the predictions and that entropy minimization amplifies this bias into collapse, whereas CAMA is guaranteed to preserve every class. Across standard corruption benchmarks, we observe collapse on nearly every VLM backbone evaluated. CAMA prevents collapse in every observed case and, in turn, achieves the best accuracy on every backbone. Quantitative and qualitative analyses confirm that CAMA reverses the feature compression behind the collapse, and ablations show that each of its components contributes.

</details>


### [74] [VICA: Video Contextual Agreement for AIGC Video Detection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28485)
*Runheng Lin,Jialiang Shen,Jiyang Zheng,Ruiqi Liu,Zonglin He,Yu Yao,Huajie Chen,Chris Russell,Runqi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一框架VICA，通过掩码视频区域并用冻结的V-JEPA从时空上下文预测其表示，以"上下文一致性残差"作为共享取证特征，同时完成AIGC视频检测与篡改定位，并取得SOTA与强泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIGC视频生成/编辑技术引发对视频真实性的担忧。现有视频检测方法依赖整体帧级表示挖掘时序不一致，可能因全局特征聚合而掩盖局部伪造痕迹；篡改定位方法聚焦单个空间区域内的伪影，却忽略该区域与周围时空上下文的不一致。两类方法各有盲区，缺乏统一视角。

Method: VICA（VIdeo Contextual Agreement）：对视频中的区域进行互补掩码以覆盖全部区域，利用冻结的V-JEPA模型依据周围时空上下文预测被掩区域的表示；将预测表示与观测表示比较，得到刻画帧内差异的空间残差，以及捕捉前后帧不一致的双向时间残差；这些残差刻画"上下文分歧"，作为两项任务共享的取证表示。

Result: 在多个基准上，VICA在两个任务（AIGC视频检测与篡改定位）均达到SOTA，并对未见过的视频生成器和编辑方法表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将取证焦点从"识别孤立伪造伪影"转向"评估区域与更广视频上下文推断期望之间的一致性"，是可行且有效的统一范式，残差式上下文一致性表示可同时服务检测与定位。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of AI-generated content (AIGC) video generation and editing technologies has raised growing societal concerns about the authenticity and reliability of digital videos. Existing video detection methods primarily exploit temporal inconsistencies through holistic frame-level representations, whereas manipulation localization methods focus on artifacts within individual spatial regions. However, holistic representations may obscure localized artifacts through global feature aggregation, while region-centric approaches may overlook disagreements between individual regions and their surrounding spatiotemporal context. To address these limitations, we propose VIdeo Contextual Agreement (VICA), a unified framework that masks individual video regions and leverages a frozen V-JEPA model to predict their representations from the surrounding spatiotemporal context. Complementary masking ensures complete regional coverage, while context-conditioned predictions establish references for assessing regional agreement with the broader video context. By comparing predicted and observed representations, VICA derives spatial residuals that characterize intra-frame discrepancies and bidirectional temporal residuals that capture inconsistencies across preceding and subsequent frames. These residuals characterize contextual disagreements, providing a shared forensic representation for both tasks and shifting the focus from identifying isolated manipulation artifacts to assessing regional agreement with expectations inferred from the broader video context. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VICA achieves state-of-the-art performance on both tasks across multiple benchmarks, with strong generalization to unseen video generators and editing methods.

</details>


### [75] [Open Vision Banana: An Open Framework for Unified Visual Perception through Generation in RGB Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28489)
*Xu Lin,Ruilin Yao,Haoran Xu,Zixiang Wang,Ming Ma,Yu Zhao,Pu Miao,Chonghan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Open Vision Banana（OVB），将 Qwen-Image-2.1 的 7B 扩散生成器改造为单一指令驱动的通用视觉模型（深度、语义/实例/指代分割）；系统实验揭示开源版本与闭源系统的差距具体而可测：几何学得早但绝对尺度未被承诺、深度与2D分割争用共享适配器、验证流损失与精度负相关而无法用于选模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证「将感知输出渲染为 RGB 图像的指令微调图像生成器成为通用视觉模型」这一结论是否能迁移到开放生成器；若不能，需定位具体失效环节。

Method: 以 Qwen-Image-2.1 的 7B 扩散生成器为基础，用单个共享低秩适配器（LoRA）和公开数据统一适配度量深度、语义分割、实例分割与指代分割；每项任务定义为「任务契约」（RGB 编码器、指令、解码器共享同一规范并在训练前校验）。分析 42 个外部评测检查点（两次 10 万步训练）、6 次精确权重空间合并、8 次受控短训练。

Result: 几何早期即学会而绝对尺度未学会：中位对齐 NYUv2 δ1 全程稳定在 0.89–0.93，而未对齐 δ1 在 0.11–0.50 间波动；十种子集成无需重训练即可将未对齐 δ1 从 0.348 提升至 0.516，说明尺度不由单样本决定。深度与 2D 分割争夺共享适配器，权重合并仅沿该权衡移动而无法跳出。验证流损失降低伴随 Cityscapes 精度下降（损失下降与 mIoU 的 Spearman ρ≈−0.9），故损失不能用于选检查点。

Conclusion: 开源生成器上「渲染式通用视觉」部分成立（几何可学），但绝对尺度承诺、多任务容量竞争与流损失—精度失配是三个可测量的关键瓶颈；作者开源代码、任务契约、训练数据与全部评测输出。

Abstract: Vision Banana showed that a closed image generator, instruction-tuned to paint perception outputs as RGB images, becomes a generalist vision model. Does this carry over to \emph{open} generators, and if not, what fails? We answer with \textbf{Open Vision Banana (OVB)}, an open framework that adapts the 7B diffusion generator of Qwen-Image-2.1 into one instruction-driven model for metric depth, semantic, instance, and referring segmentation, using a single shared low-rank adapter and public data. Each task is a \emph{task contract} whose RGB encoder, instruction, and decoder share one specification and are checked before training. Studying 42 externally evaluated checkpoints from two 100k-update runs, six exact weight-space merges, and eight controlled shorter runs, we find that the gap to the closed system is specific and measurable. Geometry is learned early while absolute scale is not: median-aligned NYUv2 $δ_1$ stays at 0.89--0.93 throughout training as unaligned $δ_1$ wanders between 0.11 and 0.50. The scale is not committed by a single sample: a ten-seed ensemble lifts unaligned $δ_1$ from 0.348 to 0.516 without retraining. Depth and 2D segmentation compete for the shared adapter, and weight merging moves along this trade-off without escaping it. Finally, lower validation flow loss comes with lower Cityscapes accuracy (Spearman $ρ\approx-0.9$ between loss reduction and mIoU), so the loss cannot select checkpoints. We release code, contracts, training data, and all evaluation outputs.

</details>


### [76] [Simple Baselines for Fetal Brain Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28505)
*Hongjia Yang,Mingxuan Liu,Yingqi Hao,Xiaotian Hu,Siyao Li,Xuguang Bai,Fenglin Jia,Yijin Li,Yifei Chen,Zihan Li,Juncheng Zhu,Jing Liu,Yi Liao,Ziyu Li,Haibo Qu,Qiyuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FetalBUAD，一种基于体素级脑龄估计的无监督胎儿脑MRI异常检测框架，联合预测全局脑龄、体素级脑龄图与脑分割以构建内部生物学脑龄参考，并利用不确定性感知的体素级脑龄差图实现异常定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿脑MRI病理数据稀缺，适合无监督异常检测；现有方法依赖脑龄预测或图像重建，受限于GA标签噪声大、缺乏病灶定位、跨域泛化差。

Method: 多任务联合网络同时预测全局脑龄、体素级脑龄图及脑分割，建立内部生物学脑龄参考以减少对临床GA的依赖；再基于不确定性感知的体素级脑龄差图进行异常定位。

Result: 在1317例内部胎儿脑MRI及一个外部基准上取得SOTA异常检测性能，跨域泛化更优，并生成可解剖解释、能定位多种胎儿脑异常的图谱。

Conclusion: 体素级脑龄估计的无监督框架可有效替代对GA标签的依赖，实现高性能且可解释的胎儿脑异常检测与定位。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection (UAD) offers a promising approach to fetal brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) analysis, where pathological data are scarce. Existing methods rely mainly on brain age prediction or image reconstruction, but are limited by noisy gestational age (GA) labels, lack of lesion localization, or poor cross-domain generalization. We propose FetalBUAD, a UAD framework based on voxel-level brain age estimation. A joint multi-task network predicts global brain age, voxel-level brain age maps, and brain segmentation to establish an internal biological brain age reference, reducing reliance on clinically recorded GA. Uncertainty-aware voxel-level brain age gap maps are then used for anomaly localization. Experiments on 1,317 internal fetal brain MRI volumes and an external benchmark demonstrate state-of-the-art anomaly detection performance and improved cross-domain generalization. FetalBUAD also generates anatomically interpretable maps that localize diverse fetal brain abnormalities. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/birthlab/FetalBUAD.

</details>


### [77] [VG3R: Video Generation Model for Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28520)
*Jingong Chen,Qingwen Zhang,Lin Lian,Xinxin Zuo,Yang Wang,Chulwoo Pack,Kyle Gao,Patric Jensfelt,Kwanghee Won*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VG3R 将预训练视频生成模型改造为前馈式 3D 重建模型，通过 clip-level shift prediction 与 multi-layer camera head 在无需迭代去噪的情况下同时重建场景并估计逐帧相机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将视频生成模型用于 3D 重建的方法要么只预测深度而不估计相机，要么依赖多步迭代去噪同时生成几何与相机，速度慢。作者观察到视频生成模型本身已隐含深度与相机运动信息，只是其形式不适合直接用于重建。

Method: 以 Wan2.1 为视频骨干，引入两个模块：(1) clip-level shift prediction，用于校正解码逆深度中未知位移造成的深度畸变；(2) multi-layer camera head，通过融合多个 transformer 块的特征预测每帧相机参数。整体为前馈推理，不需迭代去噪。

Result: 在基于视频生成模型的方法中取得最低的 ATE 与重建误差；相比 Aether 与 Geo4D 分别快约 4 倍和 14 倍；深度估计与 3D 重建性能与几何基础模型相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 预训练视频生成模型可直接转化为高效的前馈 3D 重建器，通过针对性的深度位移校正与跨层相机特征聚合即可获得准确且快速的相机估计与场景重建。

Abstract: Video generation models are widely used as 3D priors for novel view synthesis and 3D scene generation. Fine-tuned for perception, generation models also excel at dense prediction such as depth estimation. However, methods that apply video generation models to 3D reconstruction either predict depth without cameras or generate geometry and estimate cameras through many iterative denoising steps, which makes them slower. In this paper, we present $\textbf{VG3R}$, which turns a pretrained video generation model into a feed-forward 3D reconstruction model. Our key observation is that such a model already captures the depth and camera motion of a video, but not in the form required for reconstruction. We introduce two components: $\textit{clip-level shift prediction}$, which corrects depth distortion caused by the unknown shift in decoded inverse depth, and a $\textit{multi-layer camera head}$, which predicts per-frame cameras parameters by combining features across transformer blocks. Using Wan2.1 as its video backbone, VG3R reconstructs a scene and estimate the camera of every frame without iterative denoising. It achieves the lowest ATE and reconstruction errors among methods built on video generation models, runs about 4 and 14 times faster than Aether and Geo4D. VG3R also demonstrates competitive depth estimation and 3D reconstruction performance compared with geometry foundation models. Project page: https://github.com/KTH-RPL/VG3R

</details>


### [78] [Where the Image Sits: Vision-Text Order Bias in Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28535)
*Youzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究Jev式（system-1、单次前向读取选项标签分布）决策模型对图像与文本token相对顺序的敏感性，发现后缀放置导致41.0%的决策翻转与18.6个百分点的准确率下降，且随位置变化的曲线呈单调而非U型，并提出P+Q双前向读出方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明生成式视觉语言模型对图像与文本token的相对顺序敏感，但尚不清楚这种敏感性是否也存在于无生成、无解析的Jev式读出范式中。由于该范式总在提示末尾读取决策，移动图像只改变视觉证据到决策的距离而不改变读取位置，因此是一个干净的检验场景。

Method: 固定选项列表，设置前缀、问题前、选项前、后缀四种图像放置位置；度量argmax翻转率、总变差、准确率、校准度与标签边缘分布；进行多项控制实验以排除混淆因素；并提出prefix–question averaging（P+Q）两前向读出，对前缀与问题前两处分布取平均。

Result: 后缀放置翻转41.0%的决策，准确率下降18.6个百分点；四位置平均后曲线呈单调，与生成式视觉语言模型上的U型结论不同；该效应在一系列控制下依然稳健；P+Q准确率不低于前缀放置并优于既有基线。

Conclusion: 顺序敏感性在Jev式读出中同样显著，但其形态（单调）不同于生成式模型（U型），说明读出机制本身影响顺序偏差的性质；P+Q提供了一种低成本、有效的缓解手段。

Abstract: Jev-style system-1 decision models read a choice from a single prefill. They restrict the next-token distribution to the supplied option labels and return a distribution over the options, with no generation and no parsing. Recent work shows that generative vision-language models are sensitive to the relative order of image and text tokens. We ask whether that sensitivity reaches this Jev-style readout. The answer is always read at the end of the prompt, so moving the image changes the distance from the visual evidence to the decision, but never the position at which the decision is read. Using four placements---prefix, pre-question, pre-options, and suffix---and holding the option listing fixed, we measure argmax flips, total variation, accuracy, calibration, and label marginals. Suffix placement flips 41.0% of decisions and lowers accuracy by 18.6 points. Averaged over the four slots, the curve is monotone, not U-shaped. That shape differs from prior results on generative vision-language models. The effect survives a battery of controls. We also introduce prefix--question averaging (P+Q), a two-forward readout that averages the prefix and pre-question distributions. It is at least as accurate as prefix and outperforms the prior baselines. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/Youzhi333/Visual_Tex_Order_Bias_Jev.

</details>


### [79] [Centerline-Guided Multi-Class Road Surface Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28544)
*Xu Ouyang,Lin Gan,Di Ma,Sonnie Chen,Sanjay Boddhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出中心线引导的多类分割框架，将道路轴、横向宽度与交叉口/立交结构显式联合预测，在CHN6-CUG上完整路面IoU达65.53%，较二值SegFormer-B4基线提升1.01个百分点，误检像素减少11.94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星影像道路提取通常只输出二值掩膜，道路轴线、横向范围与交叉口结构被隐式化，难以支撑制图与路网检查；且窄走廊、道路汇合与遮挡需要不同几何线索，单一二值学习难以兼顾。

Method: 分层编码器提供共享特征；以预测的中心线特征与软概率作为条件，驱动常规路面、交叉口、立交三个专用解码器；双分量宽度分支为常规路面解码提供横向几何信息；通过可学习特征融合生成完整道路掩膜，各语义输出仍可单独检查。

Result: 四语义输出配置在CHN6-CUG数据集上取得65.53%完整路面IoU，相比二值SegFormer-B4基线提升1.01个百分点，误检像素减少11.94%。

Conclusion: 结果表明几何感知的路面细化有效，为道路地图检查提供了语义更丰富的栅格表示；解码阶段显式交换几何信息优于独立预测头或固定并集策略。

Abstract: Road-surface extraction from satellite imagery supports geo- graphic mapping, but a binary mask leaves road axes, transverse extent, and junction structure implicit. Learning these proper- ties together is challenging because narrow corridors, merging roads, and occlusions require different geometric cues. We present a centerline-guided multi-class segmentation framework that makes these dependencies explicit. A hierarchical encoder sup- plies shared features, while predicted centerline features and soft probabilities condition specialized regular, intersection, and over- pass decoders. A two-component width branch supplies transverse geometry to regular-surface decoding, and learned feature fusion produces the complete road mask. Unlike independent prediction heads or a fixed union of component masks, this design exchanges geometric information during decoding while retaining separately inspectable outputs. Evaluation of the four-semantic-output con- figuration on CHN6-CUG achieves 65.53% complete-surface IoU, improving on a binary SegFormer-B4 baseline by 1.01 percentage points and reducing false-positive pixels by 11.94%. The results support geometry-aware surface refinement and a richer raster representation for road-map inspection.

</details>


### [80] [Reconstruction-Guided Dual-Branch Learning for LiDAR Anomalous Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28545)
*Guanyu Jiang,Junjie Cao,Jinshan Pan,Zhixun Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构化残差推理（SRR）与重建引导双分支学习（RGDB），融合语义判别与重建一致性两类互补异常证据，提升LiDAR异常目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR异常检测器多依赖语义判别，只捕获类别判别特征，忽略对正常场景更广泛特征关系的偏离；而逐点重建误差在复杂室外点云中难以区分异常与正常可变性。

Method: SRR从特征通道、时间序列和几何空间三个维度的残差结构中提取额外异常证据；并论证语义判别（类别兼容性）与重建（与学习到的正常模式一致性）提供本质不同且互补的证据。进一步提出RGDB，用SRR导出的正常模式一致性线索在训练中引导语义条件异常解码器，推理时两分支共同利用互补证据。

Result: 在匹配背景误报率下，SRR比语义分支找回更多被完全漏检的目标，且在纯正常场景中误报更少；单独SRR无需目标场景训练数据的语义标签即可建立强重建基线；RGDB在STU验证集上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 语义判别与重建一致性是互补的异常证据来源，结构化残差与双分支融合能有效提升异常检测的召回并抑制误报。

Abstract: Detecting unexpected objects is essential for autonomous driving safety. Most existing LiDAR anomaly detectors rely on semantic discrimination, which primarily captures category-discriminative features but may overlook deviations from broader feature relationships in normal scenes. Reconstruction offers a complementary perspective by assessing consistency with learned normal patterns. However, pointwise reconstruction errors alone struggle to distinguish anomalies from normal variability in complex outdoor point clouds. We propose Structured Residual Reasoning (SRR) to extract additional anomaly evidence from residual structure across feature channels, time sequences, and geometric space. We further establish that semantic discrimination and normal-pattern reconstruction capture fundamentally different yet complementary anomaly evidence: category compatibility and consistency with learned normal patterns, respectively. At matched background false-positive rates, SRR recovers more objects missed entirely by the semantic branch while producing fewer false positives in normal-only scenes. Building on this finding, we propose Reconstruction-Guided Dual-Branch (RGDB) learning, in which SRR-derived normal-pattern consistency cues guide a semantic-conditioned anomaly decoder to complement reconstruction-based predictions during training. At inference, the two branches jointly exploit their complementary anomaly evidence. Experiments show that standalone SRR establishes a strong reconstruction-based baseline without semantic labels from the target-scene training data, while RGDB achieves state-of-the-art performance on the STU validation set.

</details>


### [81] [Analyzing Atypical Local Features in Museum Artifacts using Generative Image Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28549)
*Maho Kinoshita,Wakana Kuwata,Ryota Mibayashi,Hiroaki Ohshima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“区域排除式修复”方法：用图像生成模型学习的外观分布作参照，通过LoRA微调与掩码修复的LPIPS差异量化局部特征的重建难度，以发现博物馆藏品中的地域特异性局部视觉特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同类藏品往往共享整体形状，而纹样、装饰、面部表情等局部细节可能承载地域或文化特征，这类"非典型/意外"的局部特征缺乏有效的自动化分析手段，需要一种能识别与常规预期偏离的局部视觉特征的方法。

Method: 以图像生成模型学习的外观分布作为同类别藏品的"预期"参照。对每个目标区域，排除该区域图像，仅用其余区域图像微调生成模型（区域排除LoRA）；随后对目标区域的藏品图像中预定义的局部部位加掩码，让排除模型进行修复（inpainting）。用LPIPS度量修复结果与原图的差异，作为生成模型的重建难度指标：差异越大，说明该局部特征越难以从其他区域学到的外观中预测。

Result: 在国立民族学博物馆收藏的小芥子人偶、鼓和面具图像上实验，每类选取若干目标区域，构建区域排除LoRA并对预定义局部部位做修复。结果显示：典型形状与简单装饰通常能被稳定重建；而部分地域特有的颜色、纹样、绳结排布与彩绘细节则被替换为更常见的外观。

Conclusion: 区域排除式修复可作为探索性工具，用于发现博物馆藏品图像中地域特异性的局部视觉特征，为藏品视觉特征的文化差异分析提供了一种无需人工标注先验的参考式检测思路。

Abstract: This study proposes a method for analyzing local visual features in museum artifacts that may appear atypical or unexpected. Objects in the same category often share a similar overall shape, while local details such as patterns, decorations, and facial expressions may reflect regional or cultural characteristics. We use the appearance distribution learned by an image generation model as a reference for what is generally expected within an artifact category. For each target region, we exclude images from that region and fine-tune a generative model using only images from the remaining regions. We then mask selected parts of artifact images from the target region and ask the region-excluded model to complete them. If the completed result differs greatly from the original image, the masked part may contain features that are difficult to predict from the appearance learned from other regions. We measure this difference using LPIPS and treat it as reconstruction difficulty for the generative model. We conduct experiments on images of kokeshi dolls, drums, and masks from the collection of the National Museum of Ethnology. For each category, we select several target regions, build region-excluded LoRA models, and perform inpainting on predefined local parts. The results show that typical shapes and simple decorations are often reconstructed consistently, whereas some region-specific colors, patterns, cord arrangements, and painted details are replaced with more common appearances. These results suggest that region-excluded inpainting can serve as an exploratory tool for finding region-specific local visual features in museum artifact images.

</details>


### [82] [WorldWeave: Rethinking Embodied Navigation through Cross-Task World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28553)
*Xuan Yao,Jiahao Zhang,Rongjunchen Zhang,Kaixuan Huang,Junyu Gao,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨任务世界模型 WorldWeave，通过从状态变化中反演隐动作建立共享转移表示，联合学习视觉语言导航（VLN）与具身视觉跟踪（EVT）的动态规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动具身智能体的观测既受自身运动影响，又受外部动态影响；不同任务（VLN 面向静止目标导航、EVT 面向移动目标交互）刻画了互补的状态演化模式，但跨此类交互联合学习世界动力学的研究尚不充分。

Method: 采用逆-前向动力学从观测到的状态变化中学习隐动作，替代异构物理动作作为状态转移条件，从而在任务间构建共享的转移表示；并将预测的未来状态自适应融合进任务特定表示以支持下游决策。

Result: 在多个 VLN 与 EVT 基准上相较专用基线取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 以隐动作对齐异质动作空间，可实现跨任务共享预测动力学与任务特定适配的有效结合，验证了跨任务世界模型的可行性与增益。

Abstract: Mobile embodied agents continuously alter their observations through their own motion, while external dynamics further shape future interactions. Different mobile embodied tasks therefore capture complementary patterns of state evolution, yet jointly learning world dynamics across such interactions remains less explored. We investigate this problem through Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) and Embodied Visual Tracking (EVT), which respectively involve navigation toward a stationary goal and interaction with a moving target. We propose WorldWeave, a cross-task world model that couples shared predictive dynamics with task-specific adaptation. Instead of conditioning state transitions on heterogeneous physical actions, WorldWeave learns latent actions from observed state changes via inverse-forward dynamics, establishing a shared transition representation across tasks. The resulting future-state predictions are adaptively integrated into task-specific representations for downstream decision making. Experiments across multiple VLN and EVT benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over specialized baselines.

</details>


### [83] [LoopSight: Looped Vision Transformers with Gated Prediction Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28575)
*Zhaozhi Wang,Mingrui Wu,Jiaming Zhao,An-Syu Li,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopSight 通过门控预测记忆（GPM）将持久的预测状态与演化中的计算状态解耦，使循环视觉 Transformer 在多轮迭代中既能保留已正确的预测，又能持续演化计算，在 ARC-AGI 等任务上显著优于 LoopViT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环视觉 Transformer（如 LoopViT）通过重复应用共享 Transformer 核心来扩展视觉推理的计算量，但存在关键矛盾：既要在多次循环迭代中保留有用的预测信息，又要允许计算状态继续演化。现有做法难以同时兼顾，导致已正确的预测在后续迭代中被破坏，读出特征不稳定。

Method: 提出 LoopSight，使用门控预测记忆（GPM）维护一个用于读出的持久预测状态，与核心的演化计算状态相分离。每次迭代时，交叉注意力门利用核心的候选特征、上一轮预测状态和当前任务状态，在 patch 级与通道级控制写入；预测由更新后的记忆解码得到。此外用渐近循环编码（asymptotic loop encoding）将共享核心条件化于当前迭代次数，并以截断高斯分布采样的循环次数作为监督训练目标。

Result: 在 ARC-AGI-1 与 ARC-AGI-2 上，三个模型规模均优于 LoopViT。ARC-AGI-1 上 LoopSight-Small 达到 67.4% pass@2，在参数量与 FLOPs 相当的情况下比 LoopViT-Small 提升 6.6 个百分点。在 Visual Sudoku 与 ImageNet 分类上同样优于 LoopViT。分析表明 LoopSight 能保留更多已正确的预测，且读出特征在计算状态持续演化时更稳定。

Conclusion: 将预测读出状态与计算状态解耦、并以门控方式选择性写入记忆，是提升循环视觉 Transformer 视觉推理性能的有效机制；该设计在推理密集型基准和常规视觉任务上均带来一致增益，并提升了迭代过程的可稳定性。

Abstract: Looped vision transformers extend computation for visual reasoning by repeatedly applying a shared Transformer core. A challenge is to preserve useful prediction information across loop iterations while allowing the computation state to evolve. In this paper we introduce LoopSight, a looped vision transformer that uses Gated Prediction Memory (GPM) to maintain a persistent prediction state for readout, separate from the core's evolving computation state. At each iteration, a cross-attention gate uses the core's candidate features, the previous prediction state, and the current task state to control writes at the patch and channel level. The model decodes its prediction from the updated memory. We condition the shared core on the current iteration using asymptotic loop encoding and train the model by supervising its prediction at loop counts sampled from a truncated Gaussian distribution. LoopSight outperforms LoopViT across three model scales on ARC-AGI-1 and ARC-AGI-2. On ARC-AGI-1, LoopSight-Small achieves 67.4% pass@2, a gain of 6.6 percentage points over LoopViT-Small with comparable parameter counts and FLOPs. LoopSight also outperforms LoopViT on Visual Sudoku and ImageNet classification. Further analyses on ARC-AGI-1 show that LoopSight preserves more already-correct predictions and maintains more stable readout features as its computation state continues to evolve. The code will be released at https://github.com/feufhd/LoopSight.

</details>


### [84] [CLASP: Deciding Structure and Claims Before Decoding for Whole-Slide Pathology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28576)
*Ziaullah Khan,Rashadul Islam Sumon,Md Ariful Islam Mozumder,Duc-Son Pham,Moon-Il Joo,Hee-Cheol*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLASP 将全切片病理报告生成从"端到端解码"改为"先决策后解码"：显式决定报告长度、组织方式与陈述内容，再用计划条件解码器成文，仅35M参数即超越7B级多模态LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全切片病理报告生成器（更强的切片编码器、检索知识、切片级多模态LLM）把所有报告级选择都交给解码器：报告长度、组织结构、陈述哪些发现及其位置。作者发现这些选择对 n-gram 重叠指标的影响占主导——在 PathText-BRCA 基准上，不做任何学习、仅按查询所在医院挑选报告即接近SOTA；而解码器本身存在生成不足、退化为高频发现的倾向。

Method: 提出 decide-then-decode 的 CLASP：(1) Exemplar Structure Prior (ESP) 从图像相似病例报告中获取句子计划与长度；(2) Calibrated Claim Decision (CCD) 基于校准后的切片后验选择发现；(3) Claim-to-Sentence Placement (CSP) 将每个发现分配到计划句子；(4) Plan-Conditioned Decoder (PCD) 在上述决策条件下生成，并以结构锚定的序列训练进行精炼。整套模型约35M参数。

Result: CLASP 优于重训的报告生成器及至多7B参数的切片级LLM，匹配最强已发表结果；在未见医院的肺癌数据上具备泛化性。其决策改变的是报告陈述内容而非仅长度，且可迁移至LLM解码器；对H&E无法呈现的受体状态加以保留后，与临床记录的矛盾减少23%。

Conclusion: 报告级结构决策应由模型显式做出而非隐式交给解码器：小模型配合显式决策即可达到大模型水平，并带来内容层面（可解释、可抑制不可见信息）的真实收益。

Abstract: Whole-slide pathology report generation has advanced through stronger slide encoders, retrieved knowledge, and, recently, slide-level multimodal LLMs. However, these generators leave every report-level choice to the decoder: how long the report is, how it is organised, and which findings it states and where. On the standard PathText-BRCA benchmark, we find that these choices dominate n-gram overlap, as reports from the query's hospital selected without learning approach the state of the art, while decoders under-generate and fall back to the most frequent findings. We present CLASP, a decide-then-decode generator that makes these choices explicitly, once per slide. Before decoding, an Exemplar Structure Prior (ESP) takes the sentence plan and length from reports of image-similar cases, a Calibrated Claim Decision (CCD) selects the findings from calibrated slide posteriors, and a Claim-to-Sentence Placement (CSP) assigns each finding to a planned sentence. A Plan-Conditioned Decoder (PCD), refined by structure-anchored sequence training, then writes the report under these decisions. With 35M parameters, CLASP outperforms retrained report generators and slide-level LLMs of up to 7B parameters, matches the strongest published results, and generalises to lung cancer from unseen hospitals. Its decisions change what the reports state, not only their length, transfer to an LLM decoder, and can withhold what H&E cannot show: withholding receptor status leaves 23% fewer contradictions with clinical records than the same network without decisions.

</details>


### [85] [CLOSE: A Calibrated Label-free Online Self-Evaluating Verifier for Object Hallucination in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28589)
*Zhaoxi Wang,Jiahao Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CLOSE：一种校准的、无标签的在线自评估验证器，利用模型自身判断能力在生成过程中抑制视觉语言模型的物体幻觉，通过分位数阈值、秩融合打分与回滚禁词实现无需训练的去幻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型因语言先验常描述图中不存在的物体（幻觉）。作者发现模型本身已是较强的自评判者：单独询问某物体是否存在时，可拒绝 44%-62% 的幻觉，其 yes 概率在无训练下达到 0.90-0.95 的 AUROC。但该判断无法直接使用：不同模型概率尺度不统一；剩余 38%-56% 的幻觉其 yes 概率与真实物体重叠，单一阈值无法区分。

Method: CLOSE 在生成过程中强制执行自评判：阈值取记忆库的分位数而非绝对概率，仅依赖秩，记忆库由无标签图像冷启动并在线更新；将在四类隐状态信号伪标签上自训练的评分器与自询问结果按秩融合，以区分自询问混淆的样本；对拒绝的物体进行回滚并通过阶梯式回退（laddered back-off）禁用，同时用识别表拦截其以其他措辞再次出现。

Result: COCO 上，拒绝 20% 与 40% 的物体提及使 LLaVA-1.5-7B 的 CHAIRs 分别下降 42% 与 87%（F1 +1.9 与 -6.1 点）；拒绝 20% 与 35% 使 Qwen3-VL-8B 的可达幻觉（官方计数减去 CHAIR 脚本误计）下降 79% 与 96%（F1 +0.5 与 -5.3）。外部词表可进一步将检查扩展到官方词表之外。

Conclusion: 模型的自评判能力虽强但概率不可直接阈值化，CLOSE 通过秩化校准与在线记忆将其转化为可用的生成期验证机制，显著降低幻觉率，且无需训练、无需标签，并具备跨模型与词表外扩展能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models frequently describe objects absent from the image because of language priors. Yet the model itself is already a strong judge of its own output: asked separately whether an object is present, it rejects 44%-62% of its hallucinations, and its yes probability reaches an AUROC of 0.90-0.95 without training. This judgment, however, is not directly usable: (1) its probabilities lack a common scale across models; (2) for the remaining 38%-56%, the model still answers yes with probabilities that overlap those of real objects, so no threshold separates them. We therefore propose CLOSE, a Calibrated Label-free Online Self-Evaluating verifier that enforces this judgment during generation. Its threshold is a quantile of a memory rather than a probability and thus depends only on ranks; the memory is cold-started from unlabeled images and updated online. A scorer self-trained on pseudo-labels from four hidden-state signals is fused with self-asking by rank to separate what self-asking leaves mixed. Refused objects are rolled back and banned through a laddered back-off, and a recognition table intercepts their reappearance under other wordings. On COCO, refusing 20% and 40% of object mentions reduces the CHAIRs of LLaVA-1.5-7B by 42% and 87% (F1 +1.9 and -6.1 points); refusing 20% and 35% reduces the reachable hallucinations of Qwen3-VL-8B (official counts minus CHAIR-script miscounts) by 79% and 96% (F1 +0.5 and -5.3). An external vocabulary further extends the check beyond the official vocabulary.

</details>


### [86] [CAR3T: Generalizable Calibration-Guided Reference-View Attention Reuse for Multi-View 3D Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28591)
*Lan Wei,Haosong Peng,Sicheng Tian,Tianyu Qi,Wenchao Xu,Lei Xue,Yufeng Zhan,Yuanqing Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架CAR3T，通过参考视图注意力输出复用、离线风险校准与在线动态层选择，将前馈多视图3D重建中被选层的全局注意力计算由二次降为线性，实现最高1.41×（≤100视图）与1.75×（更长序列）的前向加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈多视图3D重建依赖全局注意力进行跨视图聚合，其代价随视图数二次增长，大输入推理昂贵。现有加速方法（token压缩、注意力稀疏化）可能损失几何细节或限制跨视图聚合，且未充分利用注意力输出间的冗余。观察到某些层跨视图注意力分布相似，存在输出复用的机会；但可复用层随输入变化，且复用某层会改变后续层接收的特征，单层相似性无法刻画多次复用决策对输出漂移的累积影响。

Method: CAR3T为免训练框架：离线风险校准对累积复用风险建模，在线动态层选择在校准预算下为每个输入自适应决定复用层；复用参考视图的注意力输出，保留全部输入token并仍对全部视图的key/value进行注意力，使被选层的核心注意力计算从二次降为线性。

Result: 在VGGT、DA3和π³上实验，最多100视图时前向加速最高1.41×，更长序列上达1.75×，不同骨干与数据集上存在精度权衡。

Conclusion: 以输出复用替代重复全局聚合、并以校准-在线选择应对输入相关的复用层与累积漂移，是免训练加速多视图3D重建的可行路径，但需在速度与精度间权衡。

Abstract: Feed-forward multi-view 3D reconstruction relies on global attention for cross-view aggregation, but the attention cost grows quadratically with the number of views, making inference increasingly expensive for larger inputs. Existing acceleration methods compress tokens or sparsify attention, potentially losing geometric detail or restricting cross-view aggregation while leaving redundancy among attention outputs underexploited. We observe similar cross-view attention distributions in certain layers, revealing an opportunity to reduce repeated global aggregation through output reuse. However, the layers suitable for reuse vary with the input, and reusing one layer alters the features received by later layers. Similarity at individual layers therefore cannot characterize the combined effect of multiple reuse decisions on output drift. To address these challenges, we propose CAR3T, a training-free framework combining reference-view attention output reuse with offline risk calibration and online dynamic layer selection. Calibration models cumulative reuse risk, while online selection adapts reuse to each input under a calibrated budget. CAR3T retains all input tokens and attends to the keys and values of all views, reducing the core attention computation in selected layers from quadratic to linear. Experiments on VGGT, DA3, and $π^3$ show forward speedups up to $1.41\times$ with at most 100 views and $1.75\times$ on longer sequences, with accuracy trade-offs across backbones and datasets.

</details>


### [87] [When Fog Becomes a Weapon: Atmospheric Attacks on Visible-Infrared Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28592)
*Chengyin Hu,Dehe Zhao,Dingyi Lu,Qike Zhang,Jiaju Han,Banglong Nong,Xu Kang,Jiujiang Guo,Kalibinuer Tiliwalidi,Jiahuan Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出零梯度"雾操控攻击"(FMA)，通过优化一个共享的3D雾状态，同时破坏可见光与热红外视觉语言模型的互补线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对VLM与跨光谱感知的对抗攻击依赖醒目、不自然的伪影（如彩色对抗补丁、主动光束），缺乏物理隐蔽性；且以往天气攻击忽视同一大气环境如何同时作用于异质传感器。核心科学问题：纯粹自然、共享的雾环境能否在无人为表面修改下同时扰乱可见光与热红外的互补线索。

Method: FMA将沿场景路径的、具有深度与分层感知的共享大气传播，与波段特定成像物理（可见光airlight散射与热红外路径辐射）相耦合，以零梯度方式优化一个共同3D雾状态，实现单一真实雾态对两种模态不同线索的同时侵蚀。

Result: FMA的同时失效率达到保真度匹配的随机雾的两倍以上；可迁移至图像描述与VQA任务；并消除跨模态补偿——恢复干净伴随视图可挽回64%–74%丢失的双输入VQA答案。

Conclusion: 纯粹自然、物理一致的雾环境可作为隐蔽的跨模态对抗媒介，同时破坏可见光-红外VLM，并揭示跨模态补偿机制的重要性。

Abstract: Existing adversarial attacks on vision-language models (VLMs) and cross-spectral perception primarily rely on conspicuous, unnatural artifacts, such as vivid adversarial patches or active light beams, that lack physical stealthiness, whereas prior weather attacks largely overlook how one atmosphere simultaneously affects heterogeneous sensors. This raises a key scientific question: can a purely natural, shared fog environment simultaneously disrupt complementary visible and thermal infrared cues without artificial surface modifications? We introduce Fog Manipulation Attack (FMA), a zero-gradient attack that optimizes a common 3D fog state to induce simultaneous semantic failures in visible-infrared VLMs. FMA links shared, depth- and layer-aware atmospheric propagation along scene paths with band-specific imaging physics---visible airlight scattering and thermal path radiance---allowing one realistic fog state to erode both modalities' distinct cues. Experiments show that FMA more than doubles simultaneous failure rates over fidelity-matched random fog, transfers to captioning and VQA, and removes cross-modal compensation, where restoring a clean companion view recovers 64%--74% of lost dual-input VQA answers.

</details>


### [88] [OKB-CFM: Observation-Keyed Barycentric Conditional Flow Matching for Unpaired Sparse-to-Dense Cardiac Shape Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28600)
*Kangneng Zhou,Shuang Song,Yitian Zhao,Dian Song,Yujia Wang,Xinghua Zhang,Li Wang,Xi Li,Hao Dai,Feng Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"观测键控的重心条件流匹配"方法，实现临床非配对队列间由稀疏电影MRI标注到稠密心脏分割的补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏电影MRI平面无法唯一确定潜在解剖结构，且缺乏与患者匹配的稠密标注，跨队列稠密分割补全困难。

Method: 用独立训练的VAE分别表征稀疏观测与稠密形状；固定记忆库将模拟稀疏键与源稠密编码关联，在稀疏潜空间检索候选；匹配网络将检索值聚合为查询相关的重心端点，以端点条件流将稀疏编码输运至该端点；各候选值可提供备选补全结果。

Result: 327例真实非配对输入上，编码器特征Fréchet与平方MMD为7.93和2.02（分别按1万、1000万缩放），较CUT降低47.0%与94.2%；34例模拟测试集上五类平均Dice 0.889，平方Chamfer距离6.15体素²，95%表面距离3.88体素，平均对称表面距离1.50体素。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了队列级特征一致性、观测标注一致性与指定参考构建下的重建质量；该方法为在常规电影功能评估基础上推断多腔解剖背景、研究腔室形态与空间关系同心功能联系提供基础。

Abstract: Completing cardiac segmentations from sparse cine magnetic resonance labels is challenging because acquired planes do not uniquely determine the underlying anatomy and patient-matched dense annotations are rarely available. We propose observation-keyed barycentric conditional flow matching for completion between clinically unpaired cohorts. Independently trained variational autoencoders represent sparse observations and dense shapes. A fixed memory associates simulated sparse keys with their source dense encodings, enabling candidate retrieval within the sparse latent space. A matching network aggregates the retrieved values into a query-dependent barycentric endpoint, toward which an endpoint-conditioned flow transports the sparse encoding. Individual candidate values provide alternative completion endpoints. On 327 real unpaired inputs, the method obtains encoder-feature Fréchet and squared maximum mean discrepancy scores of 7.93 and 2.02 after scaling by 10,000 and 10,000,000, respectively, corresponding to reductions of 47.0\% and 94.2\% relative to contrastive unpaired translation. On a 34-file simulated test partition, evaluation against dense-VAE-reconstructed references yields a five-class mean Dice of 0.889, squared Chamfer distance of 6.15 square voxels, 95th-percentile surface distance of 3.88 voxels, and average symmetric surface distance of 1.50 voxels. These results characterize cohort-level feature agreement, observed-label consistency, and reconstruction under the stated reference construction. By complementing routine cine-based functional assessment with inferred multi-chamber anatomical context, this framework provides a basis for investigating how chamber morphology and spatial relationships relate to cardiac function.

</details>


### [89] [VUOD: A Versatile Unsupervised Outlier Detection Framework for Natural, Industrial, Medical Images and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28608)
*Zhonghang Liu,Siyuan Chen,Jingwen Yu,Changshuo Wang,Kunyang Li,Jiangbo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个通用无监督离群检测框架，通过利用内在分布先验提升特征判别性，摆脱对冻结预训练特征提取器的依赖，在3个领域14个基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督离群检测方法依赖冻结的预训练特征提取器，局限于自然图像，在工业检测、医学影像等实际场景中性能难以维持。

Method: 重新审视该任务，提出通用无监督离群检测框架，核心思想是利用内在分布先验增强特征判别性。

Result: 在3个领域、14个基准数据集上达到SOTA，显著优于现有方法；并具备即插即用特性，可集成到图像分类、3D重建等多种视觉应用中提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架拓展了无监督离群检测的适用领域，普适性强，可作为通用模块增强下游视觉系统的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Unsupervised outlier detection that automatically identifies whether visual systems involve anomalous images is a significant research topic. However, most approaches are limited to natural images because they rely on frozen and pre-trained feature extractors. So that their performance cannot be maintained in practical scenarios, especially for industrial inspection and medical imaging. In this work, we first revisit this task and then introduce a versatile unsupervised outlier detection framework to enrich the application domains. The core idea of this framework is to improve feature discriminativess via exploiting intrinsic distribution priors. Evaluated on 3 domains and 14 benchmark datasets, our proposed solution achieves state-of-the-art performance and significantly outperforms existing methods. More importantly, we show its plug-and-play property that can be integrated into diverse visual applications to improve their robustness, such as image classification and 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [90] [Seeing 3D Occupancy Through Scene-Adaptive Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28629)
*Yuan Wu,Zhiqiang Yan,Zhengxue Wang,Guangfa Wang,Jun Li,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAP：利用深度先验引导视角变换的场景自适应3D占据预测框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后向投影范式将均匀分布的3D参考点投影到图像平面聚合多视图特征，其场景无关的采样模式忽视场景几何的非均匀分布，把大量参考点分配给空白空间，在固定采样预算下降低采样效率。

Method: 将冻结深度模型预测的度量深度图反投影到3D空间获得几何先验，并设计场景自适应投影编码器：(1) 先验引导参考生成，在先验支持的高度区域内分配参考点，无有效支持处保留全范围采样，构建混合参考集；(2) 径向结构特征聚合，通过可学习的径向偏移探索多个候选位置，以应对深度误差引起的空间错位，聚合可能偏移的特征证据。

Result: 在Occ3D上大量实验表明SAP达到SOTA性能，并在多种占据预测框架中稳定带来提升。

Conclusion: 以深度几何先验引导视角变换的采样分配可有效提升固定采样预算下的效率与精度，且具备良好的框架通用性。

Abstract: Vision-based 3D occupancy prediction infers voxel-wise geometry and semantics from multi-view images, where 2D-to-3D view transformation is a crucial step. Many existing methods adopt a backward-projection paradigm, projecting uniformly distributed 3D reference points onto image planes to aggregate multi-view features. However, their scene-agnostic sampling patterns overlook the non-uniform distribution of scene geometry, allocating many references to empty space and reducing sampling efficiency under a fixed sampling budget. To address this challenge, we propose SAP, a scene-adaptive projection framework that leverages depth-based geometric priors to guide view transformation. Specifically, we back-project metric depth maps predicted by a frozen depth model into 3D space to obtain geometric priors. To leverage these priors, we design a Scene-Adaptive Projection Encoder comprising two complementary components. First, Prior-Guided Reference Generation constructs a hybrid reference set for feature sampling, allocating references within prior-supported height regions and retaining full-range sampling where valid support is unavailable. Furthermore, to account for potential spatial misalignment caused by depth estimation errors, Radial-Structured Feature Aggregation explores multiple candidate locations through learned radial offsets to aggregate potentially displaced feature evidence. Extensive experiments on Occ3D demonstrate that SAP achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently improves results across diverse occupancy frameworks.

</details>


### [91] [Self-prediction residuals improve single-pass detection of wrong-object boxes in grounded vision-language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28632)
*Enhao Cui,Junzhi Yang,Yaoshun Lin,Yuhan Feng,Zijian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出"自预测残差"（self-prediction residual）：用无标签拟合的线性读出从隐藏状态预测边界框，其与写出框的差异可作为单遍信号，显著提升对"框到另一物体"类错误的检测，但对整体错误数量的检出规模几无改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: grounded 视觉-语言模型以文本形式输出框，坐标 token 的平均概率几乎无法判断框是否正确；而"框到了另一个物体上"这类错误在只关心物体的场景下尤为关键，因此需要一个单遍、可得的判别信号。

Method: 按模型各自答案以无标签方式拟合一个线性映射，在写出框之前从隐藏状态读出框；将该读出与已写框的不一致定义为自预测残差。在 1,200 条 RefCOCOg 测试复现清单上，对 15 个单遍分数（无标签探针与基于 2,000 条标注训练的探针）在六个模型上取平均，比较引入残差前后 AUROC；残差权重在两个模型上用标签固定。并以 20% 答案复核量评估成本加权召回。

Result: 残差对每个单遍分数均提升"框到另一物体"错误的检测，AUROC 提升 0.026–0.049；对全部错误整体，加入残差使 AUROC 变化小于 0.02（95% 区间）。复核 20% 答案时，全部错误捕获率由 0.518 升至 0.524，而框到另一物体错误的捕获率由 0.505 升至 0.607（事后分析）。成本加权召回显示：当该类错误成本约为其他错误的 0.86 倍时打平，等成本下点估计为正。

Conclusion: 自预测残差改变的是"被发现的错误是哪一类"而非错误总数，适合作为针对框错物体的廉价单遍筛选信号；但其收益仅来自 MS-COCO 图像上的 RefCOCOg 清单，泛化范围有限，且残差权重依赖少量标注固定。

Abstract: Grounded vision-language models write boxes as text, and the mean probability of their coordinate tokens says little about whether a box is right. We study a single-pass signal for boxes on another object, which matter when only the object counts. A linear map, fitted per model to its own answers without labels, reads a box from the hidden state before writing. Its disagreement with the written box, the self-prediction residual, flags boxes on another object although the read-out is far less accurate; the residual's weight was fixed with labels on two models. On a replication list of 1,200 RefCOCOg test items, the residual, averaged over six models, raises the detection of these errors for each of fifteen single-pass scores, by 0.026 to 0.049 in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) on a label-free probe and probes trained on 2,000 labelled items. Over all errors, adding it to the label-free probe moves AUROC, averaged over the models, by less than 0.02 (95% interval). With a fifth of answers reviewed, the share of all errors caught moves from 0.518 to 0.524, that of boxes on another object from 0.505 to 0.607 (post hoc): the residual changes which errors are found, not how many, and in cost-weighted recall it breaks even when such a box costs about 0.86 times another error, and its gain is positive in point estimate at equal costs. These gains come from RefCOCOg lists of MS-COCO images.

</details>


### [92] [LoopSeek: Decoupling System One Search from System Two Answering for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28635)
*Chengzhong Wang,Lang Lin,Xinyang Liu,YueQin Yin,Mingyuan Zhou,Shengwei An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopSeek 将长视频问答中的证据搜索与答案生成解耦：由非思考型 System I 通过四类角色智能体在视频窗口树上做类型化决策式搜索，冻结的 System II 仅在选中帧上作答一次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答需先定位证据再推理，但帧预算有限：单轮帧选择易漏关键证据；自适应搜索又常依赖反复文本生成来决定下一步看哪里，计算开销大。

Method: 核心思想是把搜索表达为一系列小的、带类型的决策，使 System I 能找出 System II 所需证据。四个 System I 角色智能体在视频窗口树上导航，通过概率输出协同完成选帧、证据评估与搜索修正，不生成文本。为与 System II 对齐，System I 从"System II 仅凭该证据即可答对"的问题的标注证据上学习；验证器的证据估计在搜索中直接引导、无需调用 System II，System II 保持冻结，只在选中帧上作答一次。

Result: 在五个长视频基准上，冻结的 Qwen3-VL-8B 读取 32 帧时，平均准确率由均匀基线的 53.5 提升至 63.9；每题 GPU 时间比智能体式方法减少 32%–80%。

Conclusion: 将证据搜索交由轻量非生成式 System I、并将答案生成限制为单次冻结推理，可在有限帧预算下同时提升长视频问答准确率与计算效率。

Abstract: Long video question answering requires finding relevant evidence before reasoning over it. With a limited frame budget, single-pass frame selection can miss critical evidence, while adaptive search often relies on repeated text generation to decide where to look. We introduce LoopSeek, a framework that decouples evidence search from answer generation through a division of labor between a non-thinking System I and a generative System II. Our key insight is that search can be expressed as a sequence of small, typed decisions, allowing System I to find the evidence that System II needs. Four System I role agents navigate a tree of video windows and coordinate through probability outputs to select frames, assess evidence, and revise the search without generating text. To align search with System II, System I learns from annotated evidence on questions that System II answers correctly only from that evidence, and the verifier's evidence estimates steer the search without calling System II, which stays frozen and answers once from the selected frames. On five long-video benchmarks, with a frozen Qwen3-VL-8B reading 32 frames, LoopSeek improves average accuracy from 53.5 to 63.9 over the uniform baseline and takes 32--80\% less GPU time per question than agentic methods.

</details>


### [93] [CrossPanel: Exposing the Answer-Evidence Gap in Scientific Figure Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28641)
*Zhengyang Lu,QianhongWen,Chenpeng Wang,Qiang Huang,Ziyang Ling,Huan Li,Chenziao,Yibo Qiu,Xiaojie Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 CrossPanel 基准（2,650 条人工复核问题），揭示"答案正确但证据错误"的 gap：15 个视觉语言模型中 8.42%–56.64% 的正确答案含显式事实错误，并通过干预实验证明显式对象—条件提示比单纯增加图像切块更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表题中，正确答案可能掩盖对实验的错误描述。跨面板阅读证据时，必须把每个观察绑定到赋予其意义的对象与实验条件上；而现有评测只以最终答案准确率为指标，无法暴露答案与证据之间的错配。

Method: 构建 CrossPanel 基准，含 2,650 条人工复核问题，任务形式为并行观察组合或依赖式查询；设计响应审计，将回答分解为对象选择、实验条件、跨面板绑定和局部读数四个环节；在 251 道诊断题上评测 15 个视觉语言模型；进一步开展受控干预，对比"经过验证的对象—条件提示"（不含所请求的测量值）与"增加图像切块"两种辅助方式。

Result: 在 251 道诊断题上，15 个视觉语言模型的正确答案中有 8.42%–56.64% 含显式事实错误；审计可区分"对错误目标的连贯解读"与"对正确目标的错误解读"；在 57 道题上，对象—条件提示使 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 的目标查询成功率提升 22.81 个百分点，而仅增加图像切块收效甚微；其他模型从两类辅助的组合中获益最大。

Conclusion: 实验归因是独立于局部感知的一项独立能力要求；CrossPanel 的审计与干预框架使答案—证据 gap 与归因能力均可被测量，超越最终答案准确率这一单一指标。

Abstract: A correct answer to a scientific-figure question can conceal an incorrect account of the experiment. Reading evidence across panels requires keeping each observation attached to the object and conditions that give it meaning. We introduce CrossPanel, a benchmark of 2,650 human-reviewed questions that exposes this answer-evidence gap. Its tasks combine parallel observations or follow dependent queries, while a response audit separates object selection, experimental conditions, cross-panel binding, and local readout. Across 15 vision-language models on 251 diagnostic questions, 8.42%-56.64% of correct answers contain explicit factual errors. The audit distinguishes coherent readings of the wrong target from incorrect readings of the right one. Controlled interventions then test how these failures can be repaired: verified object-condition hints, which withhold the requested measurement, raise Qwen3.5-35B-A3B's target-query success by 22.81 percentage points on 57 questions, while additional image tiles alone yield little improvement. Other models benefit most from combining both forms of assistance. These findings identify experimental attribution as a distinct requirement alongside local perception, and make both measurable beyond final-answer accuracy.

</details>


### [94] [OrthoLatentCAD: Orthographic Spatial Latents for Hand-Drawn CAD Program Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28646)
*Xiaolei Zhou,Xinjie Zhu,Chuangjie Fang,Chenyu Wang,Jianwei Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Hand2CAD 基准与 OrthoLatentCAD 方法，从手绘三视图恢复可执行的草图-拉伸 CAD 程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从栅格化的正/右/俯三视图恢复可执行 CAD 程序具有挑战性：2D 布局噪声大、无尺寸标注，且需协调为有序的建模历史；同时需要跨视图几何推理与视觉特征—程序表示的对齐。

Method: 构建 Hand2CAD 基准（215K 合成视图-CAD 程序对 + 1,000 组真人三视图）；提出 OrthoLatentCAD：在规范平面上融合局部几何与全局上下文，在共享轴坐标索引的成对平面线间交换证据，并经冻结 CAD 自编码器的查询将空间表示映射为有序程序潜变量。

Result: 在一至三视图配置下，程序准确率、编译几何与 CAD 内核有效性均优于同协议基线，且视图增多带来收益；未用真实数据微调时在留出真实手绘图上亦优于基线。

Conclusion: OrthoLatentCAD 有效连接手绘正交视图与可执行 CAD 程序，基准与代码将公开。

Abstract: Freehand orthographic drawings capture early design intent, yet recovering executable sketch--extrude CAD programs from raster front, right, and top views remains challenging: noisy, dimension-free 2D layouts must be reconciled into an ordered construction history. We introduce Hand2CAD, a benchmark of 215K ABC-derived synthetic orthographic-drawing--CAD program pairs and 1,000 real three-view sets drawn by CAD-trained participants. Recovering CAD programs from orthographic drawings requires both cross-view geometric reasoning and alignment between visual features and program representations. We propose OrthoLatentCAD to address these two challenges. It fuses local geometry with global context on canonical planes, exchanges evidence between paired plane lines indexed by the same shared-axis coordinate, and maps the spatial representation to ordered program latents through queries inherited from a frozen CAD autoencoder. Across the evaluated one- to three-view configurations, OrthoLatentCAD outperforms protocol-matched baselines in procedural accuracy, compiled geometry, and CAD-kernel validity while benefiting from additional views. Without real-data fine-tuning, it also outperforms the baselines on held-out real drawings. We will release the dataset, code, and evaluation scripts.

</details>


### [95] [Beyond Full-Context Accuracy: Context-Marginalized Evaluation of Test-Time Structure-Aware Inference in EEG-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28648)
*Ke Xiao,Banghua Yang,Yingjie Li,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文边际化检索评估（CMRE），在采样子集上下文下反复评估跨被试EEG-to-image检索，将"结构可利用性"（相对独立检索的增益）与"上下文稳健性"（同查询Top-1一致性）分离，揭示现有全上下文评估结论在上下文变化下并不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时结构感知推理利用无标签测试查询间的相互关系提升跨被试EEG-to-image检索，但使预测依赖于查询集合的构成；而现有评估通常只在单一"全查询上下文"下报告准确率，无法判断增益与比较性结论是否随上下文变化而成立。

Method: 提出CMRE：对每个目标查询在采样的上下文中反复评估，复用固定的查询-图库相似度矩阵的对应行。该框架区分两类指标——结构可利用性（相对独立检索的Top-1增益）与上下文稳健性（同查询Top-1的上下文一致性）。在THINGS-EEG2上采用留一被试评估，覆盖6种冻结表征与6种结构感知推理规则，共24种"表征×上下文规模"条件。

Result: 结构感知推理在全上下文下Top-1最多提升20.40个百分点；但全上下文选出的最优规则在17/24（70.8%）条件中不再最优，沿用全上下文冠军在缩减上下文下平均损失2.70、最大损失8.29个百分点。表征排序随推理规则与上下文变化，独立检索下的排序在结构感知推理下不能稳定保持；且更大检索增益不必然意味着更强上下文稳健性。

Conclusion: 全上下文准确率仅刻画上下文依赖推理的一个工作点；CMRE作为补充，刻画结构增益、上下文一致性与规则/表征比较如何随查询上下文变化，说明检索有效性与预测稳定性是两个互补视角。

Abstract: Test-time structure-aware inference can improve cross-subject EEG-to-image retrieval by exploiting relations among unlabeled test queries, but it also makes predictions depend on query-set composition. Existing evaluations typically report accuracy under a single full-query context, leaving unclear whether gains and comparative conclusions hold as context changes. We introduce Context-Marginalized Retrieval Evaluation (CMRE), which repeatedly evaluates each target query in sampled contexts using the corresponding rows of a fixed query-gallery similarity matrix. CMRE distinguishes structural exploitability, quantified by gains over independent retrieval, from context robustness, measured by same-query Top-1 context consistency. Under leave-one-subject-out evaluation on THINGS-EEG2 across six frozen representations and six structure-aware inference rules, structure-aware inference improves full-context Top-1 accuracy by up to 20.40 percentage points over independent retrieval. However, the rule selected under full-context evaluation is no longer optimal in 17 of 24 representation-context-size conditions (70.8%). Selecting the full-context winner incurs an average loss of 2.70 percentage points and a maximum loss of 8.29 percentage points under reduced contexts. Representation rankings are likewise inference- and context-dependent: the ordering under independent retrieval is not consistently preserved under structure-aware inference. Moreover, larger retrieval gains do not necessarily imply greater context robustness, showing that retrieval effectiveness and prediction stability provide complementary views of structure-aware inference. Full-context accuracy captures one operating point of context-dependent inference. CMRE complements it by characterizing how structural gains, context consistency, and comparisons of rules and representations change with query context.

</details>


### [96] [S-Mind: Internalizing Multi-View Geometry for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28649)
*Muxin Fu,Peike Yu,Guibin Zhang,Wenrui Zhou,Runze Liu,Lang Feng,Fuchao Yang,Feng Chen,Yixuan Li,Mengao Zhang,Shiying Zhang,Wen Shen,Ling Pan,Shuicheng Yan,Bo An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S-Mind 通过紧凑空间 token 将多视角几何内化到 MLLM 中，监督其承载融合的 3D 场景并在每个推理视角下重新表达，从而赋予模型内禀的空间智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 走向物理世界需要空间智能，其两大认知基石是多视角融合成一致的 3D 场景、以及随推理进程对场景做视角变换的心智操作。作者的空间探测表明，即使经过空间微调，MLLM 基本不具备这两项能力。

Method: 提出 S-Mind：用一小组紧凑的空间 token 在 MLLM 内部表征多视角几何；监督这些 token 承载融合后的 3D 场景，并在推理切换到任一新视角时重新发射这些 token，以从该视角重新表达该场景。

Result: 在四个空间基准上，S-Mind 将 Qwen3.5 主干提升最多 22.26 分，平均准确率超过 GPT-6-Astra（+0.45）、Kimi-K3.1-Pro（+1.97）、GLM-5.3-Flash（+7.80）等前沿通用模型；探测显示融合与变换准确率分别达 65.11% 与 82.17%（基线最高仅 16.02% 与 37.28%）；推理速度约为同主干工具型智能体的 100 倍。

Conclusion: 经空间 token 在训练中注入后，空间能力成为主干的内禀属性，即使抽走这些 token 仍能保持，为 MLLM 的空间智能提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence, the ability to reason about 3D space from visual observations, becomes essential as multimodal large language models (MLLMs) move into the physical world. Beyond perceiving each view, it rests on two cornerstones of human cognition: fusing multiple views into one coherent 3D scene and mentally transforming that scene to new viewpoints as reasoning unfolds. Yet our spatial probes reveal that MLLMs largely lack both capabilities, even after spatial fine-tuning. We therefore present S-Mind, which internalizes multi-view geometry within the MLLM through a compact set of spatial tokens. It supervises these tokens to hold the fused 3D scene and re-emits them to re-express this scene from every viewpoint the reasoning adopts. Across four spatial benchmarks, S-Mind improves its Qwen3.5 backbones by up to 22.26 points and surpasses frontier general models such as GPT-6-Astra (+0.45), Kimi-K3.1-Pro (+1.97), and GLM-5.3-Flash (+7.80) in average accuracy. Moreover, the same probes confirm that it acquires both capabilities, reaching fusion and transformation accuracies of 65.11% and 82.17%, against at most 16.02% and 37.28% for the baselines. At inference, S-Mind also runs about 100x faster than a tool-based agent on the same backbone. Instilled through these spatial tokens during training, spatial capability becomes intrinsic to the backbone and persists even when the tokens are withheld, offering a promising path toward spatial intelligence in MLLMs.

</details>


### [97] [UACR: Uncertainty-Aware Collaborative Routing for Explainable Industrial Anomaly Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28655)
*Yiwei Li,Qiyang Chen,Zheming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UACR——面向可解释工业异常检测的不确定性感知协同路由框架，通过按样本不确定性自适应分配计算，在在线检测预算下同时兼顾检测精度、推理效率与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测既要准确判别异常，又要向操作员提供可信解释，还须满足在线检测的严格时延约束。现有方法对所有样本采用固定计算路径，难以在精度、效率与可解释性之间取得平衡。

Method: UACR分两阶段：阶段一为离线训练与路由超参数搜索，学习异常检测模块并校准驱动路由的不确定性估计器；阶段二为不确定性感知自适应路由，依据每个输入的估计不确定性将其协同分派到不同检测路径，使模糊样本获得更深度的计算审查。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上，UACR相较基线方法精度提升超过5.16%，时延降低超过31.5%，工业检测可解释性提升超过15%。

Conclusion: 按不确定性动态分配计算资源的协同路由可在在线预算内同时提升工业异常检测的精度、效率与可解释性，为面向操作员的可信检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly inspection requires not only accurate anomaly discrimination but also trustworthy explanations for operators, while satisfying the strict latency constraints of online inspection. Existing methods, however, typically apply a fixed computational path to all samples, making it difficult to strike a good balance among detection accuracy, inference efficiency, and interpretability. To address this issue, we propose UACR, an uncertainty-aware collaborative routing framework for explainable industrial anomaly inspection. UACR aims to deliver reliable anomaly decisions together with operator-facing explanations under the online inspection budget, while adaptively allocating computation so that ambiguous cases receive deeper scrutiny. The framework consists of two stages. Stage I, Offline Training and Routing Hyperparameter Search, learns the anomaly inspection modules and calibrates the uncertainty estimator that drives routing. Stage II, Uncertainty-Aware Adaptive Routing, collaboratively dispatches each input across inspection paths according to its estimated uncertainty. Experimental results on multiple public datasets show that UACR achieves over 5.16% improvement in accuracy compared to the baseline method, while reducing latency by over 31.5%. It also enhances the interpretability of industrial detection by over 15%.

</details>


### [98] [Dual-Stream Event-to-Video Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28656)
*Lei Sun,Zhendong Li,Chenxin Shao,Weilun Li,Ruibo Ming,Kaiwei Wang,Jinjin Gu,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EvDualDiff 双流扩散框架，借助时间映射事件提供的亮度参考与运动事件线索重建视频，并配套设计镜头光调制硬件与 EvDual 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机缺乏绝对强度信息，事件转视频本质不适定；需引入与事件强度变化互补的亮度参考来消解该模糊性。灵感来自人类视觉中 parvocellular（空间/亮度）与 magnocellular（时间/运动）通路的互补处理。

Method: 双流框架：一路由时间映射（temporal-mapping）事件提供亮度参考，另一路提供运动事件线索；以双流为条件，视频扩散模型从单张时间映射图自回归重建长序列；采用光度约束与基于自生成 rollout 的微调抑制漂移。硬件上仅在事件相机镜头前加装极简光调制机制，并采集含时间映射事件、运动事件与同步 RGB 真值的数据集 EvDual。

Result: 在公开基准与自建 EvDual 数据集上，EvDualDiff 在保真度、感知质量和视频级真实性上均优于现有事件转视频重建方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入互补亮度参考的双流扩散建模，可有效缓解事件转视频的不适定性；方法硬件成本低、可长序列重建，代码与数据集将开源。

Abstract: Event cameras capture brightness changes with high temporal resolution and dynamic range, but converting event streams into videos remains fundamentally ill-posed due to the lack of absolute intensity information. Resolving this ambiguity calls for a brightness reference that complements the intensity changes recorded by events. Inspired by the complementary spatial and temporal processing of the parvocellular and magnocellular pathways in human vision, we propose EvDualDiff, a dual-stream framework that combines a brightness reference from temporal-mapping events with motion-event cues for video reconstruction. Conditioned on both streams, our video diffusion model autoregressively reconstructs long sequences from a single temporal map. Photometric constraints and finetuning on self-generated rollouts reduce accumulated drift. At the hardware level, our approach requires only a minimal light-modulation mechanism on the lens of an event camera. We build this setup and collect a dataset comprising temporal-mapping events, motion events and synchronised ground-truth RGB frames. Experiments on a public benchmark and on our collected EvDual dataset demonstrate that EvDualDiff outperforms existing event-to-video reconstruction methods in fidelity, perceptual quality and video-level realism. The code and dataset will be released.

</details>


### [99] [Seeing Right, Writing Wrong: Where Short Chain-of-Thought Breaks Spatial VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28665)
*Chenhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 短链式思维（CoT）会把空间问题从"感知问题"退化为"转录问题"：模型写下的空间关系会取代图像成为答案依据，故CoT是助益还是损害取决于所写关系是否比直接作答更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs在图像仍在上下文中的情况下，逐步推理反而在空间问题上表现更差，现有解释不足，需厘清CoT劣化的机制。

Method: 测试15个开源VLM（2B–38B）与6个前沿闭源模型；排除token数、轮次格式、视觉遗忘等因素；将失败拆解为三阶段：写关系时视觉线索重加权、文本接管答案、结论句映射翻转；用"固定去掉结论的推理、替换反深度图像"的对照实验量化文本依赖，并比较闭源模型的再接地行为；再做单模型微调试点。

Result: 写关系普遍削弱"越低越近"捷径（13个开源模型全部）并丢弃有效线索产生错误关系；固定自身推理文本后换反深度图，渲染孪生图上答案仅动2–9分（直接作答可达77分），自然照片上平均3.0–3.4分（直接作答20.5–69.6分）；结论句可反转真关系，多见于反向问题；错误关系与结论误映射占CoT损失的4/5；Qwen3-VL-32B同样如此，规模无法消除瓶颈；闭源模型不受损，写关系与作答同样准确，6个中3个写后会复核图像，关掉推理的3个则像开源模型一样锁定。

Conclusion: 开源VLM缺失的是"答案步再接地"（answer-step re-grounding）；用图像决定标签的固定推理微调可在自然照片上恢复该能力；数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Asking a vision-language model (VLM) to think step by step often makes it worse at spatial questions, even though the image stays in context. We show that short chain-of-thought (CoT) turns a perception problem into a transcription problem. Once a VLM writes a spatial relation, its answer follows that sentence instead of the image, so whether CoT helps or hurts depends largely on whether the written relation is more accurate than answering directly. Studying 15 open VLMs (2B-38B) and six frontier closed models, we rule out token count, turn format, and visual forgetting in open models and trace the failure through three stages. First, writing re-weights visual cues, weakening a "lower is closer" shortcut in all 13 open models tested but also discarding valid cues, which produces false relations. Second, the written text takes over. With the model's own conclusion-removed rationale held fixed, swapping in an opposite-depth image moves the answer by 2-9 points on rendered twins (vs. up to 77 for direct answers) and by 3.0-3.4 points on average on natural photographs (vs. 20.5-69.6). Third, the concluding sentence can invert even a true relation, mainly on converse questions. Together, false relations and conclusion mis-mapping account for four in five CoT losses. Scale does not remove this bottleneck: Qwen3-VL-32B shows the same pattern. Frontier closed models are not hurt: they write relations as accurately as they answer, and three of six re-check the image after writing. The three queried with reasoning off lock in like open models, so re-grounding co-occurs with answer-turn reasoning; we identify answer-step re-grounding as the step open VLMs lack. In a single-model pilot, fine-tuning on fixed rationales with image-determined labels restores it on natural photographs. We open-sourced all our datasets and code at https://ming-zch.github.io/seeing-right-writing-wrong.

</details>


### [100] [RecallWorld: Episodic Memory for Persistent Embodied Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28666)
*Sarosij Bose,Gitika Karumuri,Chenqi Li,Allen Tu,Srikumar Sastry,Mellon Zhang,Avinash Ravichandran,Alexander Schwing,Konstantinos Karydis,Amit K. Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RecallWorld，一种具备持久记忆的具身视频世界模型：将短多视角片段联合重建为 Scenelets 并保持跨片段几何独立，结合覆盖度检索与目标视角可见性几何判定实现一致的记忆条件化，在回访场景时显著提升几何与外观保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型虽能生成合理的新视角，但缺乏持久记忆，智能体回访同一场景时无法保持该地点的几何与外观。核心难点在于如何有效利用历史视角构建记忆：独立重建每个视角会浪费邻近视角间的几何约束，而反复重建不断增长的历史又会让后期预测误差污染早期几何。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) Scenelet 记忆——将短的多视角片段联合重建为一个 Scenelet，同时令不同 episode 之间几何相互独立，避免误差跨片段传播；(2) 相机感知的射线抬升——从单张源图像出发，把透视图像与原生鱼眼图像统一映射到共同世界坐标系，保留宽视场内容以支持透视生成，每段生成结果延展 Scenelet 记忆并作为后续生成的条件；(3) 记忆检索与融合——基于覆盖度的检索从完整历史中选取互补的 Scenelet，并在外观融合前以几何方式判定目标视角的可见性，从而提供一致的记忆条件化。

Result: 在四个数据集上，相较于最强评测基线，RecallWorld 在部分回访场景下 PSNR 提升 2.6 dB，在完整循环回访下提升 3.9 dB，表明其在探索与返回过程中对场景同一性的保持能力显著更强。

Conclusion: 通过 Scenelets 的片段内联合重建与片段间几何独立、配合覆盖度检索和几何可见性判定，RecallWorld 在记忆一致性与回访保真度上取得明显增益，为具身视频世界模型的持久记忆提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Video world models generate plausible novel views but often lack persistent memory, failing to preserve a place's geometry and appearance when an agent revisits a scene. A central challenge is how previous views should be effectively used for creating memory. Reconstructing them independently underutilizes geometric constraints between nearby views, while repeatedly reconstructing a growing history can propagate later prediction errors into earlier geometry. We introduce RecallWorld, a persistent embodied video world model that jointly reconstructs short multi-view episodes into Scenelets while keeping geometry independent across episodes. Starting from a single source image, camera-aware ray lifting places perspective and native fisheye images in a common world frame, retaining wide-field content for perspective generation. Each generated segment extends the Scenelet memory and conditions subsequent generation. Coverage-based retrieval selects complementary Scenelets from the full history, while target-view visibility is resolved geometrically before appearance fusion, providing consistent memory conditioning for guiding subsequent generation. Across four datasets, RecallWorld improves revisit PSNR over the strongest evaluated baseline by 2.6 dB on partial revisits and 3.9 dB on full cycles, demonstrating substantially stronger preservation of scene identity across exploration and return.

</details>


### [101] [Learning Continuous Scene Depth Representations via Cross-Modal Gaussian Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28670)
*Zhiwei Zhong,Lingyu Zhu,Baoliang Chen,Peilin Chen,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨模态高斯渲染框架，学习连续深度表征，实现任意尺度深度超分辨率，兼顾边界锐度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际RGB-D系统中深度与彩色图像分辨率差距多变，需灵活上采样因子；现有方法多针对预设整数倍尺度，隐式方法虽支持任意尺度但依赖逐像素神经解码、计算开销大，且难以恢复锐利深度边界与精细结构。

Method: 1) 构建分层高斯表征，以可学习高斯基元定义显式空间支撑，实现渐进式深度重建；2) 设计结构感知高斯聚合模块，结合空间权重与跨模态特征亲和度，抑制跨深度不连续区域的聚合；3) 提出尺度自适应高斯渲染模块，使高斯空间核适配目标像素足迹；4) 采用训练策略在训练过程中逐步引入更高频引导信息以促进跨模态学习。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，方法在分布内与分布外上采样尺度下均有效。

Conclusion: 基于高斯基元的连续深度表征可有效替代逐像素隐式解码，实现任意尺度、边界锐利的彩色引导深度超分辨率。

Abstract: Depth super-resolution with color guidance is a fundamental problem in geometric scene understanding that aims to reconstruct high-resolution depth using structural cues from color images. In practical RGB-D systems, the resolution gap between depth and color images often varies, requiring flexible upsampling factors. However, many existing methods are designed for predefined integer scales. Although implicit methods support arbitrary-scale reconstruction, they typically rely on computationally demanding per-pixel neural decoding and may struggle to recover sharp depth boundaries and fine structures. To this end, we propose a cross-modal Gaussian rendering framework that learns continuous depth representations for arbitrary-scale depth super-resolution. Specifically, we first construct hierarchical Gaussian representations for progressive depth reconstruction, with explicit spatial support defined by learnable Gaussian primitives. Building on this representation, we design a Structure-Aware Gaussian Aggregation module that combines spatial weights with cross-modal feature affinities to reduce aggregation across depth discontinuities. We then develop a Scale-Adaptive Gaussian Rendering module that adapts Gaussian spatial kernels to the target pixel footprint. To further facilitate cross-modal learning, we propose a training strategy that gradually introduces higher-frequency guidance information during training. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method at both in-distribution and out-of-distribution upsampling scales.

</details>


### [102] [ViewAgent3D: A Training-Free Multi-View Pipeline for 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28673)
*Sarosij Bose,Arindam Dutta,Konstantinos Karydis,Amit K. Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练、查询条件化的多视图RGB证据选择流水线，仅用2D图像与推理期工具完成多种3D场景理解任务，在3D VQA上约提升10%、稠密描述与具身问答约提升5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景理解多依赖显式3D表示（点云、体素、场景图）或专门训练的多模态模型；作者认为许多查询的瓶颈不在于缺乏更强的3D表示，而在于能否选出使查询可回答的一小部分多视图RGB视角。

Method: 免训练、查询条件化流水线：先将查询分解为若干概念（识别有助于推理的独特属性），驱动由粗到细的证据选择——粗检索对所有视图打分，多样性感知选择器降低冗余，重排序阶段依据目标可见性或可回答性筛出候选视图；所得证据对需要显式定位的查询可选地做grounding，并通过自验证步骤精炼最终回答，尤其针对开放式问题。

Result: 推理期不使用任务特定训练与显式3D表示，3D VQA约提升10%，稠密描述与Embodied QA约提升5%，且适用于不同场景；另提出基于评判者的评估体系，可容纳多个语义正确或部分正确的答案。

Conclusion: 证据/视角选择而非更强的3D表示可成为3D场景理解的有效切入点；免训练的多视图流水线具备跨任务泛化性，配套的评判者式评估有助于更全面地衡量场景理解质量。

Abstract: 3D scene understanding tasks are often approached by building explicit 3D scene representations or by training specialized multi-modal models over point clouds, voxels, or scene graphs. We take a complementary view: for many queries, the main bottleneck is not the absence of a stronger 3D representation but the selection of a small subset of multi-view RGB views that actually makes the query answerable. We present a training-free, query-conditioned multi-view pipeline that performs diverse tasks using only 2D images and a set of tools used purely for inference. Our method first decomposes each query into a set of concepts that identify unique attributes that assist with reasoning. This drives a coarse-to-fine evidence selection process: a coarse retrieval selects and scores all views, a diversity-aware selector reduces redundancy, and a reranking stage helps shortlist views by target visibility or answerability. The resulting evidence is optionally grounded for queries that benefit from explicit localization, and a self-verification step helps refine the final response, especially for open-ended cases. Despite using no task-specific training and no explicit 3D representations at inference time, our approach provides approximately 10% gain on 3D VQA and 5% gain on dense captioning and Embodied QA while being applicable across different scenarios. We further propose a unique judge-based evaluation system, motivating a judge who takes into account many semantically correct or partially correct answers, thereby improving holistic scene understanding.

</details>


### [103] [CHORD: Closed-loop Human-Object Rectified-flow Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28682)
*Yi Wang,Yubo Zhao,Chi-Keung Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHORD：两阶段rectified-flow框架，用12个part token在紧凑空间中联合规划物体6-DoF轨迹、接触状态与各运动链端/中关节，并在生成过程中将计划对物体表面进行测量反馈；第二阶段固定计划并补全其余身体与手指关节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本驱动的3D人-物交互生成需全身与物体运动保持接触耦合。现有方法要么把物体位姿、接触区域、肢体姿态等核心变量与大量从属关节纠缠在一起，要么只规划末端执行器或粗粒度轨迹。生成过程中没有任何阶段以"既已确定又可被观测"的形式保持交互，导致接触依赖外部引导、后处理精修或无表面测量的内部引导。

Method: 两阶段rectified flow。第一阶段Draft：每个时间patch使用12个part token联合规划物体6-DoF轨迹、接触状态及每条运动链的末端与中间关节；在每个flow step由闭式clean estimate解码期望交互，对物体表面进行测量，并将反馈以零额外网络评估路由到相应的part token。第二阶段Full：保持该计划不变，补全其余身体与手指关节。

Result: 在InterAct数据集上，FID降低33%，手部接触F1由0.730提升至0.834；反馈机制在未见物体上改善接触质量，并可即插即用地迁移到现有HOI生成器。

Conclusion: 将交互压缩为可测量、可反馈的紧凑计划并显式对物体表面观测，是提升文本驱动HOI生成接触质量的有效途径，且该反馈模块具备通用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D human-object interaction (HOI) generation requires synthesizing whole-body human and object motions that stay coupled through contact.
