Yet existing generators either entangle the core variables that govern an interaction (object pose, contacting regions, and limb posture) with dozens of subordinate joints, or plan only end-effectors or coarse trajectories. No stage of generation holds the interaction in a form that is both settled and observed, which leaves contact to external guidance, post-hoc refiners, or internal guidance without a surface measurement. We present CHORD, a two-stage rectified-flow framework that settles the interaction in a compact plan and observes that plan against the object surface while it is being generated. First, Draft uses twelve part tokens per temporal patch to jointly plan the 6-DoF object trajectory, contact states, and the end and middle joints of every kinematic chain. At each flow step, Draft decodes the expected interaction from the closed-form clean estimate, measures it against the object surface, and routes this feedback to the responsible part token with zero extra network evaluations. Second, Full keeps this plan unchanged and infills the remaining body and finger joints. On InterAct, CHORD reduces FID by 33% and lifts hand contact F1 from 0.730 to 0.834, while its feedback improves contact on unseen objects and transfers plug-and-play to existing HOI generators.

</details>


### [104] [Rethinking Visual Token Retention in Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28684)
*Fawaz Sammani,Nikos Deligiannis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究视觉token在生成时间与Transformer深度两维度上的影响力演化，发现视觉依赖既非时间上持续也非深度上均匀分布，据此可在生成过程中移除至多59%输入prompt视觉token、跨所有层移除11%，性能几乎不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大型视觉语言模型依赖数百上千个视觉token支持多模态推理，但"是否必须持续访问完整视觉token"尚不清楚，存在冗余浪费。

Method: 在长形式视觉推理基准与多个模型规模上，沿生成时间和Transformer深度分析视觉注意力动态；并用因果干预（在特定时间步/层移除全部视觉token）验证依赖结构。

Result: 视觉注意力在生成初期峰值后迅速下降、转向已生成推理token；深度上集中于中间层、末层极弱。因果干预证实依赖非时间持续、非深度均匀。生成过程中可移除最多59%输入prompt token（视频基准65%），跨所有层移除11%，性能损失很小。

Conclusion: 多模态推理中保留每层、每步的完整视觉上下文并非必要，视觉token存在显著可剪枝冗余，为高效推理与token压缩提供依据。

Abstract: Autoregressive Large Vision-Language Models solve multimodal reasoning tasks by retaining hundreds or thousands of visual tokens, allowing every generated token to attend to the visual tokens at any time during the generation process. However, whether continuous access to these visual tokens is necessary remains poorly understood. In this work, we investigate how visual token influence evolves along two dimensions: generation time and transformer depth. Using long-form visual reasoning benchmarks and multiple model scales, we first analyze the attention dynamics across generation time and transformer depth. We identify a temporal and depth-wise structure in the use of visual information. Visual attention peaks during the initial generation steps and then rapidly declines as attention shifts toward previously generated reasoning tokens. Across depth, visual influence is concentrated in intermediate layers and becomes very weak in the final layers. We further verify these observations with causal intervention that removes all visual tokens across generation time and model depth. These interventions confirm that visual-token dependence in multimodal reasoning is neither temporally persistent nor uniformly distributed across transformer depth. Thus, retaining the complete visual context at every layer and decoding step may be unnecessary. Our experiments demonstrate that we can remove up to 59\% of tokens from the full input prompt across generation time and 11\% across all transformer layers, while incurring only a very small performance drop. On video benchmarks, up to 65\% of the input prompt tokens can be removed during generation without affecting performance.

</details>


### [105] [CoopOcc: Cooperative 3D Semantic Occupancy Perception via Multi-Agent and Cross-Modal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28710)
*Zengle Zhu,Zhen Li,Tianyi Huai,Xinhu Zheng,Liuqing Yang,Rongqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoopOcc，通过多智能体与跨模态融合实现协同3D语义占用感知，并构建SemanticVVSim仿真数据集与统一基准，配合CHORD蒸馏实现仿真到真实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一视角语义占用表示无法弥补遮挡与传感器覆盖造成的信息缺失；多智能体协作可缓解该问题，但不同智能体信息在不同空间区域效用不均；且可靠多智能体融合依赖各智能体的特征表示，需借助跨模态互补的语义与几何信息来构建，而在多智能体间建立可靠3D对应关系的同时保持模态特定结构仍具挑战。

Method: CoopOcc包含两个核心模块：ego-query cross-agent attention (EQCA)，以自车查询选择性聚合跨智能体的对齐特征；anchor-based bidirectional feature interaction (ABFI)，在匹配坐标处进行跨模态双向特征交互。此外构建仿真数据集SemanticVVSim并建立统一基准；针对仿真到真实迁移提出confidence-aware hierarchical occupancy and relational distillation (CHORD)。

Result: 在SemanticVVSim上，CoopOcc在十个对比方法中总体性能最佳；在Occ3D-nuScenes上，CHORD利用SemanticVVSim的知识带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作与跨模态融合相结合可有效提升3D语义占用感知，且通过置信度感知的分层占用与关系蒸馏可将仿真知识迁移至真实域。

Abstract: Semantic occupancy perception has emerged as a promising paradigm for jointly modeling 3D scene geometry and semantic information. However, this unified representation cannot compensate for missing information caused by occlusion and sensor coverage. Multi-agent cooperation alleviates this limitation, but information from different agents is not equally useful across spatial regions. Meanwhile, feature representations from each agent form the basis of reliable multi-agent fusion. Cross?modal fusion constructs such representations by integrating complementary semantic and geometric information. Despite this complementarity, jointly establishing reliable 3D correspondence across agents and preserving modality-specific structures remains challenging. To address these limitations, we propose CoopOcc, which performs cooperative 3D semantic occupancy perception through multi-agent and cross-modal fusion. CoopOcc uses ego?query cross-agent attention (EQCA) to selectively aggregate aligned features across agents, and anchor-based bidirectional feature interaction (ABFI) for cross-modal exchange at matched coordinates. We further construct SemanticVVSim, a simulation dataset, and establish a unified benchmark. For simulation-to-real transfer, we propose confidence-aware hierarchical occupancy and relational distillation (CHORD). CoopOcc performs best overall among ten evaluated methods on SemanticVVSim. On Occ3D-nuScenes, CHORD improves performance using knowledge from SemanticVVSim. The code is available at https://github.com/LOTEAT/CoopOcc.

</details>


### [106] [When Local Semantics Disagree: Learning to Detect Fake News through Weakly Supervised Cross-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28711)
*Zeheng Wang,Hao Zhang,Yige Wang,Xi Xiao,Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DMIL：双分支多实例学习框架，将多模态假新闻检测建模为弱监督问题，分别在嵌入空间与实例空间捕捉跨模态语义与细粒度不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态假新闻检测依赖图文间的粗粒度全局相似度，忽略情感-场景错配、对象级篡改、语义不协调等细微且不均衡的跨模态不一致；同时将所有不一致视为同等重要，忽视领域差异，限制了泛化性与可解释性。

Method: 提出DMIL双分支多实例学习框架：将每篇新闻视为视觉与文本实例的"包"。分支一为嵌入空间分支，通过专家引导的模态融合聚合全局语义；分支二为实例空间分支，在局部到全局多个粒度上建模跨模态不一致。另引入检索增强的领域自适应路由器，利用检索到的领域样例对不一致进行情境化，并动态融合多层次线索。

Result: 在多个公开虚假信息基准上取得具有竞争力的SOTA性能，并在跨领域场景下展现出对假新闻较强的判别能力。

Conclusion: 以弱监督多实例学习替代粗粒度全局相似度建模，可更有效地刻画细粒度跨模态不一致与领域差异，兼顾检测性能与可解释性。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of multimodal social media content has made fake news detection a pressing challenge for the vision-language community. Most existing approaches rely on coarse global similarity between text and images, which overlooks subtle, uneven cross-modal inconsistencies -- such as sentiment-scene mismatches, object-level manipulations, or semantic dissonance between visual and textual cues. Moreover, these models treat all inconsistencies as equally informative, ignoring domain-specific variation in how such conflicts manifest, which limits both generalization and interpretability. To address these challenges, we propose DMIL, a dual-branch multiple instance learning framework that formulates multimodal fake news detection as a weakly supervised learning problem. Each news article is treated as a bag of visual and textual instances processed through two complementary branches: an embedding-space branch that aggregates global semantics via expert-guided modality fusion, and an instance-space branch that models cross-modal inconsistencies across local-to-global granularities. A retrieval-augmented, domain-adaptive router further contextualizes inconsistencies using retrieved domain exemplars and dynamically fuses multi-level cues. Extensive experiments on public misinformation benchmarks demonstrate that DMIL achieves competitive state-of-the-art performance and demonstrates strong discriminative capability against fake news across different domains.

</details>


### [107] [Domain-Specific Self-Supervised Learning for Cuneiform Sign Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28712)
*Asim Niaz,Morris Alper,Shai Gordin,Enrique Jiménez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在90余万张未标注楔形文字字形切图上预训练掩码自编码器CuneiMAE，其自监督表示在楔形文字符号识别任务上全面优于ImageNet预训练的MAE、CLIP与DINOv2，尤其在标签稀缺时优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 楔形文字符号识别困难：字形随历史时期、书写习惯与保存状况变化大，且专家标注稀缺。作者要验证领域特定的自监督预训练能否比通用自然图像预训练学到更好的楔形文字符号表示。

Method: 构建CuneiMAE——在900K+未标注字形切图上训练的掩码自编码器；与ImageNet预训练的MAE、CLIP、DINOv2在218类符号识别基准上对比，评估冻结线性探测、跨时期迁移、全量及少标签（如1%）微调；并检验边缘引导掩码、部件瓶颈等结构先验。

Result: 冻结表示线性探测43.7%（ImageNet-MAE为25.2%），跨时期迁移亦有提升；微调后在内部基准优于ImageNet预训练基线，1%标签时较ImageNet-MAE高20.4个百分点；外部基准微调后top-1 69.9%，较先前ResNet-18基线高约8个百分点；边缘引导掩码与部件瓶颈等显式结构先验未带来提升。

Conclusion: 领域特定自监督在楔形文字识别上表现强劲，标签受限场景收益最大；标准掩码重建已足够，额外结构先验无必要。

Abstract: Recognizing cuneiform signs is challenging: sign shapes vary across historical periods, scribal practices, and preservation conditions, while expert annotations are scarce. We study whether domain-specific self-supervised pretraining yields better cuneiform sign representations than generic natural-image pretraining. We pretrain a masked autoencoder, CuneiMAE, on over 900K unlabeled sign crops and compare it with ImageNet-pretrained MAE, CLIP, and DINOv2 on a 218-class sign recognition benchmark. CuneiMAE learns substantially stronger frozen representations (43.7% linear-probe accuracy vs. 25.2% for ImageNet-MAE), with gains over ImageNet-MAE on cross-period transfer. Under fine-tuning, CuneiMAE outperforms ImageNet-pretrained baselines on our internal benchmark, with the largest gains when labels are scarce (+20.4 pp over ImageNet-MAE with 1% of labels). On an external benchmark, CuneiMAE reaches 69.9% top-1 accuracy after fine-tuning, about 8 pp above the ResNet-18 baseline from prior work. Explicit structural priors, such as edge-guided masking and part bottlenecks, do not improve over standard masked reconstruction. Overall, domain-specific self-supervision shows strong performance on cuneiform recognition tasks, particularly when labels are limited.

</details>


### [108] [DynFlux: Learning to Dynamically Balance Consensus and Conflict in Multimodal Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28716)
*Zeheng Wang,Hao Zhang,Yige Wang,Xi Xiao,Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DynFlux，以时间异构图显式编码跨模态一致性与冲突关系，并在特征提取、特征整合与多模态融合三阶段进行调制，动态平衡一致-冲突以提升情感识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别受模态异质性困扰；跨模态一致性（共识）与冲突（不一致）共同提供互补线索，但传统方法存在语义歧义、很少显式建模二者交互，且难以捕捉其动态相互作用。

Method: DynFlux由时间异构图引导：引入专门的图分支，通过稀疏时间图显式捕捉跨模态依赖；在三个层级阶段调制常规多模态情感识别流程——特征提取阶段的自适应特征调制、特征整合阶段的引导聚合调制、多模态融合阶段的融合策略调制。

Result: 在基准数据集上的一致实验表明DynFlux持续优于SOTA方法；并在多模态意图识别与多模态讽刺检测任务上验证了其泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模并动态平衡跨模态一致性与冲突，DynFlux实现更丰富稳健的情感理解，且具备跨任务的通用性。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition remains challenging due to the intrinsic heterogeneity of different modalities. Effectively leveraging both cross-modal consensus (consistency) and conflict (inconsistency) is essential, as they jointly provide complementary cues that enhance emotion understanding from diverse perspectives. Traditional multimodal methods often suffer from semantic ambiguity and rarely explicitly model the interplay between cross-modal consensus and conflict. Moreover, existing approaches struggle to capture and exploit the dynamic interactions between these two complementary factors. To overcome these limitations, we propose DynFlux, a model guided by a temporal heterogeneous graph that encodes both consensus and conflict relationships across modalities. DynFlux introduces a dedicated graph branch that explicitly captures cross-modal dependencies through sparse temporal graphs, and modulates the conventional multimodal emotion recognition pipeline at three hierarchical stages: adaptive feature modulation during feature extraction, guided aggregation modulation during feature integration, and fusion strategy modulation at the multimodal fusion stage. Through this design, DynFlux dynamically balances cross-modal consensus and conflict, enabling richer and more robust emotion understanding. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets confirm that DynFlux consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, and its generality is further validated on multimodal intent recognition and multimodal sarcasm detection tasks.

</details>


### [109] [ReCoRain: Reliability-Coupled Content and Noise Conditioning for Event-Guided Video Deraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28717)
*Runzhe Li,Zhaocheng Yu,Binbin Song,Kui Jiang,Junjun Jiang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReCoRain是一个事件引导的残差视频扩散去雨框架，通过统一评估跨帧对应可靠性来同时耦合时间内容迁移与噪声相关性，在NTURain-Syn上达32.09 dB、RainSynLight25上达28.73 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件引导视频去雨依赖可靠的跨帧对应关系，但场景运动、雨水和传感器噪声产生的事件会带来模糊的匹配证据。在扩散式复原中，错误匹配会迁移无关内容，并使无关场景位置的初始噪声产生相关性，损害复原质量。

Method: 提出ReCoRain：(1) 以RGB外观、区间事件证据和前向-后向运动一致性共同评估对应可靠性；(2) 内容路径依据对齐可靠性聚合邻域特征；(3) 噪声路径利用组合的后向运动场及其可靠性估计，调控隐序列中初始噪声的共享；(4) 训练与推理阶段采用相同条件噪声构造，将时间噪声协调纳入残差flow-matching过程；(5) 时间监督在相同教师估计光流与可见性掩码下对齐复原视频与干净视频的运动补偿帧间差异，以容纳合理的外观变化。

Result: NTURain-Syn上PSNR达32.09 dB，RainSynLight25上达28.73 dB；两条路径同时启用可靠性门控相对全部关闭可将warping误差降低33.0%。

Conclusion: 统一的对应可靠性评估可有效抑制错误匹配引起的无关内容迁移与噪声伪相关，内容门控与噪声门控协同带来显著性能提升，验证了可靠性与扩散视频复原过程联合建模的有效性。

Abstract: Event-guided video deraining depends on reliable cross-frame correspondences, yet events generated by scene motion, rain, and sensor noise can provide ambiguous matching evidence. In diffusion-based restoration, an incorrect match can transfer unrelated content and correlate initial noise between unrelated scene locations. We propose ReCoRain, an event-guided residual video diffusion framework that couples temporal content transfer and noise correlation through a common assessment of correspondence reliability. This assessment combines RGB appearance, interval-event evidence, and forward-backward motion consistency. The content path aggregates neighboring features according to alignment reliability, while the noise path uses composed backward motion fields and their associated reliability estimates to regulate initial-noise sharing across the latent sequence. We use the same conditional noise construction during residual flow-matching training and inference, incorporating temporal noise coordination into the learned restoration process. Temporal supervision matches motion-compensated inter-frame differences in restored and clean videos under the same teacher-estimated flow and visibility mask, accommodating legitimate appearance changes. ReCoRain achieves 32.09 dB PSNR on NTURain-Syn and 28.73 dB on RainSynLight25. Enabling reliability gates in both paths reduces warping error by 33.0% relative to disabling both gates.

</details>


### [110] [AERIN: Aerial Instance Navigation with a Visual World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28723)
*Zhenzhen Jiao,Rui Tian,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AERIN视觉世界模型，通过动作条件预测引导无人机从高空搜索并接近参考图像指定的目标，同时主动调整视角获取高质量观测以利于实例验证，用紧凑的固定尺寸、目标条件化的潜在状态高效表示目标细节与场景上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高空观测覆盖广、利于高效搜索，但目标像素少，导致实例验证与动作选择困难；同时导航需要细粒度目标细节与和可见性相关的场景上下文，而反复预测稠密视觉表示计算代价高昂。

Method: AERIN作为一种动作条件预测的视觉世界模型，用紧凑、固定尺寸、目标条件化的潜在状态分别表示目标细节与场景上下文，以支持高效的动作评估，并据此引导无人机接近目标、调整视点以获取更高质量观测。

Result: 实验表明AERIN在目标接近与观测质量上优于所评估的基线方法，且决策时间显著低于同类世界模型。

Conclusion: 以目标条件化紧凑潜在状态为核心的世界模型可在高空参考图像目标导航中兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Searching from high altitude and navigating to a target instance specified by a reference image is a common application for UAVs. In such tasks, high-altitude observations provide broad ground coverage for efficient search, but targets occupy fewer pixels, making instance verification and action selection more difficult. We present AERIN, a visual world model that uses action-conditioned prediction to guide the UAV toward the target while adjusting its viewpoint to obtain higherquality observations for subsequent instance verification. Highaltitude navigation requires fine-grained target details and scene context relevant to visibility, but repeatedly predicting dense visual representations of this information is computationally expensive. AERIN addresses this challenge with a compact, fixedsize, target-conditioned latent state that separately represents both for efficient action evaluation. Experiments demonstrate that AERIN outperforms the evaluated baselines in target approach and observation quality, while requiring substantially less decision time than similar world models.

</details>


### [111] [Seeing Is Not Planning: Spatiotemporal Planning and Plan Repair in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28726)
*Siyuan Li,Ethan Lin,Xiang Li,Tianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Railbound-Bench：让模型在铁路拼图里加轨、一次性提交完整布局并由模拟器同步运行所有列车判定成败，失败后最多修复 5 次；九模型中最佳解出 59.5%，次佳 39.5%，其余 ≤10.0%。控制实验显示"知道任务规则≠会用规则"，视觉反馈仅能修正报告故障却少有实质修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界中的规划要求同时读取布局、预判状态演化并遵守任务规则；规划失败后还应利用错误信息进行修复。现有基准缺乏对"时空规划"与"计划修复"这两项能力的同时、可控评测。

Method: 构建 Railbound-Bench：模型观察铁路拼图，在预算内铺设轨道并提交一个完整布局；模拟器同步移动所有列车判定布局是否可行。失败后模型可见运行结果并重新提交，最多 5 次。主集 200 题、扩展集 1000 题（难度跨度更大）；另在 180 道固定题上做控制研究（棋盘复制、给定正确布局的文本形式预测四个未来状态、首次布局成功率、路线交互程度的影响、视觉反馈 vs 等预算 40k token 的全新重试）。

Result: 主集上最佳模型 5 次内解出 59.5%，次佳 39.5%，其余七个 ≤10.0%；1000 题扩展集更容易，最佳模型达 90.9%。GPT-6.1 Terra 精确复制 97.2% 棋盘，给定正确文本布局时 91.3% 能预测全部四个未来状态，但仅 22.8% 的首次布局可行。列车路线交互越多规划越易失败，即使在搜索求解器看来同等简单的题上亦然。视觉反馈在 417 次首轮修改中消除了 175 次所报故障，但其中仅 43 次真正解出题目；等 40k token 预算下，其效果不优于重新生成。

Conclusion: 模型具备读懂任务、复制棋盘与进行状态推演的能力，却难以将其整合为可行的一次性全局规划；错误反馈带来的"修复"多为表面纠错，而非实质求解，说明时空规划与计划修复仍是独立瓶颈。

Abstract: Planning in a visual world means reading the layout, foreseeing how the state will change, and following the task rules all at once. When a plan fails, a model should also use what went wrong to fix it. We call these two abilities spatiotemporal planning and plan repair, and test them with Railbound-Bench. A model sees a railway puzzle, adds tracks under a budget, and submits one complete layout; a simulator then moves all cars at once and decides whether the layout works. After a failure, the model sees what happened and may submit a new layout, up to five attempts in total. On the 200-puzzle main set, the best of nine models solves 59.5% within five attempts, the next 39.5%, and the other seven at most 10.0%; a 1,000-puzzle extended set with a wider range of difficulty is far easier, at 90.9% for the best model. Controlled studies on 180 fixed puzzles show that knowing the task is not the same as using it. GPT-6.1 Terra copies 97.2% of boards exactly and, given a correct layout as text, predicts all four requested future states in 91.3% of cases, yet only 22.8% of its first layouts work. Planning fails more often as the cars' routes interact, even among puzzles that a search solver finds equally easy. Visual feedback removes the reported fault in 175 of 417 first revisions, but only 43 of these revisions solve the puzzle, and at an equal budget of 40k tokens it does no better than fresh retries. The code and data are open-sourced on GitHub at https://github.com/liaoyanqing666/Railbound_Bench.

</details>


### [112] [Dynamic Spectrum Re-parameterized Convolution for Cost-free Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28736)
*Zhiyan Zhou,Hailong Yan,Hanlin Liu,Yongrui Zhang,Xiangtao Zhang,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可插拔的Dynamic Spectrum Re-parameterized Convolution（DSRC），训练时学习任务自适应的频带响应，推理时可解析融合为标准卷积，零额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像增强任务涉及谱特性差异显著的多种退化，固定核的标准卷积缺乏适应性，而动态卷积虽可提升适应性，却带来额外推理成本。

Method: 将卷积核分解为频带，施加可学习的高/低通调制，并引入轻量残差分支；利用这些操作的线性性，在推理前将所有训练期成分解析地融合为单一卷积核。

Result: 在低光增强、水下增强与图像超分辨率的十二种骨干-任务组合上取得一致的PSNR提升，SSIM与LPIPS总体改善或保持，部署参数量与理论FLOPs不变，且无额外推理算子。

Conclusion: DSRC以重参数化方式兼顾训练期谱自适应与推理期零开销，是一种即插即用的增强模块。

Abstract: Image enhancement spans degradations with distinct spectral characteristics. Standard convolutions use fixed kernels, whereas dynamic convolutions gain adaptability at additional inference cost. We propose Dynamic Spectrum Re-parameterized Convolution (DSRC), a plug-and-play module that learns task-adaptive spectral responses during training while retaining standard convolution at inference. DSRC decomposes convolution kernels into frequency bands, applies learnable high/low-pass modulation, and incorporates lightweight residual branches. Owing to the linearity of these operations, all training-time components are analytically fused into a single kernel for inference. Experiments on low-light enhancement, underwater enhancement, and image super-resolution show consistent PSNR gains and generally improved or preserved SSIM and LPIPS across twelve backbone-task configurations, with unchanged deployed parameter counts and theoretical FLOPs, and no additional inference operators.

</details>


### [113] [Learning Optical Flow with Task-Oriented Feature Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28744)
*Mengtan Zhang,Dazhong Wang,Haoran Zhang,Hongbo Zhao,Yuhang Ming,Weihao Yuan,Wei Ye,Hanli Wang,Rui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TO-Flow 是一个面向光流估计的任务导向特征适配框架，通过通道调制与空间重组分别对齐匹配特征和上下文特征的需求，在 Sintel、KITTI 2015、Spring、LayeredFlow 上验证了有效性与通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的光流估计依赖帧间对应证据与帧内上下文引导，但通用图像编码器特征存在两类失配：一是强激活但判别性弱的通道在计算帧间相似度时会压过更有信息量的通道；二是上下文特征亲和度在运动一致区域内部偏低、在运动边界处偏高。二者共同削弱匹配与上下文引导的作用。

Method: 提出基于亲和度的软划分，将运动区域内部与边界邻域区分开以指导两个处理过程：(1) 通道调制利用模拟光流细化，评估各通道对估计对应点是否赋予比邻近竞争候选更高的相似度，将相似度差按区域类型聚合并在迭代间平均，得到通道权重以强化有信息通道、抑制模糊通道；(2) 空间重组在由粗到细的层级上结合亲和度引导的特征吸引与排斥，同时提升运动一致区域内的一致性和跨运动边界的可区分性，并用特征重采样缓解跨尺度融合中的边界模糊。

Result: 在 MPI Sintel、KITTI 2015、Spring 和 LayeredFlow 数据集上的大量实验表明，TO-Flow 在多种迭代细化架构以及监督与无监督训练范式下均有效且具备良好通用性。

Conclusion: TO-Flow 以任务导向方式对通用编码器特征进行适配，说明针对匹配与上下文两类角色的显式特征调制可稳定提升光流估计性能，并可跨架构与训练范式迁移；源码将在论文发表后于 https://mias.group/TO-Flow 公开。

Abstract: Learning-based optical flow estimation approaches generally rely on both inter-frame correspondence evidence and intra-frame contextual guidance, yet features from general-purpose image encoders may not fully support these roles. Strongly activated but weakly discriminative channels can outweigh more informative ones when computing inter-frame feature similarity, while context feature affinities can be low within motion-coherent regions yet high across motion boundaries. To address these mismatches, this study proposes TO-Flow, a task-oriented feature adaptation framework that aligns matching and context features with their respective requirements through dedicated channel modulation and spatial reorganization approaches. An affinity-based soft partition distinguishes motion-region interiors from boundary-adjacent regions to guide both processes. Channel modulation uses simulated flow refinement to assess whether each channel assigns higher similarity to estimated correspondences than to nearby competing candidates. The resulting similarity differences are aggregated within each region type and averaged across iterations to derive channel weights that emphasize informative channels and suppress ambiguous ones. Spatial reorganization combines affinity-guided feature attraction and repulsion from coarse to fine to simultaneously improve consistency within motion-coherent regions and distinctiveness across motion boundaries, with feature resampling mitigating boundary blurring during cross-scale fusion. Extensive experiments on the MPI Sintel, KITTI 2015, Spring, and LayeredFlow datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and general applicability of TO-Flow across diverse iterative refinement architectures as well as supervised and unsupervised training paradigms. The source code will be publicly available at https://mias.group/TO-Flow upon publication.

</details>


### [114] [UNIFY: Unified Learning with One Model via Batch Norm Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28746)
*Shaharyar Ahmed Khan Tareen,Xiaojing Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UNIFY统一学习框架，通过批归一化切换（BNS）让单一模型在多个数据集上联合训练与推理，共享骨干网络但保留数据集特定的BN统计/参数与分类头，在五个数据集、六种CNN上以更小内存取得优于独立训练平均的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络通常按单个数据集或任务分别训练，产生大量任务专用模型，造成内存占用、部署成本和能耗的冗余，限制可扩展性。

Method: UNIFY框架：共享参数密集的骨干网络，仅维护数据集特定的Batch Normalization统计量与参数（BNS）以及数据集特定的分类头；训练时按数据集切换对应BN与分类头进行联合学习，推理时同样按数据集选择相应组件。在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet五个数据集与SqueezeNet 1.1、WideResNet-40-2、WideResNet-16-8、ResNet-18、ResNet-34、ResNet-50六种CNN上进行验证。

Result: 单一统一ResNet-34在上述五个数据集上分别达到99.53%、94.84%、96.12%、80.32%、67.67%的准确率，优于对应独立训练任务专用模型的平均性能；内存由五个独立模型的428.30 MB降至单一模型的86.71 MB，约减少4.94倍。

Conclusion: UNIFY不仅将K个模型整合为单一模型，还借助跨数据集的互补知识提升模型有效性，为可扩展、高效、能处理多样图像分类任务的统一学习提供了路径。

Abstract: Neural networks are typically trained separately for individual datasets or tasks, resulting in many task-specific models. This redundancy increases memory footprint, deployment costs, and energy consumption limiting their scalability. We propose UNIFY, a unified learning framework that enables a single model to jointly learn and perform inference across multiple datasets via Batch Normalization Switching (BNS). Instead of maintaining separate models for each dataset, UNIFY shares the parameter-intensive backbone while maintaining dataset-specific BN statistics and parameters, along with dataset-specific classification heads. We validate our framework using five datasets (MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, Tiny-ImageNet) across six Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) of different sizes (SqueezeNet 1.1, WideResNet-40-2, WideResNet-16-8, ResNet-18, ResNet-34, ResNet-50). Notably, a single UNIFIED ResNet-34 trained on these datasets achieves accuracies of 99.53%, 94.84%, 96.12%, 80.32%, and 67.67%, respectively, outperforming the average performance of the corresponding independently trained task-specific models while reducing the memory footprint by approximately 4.94x, from 428.30 MB (five separate models) to 86.71 MB (one unified model). UNIFY aims not only to consolidate (K) models into a single model but also to enhance model-effectiveness by leveraging complementary knowledge across datasets. It paves the way for scalable, efficient, and unified learning capable of handling diverse image classification tasks. Code is available at: https://github.com/saktx/UNIFY.

</details>


### [115] [From Pairwise to Collective: Encoder Collections Make Unpaired Vision-Language Alignment Reliable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28753)
*Xuanhua Yin,Shunqi Mao,Chuanzhi Xu,Boyu Wei,Wei Guo,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ConsensusAlign，通过同模态池化构建共享空间，仅拟合一次无配对跨模态桥接并以闭式解转移到所有编码器对，实现稳健的无配对视觉-文本对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无配对对齐可仅凭特征几何在视觉与文本编码器间建立映射，但逐对独立拟合存在搜索脆弱、重复拟合和映射质量不确定的问题；同时多编码器共享同一批图像或文本，蕴含已知的模态内对应关系这一额外监督信号未被利用。

Method: 将所有视觉编码器与文本编码器分别池化为一个共享空间，拟合单一无配对跨模态桥接（WP bridge），再以闭式解将该桥接转移到每一对编码器；新编码器仅需同模态拟合即可接入，新语料通过拟合到无标签目标特征的native map复用该桥接。

Result: 在21个视觉-文本编码器对、5个随机种子上，池化拟合使所有对均成功对齐，而逐对拟合在105个"对-种子"单元中有68个失败；平均对齐误差FOSCTTM由0.1457降至0.0099，十个数据集上的零样本准确率近乎翻倍；增益在不同同模态池化构造下保持稳定；在COCO上，与独立拟合参考图的一致性可用于检测失败。

Conclusion: 利用同模态已知对应关系进行池化共享监督，可显著提升无配对跨模态对齐的稳健性与精度，并支持编码器与语料的高效增量扩展。

Abstract: Strong vision and text encoders can be trained independently, but their representations are not directly comparable. Connecting them usually requires paired image-text data, which may be unavailable. Unpaired alignment instead fits a map between a vision and a text encoder from feature-space geometry alone, enabling text-based retrieval and zero-shot classification without paired data. Treating each pair independently leads to fragile searches, repeated fitting, and uncertain map quality. A collection of encoders supplies additional supervision: all vision encoders embed the same images and all text encoders the same texts. Correspondences within each modality are therefore known exactly, while the cross-modal correspondence remains hidden. ConsensusAlign pools each modality into a shared space, fits one unpaired cross-modal bridge, and transfers it in closed form to every encoder pair. New encoders join through same-modality fitting without repeating the cross-modal search. On 21 vision-text encoder pairs and five seeds, pooled fitting aligns every pair, whereas pairwise fits fail in 68 of 105 pair-seed cells. Mean alignment error (FOSCTTM) falls from 0.1457 to 0.0099, and zero-shot accuracy nearly doubles on ten datasets. Gains persist across the tested within-modality pooling constructions with a WP bridge. New corpora reuse the bridge through native maps fitted to unlabeled target features from every source encoder. Agreement with separately fitted reference maps detects failures on COCO.

</details>


### [116] [PSC-Gen: Physical Sequence Conditioning for Video Generation via Agentic Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28756)
*Wonsik Shin,Jinju Jang,Junhoo Lee,Yeonjin Chang,Jimyeong Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PSC-Gen 是一个免训练框架，通过智能体方法合成视觉条件并利用低频滤波注入核心动态，使视频扩散模型生成物理上合理的视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型虽在互联网规模数据上训练，但难以理解物理规律、生成物理合理的动态；现有以显式物理推理为条件的方法无法将细粒度物理引导有效融入生成，导致运动不合理或视觉伪影。

Method: 提出 PSC-Gen 免训练框架：(1) Physics Agent 迭代规划物理状态并验证生成的关键帧，构建视觉与物理一致的关键帧序列；(2) 将关键帧插值为连续物理序列，仅通过低频滤波注入其低频分量，引导模型在合成细节的同时保持物理动态。

Result: 在物理信息与机器人操作基准上的定性与定量评估表明，PSC-Gen 能忠实遵循指令并生成物理上合理的视频。

Conclusion: 以低频分量注入物理动态条件是一种有效、免训练的物理一致性视频生成范式，兼顾物理合理性与视觉细节。

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion models have enabled the synthesis of high-fidelity, visually compelling videos. However, despite being trained on internet-scale video data, understanding underlying physical laws and generating physically plausible dynamics remain challenging. To mitigate this, conditioning video generators on explicit physical reasoning has emerged as a promising direction. Nevertheless, existing conditioning approaches often fail to incorporate detailed physical guidance into generation, resulting in physically implausible motion or visual artifacts. We propose PSC-Gen, a training-free framework that employs an agentic approach to synthesize visual conditions and inject their core dynamics via low-frequency filtering. By iteratively planning physical states and verifying generated keyframes, our Physics Agent constructs visually and physically consistent keyframe sequences. We then interpolate these keyframes into a continuous physical sequence and inject only its low-frequency components, steering the video model to preserve physical dynamics while synthesizing fine visual details. Qualitative and quantitative evaluations across physics-informed and robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that PSC-Gen faithfully follows instructions and generates physically plausible videos.

</details>


### [117] [Frozen by the Mean: Early Temporal Guidance Unfreezes Motion in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28762)
*Xuanhua Yin,Boyu Wei,Chengkun Yue,Shunqi Mao,Chuanzhi Xu,Wei Guo,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 THAW：在去噪前期窗口内对 CFG 差分加权的残差项做谐波式放大，以独立控制帧间时间变化，训练-free、评估次数不变，显著提升运动强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型常出现画面逼真但未生成指定动作的问题。提高 CFG 系数虽能增强运动，却会饱和并使颜色漂移；而标量 CFG 对预测差分的“均值分量”与“帧变分量”赋同一系数，无法独立调节时间变化程度，因此缺乏解耦控制运动的手段。

Method: THAW（Temporal Harmonic Amplification during Warm-up）保留原始 CFG，在初始去噪窗口内额外加入一项单独加权的残差；其剂量定义为相对当前 CFG 差分的范数增量，使额外引导在各采样步具有一致的相对预算。方法无需训练，复用 CFG 已有的两次模型预测，网络评估次数不变。

Result: 在五个视频模型上，成本匹配条件下 THAW 的运动生成优于标量 CFG 与调度式 CFG；其中三个模型上对现有引导方法的点估计为正。VBench 上，相对原始 CFG 动态程度提升约 14–15 分，并在另一组三个模型上总分提高。

Conclusion: 通过将时间维引导从标量 CFG 中解耦并在预热阶段按相对范数剂量注入，可在不增加采样成本、无需训练的前提下缓解运动–画质饱和与颜色偏移的权衡，提升动作遵循性与整体质量。

Abstract: Video diffusion models can render convincing frames while failing to produce the requested action. Increasing classifier-free guidance (CFG) can strengthen motion, but also saturates and shifts colors. Scalar CFG assigns the same coefficient to the averaged and frame-varying components of its prediction difference, limiting independent control of temporal variation. THAW, Temporal Harmonic Amplification during Warm-up, retains stock CFG and adds a separately weighted residual during an initial denoising window. Its dose specifies the added norm relative to the current CFG difference, giving the extra guidance a consistent relative budget across sampling steps. THAW is training-free and reuses CFG's two model predictions with an unchanged network evaluation count. It improves cost-matched motion over scalar and scheduled CFG on five video models, with positive point estimates against existing guidance methods on three of them. On VBench, THAW raises dynamic degree by about 14 to 15 points over stock CFG while increasing total scores on a different set of three models.

</details>


### [118] [Contrastive Gradient Guidance for Test-Time Multi-Preference Alignment of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28764)
*Po-Yi Lu,Jui-Chao Lu,Paul Kuo-Ming Huang,Shih-Hsin Wang,Chun-Liang Li,Hsuan-Tien Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出对比梯度引导（CGG），一种测试时组合生成框架，将多偏好对齐重构为分布组合：用"偏好专家—反专家"对比对的得分差构成引导信号，在采样中融合多个现成扩散模型的去噪方向，实现多偏好平衡控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需为每个偏好权衡进行离线微调（需调超参、重新训练），或将多样偏好压缩为单一标量奖励，无法直接复用为不同目的、经不同微调方式得到的已有生成专家；而多上下文依赖偏好要求在测试时进行生成适配。

Method: CGG 将每个偏好表示为一对"受偏好专家"与"不受偏好反专家"的对比偏好对，二者得分之差形成对比梯度引导信号；在采样过程中组合来自现成扩散模型的去噪方向（梯度），把多偏好对齐重构为分布的组合，从而直接复用预训练与微调模型作为专家/反专家。

Result: 实验表明 CGG 可复用预训练及微调模型控制输出风格与安全性，并能与现有基于采样的奖励引导结合；每组对比仅需单一固定尺度，即可同时组合五种偏好，在文本-图像偏好、概念控制、照片保真度与安全性上取得比离线微调、权重空间合并和奖励引导更均衡的权衡。

Conclusion: CGG 以分布组合替代标量奖励压缩，实现了无需再训练、可组合、可复用现成专家的测试时多偏好对齐，并在多偏好权衡上优于离线微调、权重合并与奖励引导等基线。

Abstract: Aligning pre-trained diffusion models with multiple context-dependent preferences requires adapting generation at test time rather than relying on offline fine-tuning, which requires hyperparameter tuning and a new training run for each preference trade-off. Existing approaches compress diverse preferences into a single scalar reward objective for fine-tuning or test-time guidance rather than directly reusing existing generative experts released for different purposes and obtained through different fine-tuning methods. In this work, we propose Contrastive Gradient Guidance (CGG), a test-time compositional generative framework that reformulates multi-preference alignment as a composition of distributions. CGG represents each preference as a contrastive preference pair of favored experts and unfavored anti-experts, whose score difference forms a contrastive gradient guidance signal that combines denoising directions (gradients) from off-the-shelf diffusion models during sampling. Experiments show that CGG reuses pre-trained and fine-tuned models as favored experts or anti-experts to control output style and safety, and that it can be combined with existing sampling-based reward guidance. With a single fixed scale per pair, CGG composes five preferences at once and gives a more balanced trade-off across text-image preference, concept control, photographic fidelity, and safety than offline fine-tuning, weight-space merging, and reward steering.

</details>


### [119] [JevNavAgent: A Single-Forward Visual Navigation Agent with Trajectory-Level RL for Gigapixel Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28766)
*Yonghuang Wu,Zhenyang Liang,Pengfei Wang,Yize Ma,Guoqing Wu,Jinhua Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JevNavAgent：2B参数WSI导航智能体，采用"System One"单次前向预测动作分布（0生成token）+确定性代码执行导航，结合分阶段离线反事实对齐与在线Traj-GRPO，在WSI-VQA与CAMELYON16上以近12倍速度优势超越前沿VLM智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 千兆像素WSI超出单次VLM处理能力；MIL依赖与查询无关的离线切块、无交互推理；多步VLM导航智能体受两大瓶颈制约：(1)自回归动作解码延迟高，限制探索与在线多轨迹RL；(2)离线蒸馏存在"存在性vs全称性"的停止不对称——阳性检测与阴性排除的判定逻辑不对称，导致缩略图捷径与运动学捷径（kinematic shortcut），策略易"路过"转移灶而不停止。

Method: Jev（System One）范式：一次前向预测预定义决策空间上的分布（0生成token），导航由确定性代码执行。构建2B参数JevNavAgent，包含因子化停止头、空间候选头、上下文双线性答案头，并采用概览模态dropout。训练分两阶段：Stage-1离线反事实轨迹对齐（缓解深度与平移捷径）；Stage-2在线轨迹级GRPO（Traj-GRPO），利用无解码rollout优化分支级病灶发现、多边形内停止与逐步置信增益。

Result: Clean Visual WSI-VQA：多尺度总体51.57%（终步50.43%），焦点准确率51.39%，优于前沿VLM智能体及4B–11B开源级联模型；运行速度约为前沿API的12倍（5.63s vs 66.64–70.04s）。CAMELYON16官方测试：Traj-GRPO达22.45%定位率（将11/12即91.7%的已访问肿瘤转为有依据停止），特异性93.75%，切片准确率69.77%，在相同停止规则下优于Stage-1 SFT，并具备零样本预算扩展能力。

Conclusion: 以无解码的"一步决策分布+代码执行"替代自回归动作生成，可同时解决延迟与在线RL可行性问题；两阶段（反事实离线对齐+轨迹级GRPO）训练有效缓解捷径与停止不对称，使小参数智能体在精度与效率上均优于大型VLM智能体级联。

Abstract: Gigapixel whole-slide images (WSIs) exceed single-pass vision-language model (VLM) capacity at cellular resolution, while multiple instance learning relies on query-agnostic offline patching without interactive reasoning. Multi-step VLM navigation agents inspect regions on demand, yet face two bottlenecks: (1) autoregressive action decoding incurs heavy latency, throttling exploration and online multi-trajectory RL; and (2) offline distillation suffers from an existential-versus-universal stopping asymmetry between positive detection and negative exclusion, inducing thumbnail and kinematic shortcuts while leaving policies prone to passing metastases without stopping. Under the Jev (System One) paradigm, a model predicts distributions over predefined decision spaces in one forward pass (0 generated tokens) while deterministic code executes navigation. We build JevNavAgent, a 2B-parameter agent combining factorized stopping, spatial-candidate, and contextual-bilinear answer heads with overview modality dropout. We pair Stage-1 offline counterfactual trajectory alignment (mitigating depth and panning shortcuts) with Stage-2 online trajectory-level GRPO (Traj-GRPO), using decoding-free rollouts to optimize branch-level lesion discovery, in-polygon stopping, and step-wise confidence gain. On Clean Visual WSI-VQA, JevNavAgent achieves 51.57% multi-scale (50.43% terminal-step) overall and 51.39% focal accuracy, outperforming frontier VLM agents and 4B-11B open-source cascades while running nearly 12x faster than frontier APIs (5.63s vs. 66.64-70.04s). On CAMELYON16 Official Test, Traj-GRPO reaches 22.45% localization (converting 11/12, or 91.7%, of visited tumors into grounded stops) with 93.75% specificity and 69.77% slide accuracy, surpassing Stage-1 SFT under matched stopping rules alongside zero-shot budget scaling.

</details>


### [120] [AdapToC: A Unified Framework for Multi-Task Visual Token Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28775)
*Zhicheng Liu,Chenliang Wu,Zhouxiang Zhao,Zhaohui Yang,Zhaoyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdapToC是一种单遍无线视觉token传输收发机，通过软比特后验与码本几何对齐，在无需修改tokenizer权重或对像素解码器求导的前提下恢复多层级率失真折中，在冻结VQGAN-f16 token上显著优于TONIC与TokenCom+AMC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练离散视觉tokenizer为合成、识别与具身感知提供紧凑潜变量，但无线token流传输脆弱：衰落与导频噪声下硬CRC擦除把受损块变成缺失token，冻结解码器又放大边缘误差，使UEP退化为"强编码或直接丢弃"的二元选择。

Method: 提出AdapToC单遍收发机：(1)离线频率加权Pseudo-Gray重标定与Halton散布对齐码本几何与信道，零开销收缩比特与突发错误；(2)软比特后验替代硬判决，使中间码率落到三层凸包上；(3)单位门控AdaLN发送端在信道预算下调度保护层级，导频校准接收端将因子化码本后验送入带CRC旁路的ConvNeXt-attention恢复器；(4)仅在潜空间通过反事实效用估计与码本交叉熵正则协同训练。

Result: 在相同VQGAN-f16 token上，于冻结tokenizer的PSNR上限内将多SNR瑞利LPIPS从0.6551降至0.3312，优于TONIC与自适应TokenCom+AMC；匹配质量下节省43.8% Rician-K6符号；单遍恢复器比迭代重采样快8.3倍，解码器无关识别加速20.6倍且精度提升；在TiTok、VAR与零样本NuPlan上即插即用地提升保真度。

Conclusion: 软后验可打破UEP在严重衰落下的二元坍缩，配合码本几何对齐与潜空间协同训练，能以单遍、低开销方式实现多层级率失真传输，并具备跨tokenizer与任务的通用即插即用性。

Abstract: Pretrained discrete visual tokenizers supply compact latents for synthesis, recognition, and embodied perception, yet wireless token streaming remains fragile. Under fading and noisy pilots, hard cyclic redundancy check (CRC) erasures turn corrupted blocks into missing tokens while frozen decoders amplify edge errors, which can collapse unequal error protection (UEP) into a binary choice between strong coding and omission under severe fading. Soft bit posteriors break this collapse. We present AdapToC, a single-pass transceiver that restores multi-tier rate-distortion tradeoffs without altering tokenizer weights or differentiating through pixel decoders. We align codebook geometry to the channel via offline frequency-weighted Pseudo-Gray relabeling and Halton dispersion to contract bit and burst errors with zero overhead. Because soft recovery pulls intermediate rates onto the three-tier convex hull, our unit-gated AdaLN sender schedules protection tiers across channel budgets, while our pilot-calibrated receiver feeds factorized codebook posteriors into a ConvNeXt-attention restorer with CRC bypass. We co-train AdapToC solely in latent space via counterfactual utility estimation and codebook cross-entropy regularization. On identical VQGAN-f16 tokens, AdapToC outperforms TONIC and adaptive TokenCom+AMC, cuts multi-SNR Rayleigh LPIPS from 0.6551 to 0.3312 within the frozen-tokenizer PSNR ceiling, and saves 43.8% Rician-K6 symbols at matched quality. Our single-pass restorer runs 8.3x faster than iterative resampling, raises accuracy while accelerating decoder-free recognition 20.6x, and improves fidelity plug-and-play across TiTok, VAR, and zero-shot NuPlan.

</details>


### [121] [Imagine Smarter, Drive Faster: Efficient World Modeling for Decision-Aware Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28779)
*Feiyang Jia,Lin Liu,Zhicheng Bao,Caiyan Jia,Ziying Song,Shengkai Zhang,Lina Han,Yan Zhao,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出原生轻量化世界模型框架FastWorld，通过紧凑场景表征、短时域动作条件预测与选择性候选验证实现高效自动驾驶规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型对自动驾驶规划有潜力，但想象未来的计算开销巨大；现有蒸馏与领域预训练方案只是把成本转移给昂贵教师或训练管线，轻量化世界建模仍是开放问题。

Method: 采用三方面设计降低计算：紧凑场景表征、短时域动作条件预测、选择性候选验证与分数精炼；无需教师蒸馏，也无需额外领域特定预训练，即可完成未来信息驱动的规划。

Result: NAVSIMv1上单张4090 GPU达92.2 PDMS、132 FPS；NAVSIMv2上89.4 EPDMS；nuScenes上3秒平均L2为0.40，17 FPS。

Conclusion: FastWorld证明了不依赖蒸馏与额外预训练也能实现高精度、高帧率的未来信息规划，为轻量化世界模型用于自动驾驶提供了可行范式。

Abstract: World models hold great promise for autonomous driving planning, yet their ability to imagine the future often comes at a formidable computational cost. Efficiency efforts, such as knowledge distillation and domain-specific pre-training, often shift this burden to costly teachers or training pipelines, leaving lightweight world modeling an open challenge. We introduce FastWorld, a natively lightweight world-model framework for efficient autonomous driving planning. FastWorld reduces computation through compact scene representations, short-horizon action-conditioned prediction, and selective candidate verification and score refinement. These designs support efficient future-informed planning without teacher distillation or additional domain-specific pre-training. On NAVSIMv1, FastWorld achieves 92.2 PDMS at 132 FPS on a single 4090 GPU. It also achieves 89.4 EPDMS on NAVSIMv2 and an average L2 of 0.40 over 3 seconds at 17 FPS on nuScenes. Code and models can be found at https://github.com/MoyangSensei/FastWorld.

</details>


### [122] [Where Does a Weight-Editing Agent Help? Blind Diagnosis and Repair of Deployment Faults in Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28781)
*Zhuangzi Li,Shilv Cai,Jian Jin,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在"已知答案"的可控设定下评估语言模型智能体通过无梯度权重编辑修复视觉模型的能力，发现其价值在于免人工指定故障类型，且其失效条件可预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无梯度地读写神经网络权重正被宣传为一种新的模型修复方式，但缺少在有标准答案的设定下对其真实增益的严格检验：智能体相比事后编写的脚本究竟多了什么？

Method: 构造故障注入基准：将视觉模型迁移到新系统并引入预处理、权重转换、输出约定等故障；智能体仅能前向推理，需自行判断故障是否存在、命名故障并编辑权重予以撤销。覆盖分类、分割、检测任务，共183次运行，以无故障模型为评分基准。

Result: (1) 故障类型已知时智能体非必需：事后脚本修复48个故障中的30个，智能体为33个；(2) 在脚本未见过的五类故障上，智能体修复15/15而脚本仅4/15；(3) 智能体对21个无故障模型均未误编辑；(4) 其局限可预测：依赖可信的逐层激活参考，且故障须为可逆的结构化变换，否则小故障被漏检、修复不完整。

Conclusion: 智能体的核心价值不在于已知故障下的修复率优势，而在于无需预先告知故障类型即可泛化诊断与修复，且具备良好误报控制；适用性可由"是否有逐层可信参考+故障是否为可逆结构化变换"判据决定，据此给出何时用智能体、何时用脚本的指导。

Abstract: Language-model agents can read and edit the weights of neural networks without gradients, and this is increasingly presented as a new way of repairing models. We ask what such an agent adds, in a setting with a known answer. A vision model moved into a new system may be broken by a preprocessing, weight-conversion or output-convention fault; an agent restricted to forward passes must decide whether a fault exists, name it, and undo it by editing the weights. Over 183 runs on classification, segmentation and detection, scored against the fault-free model, we find three things. When the kind of fault is known, the agent is not needed: a script written afterwards repairs 30 of 48 faults against the agent's 33. But the agent did not have to be told: on five fault types outside every script it repairs 15 of 15 where that script repairs 4, and it never edits any of 21 fault-free models. And its limits are predictable: it needs a trustworthy per-layer reference for the model's activations and a fault that is an invertible structured transformation; without them, small faults are missed and repairs stay partial. We derive guidance on when to use an agent and when a script.

</details>


### [123] [3DCodeFactory: Scaling Training Data for 3D Code Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28783)
*Zhaoyu Wang,Xueru Wen,XingYu Li,Jie Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自动化框架3DCodeFactory，从原始文本直接构建复杂、可交互的3D代码训练数据，并发布5,045条高质量多轮数据，微调后在多个3D代码基准上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码智能体的任务范围已从生成简单静态3D网格扩展到构建结构完整、可交互的复杂3D场景，但现有数据集多依赖对已有3D资产的转换或描述，以静态单物体的单轮代码数据为主，任务多样性与场景复杂度难以扩展，导致此类训练数据稀缺。

Method: 提出3DCodeFactory自动化框架：先利用图像生成器从原始文本（如扩散提示与商品描述）合成3D参考图像作为视觉锚点，据此生成具体建模规格与交互功能需求；同时从参考图像与需求联合构建视觉、规格、功能三类评分标准（rubrics），用于自动打分并筛选多轮智能体轨迹。

Result: 基于该框架发布3DCodeFactory-Data，包含5,045个高质量任务实例及多轮交互轨迹。在3DCodeFactory-Data上微调Qwen3.5-9B与Qwen3.8-27B后，在BlenderWorld、P3D-Bench与3DCodeBench上性能显著提升，持续优于使用现有开源3D代码数据集训练的模型；27B模型的整体表现进一步超越同系列2.4T旗舰模型Qwen3.8-Max。

Conclusion: 以文本为源头、借助图像锚点与多维评分标准自动合成并筛选复杂可交互3D代码数据，是一条可扩展且有效的路径，可显著提升代码智能体的3D场景构建能力。

Abstract: As code agents evolve, their scope is expanding from generating simple static 3D meshes to constructing structurally complete, complex, interactive 3D scenes. However, existing datasets mainly rely on converting or captioning pre-existing 3D assets, consisting mostly of single-turn code data for static single objects and struggling to scale task diversity and scene complexity, leaving such training data scarce. We present 3DCodeFactory, an automated framework that directly constructs diverse, complex 3D training data from abundant raw text (e.g., diffusion prompts and product descriptions). The framework first leverages an image generator to synthesize 3D reference images from raw text as visual anchors, guiding the generation of concrete modeling specifications and interactive functional requirements; meanwhile, it jointly constructs visual, specification, and functional rubrics from the reference images and requirements to automatically score and filter multi-turn agent rollouts. Based on this framework, we release 3DCodeFactory-Data, comprising 5,045 high-quality task instances and multi-turn interaction trajectories. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B and Qwen3.8-27B on 3DCodeFactory-Data significantly improves performance on BlenderWorld, P3D-Bench, and 3DCodeBench, consistently outperforming models trained on existing open-source 3D code datasets, with our 27B model further surpassing the same-series 2.4T flagship Qwen3.8-Max in overall performance.

</details>


### [124] [ArgsWorld: Unifying 3DGS Scene Reconstruction, Generation and Extrapolation with Autoregressive Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28792)
*Weiming Chen,Shuaiyan Liu,Xinyu Shao,Zhewen Chen,Tian Guan,Yonghong He,Xitong Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArgsWorld 是一个自回归扩散世界模型，在生成 RGB 帧的同时显式输出 3D Gaussian Splatting 场景表示，统一了基于位姿图像的 3D 重建、图像/文本驱动的场景生成与跨相机片段的外推，并在多项 3D 场景建模任务上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归扩散世界模型仅输出 RGB 帧，缺乏显式 3D 表示，导致所学视觉世界动态与下游机器人、仿真、渲染可用的 3D 资产之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出扩散 Transformer，在每个生成 chunk 中联合处理干净的历史图像 latent 与新视角的噪声 latent；固定历史图像 latent 并刷新场景特征，使高斯表示随新区域进入视野而更新。训练上采用 3D Self-Forcing Distillation，基于模型自身生成的历史，混合单视图与多视图数据；并用分布匹配同时监督直接图像 latent 与高斯渲染 latent。

Result: 在多个 3D 场景建模任务上取得 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: ArgsWorld 将自回归扩散世界模型与显式 3DGS 表示结合，统一了 3D 场景重建、条件场景生成与跨相机片段外推，弥合了视觉世界动态建模与可用 3D 资产之间的差距。

Abstract: Autoregressive diffusion world models deliver strong video generation performance, yet they typically output only RGB frames rather than explicit 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scene representations, creating a gap between learned visual world dynamics and usable 3D assets for downstream robotics, simulation and rendering. To bridge this gap, we introduce ArgsWorld, an autoregressive diffusion model that also produces an explicit 3DGS scene. For each generation chunk, the diffusion transformer jointly processes clean historical image latents and noisy latents for new views. It keeps the historical image latents fixed and refreshes the scene features so that the Gaussian representation can be revised as new regions come into view. Our 3D Self-Forcing Distillation trains the model on its own generated histories using a mixture of single-view and multi-view data. Distribution matching supervises direct image latents and latents of Gaussian renderings at both generation and additional cameras. ArgsWorld unifies 3D scene reconstruction from posed images, scene generation from image or text prompts, and scene extrapolation across camera segments with disconnected endpoints. Experimental results demonstrate that ArgsWorld achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple 3D scene modeling tasks. Project page: https://m4a1tastegood.github.io/ArgsWorld/

</details>


### [125] [Inference Dynamics: Image Classifiers Keep Using Visual Cues After They Are Removed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28795)
*Ruiqi Wang,Mingming Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出“推理动力学”框架，发现视觉线索被移除后其影响仍分布于深层表征中，呈现“局部性先衰减、可撤回性后崩溃”的规律，并据此提出证据重置（Evidence Reset）干预方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像分类器评估只看最终预测，把推理视为静态的图像到标签映射；但可靠性还取决于模型能否在证据消失后放弃该证据。作者观察到反直觉现象：将局部视觉线索从原位置移除，未必消除其对预测的影响。

Method: 提出 Inference Dynamics 过程级框架：给定原图与匹配的去线索参考图，在逐层用参考分支的对应状态替换原分支中与线索对齐的状态，测量预测向无线索预测的恢复程度，从而得到轨迹，分离“证据局部性”与“决策级可撤回性”。据此提出 Evidence Reset：在锁定期之前选择依赖输入的层，将线索对齐状态向无线索对应状态重建。

Result: 跨数据集与架构实验显示：局部性先下降，而可撤回性随后才崩溃，即线索信息在原支持区域不再集中但作用仍活跃，之后进入“证据锁定”阶段。因此固定在后期的纠错可能只改变最终读出而不消除累积的线索依赖。

Conclusion: 将“证据可撤回性”确立为分类器可靠性的过程级维度，并提出新干预原则：纠错深度应跟随证据动力学而非预先固定。

Abstract: Image classifiers are typically evaluated by their final predictions, treating inference as a static image-to-label mapping. Reliability, however, also depends on whether a model can relinquish evidence after that evidence disappears. We reveal a counterintuitive phenomenon: removing a local visual cue from its original location may not remove its influence on the prediction. Once captured by early layers, the cue can become distributed across later representations, making ``no longer visible'' different from ``no longer relied upon.'' We introduce Inference Dynamics, a process-level framework for tracing how cue influence evolves and remains withdrawable across network depth. Given an image and a matched cue-removed reference, we replace cue-aligned states in the original branch with their reference counterparts at successive layers and measure recovery toward the cue-free prediction. The resulting trajectory separates evidence locality from decision-level withdrawability. Across datasets and architectures, we find locality declines before withdrawability collapses: cue-induced information becomes less concentrated at its original support while its effect remains active, before entering an evidence lock-in stage. Consequently, fixed late-stage correction may alter the final readout without removing accumulated cue dependence. We therefore propose Evidence Reset, which selects an input-dependent layer before lock-in and reconstructs cue-aligned states toward their cue-free counterparts. Our results establish evidence withdrawability as a process-level dimension of classifier reliability and suggest a new intervention principle: correction depth should follow the dynamics of evidence rather than be fixed in advance.

</details>


### [126] [NavDecision: Parallel Multi-Step Action Prediction with Persistent Self-Referential Learning for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28796)
*Kai Wu,Yufan Hu,Quan Fang,Boning Zhang,Junyu Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NavDecision——一种高效的多步VLN决策模型，通过并行多步动作预测并行（PMAP）与记忆增强自指策略优化（M-SRPO）兼顾短程动作连贯性与长程目标对齐，在保持相当导航性能的同时实现最高22.28×/18.96×推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归VLN模型逐token生成动作描述，推理开销大；而决策模型虽高效，却难以同时保证短时间跨度的动作连贯性和长时间跨度的目标对齐。

Method: 1) 并行多步动作预测（PMAP）：从共享的视觉-语言状态一次性预测多个动作分布；2) 记忆增强自指策略优化（M-SRPO）：以成功轨迹为参照，从失败轨迹中学习；3) 跨迭代成功记忆：累积历史成功轨迹，为训练提供信息性反馈。

Result: 在4B骨干下于R2R-CE取得64.60%、RxR-CE取得64.57%的成功率；相较现有自回归VLN方法，推理分别最高加速22.28×和18.96×，导航性能相当。

Conclusion: 多步并行决策范式可在几乎不损失导航成功率的前提下大幅降低VLN推理开销，是自回归VLN模型的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) requires timely decisions based on instructions and visual observations. However, autoregressive VLN models generate action descriptions token by token, introducing substantial inference overhead. Decision models offer an efficient alternative but face challenges in maintaining short-horizon action coherence and long-horizon goal alignment. To address these challenges, we propose NavDecision, an efficient multi-step VLN decision model. We introduce parallel multi-step action prediction (PMAP) to predict multiple action distributions from a shared vision-language state, and memory-augmented self-referential policy optimization (M-SRPO) to learn from unsuccessful trajectories using successful trajectories as references. A cross-iteration success memory further accumulates historical successful trajectories to provide informative training feedback. NavDecision achieves success rates of 64.60% on R2R-CE and 64.57% on RxR-CE with a 4B backbone. Compared with existing autoregressive VLN methods, it delivers significant inference speedups of up to $22.28\times$ and $18.96\times$ while maintaining comparable navigation performance.

</details>


### [127] [HighwayVerse: A Chain-of-Inspection Benchmark for Highway Infrastructure Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28800)
*Junyi Yang,Zheng Si,Yilin Zhai,Pengpeng Ouyang,Hongxi Wang,Kangze Li,Mo Chen,Sicong Zhang,Hua Wang,Dongyuan Wu,Dong Chen,Mingliang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出公路基础设施检测递进链HIIP-Chain与数据集基准HighwayVerse，揭示现有MLLM在链式诊断中"初判尚可、下游薄弱"的能力断层，并提出免训练检索增强框架LaneRAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准局限于孤立任务，无法评估MLLM在"视觉感知→养护决策"递进诊断链上的表现；且数据集多聚焦路面病害，缺少护栏、防眩屏、排水系统等多元构件的真实检测场景。

Method: 构建HIIP-Chain四阶段任务链（异常检测、异常识别、状态分析、养护决策）；发布HighwayVerse数据集（14,143条地面与无人机真实巡检记录，分别覆盖100与17类异常，84,858条专家审核问答对）；采用链式指标Final评估12个开源/闭源MLLM；提出免训练检索增强框架LaneRAG提供分层参考引导。

Result: 模型初始异常存在性判断准确率较高，但Final分数显著偏低，表明下游识别、分析与决策环节存在明显短板，模型对具体病害模式的深层理解不足；LaneRAG在多种MLLM上带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 公路巡检需以递进链式方式评估MLLM，HighwayVerse与Final指标能暴露表层判断之外的深层缺陷；免训练的分层检索增强是提升链式诊断表现的有效通用手段。

Abstract: Practical highway infrastructure inspection entails progressive diagnostic reasoning from visual perception to maintenance decisions, yet existing benchmarks remain confined to isolated tasks, leaving Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) insufficiently evaluated across sequential inspection stages. Meanwhile, current datasets focus primarily on highway pavement defects, falling short of real-world inspection across diverse infrastructure components such as highway pavements, guardrails, glare screens, and drainage systems. To address these gaps, we formulate the Highway Infrastructure Inspection Progression Chain (HIIP-Chain), spanning Anomaly Detection, Anomaly Recognition, Condition Analysis, and Maintenance Resolution, and introduce HighwayVerse. HighwayVerse comprises 14,143 real-world Ground and Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) inspection records spanning 100 and 17 anomaly categories, respectively, with 84,858 expert-reviewed question-answer pairs. We evaluate 12 open-source and closed-source MLLMs using the chained metric Final, which credits a correct maintenance decision only when all preceding diagnostic stages are completed correctly. Results show that although models achieve relatively high accuracy when initially judging whether an anomaly is present, their markedly lower Final scores expose struggles across downstream recognition, analysis, and decision-making. This discrepancy indicates that current MLLMs do not fully comprehend specific highway anomaly patterns beyond superficial initial judgments. Furthermore, we propose LaneRAG, a training-free retrieval-augmented framework that provides hierarchical reference guidance achieving consistent performance improvements across diverse MLLMs. Our dataset, evaluation benchmark, and source code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [128] [Not All Directions Are Flat: Decomposed-Gradient Flatness for Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28802)
*Ungbae Yoon,Kwanyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DecoFlat 针对稀疏视角下 3DGS 过拟合训练视角的问题，发现梯度扰动方向决定泛化性：沿与平均梯度正交的分量扰动可提升泛化，并据此提出正交扰动+冲突感知校正+球谐颜色重调度的插件式优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角（如 AR、机器人）场景下稠密采集昂贵，但 3DGS 在稀疏视角下会过拟合训练视角、未见过视角性能下降。现有 flat-minima 优化通过随机噪声或沿训练梯度扰动高斯来追求平坦极小值，但这些扰动忽略了其相对于其他训练视角的方向，未考虑扰动方向对泛化的影响。

Method: 作者将每个训练视角的梯度分解为与所有视角平均梯度平行的分量（视角一致）和正交分量（视角不一致）。基于"仅沿正交分量扰动可提升泛化"及"平坦性校正常与重建梯度相反、削弱甚至反转重建步"的发现，提出 DecoFlat：(1) 沿正交分量叠加随机噪声扰动每个高斯；(2) 冲突感知校正，剔除与重建梯度相对抗的平坦性校正部分；(3) 对球谐颜色系数进行重调度，抑制视角相关外观的过拟合。

Result: 大量实验表明，DecoFlat 作为插件式优化器，能一致地提升原始 3DGS 以及多种稀疏视角 3DGS 方法的性能。

Conclusion: 扰动方向是影响泛化的关键因素，正交方向扰动优于平行方向；通过正交扰动、冲突感知校正与球谐颜色重调度可有效缓解稀疏视角 3DGS 的过拟合，且方法具有良好的通用性与即插即用性。

Abstract: Many applications of novel view synthesis, such as augmented reality and robotics, can afford only a few input views, since dense capture is costly. With such sparse views, however, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) overfits the training views and degrades at unseen viewpoints. Among the approaches to this problem, flat-minima optimization treats the Gaussians as trainable parameters and steers training toward flat minima, which generalize better, by perturbing the Gaussians with random noise or along the training gradient. However, these perturbations ignore their direction relative to the other training views. Interestingly, we find that the direction of the perturbation determines whether it improves generalization. Specifically, we decompose the gradient of each training view into a component parallel to the average gradient of all views, on which the views agree, and an orthogonal component, on which they disagree. Perturbing along the orthogonal component improves generalization, whereas perturbing along the parallel component does not. We further find that the flatness correction often opposes the reconstruction gradient, shrinking or even reversing the reconstruction step. Based on these findings, we propose DecoFlat, which perturbs each Gaussian along the orthogonal component together with random noise. Furthermore, we introduce a conflict-aware correction, which removes the part of the flatness correction that opposes the reconstruction gradient, and a rescheduling of the spherical-harmonics color, which suppresses the overfitting of view-dependent appearance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DecoFlat, as a plug-in optimizer, consistently improves vanilla 3DGS and a wide range of sparse-view 3DGS methods.

</details>


### [129] [ViTrust: Learning 3D Observation Trust for Deformable Physical World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28815)
*Zhihao Guo,Zhihao Li,Xu Ge,Lijian Zhong,Yu Lu,Haitao Wu,Longqing Zhao,Yue Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViTrust学习3D观测信任度，为每个跟踪点预测随时间变化的权重以影响物理辨识，从而提升可变形物理世界模型的可泛化未来预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠精确重建不足以保证未来预测准确；而仅凭视觉可靠性也不能判断某观测对物理动力学有多大信息量，因此需要学习"观测应如何影响物理辨识"。

Method: 提出视觉-接触信任适配器，将逐点时空证据与持久接触图上的交互历史结合，预测时变观测权重；采用单阶段共享前向目标，通过加权几何损失与对应损失联合优化权重与物理参数，并加约束防止权重坍塌；仅用观测前缀学习，拟合后物理固定用于给定控制下的预测。

Result: 在真实序列上相比所评估基线取得更好的未来预测，并给出新控制rollout与跨物体迁移的定性展示。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模观测信任度，可更有效地辨识物理参数并提升未来预测与跨场景迁移表现。

Abstract: Learning deformable physical world models requires dynamics that generalize beyond observed motion. Accurate reconstruction alone does not ensure accurate future prediction, motivating us to learn how observations should influence physical identification. However, visual reliability alone does not determine how informative an observation is about physical dynamics. We propose ViTrust to learn 3D observation trust, determining how strongly each tracked point influences physical identification. Its key design is a visual-contact trust adapter that combines pointwise spatiotemporal evidence with interaction history on a persistent contact graph to predict time-varying observation weights. A single-stage, shared-forward objective jointly optimizes these weights and physical parameters through weighted geometry and correspondence losses, with constraints to prevent observation-weight collapse. All learning uses only the observed prefix. The fitted physics remains fixed for future prediction under supplied controls. Experiments on real-world sequences demonstrate improved future prediction over the evaluated baselines, alongside qualitative demonstrations of novel-control rollouts and cross-object transfer.

</details>


### [130] [PointNeXt Adapter Improved Model for BIM Component Defect Association under Construction Images and Design Model Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28820)
*Na Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种PointNeXt Adapter改进模型，将施工图像与BIM属性对齐到构件级检查窗口，实现缺陷与BIM构件的可追溯关联，在合成数据上提升关联准确率、缺陷召回与置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工现场缺陷（裂缝、剥落、缺失套管、支撑错位等）只有与构件身份、位置、责任归属和设计意图相连接，才对质量控制有意义；现有方法难以将现场证据准确链接到正确的BIM对象。

Method: 将现场图像裁剪、局部点云块、BIM构件标识、几何属性、材料类别、安装阶段、检查距离、视角质量与来源可靠性标签对齐为构件级检查窗口；PointNeXt学习局部几何与实建偏差，Adapter注入BIM属性使同一几何异常在结构、围护、MEP与预埋构件中获得不同解释；融合头输出构件链接、缺陷类别、概率、置信度、主导证据与建议复核动作。

Result: 在合成的多源BIM检查数据集（含重复布局、遮挡、稀疏点云、配准误差及未见构件布置）上，属性感知点学习在关联准确率、缺陷召回和置信度校准方面优于仅图像、仅点云、简单融合及无Adapter基线。

Conclusion: 该框架定位为可追溯的BIM问题复核辅助工具，运营部署前仍需人工确认与分阶段现场验证。

Abstract: Construction site defect analysis increasingly requires linking field evidence to the correct BIM object, because a crack, spalling area, missing sleeve or misaligned support is useful for quality control only when it is connected with component identity, location, responsibility and design intent. This paper proposes a PointNeXt Adapter improved model for BIM component defect association under construction images and design model attributes. The method aligns site image crops, local point cloud patches, BIM component identifiers, geometry attributes, material categories, installation stage, inspection distance, viewpoint quality and source reliability tags into component level inspection windows. PointNeXt learns local geometry and deviation from as built point evidence, while the adapter injects BIM attributes so the same geometric irregularity can be interpreted differently for structural, envelope, MEP and embedded components. The fusion head outputs component link, defect class, defect probability, confidence, dominant evidence and recommended review action. A synthetic multisource BIM inspection dataset is used to evaluate repeated layouts, occlusion, sparse point clouds, registration errors and unseen component arrangements. Results show that attribute aware point learning improves association accuracy, defect recall and confidence calibration compared with image only, point only, simple fusion and adapter free baselines. The framework is positioned as traceable BIM issue review support, requiring manual confirmation and staged field validation before operational deployment.

</details>


### [131] [Omni-H3-Image: Constrained Foundation Priors Usher in a New Era of All-in-One Blind Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28836)
*Zihao He,Songhua Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Omni-H3，一个在未修改的MiniMax-H3-Image与Qwen3-VL-32B之上、仅用0.92M参数辅助预条件器的三阶段约束后验投影框架，在925个盲任务上将退化场景PSNR从16.74 dB提升至21.47 dB，并在DISTS、NIQE、Edge-F1等指标上取得多项第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一图像模型（如33B MiniMax-H3-Image、FLUX.1-Kontext、Qwen-Image-Edit-2511）虽能生成清晰纹理，但在429个退化公开复原场景中因空间漂移、色度幻觉与语义捏造而PSNR骤降至11.33–16.74 dB，需在不重训大 backbone 的前提下恢复退化场景的保真度。

Method: Omni-H3为近似三阶段约束后验投影框架：(1) 盲物理-VLM感知，结合精确32 px格点锚定；(2) 在对立色YCbCr空间做联合仿射-维纳-巴特沃斯投影；(3) 闭环 Z=4 文本验证与亚奈奎斯特弃权。仅训练0.92M参数的辅助天气预条件器（&lt;0.003% backbone权重），backbone保持冻结。

Result: 在925个盲任务、11个外部SOTA基线上：427/429退化公开场景PSNR +4.73 dB（21.47 vs 16.74），较其他omni backbone高6.27–10.14 dB；429退化场景上DISTS(0.207)、NIQE(4.45)、Edge-F1(0.807)均第一，MUSIQ 66.8（判别式SOTA为40–44）；CBSD68 Clean达76.59 dB；Urban100色度漂移降低91.6%；2K Dev Non-Clean DISTS 0.202、locked_test 2K DISTS 0.173及132-ROI SubCER均第一；PP-OCRv4下132-ROI SubCER由0.456降至0.319（TrOCR-large-str下存在识别器相关权衡）。

Conclusion: 轻量约束后验投影可在冻结的大型统一图像模型上显著修复退化复原性能，兼顾感知质量与文本保真，但OCR类指标对识别器存在依赖性权衡。

Abstract: Off-the-shelf unified image models such as 33B MiniMax-H3-Image, FLUX.1-Kontext, and Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 synthesize crisp textures yet collapse to 11.33-16.74 dB PSNR across 429 degraded public restoration scenes due to spatial drift, chromatic hallucination, and semantic fabrication. We present Omni-H3-Image (Omni-H3), an approximate three-stage constrained posterior projection framework over unmodified MiniMax-H3-Image and Qwen3-VL-32B using only a 0.92M-parameter auxiliary weather pre-conditioner (<0.003% of backbone weights). Omni-H3 unifies blind physics-VLM sensing with exact-32 px lattice anchoring, joint affine-Wiener-Butterworth projection in opponent YCbCr space, and closed-loop Z=4 text verification with sub-Nyquist abstention. Across 925 blind tasks against 11 external SOTA baselines -- five public benchmarks (N=647), Dev-160, and a held-out locked_test suite (N=118) -- Omni-H3 recovers +4.73 dB degraded-scene PSNR over Base H3 (21.47 vs. 16.74 dB; +6.27-10.14 dB over other omni backbones) while winning overall #1 DISTS (0.207), #1 NIQE (4.45), and #1 Edge-F1 (0.807) on all 429 degraded public scenes (MUSIQ 66.8 vs. 40-44 for discriminative SOTA), 76.59 dB on CBSD68 Clean, -91.6% Urban100 chroma drift, #1 2K Dev Non-Clean DISTS (0.202), and #1 locked_test 2K DISTS (0.173) and 132-ROI SubCER, cutting pre-annotated 132-ROI SubCER from 0.456 to 0.319 under non-Qwen RapidOCR (PP-OCRv4) (with recognizer-dependent tradeoffs under TrOCR-large-str).

</details>


### [132] [Spatially Grounded Latent Lookahead for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28839)
*Qihang Chen,Tengjie Li,Hongwei Lu,Jason Wu,Chyna Qu,Shikui Tu,Lei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SG-LL以"已落地的当前空间状态"为参照预测可执行的近未来表征，并将其残差式注入动作生成，在LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN及三个真机任务上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型语义表征强，但精细操作还需精确解析物体位置与工作空间关系，并在预估场景演化下选择动作。现有几何感知方法只显式化当前场景，预测式方法只建模未来观测/表征，未来信息既未锚定到与控制相关的空间状态，也未直接用于引导动作生成。

Method: 三部分：(1) 冻结的空间教师将几何先验蒸馏到与策略相连的空间token中；(2) 当前条件瓶颈学习紧凑潜在变量，以当前场景为参照编码未来信息，并从当前策略上下文与初始Action Expert特征预测该潜在，不访问未来观测与空间教师；(3) 零初始化残差融合把预测潜在注入Action Expert以精化动作生成。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN及三个真实世界操作任务上，仿真与真机设置均取得相对基线的性能提升（摘要原文此处表述缺失基线段）。

Conclusion: 将未来预测锚定于接地空间状态并以潜空间前瞻直接引导动作生成，是一种有效且无需未来观测的VLA增强范式，具备仿真到真机的迁移性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models benefit from strong semantic representations that support object recognition and instruction understanding. However, precise manipulation also depends on resolving object locations and workspace relations, and on selecting actions with an estimate of how the scene will evolve. Existing geometry-aware methods make the current scene more explicit, while predictive methods model future observations or representations. Yet future information is often neither grounded in a control-relevant spatial state nor directly used to guide action generation. We introduce Spatially Grounded Latent Lookahead (SG-LL), which uses the grounded present as the reference for predicting an actionable near-future representation. A frozen spatial teacher first distills geometric priors into policy-connected spatial tokens. A current-conditioned bottleneck then learns a compact latent that captures future information relative to the present scene. This latent is predicted from the current policy context and initial Action Expert features, without access to future observations or the spatial teacher. Finally, zero-initialized residual fusion incorporates the predicted latent into the Action Expert to refine action generation. Experiments on LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN, and three real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improvements over
  in both simulation and real-robot settings.

</details>


### [133] [Task-Conditioned Detail Allocation for Synthetic Segmentation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28841)
*Chunming He,Dingming Zhang,Jifan Zhang,Yuhan Dong,Longxiang Tang,Youwei Pang,Xiaoqi Zhao,Fengyang Xiao,Sina Farsiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出任务条件细节分配（TCDA）方法，用于筛选对分割训练有价值、并可按需局部编辑的合成图像，实验表明选择与修复结合能提升下游分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享同一分割标签的合成图像在训练价值上差异显著，直接使用全部合成数据并非最优，需要判断图像细节是否在标签标记区域内提供了类别特有的线索，从而筛选有用样本并修复部分剩余图像。

Method: 在冻结的真实图像特征上训练一个小型任务参考模型；对每个候选图像施加模糊，比较模糊前后该模型的类别响应变化，并度量这些响应变化与标注区域的对齐程度，得到细节分配分数，用于在同源候选中排序。将该响应—标签对齐与真实源图像比较，指导修复决策；在多类任务中据此选择待编辑区域。被选样本与通过修复的样本以原始标签、不改变下游分割器的方式参与训练。

Result: 在伪装目标检测（COD10K 上 SA 最多提升 0.019）、退化显著目标检测以及恶劣条件语义分割（ACDC 上提升 2.86 mIoU）上，选择策略优于同等规模的随机子集与标签一致性子集；选择与修复结合优于仅用真实数据训练。

Conclusion: 利用任务条件化的细节分配分数可以有效评估合成图像在标注区域内的类别判别价值，实现样本选择与局部修复的统一框架，在不改动下游分割器的情况下提升多种分割任务的性能。

Abstract: Synthetic images sharing a segmentation label can differ in their training value. We propose Task-Conditioned Detail Allocation (TCDA) to select useful candidates and locally edit some remaining images. We ask whether image details provide class-specific cues in the regions marked by the label. We train a small task reference on frozen image features from real training images. We compare its class responses before and after blurring each candidate, and score how well these response changes align with the labeled regions. This detail allocation score ranks candidates from the same source. Comparing their response--label alignment with that of the real source image also guides repair decisions and, in multiclass tasks, the choice of regions to edit. Selected candidates and accepted repairs are used for training with the original labels and the unchanged downstream segmenter. Experiments on camouflaged object detection, degraded salient object detection, and adverse-condition semantic segmentation show that our selection outperforms equally sized random and label-agreement subsets. Combining selection and repair improves over real-only training by up to 0.019 in $S_α$ on COD10K and 2.86 mIoU points on ACDC.

</details>


### [134] [Learning How a Body Moves: Few-Shot Body Inference for Visual World Models of Animated Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28842)
*Amarnath R*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出in-context world model：用角色其他动作片段编码"身体表征"，以对比损失区分外观相同但动力学不同的角色；发布ToonTwins基准，新形状上双胞胎识别85.9% vs 基线76.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外观完全相同的动画角色因身体动力学不同，对同一动作指令响应不同；仅凭首帧+动作指令，视觉世界模型无从区分这些"双胞胎"，需要额外身体信息。

Method: 构建in-context world model：将角色其他动作的片段编码为身体表征（body code），并用对比损失在潜空间中拉开外观相似但动力学不同的角色。同时提出ToonTwins基准（144个2D卡通角色，跑/跳/落/转身/蹲，外观相似四元组仅动力学不同），并在DeformingThings4D的32个动画动物上评测。

Result: 潜像素世界模型至多还原25%的双胞胎跳跃高度差；新形状上正确识别双胞胎85.9%，Transformer基线（读同样片段但无身体摘要）76.7%，消融显示对比损失贡献过半增益；动画动物上平均路径预测误差较无身体信息模型降低8.5%。

Conclusion: 显式身体表征+对比学习有效提升对动力学差异的辨识与预测，但现有潜像素世界模型对动力学差异的还原能力仍有限。

Abstract: Two visually identical animated characters can respond differently to the same action command because their body dynamics differ. For example, a stiff blob and its bouncy twin reach different heights when both receive the jump command. Given only the first frame and the action command, a visual world model has no information to distinguish these twins, because their initial appearances are identical. We therefore propose an in-context world model that encodes clips of a character's other actions into a body representation, using a contrastive loss to separate look-alike characters. We introduce ToonTwins, a benchmark of 144 2D cartoon characters that run, jump, fall, turn and crouch. Its characters form look-alike groups of four that differ only in their dynamics profile, and we also evaluate the model on 32 animated animals from DeformingThings4D. Even with a body code from these clips, the latent pixel world model we tested recovered at most 25 percent of the twins' jump height difference. On new shapes, our model identified the correct twin in 85.9 percent of cases, compared with 76.7 percent for a transformer baseline that reads the same clips without a body summary, and in a controlled ablation the contrastive loss gave more than half of this gain. On the animated animals, it reduced the mean path prediction error by 8.5 percent against a model without body information.

</details>


### [135] [MQCODet: Modality Quality-Guided Collaborative Optimization for UAV-Based RGB-T Tiny Pedestrian Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28850)
*Hui Li,Jianbo Ma,Hui Luo,Guanglin Qi,Zhixing Wang,Haorui Zuo,Jianlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MQCODet，将模态质量从融合先验扩展为定位监督线索，结合DCRF融合、ECI编码器与MAA损失，在两个无人机RGB-T微小行人检测基准上超越SOTA并实现边缘端实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机RGB-T微小行人检测受跨模态空间异质性困扰，表现为模态质量不均衡与空间位置错位；现有检测器仅在特征融合阶段处理该问题，未建立融合与目标定位之间的有效耦合，导致检测性能次优。

Method: 1) 动态校准与区域感知融合（DCRF）模块：渐进式细化跨模态空间对齐，进行区域感知融合，产出可靠多尺度特征与场景级模态质量先验；2) 高效上下文交互（ECI）编码器：增强多尺度特征交互并保留细粒度空间细节；3) 模态感知自适应（MAA）损失：结合模态先验与目标尺度，自适应平衡空间一致性与几何相似性以监督定位。

Result: 在两个无人机RGB-T微小行人检测基准上优于现有SOTA方法，并在边缘平台上实现实时推理。

Conclusion: 通过将模态质量先验贯穿融合与定位监督，可有效缓解跨模态空间异质性，验证了融合—定位协同优化的有效性及边缘部署可行性。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based RGB-thermal (RGB-T) remote sensing provides complementary visual and thermal information for pedestrian perception under complex aerial observation conditions. However, detecting tiny pedestrians in UAV-based RGB-T imagery remains challenging due to cross-modal spatial heterogeneity, manifested as modality quality imbalance and spatial position misalignment. Existing RGB-T detectors primarily address these challenges at the feature fusion stage, without establishing an effective coupling between feature fusion and object localization, potentially resulting in suboptimal detection performance. To address this limitation, we introduce a Modality Quality-Guided Collaborative Optimization Detector (MQCODet), which extends modality quality from a fusion prior to a localization supervision cue. Specifically, we propose a Dynamic Calibration and Region-Aware Fusion (DCRF) module that progressively refines cross-modal spatial alignment and performs region-aware fusion to generate reliable multi-scale features and a scene-level modality quality prior. We further design an Efficient Contextual Interaction (ECI) encoder to enhance multi-scale feature interaction while preserving fine-grained spatial details. Moreover, we propose a Modality-Aware Adaptive (MAA) loss that incorporates the modality prior and object scale to adaptively balance spatial consistency and geometric similarity for localization supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MQCODet surpasses state-of-the-art methods on two UAV-based RGB-T tiny pedestrian detection benchmarks while achieving real-time inference on the edge platform.

</details>


### [136] [CivilCADBench: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs for Understanding, Reconstructing, and Designing Civil Engineering Drawing Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28853)
*Zelin Zheng,Difei Wu,Xinyan Liu,Minghong Chen,Antoni B. Chan,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CivilCADBench——面向土木工程图纸集的基准，含理解、重建与设计三类任务，基于真实 CAD 源文件（>25 万实体），用确定性评分器对比几何/图层/文字/标注，揭示前沿 MLLM 能读图却难还原文件结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土木基础设施的设计、建造与维护依赖工程图纸集，但其编制与修订高度依赖人工，亟需自动化。多模态大模型（MLLM）是潜在方案，然而缺乏贴合真实工程实践的评测基准：现有 3D CAD 基准多为参数化模型，无法反映工程图纸密集、分层、多图幅，以及"打印正确但文件组织错误"的痛点。

Method: 构建 CivilCADBench，来自真实基础设施图纸及源 CAD 文件（>25 万实体）。设三类难度递增任务：理解类（2,183 道专家审核问题，覆盖单图幅到整套图纸）、重建类（智能体化，将打印页面重建为可编辑 CAD 文件，含单页与整套）、设计类（19 项新设计任务）；覆盖 232 页、28 套图纸。采用确定性评分器，与设计师源文件在几何、图层、文字、标注四个维度比对。

Result: 评测 7 个前沿 MLLM：理解与单页重建最佳模型均超 90%，但图层分配仅约 60%、设计任务仅 48%；模型能准确读取并重绘打印内容，却难以恢复文件结构。

Conclusion: CivilCADBench 扎根真实工程实践，同时暴露前沿模型在专业土木工程中的能力上限与短板，为下一代模型与智能体系统、面向智能土木工程的研发提供基础。

Abstract: Civil infrastructure is designed, built, and maintained through engineering drawing sets, yet preparing and revising them remains labour-intensive and calls for automation. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are promising candidates, but assessing them requires a benchmark grounded in real engineering practice. We introduce CivilCADBench, a benchmark for understanding, reconstructing, and designing civil engineering drawing sets, built from real infrastructure drawings and source CAD files containing over 250,000 CAD entities. Unlike parametric 3D CAD models, these drawings are dense, layered, and multi-sheet; and since prints are lossy projections with no pixel trace of layers, a file can print correctly yet remain wrongly organised. CivilCADBench comprises three task families of increasing difficulty: understanding tasks are evaluated with 2,183 expert-reviewed questions ranging from single sheets to whole sets, while reconstruction and design tasks are agentic, requiring models to rebuild printed pages as editable CAD files, either as single pages or entire drawing sets, and to produce new designs. These tasks are performed across 232 pages, 28 sets, and 19 design tasks, and evaluated by a deterministic scorer that compares model outputs with the designers' source files on geometry, layers, text, and dimensions. We evaluate seven frontier MLLMs on CivilCADBench, and find that they read and redraw printed content accurately but recover the file structure poorly: the best model scores above 90% in understanding and single-page reconstruction, yet only 60% on layer assignment and 48% on design. Grounded in real engineering practice, CivilCADBench exposes both the capabilities and the limitations of frontier models in professional civil engineering, laying a foundation for next-generation models and agentic systems for intelligent civil engineering.

</details>


### [137] [KineState-SWAM: A Surgical World Action Model with Explicit Kinematics and Causal Interaction Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28871)
*Rulin Zhou,Yujie Ma,Rui Tang,Qiujie Song,Wanhao Liu,Guoheng Ma,Liangjing Shao,An Wang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KineState-SWAM手术世界-动作模型，以持久交互状态耦合动作预测与视频生成，将运动学决定的器械运动与学到的场景演化分离，并构建dVRK-Chole5.2k数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术世界模型未显式建模器械运动如何驱动局部组织响应，也未将交互历史用于后续动作预测。

Method: 流匹配策略在交互状态、机器人构型与任务指令条件下预测关节轨迹；Kinematic Geometry Conditioning基于标定URDF将轨迹转为表面运动token与空间对齐的器械渲染；Causal Interaction Dynamics通过几何引导注意力与运动对齐特征传输循环更新状态（无需中间视频合成）；State-Geometry Adapter以状态相关空间调制与局部几何残差引导视频生成；交互感知监督聚焦器械工作区附近组织响应。

Result: 在匹配动作条件设置下，动作精度与视频保真度优于基线；消融实验支持交互状态的保留与传输对长时程预测的必要性。

Conclusion: 显式分离运动学运动与场景演化、并持久化交互状态，可有效提升手术世界-动作模型的联合预测性能与长时程一致性。

Abstract: Existing surgical world models often leave implicit how instrument motion drives localized tissue responses and how interaction history informs subsequent actions. We introduce KineState-SWAM, a surgical world-action model coupling action prediction and video generation through a persistent interaction state, separating kinematically determined instrument motion from learned scene evolution. A flow-matching policy predicts joint trajectories conditioned on interaction state, robot configuration, and task instruction. Kinematic Geometry Conditioning converts these trajectories into surface-motion tokens and spatially aligned instrument renderings using calibrated URDF kinematics.
