Causal Interaction Dynamics recurrently updates the state through geometry-guided attention and motion-aligned feature transport, carrying interaction history into subsequent action predictions without intermediate video synthesis. The State-Geometry Adapter combines state-dependent spatial modulation with localized geometric residuals to guide video generation. Interaction-aware supervision emphasizes tissue responses near instrument working regions. We introduce dVRK-Chole5.2k, comprising 5,255 videos spanning 17 surgical tasks. Experiments demonstrate improved action accuracy and video fidelity over evaluated baselines within matched action-conditioning settings. Ablations support preserving and transporting interaction states for long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [138] [SENSA: Visual-to-Brain Generation with Individualized Subject-State Transfer and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28872)
*Yihang Zou,Chunmei Qing,Ruixi Liang,Haichuan Wei,Junpeng Tan,Qifan Yang,Wanxiang Luo,Haodong Li,Xiangmin Xu,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SENSA结构化连续神经响应生成框架，将共享活动与图像特异响应解耦，并用少量配对数据校准紧凑受试者状态，实现准确的视觉到脑编码与跨受试泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉到脑编码任务面临三大难题：难以分离共享活动与图像特异活动、受试者间分布偏移、表征异质性，导致在有限校准数据下编码精度与跨受试泛化受限。

Method: SENSA由独立响应先验捕获共享活动，以互补视觉描述子驱动可分离的传感器-时间基与共享Response Refiner；Subject-Adaptive Factor Transport调整共享基的时空表达，Source-Grounded Residual Memory在源响应坐标下预测图像相关校正；二者在冻结生成器上利用有限配对支持集实现个性化。

Result: 生成主干在三个数据集上优于近期生成器，图像身份扰动验证了刺激特异性；仅用400对图像，EEG与NSD校准的残差Pearson相关分别为0.1091与0.2995。

Conclusion: 通过共享-特异解耦与紧凑受试者状态校准，SENSA在少样本条件下实现有效脑响应生成与跨受试适应，为视觉认知研究与神经解码提供可行方案。

Abstract: Visual-to-brain encoding predicts neural responses to visual stimuli, supporting visual cognition research and neural decoding. However, challenges in disentangling shared from image-specific activity, together with inter-subject distribution shifts and representational heterogeneity, limit accurate encoding and cross-subject generalization with limited calibration data.
  We introduce SENSA, a structured continuous neural response generation framework that separates shared activity from image-dependent responses and calibrates a compact subject state. An independent response prior captures shared activity, while complementary visual descriptors drive separable sensor-time bases and a shared Response Refiner. Subject-Adaptive Factor Transport adjusts the spatiotemporal expression of shared bases, while Source-Grounded Residual Memory predicts image-dependent corrections in source-response coordinates. Together, they personalize a frozen generator using limited paired support.
  Experiments demonstrate that the generation backbone outperforms recent generators across three datasets, with image-identity perturbations confirming stimulus specificity. Using only 400 paired images, electroencephalography (EEG) and the Natural Scenes Dataset (NSD) calibration achieve residual Pearson correlations of 0.1091 and 0.2995, respectively, demonstrating effective brain-response generation and cross-subject adaptation.

</details>


### [139] [Eidetic Code World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28878)
*Wenqing Tian,Zijian Zhang,Qiang Liu,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Paintress 将可执行几何代理与独立参数化的 3D 高斯外观记忆耦合于共享度量坐标系，并通过"写-读分离"机制解决生成式世界模型在离开视野后外观漂移的问题，同时提出 Expedition-33 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可执行代码能保持世界结构布局，但视频渲染器在场景离开相机视野后常改变已访问地点的外观，导致生成外观的长期一致性（retention）不足。

Method: 代码条件世界模型：1) 可执行几何代理约束结构与可见性；2) 独立参数化 3D 高斯记忆捕获视觉实现；3) 写入-读取分离——新帧在置信度门控下增量更新持久原始高斯图，读取副本针对观测档案全局精化并以覆盖掩码渲染以条件化生成。基准 Expedition-33：11 个静态场景、5 类路线族、15/30/60 s 目标缺失，共 165 设置；目标被排除于首帧，在共享前缀中合成，并在匹配返回相机位姿下与系统自身首次访问比对评分。

Result: 在 10 个系统、1650 段视频上，Paintress 达 0.1220 ROI-LPIPS、0.0613 MAE、52.12% 细节保持率（CWM 为 0.2731、0.1401、10.91%）；60 s 缺失时差异为 0.1630，低于 CWM、AgentGarten、Lyra 2.0 在 15 s 时的表现。

Conclusion: 几何代理与外观记忆解耦、读写分离的架构能显著提升生成式世界模型对已访问场景外观的长期保持能力，Expedition-33 为该能力提供可量化评测。

Abstract: Executable code can preserve a world's structural layout, yet video renderers frequently alter the appearance of previously visited places once they leave the camera view. We present Paintress, a code-conditioned world model that couples an executable geometric proxy with an independently parameterized 3D Gaussian appearance memory in a shared metric coordinate frame. While the proxy enforces structural and visibility constraints, 3D Gaussians capture the visual realization synthesized by emitted observations. To balance stable incremental fusion with consistent readout, Paintress separates writing from reading: newly emitted frames update a persistent raw Gaussian map under confidence-gated selection, whereas a cloned reader copy is globally refined against the observation archive and rendered with coverage masks to condition generation. We also introduce Expedition-33, a benchmark for generated-appearance retention across 11 static scenes, five route families, and 15, 30, and 60 s target absences (165 settings per system). Excluded from the opening image, targets are synthesized during a shared prefix and scored against each system's own first visit at matched return cameras. Across ten systems (1,650 videos), Paintress achieves 0.1220 ROI-LPIPS, 0.0613 MAE, and 52.12% detail retention (vs. 0.2731, 0.1401, and 10.91% for CWM), with lower discrepancy at 60 s (0.1630) than CWM, AgentGarten, and Lyra 2.0 achieve at 15 s.

</details>


### [140] [Controlled One-Click Benchmarking and Post-Fusion Recalibration for Support-Conditioned Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28890)
*Wang-Chi-Shiun,Wei-Huan Hu,Liu-En-Chi,Liu Zhi Shun,ChengYi Peng,Shin-Jye Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Support-Aware Recalibration (SAR)后融合重校准模块，在受控单击提示协议下提升MultiverSeg的医学图像分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏提示（单击）医学分割中，提示、支持样本、阈值和数据集划分不一致，导致方法难以公平比较；同时希望在不引入测试泄漏的前提下提升分割性能。

Method: 采用受控单击协议：每张测试图仅一个正前景点击、支持集仅来自训练集、仅用验证集选checkpoint、无测试时阈值调优。在MultiverSeg支持-查询融合后加入SAR模块，将大核空间门控与SE通道重校准结合（后期后融合重校准）。在BUSI、NerveUS-BP-present、Kvasir-SEG上用匹配的697正样本预算、共享划分与点击、无阈值Dice-AUC/IoU-AUC评估。

Result: 与协议匹配的原始支持MultiverSeg基线相比，主SAR配置在三个数据集上取得更优的平均Dice-AUC/IoU-AUC，且在该实现中记录的训练时间降低。

Conclusion: 在泄漏受控的单击提示协议下，后期后融合重校准是有效的，SAR为稀疏提示医学分割提供了可复现的对比基线配置。

Abstract: Sparse-prompt medical segmentation is hard to compare when prompts, supports, thresholds, and splits differ. This paper uses a controlled one-click protocol: each test image receives one positive foreground click, training-only support, validation-only checkpoint selection, and no test-time threshold tuning. We propose {Support-Aware Recalibration (SAR)}, a late post-fusion recalibration block for MultiverSeg that combines large-kernel spatial gating with squeeze-and-excitation channel recalibration after support--query fusion. BUSI, NerveUS-BP-present, and Kvasir-SEG are evaluated with matched 697 foreground-present sample budgets, shared splits/clicks, and threshold-free Dice-AUC/IoU-AUC. Compared with a protocol-matched raw-support MultiverSeg baseline, the main SAR configuration shows favorable mean Dice-AUC/IoU-AUC performance on BUSI, NerveUS-BP-present, and Kvasir-SEG, while reducing logged training time in this implementation. These controlled results indicate that late post-fusion recalibration is useful under a leakage-controlled one-click protocol.

</details>


### [141] [Gaze Graphs: A Scene-Level Representation of Visual Attention in the Operating Room](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28901)
*Séraphin Baributsa,Keqi Chen,Vinkle Srivastav,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以gaze graph（注视图）作为手术室场景级视觉注意力表示，仅用外视相机估计3D注视目标与受注视实体，并验证其对细粒度手术动作识别与语义场景图生成的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术室gaze-following研究仅估计2D注视点，既不识别受注视场景实体，也无法在3D空间定位注视目标，缺乏场景级、可关联实体的视觉注意力表示。

Method: 构建EgoExOR-Gaze，首个多视角手术室gaze graph生成基准；提出仅依赖外视相机、联合估计3D注视目标与受注视实体的架构，利用3D注视几何在多视角下采样局部视觉特征进行实体预测，并将预测的gaze graph用于细粒度手术动作识别与SSG生成。

Result: gaze graph生成优于纯外视与外视-自视融合基线；动作识别在所有指标上超越两类基线，时间相关指标提升尤为显著；SSG生成macro-F1由0.42提升至0.52，但仍低于自视输入结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式建模场景级视觉注意力对手术动作识别与SSG生成具有价值，即使缺少自视视角亦有效。

Abstract: Purpose: Analyzing clinicians' visual attention from exocentric camera views can support surgical scene understanding without requiring wearable eye trackers. However, existing gaze-following research in the operating room (OR) estimates only 2D gaze locations without identifying attended scene entities or localizing gaze targets in 3D space. In this work, we investigate gaze graphs as a scene-level representation of visual attention that connects clinicians to attended scene entities, and evaluate their utility for fine-grained surgical action recognition and semantic scene graph (SSG) generation. Methods: We extend the public EgoExOR dataset with gaze annotations to create EgoExOR-Gaze, the first benchmark for multi-view gaze graph generation in the OR. We propose a novel architecture that jointly estimates 3D gaze targets and predicts attended entities using only exocentric camera views. Specifically, our approach exploits 3D gaze geometry to sample local visual features across multiple views for attended entity prediction. We further utilize predicted gaze graphs for fine-grained surgical action recognition and SSG generation. Results: For gaze graph generation, our model outperforms both exocentric-only and combined exocentric-egocentric baselines. For surgical action recognition, our model incorporating predicted gaze graphs outperforms both exocentric and egocentric baselines across all evaluated metrics, with particularly strong gains in temporal metrics. For SSG generation, incorporating gaze graphs improves macro-F1 from 0.42 to 0.52 compared to exocentric inputs alone, although performance remains below that achieved with egocentric inputs. Conclusion: Our results demonstrate the value of gaze graphs for surgical action recognition and SSG generation, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling scene-level visual attention even when egocentric views are unavailable.

</details>


### [142] [From Dense to Sparse: Morton-Ordered Sparse Mapping for Efficient 3D Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28913)
*Junjie Yang,Rui Qian,Yijiang Li,Jinyi Xu,Yuhao Yan,Xinjian Yi,Yanfeng Li,Mingxuan Li,Xiang Wan,Jicong Fan,Siqi Liu,Ahmed Elazab,Changmiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPAC 提出一种共享稀疏 SDF 词表，将稀疏编码与可逆空间合并结合，用同一序列同时支持 3D 理解与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一 3D 理解与生成需要保留空间结构的紧凑序列：稠密网格导致序列过长，而串行化会割裂空间近邻关系。

Method: 1) Sparse SDF VQ-VAE：量化近表面特征并保留显式坐标，用重建监督保证表面保真与细节；2) 3D-BPE：在串行化前按相对偏移合并重复的面对面相邻模式，宏 token 可精确展开为叶 token 及坐标；3) Morton 串行化后由共享 LLM 读取几何做描述生成，或预测可解码为网格的 token 实现文本条件生成。

Result: 在固定几何、架构与体素带宽下，稀疏编码器开销随分辨率近似二次增长；3D-BPE 使 LLM 平均完整序列长度减少 45.4%（含坐标、文本与控制 token）。在 3D 描述与文本条件网格生成两项任务上均取得强结果，消融显示优于叶 token 与 Morton 1D-BPE。

Conclusion: 显式坐标 + 近邻感知的可逆稀疏合并能在显著压缩序列的同时保持空间结构，为理解与生成统一的 3D 表示提供有效方案。

Abstract: Unified 3D understanding and generation require compact sequences preserving spatial structure. Dense grids yield long sequences, while serialization can separate spatial neighbors. We propose Sparse Proximity Aware Compression (SPAC), a shared sparse SDF vocabulary combining sparse encoding with reversible spatial merging. A Sparse SDF VQ-VAE quantizes near-surface features while retaining explicit coordinates, using reconstruction supervision for surface fidelity and detail. 3D Byte-Pair Encoding (3D-BPE) merges recurring face-adjacent patterns using relative offsets before serialization. Macro tokens expand exactly into leaf tokens and coordinates. After Morton serialization, a shared LLM reads geometry for captioning and predicts tokens that decode into meshes for text-conditioned generation. With fixed geometry, architecture, and band width in voxels, sparse encoder cost grows approximately quadratically with resolution. 3D-BPE reduces mean complete LLM sequence length by 45.4 percent, including coordinates, text, and control tokens. SPAC achieves strong results in both 3D captioning and text-conditioned mesh generation. Ablations show gains over leaf tokens and Morton 1D-BPE in both tasks. The project page at https://yangjj007.github.io/spac/ .

</details>


### [143] [Div3DAfford: Diverse 3D affordance grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28917)
*Haomeng Zhang,Raymond A. Yeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多样化3D可供性定位新任务Div3DAfford，设计基于Transformer集合预测的Div3DAffordNet，并通过2D生成模型+3D重建的自动流水线构建Div3DAfford基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D可供性定位方法仅预测单一可供性掩码，忽视了人-物交互固有的多样性；同时缺乏多样化3D可供性数据。

Method: 将任务定义为对给定物体与交互查询预测多个合理区域；提出Div3DAffordNet，基于Transformer的集合预测模型生成多样化可供性区域；构建自动化数据流水线，利用2D生成模型与3D重建技术合成3D可供性，辅以轻量人工校验，形成Div3DAfford基准。

Result: 在多掩码设置下达到SOTA，GED-SIM由0.745降至0.492；在标准基准上保持19.0 mIoU的单掩码竞争性能；数据标注时间缩短72倍。

Conclusion: 多掩码多样化的3D可供性定位是可行且有效的，集合预测框架能有效刻画多区域可供性，合成+轻量校验的数据构建范式可显著降低标注成本。

Abstract: 3D affordance grounding aims to localize 3D regions specified by an interaction query. Existing methods predict a single affordance mask, overlooking the inherent diversity of human-object interactions. We introduce diverse 3D affordance grounding, a new task that requires predicting multiple plausible regions for a given object and affordance query. We then propose Div3DAffordNet, a transformer-based set prediction model capable of generating diverse affordance regions. To address the scarcity of diverse 3D affordance data, we develop an automated pipeline leveraging 2D generative models and 3D reconstruction techniques to synthesize 3D affordances, followed by lightweight human verification (reducing the annotation time by 72X), yielding the Div3DAfford benchmark. Extensive experiments show Div3DAffordNet effectively captures multi-mask affordance regions. Our method achieves the SOTA affordance grounding performance in the multi-mask setting, reducing GED-SIM from 0.745 to 0.492, while maintaining competitive single-mask performance on the standard benchmark of 19.0 mIoU.

</details>


### [144] [PIAE: Physics-Informed Continuous Autoencoding for Compact 3D Human Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28922)
*Osama Alshammari,Omar Mohammed,Kerven Durdymyradov,Naeemullah Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理信息连续自编码器PIAE，在固定8×时间压缩的token预算下显式保留运动学信号（根速度/朝向）并联合学习特征，显著降低全局MPJPE并提升下游文本驱动生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间压缩虽能缩短运动生成器需建模的序列长度，却会损害关节细节与全局轨迹；而根速度与朝向误差会经由轨迹恢复传播、影响整个身体的定位，其后果与其他误差并不对等，因此需在固定表征预算下对信息做差异化分配。

Method: PIAE为物理信息连续自编码器，将每个256维token在8×压缩下划分为224维学习特征与32维物理载体；完整token用于神经解码条件，并行恢复路径对选定输出施加物理约束。以HumanML3D官方基准评测，并设仅根、显式根混合、替代载体（躯干参考点/支撑锚点）及冻结检查点等对照。

Result: 全局MPJPE 8.936 mm，较同宽度纯学习模型降低82.0%；冻结检查点诊断中，解析校正前的面朝向局部神经身体误差下降76.6%，且输入同一真实轨迹后身体质量优势仍保持；冻结8×AE时匹配的文本到动作生成器可预测全部载体数值，FID由0.506降至0.163；冻结重建无需适配即可迁移至AIST++；相对1×对应模型生成时间减少27.8%。

Conclusion: 选择性保留可在固定表征预算下更有效利用容量，兼顾神经关节重建与紧凑人体动作的下游生成；载体类型存在权衡——躯干参考利于位置精度，支撑锚点利于支撑质量。

Abstract: Temporal compression reduces the sequence length that motion generators must model, but can damage articulated detail and global trajectory. These errors have unequal consequences: root-velocity and heading errors propagate through trajectory recovery and affect the placement of the entire body. We propose PIAE, a physics-informed continuous autoencoder that allocates a fixed token budget between explicitly retained kinematic signals and complementary learned features. At 8 x temporal compression, each 256-dimensional token contains 224 learned values and a 32-dimensional physical carrier. The complete token conditions neural decoding, while a parallel recovery path constrains selected outputs. On the official HumanML3D benchmark, PIAE achieves 8.936 mm global MPJPE, an 82.0% reduction relative to a same-width learned-only model. The benefit extends beyond trajectory restoration: facing-local neural-body error before analytical correction decreases by 76.6% in a frozen-checkpoint validation diagnostic, and supplying the same true trajectory to both models preserves the body-quality advantage. Targeted root-only and explicit-root hybrid controls establish strong trajectory-aware comparisons; alternative carriers reveal that torso references favor position accuracy, whereas stance anchors favor support quality. With the original 8 x AEs frozen, matched text-to-motion generators predict every carrier value and improve FID from 0.506 to 0.163. Frozen reconstruction also transfers to AIST++ without adaptation. The compressed carrier interface reduces measured generation time by 27.8% relative to its 1 x counterpart. Together, these findings demonstrate that selective preservation can use a fixed representation budget more effectively, improving neural articulated reconstruction and downstream generation in compact human motion.

</details>


### [145] [XShot: Cross-Shot Video Object Insertion from a Reference Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28942)
*Bangxun Tang,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XShot 是跨镜头视频物体插入框架：以同一场景另一镜头的参考视频为输入，无需对齐、mask、box 或相机位姿，通过几何感知的跨镜头注意力在视频 DiT 上做 in-context 生成，实现目标镜头中物体的准确放置与外观一致的渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频制作中同一场景由多个镜头呈现，在一个镜头中加入的物体必须在其他镜头中保持一致。现有视频编辑工具从参考图像取外观、从文本/掩码/编辑过的关键帧取位置，一致性需用户自行保证，且常依赖 mask、box 或相机位姿等额外输入。

Method: 基于视频 Diffusion Transformer，将任务建模为对参考视频、若干物体图像与目标视频的 in-context 生成；提出几何感知跨镜头注意力，利用模型自身对应关系估计两镜头间的对极几何，并据此引导目标镜头从参考视频与示例中读取信息。训练采用 erase-and-restore 数据引擎：从影片或多相机录制的一个镜头中擦除物体，再由另一镜头恢复。

Result: 实验表明，与开源及商业视频编辑器相比，XShot 物体放置更准确、渲染更忠实，且目标镜头的其余部分保持完整（未被破坏）。

Conclusion: XShot 将跨镜头一致性由用户负担转为模型内生能力，通过参考视频驱动、免对齐与免掩码的 in-context 生成，并借助对极几何引导注意力，实现跨镜头视频物体插入。

Abstract: We present XShot, a framework for cross-shot video object insertion, which adds objects to a shot by watching another shot of the same scene. Video productions show a scene through many shots, and objects added to one shot must appear consistently in the others. Most video editors take the appearance of new objects from reference images and their location from a text prompt, a mask or an edited keyframe, leaving this consistency to the user. XShot reads both from a reference video of another shot, which need not be aligned with the target, and needs no mask, box or camera pose. We build on a video Diffusion Transformer and formulate the task as in-context generation over the reference video, a few images of the objects and the target video. A geometry-aware cross-shot attention estimates the epipolar geometry between the two shots from the model's own correspondences and uses it to guide where the target reads the reference and the exemplars. We train XShot with an erase-and-restore data engine: objects are erased from one shot of a film or a multi-camera recording and restored from another shot of the scene. Experiments show that XShot places objects more accurately and renders them more faithfully than open-source and commercial video editors, while leaving the rest of the shot intact.

</details>


### [146] [Learning Land-Surface Hydrology World Model from Satellite Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28963)
*Suya Li,Zekun Qi,Xiaochong Xu,Duo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LandWM以降雨为"动作"构建陆面水世界模型，将潜状态通过物理观测算子绑定到土壤水、用质量守恒平衡式约束状态对降雨的响应。它无需见过土壤湿度真值即能匹配SMAP L4的业务精度，"动作可干预性"测试表明替换组件后预测不变但状态含义丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球系统学习模型虽预报精度高，但预报技巧无法揭示其内部状态的含义（可读性）以及状态如何响应外部驱动力变化（可干预性）。作者希望构建一个"可解释、可干预"的世界模型，而不仅是黑箱预测器。

Method: 构建LandWM：以7年SMAP与CYGNSS观测为数据，降雨作为动作输入；采用两个结构组件——(1) 物理观测算子将潜状态映射到土壤水观测，(2) 质量守恒的水量平衡方程支配状态对降雨的转移。在美南大平原用28个地面探针及SMAP L4产品做验证，并进行消融与多类型干预实验（单格点暴雨 vs 全区降雨、神经算子 vs 守恒转移）。

Result: 模型从未见土壤湿度标签，仍能追踪28个地面探针，精度与SMAP L4相当，且仅用CYGNSS亦可达到；在站点格点强迫中加入风暴，其土壤变湿的方式与自然风暴下探针的变化一致。替换组件后预报技巧保持，但语义丢失：用神经算子时仅靠拟合站点的探针才能找到土壤水；用通用循环转移时单格点降雨几乎不改变状态。然而当同一降雨覆盖全区域时，两种转移角色互换，守恒转移反而响应过弱。

Conclusion: 物理接地使世界模型"可读"；但动作响应必须在多种干预类型下检验，单一干预测试会给出误导性结论。可解释性与可干预性是独立于预报技巧的评价维度。

Abstract: A world model should tell us not only what will happen, but what would happen under a different action. Learned Earth models now forecast remarkably well, yet forecast skill says little about what their state means or how it responds to a changed driver. We build LandWM, a world model of land-surface water in which rain is the action, learned from seven years of SMAP and CYGNSS observations: physical observation operators tie its latent state to soil water, and a mass-conserving balance governs how that state responds to rain. Over the U.S. Southern Great Plains, the model, never shown a soil-moisture value, tracks 28 in-situ probes as well as the operational SMAP Level-4 product, even from CYGNSS alone, and a storm added to the forcing of a station's cell wets its soil as natural storms wet the probes. Replace either piece and the forecasts stay, but the meaning goes: with neural operators, only a probe fitted to the stations finds the soil water; with a generic recurrent transition, rain on a single cell barely moves it. Yet when the same rain covers the whole region the two transitions swap roles, and the conserving one responds too weakly. Grounding makes a world model readable; its response to actions must be tested under more than one kind of intervention.

</details>


### [147] [What the Background Hides: Thresholds, Vocabularies and Collisions in Training-Free Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28966)
*Wendong Yao,Binhua Huang,Soumyabrata Dev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文审计13个免训练开放词汇语义分割(OVSS)方法的背景类处理配置，发现官方设置与统一协议之间的mIoU差距高度依赖数据集(VOC21达22.8点，ADE20K仅0.1点)，并改变方法排序，但仍保留约79%的后续方法相对SCLIP的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免训练OVSS以mIoU排名，但含背景类的数据集上依赖极少公开、未统一的逐数据集阈值与背景词表(13个方法中11个使用逐数据集背景决策)，导致评测不可比、复现困难且可能掩盖真实进展。

Method: 审计13个方法的配置与论文，复现7个官方代码库；在预注册契约下对比官方设置与严格统一协议；分解官方背景处理为数值设置与类名文件两部分，检验各调参无关规则的跨数据集可迁移性；检查背景词与目标类别名的精确碰撞(如COCO-Object中"bed")；分析语言模型生成词表在VOC21上的逐类下降来源。

Result: 协议差距随基准强烈变化(VOC21为22.8 mIoU点，ADE20K为0.1点，与零无显著差异)并改变成对排序；统一协议下仍保留约79%的已发表增益(相对SCLIP)；背景处理的主导成分在不同基准间反转；无可测试的免调参规则可跨全部数据集可靠迁移；7个官方COCO-Object配置中6个存在背景词与类名碰撞，使受影响类别在官方后处理下不可预测；VOC21上语言模型词表导致的逐类下降主要归因于少数生成词。

Conclusion: 含背景类基准上的OVSS评测需统一、公开报告背景处理；作者提出报告规范建议，并发布评测代码、冻结配置、契约及碰撞检查工具。

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) built on CLIP has advanced rapidly, with methods typically ranked by mean intersection-over-union (mIoU) across standard benchmarks. On datasets with a background class, however, implementations rely on per-dataset thresholds and background word lists that are rarely documented or unified (11 of 13 surveyed methods configure per-dataset background decisions). We audit configurations and papers of 13 training-free OVSS methods, re-run seven from official codebases, and compare official settings against a strict unified protocol under pre-registered contracts. The protocol gap depends strongly on the benchmark---ranging from 22.8 mIoU points on PASCAL VOC (VOC21) to 0.1 points (indistinguishable from zero) on ADE20K---and alters pairwise method rankings. Despite large shifts in absolute mIoU, most of the reported improvement of later CLIP-only methods over SCLIP remains under the unified protocol (roughly 79% of the published gain). Decomposing official background handling shows that it combines numeric settings and class-name files whose dominant component reverses across benchmarks, while no tested tuning-free rule transfers reliably across all datasets. Moreover, exact collisions between background words and target names---present in six of seven official COCO-Object configurations through "bed"---make affected classes unpredictable under official post-processing, while the main per-class drops under language-model-generated vocabularies on VOC21 are largely attributable to a few generated terms. We offer reporting recommendations and release our evaluation code, frozen configurations, contracts, and a collision-check tool.

</details>


### [148] [AlignHand: Multi-View Hand Reconstruction from a Frozen Monocular Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28979)
*Zhilu Yang,Kun Yang,Shiyan Wang,Anqi Xu,Xinran Huang,Yizheng Jiao,Huan Yang,Rolandos Alexandros Potamias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlignHand：在冻结的单目手部重建先验之上，构建标定多视角框架，先对齐多视角网格，再按解剖区域选择性融合互补表面证据并做像素对齐细化，在DexYCB-MV、HanCo、HO3D-Eval-MV上取得最低MPVPE/MPJPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目手部估计器可从单张图像恢复细节丰富的3D网格，但多视角独立预测的网格在尺度、位置与关节配置上不一致，难以有效整合互补的多视角信息，因此需要一种对齐与融合机制。

Method: 基于冻结的单目手部重建先验：(1) 细化跨视角关节观测并估计其可靠性，建立共享的度量参考以实现对齐；(2) 将网格变换到公共坐标系后，按解剖区域选择性融合互补表面证据；(3) 进行像素对齐细化，从局部图像特征预测有界残差位移，形成全局到局部的整合。

Result: 在DexYCB-MV、HanCo和HO3D-Eval-MV上，按各自评测协议，AlignHand的MPVPE与MPJPE均为对比方法中最低；相比匹配几何基线，MPVPE分别降低30.1%、29.1%和8.0%。

Conclusion: 通过全局对齐加局部细化的两级设计，可在保留强单目先验的同时利用多视角互补观测，显著提升多视角手部网格重建精度。

Abstract: Modern monocular hand estimators recover detailed 3D meshes from individual images, yet effectively leveraging their predictions across multiple views remains challenging. Independently predicted meshes can disagree in scale, placement, and articulation, limiting the integration of complementary multi-view information. Hence, we present AlignHand, a calibrated multi-view framework that builds on a frozen monocular hand reconstruction prior to align and enhance its predictions. Specifically, AlignHand refines cross-view joint observations and estimates their reliability to establish a shared metric reference for alignment. Once the meshes are brought into a common frame, their complementary surface evidence is selectively fused across anatomical regions, followed by pixel-aligned refinement that predicts bounded residual displacements from local image features. This global-to-local integration enables AlignHand to exploit complementary observations while preserving the strong monocular prior. Extensive experiments on DexYCB-MV, HanCo, and HO3D-Eval-MV demonstrate that AlignHand achieves the lowest MPVPE and MPJPE among the compared methods under the respective evaluation protocols, reducing MPVPE by 30.1%, 29.1%, and 8.0%, respectively, compared with a matched geometry baseline.

</details>


### [149] [CLASP: Complementary Layer Selection and Profiling for Zero-Shot OOD Detection using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28986)
*Saahil Islam,Adarsh Bhandary Panambur,Teodora Spasojevic,Eduardo Castaneda,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CLASP：仅用ID数据的医学VLM OOD检测框架，通过高斯条件互信息排序并锚定谱选择视觉编码器层，结合层—提示profile与PCA压缩视觉特征拟合单一收缩正则化高斯，以马氏距离打分，在MIDOG近OOD上AUROC达87.54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的零样本OOD检测多依赖末层置信度或熵来选层，可能遗漏互补信息；作者指出ID置信熵低并不能可靠预测OOD判别能力，需要更稳健的层选择与特征建模方式。

Method: CLASP为仅用ID数据的框架：联合建模层—提示（layer–prompt）响应与视觉特征；用高斯条件互信息对视觉编码器各层排序，并通过锚定谱选择（anchored spectral selection）选出紧凑子集，且不固定层数；将选中的层—提示profile与PCA压缩的视觉嵌入拼接，拟合单一收缩正则化高斯，以平方马氏距离作为OOD分数；无需微调、OOD训练数据或测试时自适应。

Result: 在组织病理、胸部X光、脑MRI三类基准上，配合UniMedCLIP与BioMedCLIP，CLASP在不同分布偏移下表现强劲。MIDOG近OOD上，UniMedCLIP下AUROC为87.54%，优于基于置信度的基线；BioMedCLIP下仍有竞争力但略逊于纯视觉马氏建模。消融实验表明层—提示交互与跨特征依赖有价值，同时揭示贡献具有偏移依赖性，并在语义偏移下存在局限。

Conclusion: 层选择不应仅依赖ID置信度/熵；联合利用层—提示响应与视觉特征的互信息驱动层选择与收缩高斯建模，可在无需OOD监督的情况下提升医学VLM的OOD检测，但效果依偏移类型而异，语义偏移仍是主要挑战。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable zero-shot out-of-distribution (OOD) detection in medical imaging, yet existing approaches often rely on final-layer confidence or entropy-based layer selection, potentially missing complementary information. We show that low in-distribution (ID) confidence entropy does not reliably predict OOD discrimination. We introduce CLASP (Complementary Layer Selection and Profiling), an ID-only framework that jointly models layer--prompt responses and visual features. CLASP ranks visual encoder layers using Gaussian conditional mutual information and selects a compact subset through anchored spectral selection, without fixing the number of layers. It then combines the selected layer--prompt profiles with PCA-compressed visual embeddings and fits a single shrinkage-regularized Gaussian. OOD scores are computed using squared Mahalanobis distance, without fine-tuning, OOD training data, or test-time adaptation. Across histopathology, chest X-ray, and brain MRI benchmarks with UniMedCLIP and BioMedCLIP, CLASP performs strongly under varied distribution shifts. On MIDOG near-OOD, it achieves 87.54% AUROC with UniMedCLIP, outperforming confidence-based baselines; with BioMedCLIP, it remains competitive but trails visual-only Mahalanobis modeling. Ablations highlight the value of layer--prompt interactions and cross-feature dependencies, while exposing shift-dependent contributions and limitations under semantic shifts.

</details>


### [150] [Diagnosing Verbalized Confidence in a Medical Vision-Language Model for Selective Prediction on Colorectal Histopathology Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28993)
*Anay Sinhal,Amit Sinhal,Ashish Pandey,Arpana Sinhal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 测试MedGemma-27b-it的言语化置信度是否可用于病理patch的分流（不确定性标记），发现其在标准提示下几乎无区分度，但经提示重构或采用多提示一致性信号后可改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将不确定的病理切片标记给专家复核，需要模型在答对时给出更高置信度。作者检验开源医学视觉语言模型能否提供这种可用的置信信号。

Method: 在10,004个PathMNIST验证patch及7,180个患者不重叠外部队列patch上，对MedGemma-27b-it与其基座Gemma-3-27b-it做零样本单提示推理；评估置信度-正确性AUROC、选择性准确率AUC、标签token概率、五提示变体下的一致性信号，并在按正确性分层、加权回全集的1,800子集上做检验，同时考虑幻灯片级聚类的外部稳健性。

Result: 两者准确率过低（40.6%、35.5%）无法部署。MedGemma将96%的patch置信度压在单一取值，AUROC为0.499/0.528，分流收益为-0.004/+0.037；Gemma-3分布跨9个取值，AUROC 0.70，收益+0.114/+0.115。标签token概率无法补救（外部优于验证集之处在top-20含全部类别时反转）。多提示一致性优于随机（+0.123/+0.106），纯措辞改写为+0.100及+0.054（仅方向一致），但相对置信度的外部优势在幻灯片级聚类后不成立。在一个重构提示下，MedGemma置信度可有效分流（+0.112/+0.141）。

Conclusion: MedGemma的置信度失效与为最大化macro F1而选定的提示有关，而非模型固有缺陷；更可靠的不确定性信号来自提示一致性或经过任务适配的提示设计。

Abstract: Flagging uncertain histopathology patches for specialist review needs a confidence that is higher when the model is right. We test whether an open medical vision-language model emits one. MedGemma-27b-it and its base Gemma-3-27b-it were run zero-shot under one prompt on 10,004 PathMNIST validation patches and 7,180 patches of a patient-disjoint external cohort. Both are too inaccurate to deploy (40.6% and 35.5%); the object of study is the confidence. MedGemma's verbalized confidence puts 96% of patches at one value; its confidence-correctness AUROC is 0.499 and 0.528, and routing by it moves selective-accuracy AUC by -0.004 and +0.037. Gemma-3 spreads its confidence over nine values, reaches AUROC 0.70, and gains +0.114 and +0.115. MedGemma's label-token probabilities do not rescue it: their validation advantage reverses where the top-20 alternatives include every class. On 1,800-patch subsets of each cohort, stratified on correctness and reweighted to the full splits, agreement across five prompt variants beats random by +0.123 and +0.106, and wording-only paraphrases give +0.100 and, externally and in direction only, +0.054; agreement's external margins over confidence do not survive slide-level clustering. Under one restructured prompt variant, MedGemma's stated confidence routes (+0.112, +0.141), so the failure is tied to a prompt chosen for macro F1.

</details>


### [151] [Elicitation format changes zero-shot classification accuracy in a medical vision-language model: a two-center evaluation on colorectal histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28997)
*Anay Sinhal,Amit Sinhal,Arpana Sinhal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在医学视觉语言模型中，仅改变回答格式（是否要求九类概率分布、是否禁止推理与给答案）本身就会显著改变预测准确性；请求完整九类概率分布会使准确率下降约14个百分点，而移除“禁止评论/推理/给出答案”的指令可恢复大部分性能；首token logit熵的置信度信号优于任何被引导输出的置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有做法把托管医学视觉语言模型输出的“言语化置信度”视为对不变预测的包装，默认其不影响分类结果。作者质疑这一假设，想检验回答格式（尤其置信度与概率分布的请求方式）是否会实质改变医学图像分类器的预测，并比较不同置信度信号的质量。

Method: 固定九类结直肠组织病理提示词，仅改变要求的回答格式，在MedGemma-27b-it上评估10,004张验证集与7,180张外中心PathMNIST图像块。采用预注册终点、随机化平局处理，并为每套图像块重算随机基线。设置匹配基数与token预算的对照，单独分离“禁止评论/推理/给出答案”这一指令成分，并比较四种视觉语言模型的准确率代价与选择性预测增益；将首token logit熵与其他被引导置信度信号相对各自随机基线比较。

Result: 要求九类概率分布而非排序前三类，使验证集准确率下降14.8个百分点（-0.1475 [-0.1594, -0.1353]），外中心下降13.4（-0.1341 [-0.1484, -0.1196]），随机准确率为0.1111。九类提示是唯一禁止评论、推理与给出答案的格式；在匹配基数与token预算下取消该禁令，两套数据分别恢复+0.1878 [+0.1623, +0.2134]与+0.1911 [+0.1648, +0.2181]；而在无禁令时仅改变基数，准确率变化仅为-0.0013 [-0.0300, +0.0280]与-0.0214 [-0.0494, +0.0071]。九类格式在7.6%与7.7%的图像块上无可解析分布，多为两个纹理最少类别的零向量，延后处理这些失败案例首先解释了其可测量的选择性预测增益。四个模型中准确率与代价不对应：准确率相同的两个模型代价相差五到七倍。首token logit熵的路由效果优于任何被引导信号，相对各自随机基线为+0.1820 [+0.1722, +0.1974]对+0.0493 [+0.0377, +0.0649]。

Conclusion: 回答格式会改变医学视觉语言分类器，此处起作用的关键成分是抑制给出答案的指令。有logits时应使用logits；否则应请求简短的排序列表，且不禁止推理或给出答案。

Abstract: Background. Verbalized confidence from a hosted medical vision-language model is usually treated as packaging around an unchanged prediction. Methods. We held a nine-class colorectal histopathology prompt fixed except for the requested response format and evaluated MedGemma-27b-it on 10,004 validation and 7,180 external-center PathMNIST patches, with preregistered endpoints, randomized ties, and random floors recomputed for each patch set. Results. Requesting probabilities over all nine classes instead of a ranked top-3 reduced accuracy by 14.8 points on validation, -0.1475 [-0.1594, -0.1353], and 13.4 at the external center, -0.1341 [-0.1484, -0.1196], against chance accuracy of 0.1111. The nine-class prompt uniquely forbade commentary, reasoning, and a chosen answer; removing that prohibition at matched cardinality and token budget recovered +0.1878 [+0.1623, +0.2134] and +0.1911 [+0.1648, +0.2181], while changing cardinality with the prohibition absent moved accuracy by -0.0013 [-0.0300, +0.0280] and -0.0214 [-0.0494, +0.0071]. The nine-class format returned no parseable distribution on 7.6 and 7.7 percent of patches, mostly zero vectors in the two least-textured classes, and deferring these failures first explained its measurable selective-prediction gain. Across four vision-capable models, accuracy did not grade the cost: two models matched on accuracy differed five- to sevenfold. First-token logit entropy routed better than any elicited signal, +0.1820 [+0.1722, +0.1974] versus +0.0493 [+0.0377, +0.0649] relative to each signal's random floor. Conclusions. Response format changes a medical vision-language classifier, and the operative component here was the instruction suppressing an answer. Use logits when available; otherwise request a short ranked list without prohibiting reasoning or a chosen answer.

</details>


### [152] [Demystifying Classifier-Free Guidance for Auto-Regressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29002)
*Zhiling Zhou,Jiachun Pan,Fengzhuo Zhang,Dirk Bergemann,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过实证与理论研究揭示了自回归模型中无分类器引导（CFG）的作用机理，并据此提出加速方法AttnReuse。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFG在自回归图像生成中效果显著但机理不明，作者希望厘清其工作机制并据此改进效率。

Method: 分析CFG各分量中top-ranked语义；对浅层Transformer进行训练动力学理论分析；基于条件与无条件logits共享语义提出Attention Weight Reuse（复用条件分支前向的中间注意力计算以加速无条件分支）。

Result: 纹理信息主要编码于条件与无条件logits之差，而两者主导语义均为前 token 重复，这一重复语义源自图像纹理稀疏性而非预训练缺陷；CFG通过在推理时纠正该重复偏差提升质量；AttnReuse减少约25%注意力计算且性能几乎无损。

Conclusion: CFG的机理是纠正由数据纹理稀疏性引起的共享重复偏差；利用条件与无条件分支的语义共享可实现高效推理。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) has been widely adopted in autoregressive (AR) models for high-quality image generation. Despite its strong empirical performance, its mechanism in AR models remains unclear. This paper demystifies the mechanism of CFG through both empirical and theoretical studies. By examining the top-ranked semantics of different components in CFG, we show that texture information is primarily encoded in the difference between the conditional and unconditional logits, whereas both logits share previous-token repetition as their leading semantics. This strong repetition semantics obscures the desired texture information, causing greedy decoding from the conditional logits alone to produce nearly pure-color images. Through a training-dynamics analysis of shallow transformers, we prove that this shared repetition semantics does not arise from limitations or failures of pretraining, but instead originates from the texture sparsity of images. We further show that CFG improves generation quality by rectifying this repetition bias during inference. Motivated by the shared semantics between conditional and unconditional logits, we propose Attention Weight Reuse (AttnReuse), which reuses intermediate attention computations from the conditional-logit forward pass to accelerate the unconditional-logit computation. AttnReuse reduces about $25\%$ of attention computation with little performance degradation across different models.

</details>


### [153] [RAGA: Refreshable Animatable Gaussian Avatars from a Single Photo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29026)
*Sadia Mubashshira,John Quarles,Kevin Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RAGA 提出一种由单张图片在约 1 秒内构建、并可随新图更新而无需重训或梯度优化的全身 Gaussian 化身，通过 UV 空间可见性与角度加权累积融合并复用同一解码器，在 163 段视频上优于 LHM/LHM++/IDOL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图生成的化身一旦建成就无法改变：照片未覆盖的区域（如衣服背面）只能靠幻觉补全并永久固定。作者希望化身能随同一人物后续新图像的出现而被"刷新"，且不引入重训练或梯度优化开销。

Method: 将每张新观测映射到规范 UV 空间，按可见性与视角角度加权累积为运行和（running sums）；随后用与单图生成完全相同的解码器对融合后的 UV 图重新解码，从而复用融合证据。解码器仅在单图数据上训练。

Result: 在 5 个数据集共 163 段视频上，单图构建的 RAGA 优于 LHM、LHM++ 与 IDOL，其中三个数据集未参与训练；用后续帧刷新总体提升 0.49 dB PSNR，对与照片成角 ≥135° 的视角提升 0.68 dB，SSIM 与 LPIPS 亦改善。同等 16 张图条件下 LHM++ 提升 0.15 dB，RAGA 提升 0.48 dB。创建耗时 1.08 s（含人体拟合与抠图），动画 48.1 ms/帧（含姿态估计），在笔记本 RTX 3080 上测得。

Conclusion: 可刷新的 Gaussian 化身可行且高效：单图质量已达领先水平，后续观测能以极低成本增量融入，尤其弥补大角度盲区，为"可迭代进化"的化身范式提供实证。

Abstract: Avatars created from a single photo are fixed once made: regions the photo never showed, such as the back of a garment, are hallucinated and remain that way. We present Refreshable Animatable Gaussian Avatars (RAGA), a full-body Gaussian avatar that is created from a single image in about one second and can be refreshed whenever new images of the same person become available, without retraining or gradient-based optimization. RAGA maps each new observation into canonical UV space, accumulates it as visibility- and angle-weighted running sums, and re-decodes the fused map with the same decoder used for the photo. Although this decoder is trained only on single images, it makes use of the fused evidence. On 163 clips from five datasets, RAGA created from one photo outperforms LHM, LHM++ and IDOL, including on three datasets never used in training. Refreshing from later frames adds 0.49 dB PSNR overall and 0.68 dB on views turned at least 135° from the photo, where SSIM and LPIPS also improve. Given the same 16 images on all clips, LHM++ gains 0.15 dB, against 0.48 dB for RAGA. Creating an avatar takes 1.08 s and animating it 48.1 ms per frame on a laptop RTX 3080 GPU, with body fitting and matting included in creation and pose estimation included in animation. Code will be released on GitHub.

</details>


### [154] [T2ISpec: Benchmarking Speculative Decoding for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29029)
*Yifan Zhang,Shatong Zhu,Hui Shen,Yunta Hsieh,Jianing Ma,Muxin Fu,Xin Wang,Ping Zhang,Qian Chen,Chaofan Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出T2ISpec基准（1400条提示，覆盖7个渲染主题、2个难度级别），系统评测T2I投机解码，揭示三项关键发现，并提出即插即用方法TopicRoute。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2I生成器推理慢，投机解码可降本，但其在T2I提示各类渲染需求下的行为缺乏系统理解，且现有评测聚合指标掩盖了主题间差异。

Method: 构建T2ISpec基准，从延迟、目标模型实际工作量、图像质量三维度评测自回归与扩散/流匹配加速器；发现聚合结果掩盖主题差异、对齐/偏好分与提示约束满足度可能不一致、目标工作量大幅减少不一定带来显著延迟收益；据此提出TopicRoute，结合提示路由与投机、缓存控制，无需重训练生成器。

Result: 在六组模型—方法组合上，TopicRoute相对原生方法取得1.0308倍几何平均加速；相同设置下加速效果可迁移至外部提示；质量增益与损失随配置而异。

Conclusion: T2I投机解码需按主题细粒度评测；TopicRoute以路由+投机+缓存控制的即插即用方式获得稳定但幅度有限的加速，质量影响具配置依赖性。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generators produce detailed images but remain slow at inference. Speculative decoding can reduce this cost, yet its behavior across the rendering requirements of T2I prompts remains insufficiently understood. We introduce T2ISpec, a benchmark of 1,400 prompts balanced across seven rendering topics and two difficulty levels. It evaluates autoregressive and diffusion/flow accelerators by latency, work performed by the target model, and image quality. Our study reveals three key findings: (1) aggregate results hide large differences between topics, (2) alignment and preference scores can disagree with prompt constraint satisfaction, and (3) large reductions in target work can yield limited latency gains. Motivated by these findings, we propose TopicRoute, a plugin that combines prompt routing with speculative and cache controls without retraining the generator. Across six model--method pairs, it achieves a geometric mean speedup of 1.0308x over the native methods, and the speedup carries over to external prompts with the same settings, while quality gains and losses vary across configurations.

</details>


### [155] [Recurrent Point Transformer via Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29059)
*Wentao Zhou,Xuweiyi Chen,Charles Saunders,Aruni RoyChowdhury,Zezhou Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PoinTTT将任意范围点云分割建模为分块循环序列处理，在PTv3编码器与解码器中加入残差大块测试时训练（LaCT）层，以紧凑快速权重作为循环状态，实现固定块大小训练、任意空间/时间范围推理且激活内存有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实点云天然无界（3D扫描随传感器移动增长、LiDAR覆盖大片林区、4D捕获可跨数千帧），但多数点云编码器面向固定尺寸输入，推理时只能裁剪或分块，导致丢失长程上下文并需额外拼接后处理。

Method: 以Point Transformer V3为骨干，在编码器与解码器中均插入残差LaCT层；每层维护紧凑快速权重作为循环状态，当前块token更新快速权重，更新后的权重用于产生该块特征并传递给下一块。训练用固定大小块，推理按块循环，激活内存受块大小约束。

Result: ScanNet与nuScenes试点显示循环TTT优于PTv3基线；FOR-instanceV2上取得87.9 mIoU的林地语义分割SOTA且内存近乎恒定；HOI4D上在线全序列循环推理跨片段保持状态，达42.6 mIoU，匹配的全序列Mamba对照约39.9。

Conclusion: 测试时训练可作为可扩展空间与时间点云理解的实用基础原语。

Abstract: Real-world point clouds are not naturally bounded: 3D scans grow as sensors move through rooms, LiDAR surveys extend across forest plots, and 4D captures can span thousands of frames. Yet most point-cloud encoders are designed for fixed-size inputs, forcing inference-time cropping or tiling that loses long-range context and requires ad-hoc output stitching. We formulate arbitrary-extent point-cloud segmentation as recurrent sequence-of-chunks processing and introduce PoinTTT, a recurrent point-cloud encoder built on Point Transformer V3 (PTv3). PoinTTT augments PTv3 with residual Large Chunk Test-Time Training (LaCT) layers in both the encoder and decoder. Each LaCT layer maintains compact fast weights as recurrent state: tokens from each chunk update the fast weights, which are then applied to produce chunk features and passed to the next chunk. This design allows PoinTTT to train on fixed-size chunks while processing scenes of arbitrary spatial or temporal extent, with activation memory bounded by the chunk size. A pilot study on ScanNet and nuScenes shows that recurrent TTT improves segmentation over PTv3 baselines. On FOR-instanceV2, PoinTTT achieves state-of-the-art forest semantic segmentation of 87.9 mIoU with nearly constant memory. On HOI4D, online full-sequence recurrent inference carries state across clips rather than resetting at benchmark boundaries, reaching 42.6 mIoU; a matched full-sequence Mamba control reaches approximately 39.9. These results suggest that test-time training is a practical primitive for scalable spatial and temporal point-cloud understanding.

</details>


### [156] [Single-Transformer Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29064)
*Hao-Yu Hsu,Shenlong Wang,Alexander G. Schwing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出UNIFIED模型，将潜扩散中的编码器、生成器、解码器三者合并到单一全共享扩散Transformer主干中，通过统一的序列映射与模式token区分任务，单阶段端到端训练，在ImageNet-1K与文生图任务上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜生成模型依赖编码器、生成器、解码器三个独立网络；UNITE已合并前两者但解码器仍独立。作者探究能否将最后一个网络也折叠进来，实现单一网络同时完成编码、生成与解码。

Method: 简单共享主干解码效果不佳。核心做法是将三种模式统一建模为共享同一主干的位置对齐序列映射，仅通过模式token与时间步加以区分。模型从零开始单阶段训练，仅使用重建损失与流匹配损失，不使用对抗损失，也不依赖预训练编码器（如DINO）。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K 256×256、参数对齐设置下，UNIFIED在约60M、115M、215M三个规模上gFID均优于UNITE；215M参数版本达到2.08，为250M参数以下模型中最低。文生图方面，UNIFIED-B（218M）在512×512下GenEval得分0.87，超过约十亿参数的单阶段像素扩散模型。

Conclusion: 潜扩散的完全统一不仅可行而且有益，单一扩散Transformer可同时承担编码、生成与解码功能，并在小参数规模下取得更强性能。

Abstract: Latent generative models typically rely on three separate networks: an encoder that tokenizes pixels, a generator that models the latent distribution, and a decoder that maps latents back to pixels. UNITE recently merged the first two into a single generative encoder trained in one stage, but its decoder remains a separate network. We ask whether this last network can be folded in too, so that one diffusion transformer encodes, generates, and decodes. Simply letting the shared backbone decode does not help. What makes it work is to cast all three modes as position-aligned sequence maps over one fully shared backbone, distinguished only by a mode token and the timestep. The resulting model, UNIFIED, is simple: one network trained from scratch in a single stage with only reconstruction and flow-matching losses, without adversarial losses or pretrained encoders (e.g., DINO). On ImageNet-1K 256x256 at matched parameters, UNIFIED improves gFID over UNITE at three sizes (~60M, ~115M, and ~215M) and reaches 2.08 with 215M parameters, the lowest among compared models under 250M parameters. On text-to-image generation, UNIFIED-B (218M) reaches a GenEval score of 0.87 at 512x512, exceeding single-stage pixel diffusion models with around one billion parameters. Full unification of latent diffusion is thus not merely feasible but beneficial.

</details>


### [157] [Projecting Beauty: Transferring Image Aesthetic Analysis onto 3D Heritage and Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29071)
*Sophia Kovalenko,Alexander M. Rusnak,Vittoria Meroni,Yasmine Kroknes-Gomez,Frédéric Kaplan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文考察2D图像美学评估跨越到3D点云时的可迁移性，比较三种免人工标注的美学伪标签路线，发现监督式路线迁移效果差，而"文本查询+去除类别文本方向"的视觉-语言路线显著更一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑与街道美学判断服务于建筑设计、城市规划与遗产保护，但如今越来越多依赖扫描3D模型而非实体材料；然而自动美学评估仍主要局限于2D照片。作者因此追问：图像美学评估跨越这一维度鸿沟后，还能保留多少有效性，并希望规避昂贵且有时存在伦理争议的大规模人工标注。

Method: 在遗产与城市点云上进行逐点稠密美学描述，测试三条路线：(1) 将图像美学模型（NIMA、SAAN）的标量分数"提升"到几何上；(2) 在冻结视觉骨干（DINOv3、TIPS、SigLIP2、PE、RADSeg）上训练AVA回归头，或3D原生变体——在前馈AVA重建上用通用点编码器探测；(3) 以自然语言美学标签查询提升后的视觉-语言特征，取相似度作为分数。并对点特征做类别-文本方向投影剔除。

Result: 两条监督路线迁移效果差：AVA域内拟合的回归头给出的建筑类别剖面彼此不一致、也不跟踪其他信号；3D原生探测因重建几何质量差而接近随机水平。文本查询明显更连贯：剔除类别-文本方向后，建筑部件剖面的平均跨模型一致性由+0.035升至+0.352，逐点由+0.113升至+0.293；通过语义合理性检查的四个骨干相关为0.31–0.73，未通过者仍为离群点。

Conclusion: 模型间一致性并不等于与人类美学感知一致，作者为此给出部件感知的评分协议，以厘清人机评分分歧之处，并提供可复现的迁移协议、各伪标签路线信号强弱的说明，以及仅作为假设而非真值的稠密美学伪标签。

Abstract: Judgments about the beauty of buildings and streets inform architectural design, urban planning, and heritage conservation, yet increasingly rely on scanned 3D models over material fabric. Automatic aesthetic assessment remains largely confined to 2D photographs. We ask what survives when image aesthetic assessment crosses this dimensional gap to produce dense, per-point aesthetic descriptions of heritage and urban point clouds. We examine three routes that sidestep costly, and sometimes ethically dubious, human labeling at scale: (1) lifting scalar scores from image aesthetic models (NIMA, SAAN) onto geometry; (2) training Aesthetic Visual Analysis (AVA) regression heads on frozen vision backbones (DINOv3, TIPS, SigLIP2, PE, RADSeg) or, in a 3D-native variant, a generalist point encoder on feed-forward AVA reconstructions; and (3) querying lifted vision-language features with a natural-language aesthetic label, taking similarity as a score. The two supervised routes transfer poorly: heads fitting AVA in-domain produce architectural class profiles that neither reproduce one another nor track other signals, while the 3D-native probe stays near chance due to reconstruction geometry quality. Text querying is markedly more coherent. Projecting out the class-text direction from point features raises mean cross-model agreement on architectural part profiles from +0.035 to +0.352, and from +0.113 to +0.293 per point; the four backbones passing a semantic sanity check correlate at 0.31-0.73, while the failing one remains an outlier. Since model agreement is not agreement with human aesthetic perception, we detail a part-aware rating protocol to clarify where human and automatic ratings disagree. We provide a reproducible transfer protocol, an account of which pseudo-labeling routes carry signal, and dense aesthetic pseudo-labels meant as hypotheses rather than ground truth.

</details>


### [158] [TesseraFlow: Scalable Video Diffusion for Dynamic Medical Imaging Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29099)
*Atakan Topcu,Mohammadhossein Momeni,Alfredo De Goyeneche,Michael Lustig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TesseraFlow——一种复值像素空间流先验，配合ReTile采样器，用于加速医学图像重建，实现约每秒一帧256x256的重建，比patch与latent视频先验快20-367倍，同时在心脏、脑、腹部MRI与肺部CT上匹配或超过最佳生成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像重建中生成式先验效果好，但现有方案效率低：视频先验每帧需数十秒至数分钟；latent先验继承自编码器误差；整帧像素空间先验内存随帧尺寸增长；patch先验慢，原因有二——(1) 每步采样都以全分辨率处理整帧，而早期步骤仅需低频；(2) 采样器每步需多次网络评估（重叠窗口、多轮重加噪或对先验反向传播）。

Method: 提出TesseraFlow：复值像素空间流先验。全局编码器每采样步仅读取每帧的低分辨率视图一次；单一浅层解码器在早期步骤作用于该低分辨率视图，后期步骤作用于全分辨率时空tile，从而无网络在全分辨率下处理整帧。另提出ReTile采样器应对第二个瓶颈：每采样步采用随机网格平移配合重加噪，以随机梯度替代重叠tile计算，使每步仅需单次网络前向。

Result: ReTile单独可将现有全局上下文patch先验加速6-9倍，所提先验再加3-5倍；TesseraFlow约每秒重建一帧256x256，比patch与latent视频先验快20-367倍；在心脏、脑、腹部MRI及肺部CT上匹配或超过最佳生成基线，且GPU时间仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: 通过低分辨率全局编码+分阶段浅层解码的网络设计，以及基于随机网格平移与重加噪的单次前向采样器，TesseraFlow在保持生成质量的同时大幅降低计算成本，为视频/像素空间生成先验在医学图像重建中的实用化提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Generative priors are state-of-the-art for modeling medical images in image reconstruction tasks, but reconstruction with video priors takes tens of seconds to minutes per frame. Latent priors inherit the errors of their autoencoder, the memory of pixel-space whole-frame priors grows with the frame size, and pixel-space patch priors are slow for two reasons. First, their deep networks process every frame at full resolution at every sampling step, even though early steps resolve only low frequencies. Second, their samplers spend many network evaluations per sampling step on overlapping windows, multiple re-noising rounds, or backpropagation through the prior. We introduce TesseraFlow, a complex-valued pixel-space flow prior that reduces the first cost. A global encoder reads a low-resolution view of every frame once per sampling step. A single shallow decoder operates on this view during early steps and on full-resolution space-time tiles during later steps, so no network processes a whole frame at full resolution. Our sampler, ReTile, addresses the second reason. We use randomized grid shifting at every sampling step along with re-noising, which acts as a stochastic gradient instead of computing overlapping tiles, so a single network pass per sampling step suffices. On its own, ReTile speeds up an existing global-context patch prior by 6-9x, and our prior adds a further 3-5x speedup. TesseraFlow reconstructs about one 256x256 frame per second, 20-367x faster than patch and latent video priors, and can match or exceed the best generative baseline on cardiac, brain, and abdominal MRI and lung CT, at a fraction of its GPU time.

</details>


### [159] [Bridging Blind Spots With Words: Decentralized Multi-View Spatial Reasoning via Language-Constrained Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29112)
*Soumyabrata Chaudhuri,Joydeep Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文形式化去中心化多视角空间推理问题：N个智能体各观察不同视角，通过预算受限的自然语言消息交换，协作使听者回答空间查询；并提出多视角空间基准，发现语言+预算约束的损失远小于既往双智能体文献所言，但仍存在显著提升空间，且带信用分配的强化学习可弥合与集中式基线的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM作为具身机器人、AR/VR与多用户系统的协作伙伴时，空间推理常分布于仅具部分环境可见性的多个智能体之间，需要空间推理的整合能力。既有语言中介的空间整合研究仅限于简化的双智能体场景，缺乏对通用多智能体、去中心化设定的刻画与评测。

Method: 1）形式化去中心化多视角空间推理问题：N个智能体观察不同视角，交换预算受限的自然语言消息，由听者完成空间查询；2）构建新的多视角空间基准，专门隔离出需要非平凡多视角整合的查询；3）开展大规模实证研究评估语言与预算约束的影响；4）采用带信用分配的强化学习方法增强去中心化模型。

Result: 实证表明，将去中心化空间推理限制为预算受限的自然语言所带来的性能损失，远小于先前双智能体文献所暗示的程度；但当前集中式性能与oracle best-of-K上界之间仍存在显著差距，说明多智能体空间推理仍有较大提升空间；采用带信用分配的强化学习可显著提升去中心化模型，并有效弥合与集中式基线的差距。

Conclusion: 去中心化多视角空间推理是一个可行且值得深入研究的问题：语言+预算约束的代价相对温和，但性能上限仍有较大空间；信用分配强化学习是提升去中心化多智能体空间推理的有效手段。

Abstract: As multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) get increasingly used as collaborative partners across embodied robotics, AR/VR, and multi-user systems, spatial reasoning is now often distributed across agents with partial environment visibility, necessitating spatial reasoning integration. Prior work in language-mediated spatial integration is however strictly limited to simplified two-agent scenarios. To address this gap, we first formalize the general problem of decentralized multi-view spatial reasoning, where N agents observe distinct viewpoints, exchange budget-constrained natural language messages, and collaborate to enable a listener to answer spatial queries. We then introduce a novel multi-view spatial benchmark to isolate queries that demand non-trivial multi-view integration. Through an extensive empirical study, we discover that the penalty of restricting decentralized spatial reasoning to natural language with limited budget is far smaller than suggested by prior two-agent literature. Nevertheless, we observe that substantial headroom still remains for multi-agent spatial reasoning in this setting, as evidenced by a significant gap between current centralized performance and an oracle best-of-K ceiling. Moreover, we show that reinforcement learning with credit assignment substantially bolsters the decentralized model, effectively closing the gap with the centralized baseline.

</details>


### [160] [GAG-Net: Graph-Based Anatomical Guidance Network for Pharyngeal Area Segmentation in VFSS Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29118)
*JunYeong Park,Myung Woo Park,Byung Chan Lee,Hyung Jin Chang,Sang-Il Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将VFSS图像中的静息咽面积(PA_rest)自动分割定义为临床测量任务，构建首个专家标注数据集(2255帧/137例)，提出融合可变形卷积与图解剖引导的GAG-Net，实现低误差的自动化测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诊断肌少症性吞咽障碍通常需CT/MRI等昂贵补充影像评估肌肉萎缩，加重老年患者负担；静息咽面积PA_rest可从VFSS直接测得，是有望替代的影像生物标志物，但重复手工测量限制其推广，且软组织边界模糊与临床规则化勾画标准使数据驱动模型难以精确捕捉。

Method: 将隐式临床测量显式化为由康复医学专家参与制定的关键点规范与标注协议；据此构建首个数据集：137例患者、2255帧单中心VFSS图像，含PA_rest轮廓与7个关键点并经资深专家核验；提出GAG-Net，结合可变形卷积应对不规则咽部边界，并引入图解剖引导模块建模勾画规则所规定的关键点间几何依赖。

Result: 在患者级留出测试集(237帧、67例)上，GAG-Net面积测量平均绝对百分比误差8.17%、患者级ICC 0.97、Dice 0.8862，均为所评估方法中最优；相较两阶段JOINED模型参数减少67.2%、训练显存降低74%。

Conclusion: 整合临床医生定义的解剖知识可实现仅凭VFSS图像的自动化PA_rest测量，为肌少症性吞咽障碍的定量评估提供无需补充影像的途径。

Abstract: Diagnosing sarcopenic dysphagia typically requires costly supplementary imaging such as CT or MRI to assess muscle atrophy, burdening elderly patients. The pharyngeal area at rest (PA_rest), measurable directly from videofluoroscopic swallowing study (VFSS) images, is a promising biomarker that could eliminate this dependency, but repeated manual measurement has limited its adoption. PA_rest segmentation is further complicated by indistinct soft-tissue boundaries and clinical rule-based delineation criteria that data-driven models capture only imprecisely. We formulate automated PA_rest segmentation as a clinically defined measurement task, translating an implicit clinical measurement into an explicit landmark specification and annotation protocol developed with a rehabilitation medicine specialist. Applying this protocol, we constructed, to our knowledge, the first dataset for this task: 2,255 single-center VFSS frames from 137 patients, with PA_rest contours and seven landmarks verified by a senior specialist. We then introduce GAG-Net, an automated rest-frame measurement pipeline combining deformable convolutions, for irregular pharyngeal boundaries, with a graph-based anatomical guidance module that models the geometric dependencies among landmarks prescribed by the delineation rules. On a patient-level held-out test set (237 frames, 67 patients), GAG-Net achieved the lowest area measurement error (mean absolute percentage error: 8.17% against specialist-verified references; patient-level ICC 0.97) and the highest Dice score (0.8862) among all evaluated methods, with 67.2% fewer parameters and 74% less training memory than the two-stage JOINED model. Integrating clinician-defined anatomical knowledge may enable automated PA_rest measurement from VFSS images alone, supporting quantitative assessment of sarcopenic dysphagia without supplementary imaging.

</details>


### [161] [SilentWAM: Planning in the Masked State of a Diffusion World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29125)
*Ziyu Zhao,Casey Sun,Zeyu Wang,Shicong Ma,Emily T Zhang,Cheng Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 无需解码未来帧，直接读取掩码未来token的隐藏状态做规划，单相机、单次前向即达92.0 PDMS（v1）与89.3 EPDMS（v2），延迟60.3 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有world-action模型依赖未来预测，但在测试时直接获取未来表征需付出高昂代价：先生成未来帧再规划会引入额外前向计算，而未来协同训练方法在推理时又缺少显式未来预测。

Method: 预训练MMaDA-Parallel-M，在驾驶视频上用帧级掩码世界建模（预测被掩码的未来帧后缀，并显式包含全部未来帧被掩码的输入）；学习到的规划查询 attends 这些掩码位置与观测上下文，连续头在同一次前向中预测轨迹，无需解码未来帧。

Result: 单前视相机下，NAVSIM v1达92.0 PDMS，NAVSIM v2达89.3 EPDMS，端到端延迟60.3 ms。

Conclusion: 通过直接读取掩码未来token的隐藏状态而非生成未来帧，可在单次前向中兼顾未来预测优势与推理效率，取得高精度规划性能与低延迟。

Abstract: World-action models learn from future prediction, but accessing their future representations directly at test time can require costly frame generation. Methods that generate future frames before planning incur additional forward passes, while future co-training methods omit explicit future predictions at inference. We introduce \method, a planner that reads the hidden states of masked future tokens without decoding future frames. We pretrain MMaDA-Parallel-M on driving video using frame-level masked world modeling, which predicts a masked suffix of future frames and explicitly includes inputs with all future frames masked. Learned planning queries attend to these masked positions and the observed context, and a continuous head predicts the trajectory in the same forward pass. With a single front camera, \method reaches 92.0 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 89.3 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, with an end-to-end latency of 60.3\,ms.

</details>


### [162] [Supply the Global, Supervise the Local: Combining Representation Conditioning and Alignment in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29138)
*Chaojie Ji,Sergio Quiroga-Sandoval,Ruxin Wang,Zheju Zheng,Yankai Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 预训练图像表征在DiT中可同时作为对齐目标(REPA)和条件输入；实验证明二者在图像级部分重叠时并非冲突而是协同，据此提出GC-REPA（供给全局、监督局部），大幅加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REPA将预训练表征作为隐状态对齐目标，而同一表征也可作为描述待生成图像的条件输入。二者结合会同时提供输入与目标，但其是否冲突未知，本文旨在检验这种重叠的作用。

Method: 将每个图像块表征拆为图像级(全局)部分（全图共享）与图内(局部)部分（块相对图像级部分的差异）；仅用一个携带图像级部分的token作为条件。这样两角色仅在图像级部分重叠（潜在冲突区），而图内部分仍只受REPA监督（可观察token是否助益REPA）。据此实现GC-REPA：REPA加该token作为额外输入，token不含超出REPA目标的信息，采样时从单独训练的token先验中抽取。

Result: token与REPA联合教会图像级与图内两部分，且效果远超二者单独教学之和，说明是协同而非冲突；图内部分是REPA提升FID的主要来源。在ImageNet 256×256上，SiT-XL/2+GC-REPA在200K与100K迭代即达到REPA(4M)与SiT-XL/2(7M)的FID。

Conclusion: 给出结合REPA与图像级条件的设计准则“供给全局、监督局部”，即GC-REPA；其显著加速收敛（分别少20×与70×迭代）。

Abstract: A pretrained image representation helps train a diffusion transformer in several roles, including as an alignment target, which the model's hidden states learn to match, as in representation alignment (REPA), and as a condition, an extra input describing the image to be generated. Combined, they would give the model an input and a target, but whether they conflict is unknown. To test this, we let the condition carry only part of REPA's target: we split the representation of each image patch into an image-level (global) part, shared by all patches of the image, and a within-image (local) part, the patch's difference from the image-level part, and use one token carrying the image-level part as the condition. Then, the two roles overlap on the image-level part, where they could conflict, while only REPA supervises the within-image part, where we can see whether the token helps REPA.
  We find that the token and REPA together teach both the image-level part and the within-image part, the main source of REPA's FID improvement, far more than the sum of what each teaches alone, so they cooperate rather than conflict. These findings give one design rule for combining REPA with an image-level condition, supply the global, supervise the local, which we implement as Global-Condition REPA (GC-REPA): REPA with the token as an extra input; the token carries no information beyond REPA's target and is drawn at sampling from a separately trained token prior. GC-REPA greatly accelerates convergence: on ImageNet 256*256, SiT-XL/2 + GC-REPA reaches REPA's 4M- and SiT-XL/2's 7M-iteration FIDs within 200K and 100K iterations (20 and 70 fewer).

</details>


### [163] [RAVEC: Reward-Aligned Visual Evidence Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29142)
*Junjie Yang,Rui Qian,Xutao Mao,Yuhe Wu,Mingda Zhang,Jiaxuan Luo,Yang Tan,Qiang Huang,Zhisheng Chen,Yanfeng Li,Dawen Tian,Changmiao Wang,Tao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RAVEC通过局部与跨问题奖励支持筛选视觉修正方向，并在响应内联合分配其强度，作为符号视觉损失增强GRPO；在十个多模态基准上以3B/7B模型取得最高平均准确率，较GRPO分别提升2.67与1.39个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带可验证奖励的强化学习提升多模态推理，但响应级反馈难定位应纠正的中间决策；视觉干预虽显示模型对图像内容敏感，但敏感性本身无法保证奖励对齐或跨问题支持。

Method: 提出RAVEC：(1) PVEC——用EMA教师在同一采样响应前缀下，比较原图与抑制标注证据区域图像的预测，得到带符号修正方向；(2) CRA——先以局部奖励对齐及来自其他问题（排除源问题全部响应）的奖励支持筛选方向，再求解响应级预算分配，将额外代价分配给能局部降低预测熵的方向；(3) 所得符号视觉损失在训练中增强GRPO，推理仅用原图与问题。

Result: 在十个多模态基准上，RAVEC在3B与7B Qwen2.5-VL-Instruct上平均准确率均为对比方法最高，较GRPO分别提升2.67与1.39个百分点；组件消融验证了证据对齐、信用资格筛选与风险感知分配的组合有效性。

Conclusion: 以奖励对齐的视觉证据修正方向构建符号视觉损失，可在不改变推理输入的前提下有效增强多模态GRPO训练，且各组件设计相互支撑。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves multimodal reasoning, but response-level feedback provides limited guidance on which intermediate decisions to correct. Visual interventions reveal sensitivity to image content, yet this sensitivity alone does not establish reward alignment or support across questions. We introduce RAVEC (Reward-Aligned Visual Evidence Correction), which selects visual corrections using local and cross-question reward support and jointly determines their strengths within each response. Paired Visual Evidence Contrast (PVEC) compares predictions from an exponential moving average teacher at the same sampled response prefix under the original image and a version with its annotated evidence region suppressed, yielding signed correction directions. Credit-Risk Allocation (CRA) screens these directions using local reward alignment and reward support from other questions, excluding all responses to the source question. It then solves a response-level budgeted allocation problem, assigning additional cost to directions that locally reduce predictive entropy. The resulting signed visual loss augments Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) during training. Inference uses only the original image and question. Across ten multimodal benchmarks, RAVEC achieves the highest average accuracy among the compared methods with both 3B and 7B Qwen2.5-VL-Instruct models, outperforming GRPO by 2.67 and 1.39 percentage points, respectively. Component ablations support the combined design of evidence alignment, credit qualification, and risk-aware allocation. The project is available at https://yangjj007.github.io/ravec/.

</details>


### [164] [RVLM: Fewer Tokens, Better Resolution Scaling, Fine-Grained Spatial Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29146)
*Savya Khosla,Chris Dongjoo Kim,Alex Schwing,Derek Hoiem,Christopher Clark,Sangho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RVLM用免训练的Felzenszwalb-Huttenlocher分割把图像划分为区域，将ViT patch按所属区域池化为可变长视觉token序列，在减少21.5%视觉token的同时保持或提升多任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM以固定空间网格切分图像，视觉token数量随像素面积增长而非随场景内容变化，导致高分辨率下token冗余严重。

Method: 按视觉外观对相邻patch分组，使token表示连贯图像区域而非固定窗口。分析发现区域需满足三点：覆盖全图、端到端训练中保持空间连通、保留小文字等细结构，而语义分组收益甚微。据此RVLM用免训练的Felzenszwalb-Huttenlocher分割划分图像，将每个ViT patch指派到区域，区域内池化为可变长token序列；保留patch到区域的指派以支持粗到细解码实现空间精确预测。不增加模型参数。

Result: 在VQA、pointing、计数、分割、深度估计等任务上匹配或超越同等训练的patch基线，视觉token少21.5%；高分辨率下优势扩大至5.3点且token减少51.8%；空间要求高的任务增益最大（此前token压缩方法在此退化最严重）；推理吞吐最高提升35.9%；在未见过的病理与卫星图像及无视频训练的视频基准上以2-3倍更少token达到与基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 区域可作为patch式视觉表征的可扩展替代方案，在保持接地预测所需空间精度的同时显著降低token开销。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) tokenize images using fixed spatial grids, so visual token count grows with pixel area rather than scene content. We propose to group neighboring patches based on their visual appearance, allowing tokens to represent coherent image regions rather than fixed spatial windows. We introduce RVLM, a VLM trained to use regions rather than patches as its visual units. Analyzing region construction for general-purpose VLMs, we find that regions must cover the full image, stay spatially connected during end-to-end training, and preserve fine structure such as small text, while semantic grouping adds little. RVLM therefore partitions images using training-free Felzenszwalb-Huttenlocher segmentation, assigns each ViT patch to a region, and pools patches within each region into a variable-length sequence of visual tokens. The patch-to-region assignment is retained to support spatially precise prediction through coarse-to-fine decoding. Across tasks spanning VQA, pointing, counting, segmentation, and depth estimation, RVLM matches or improves example-weighted task performance while using 21.5% fewer visual tokens than an identically trained patch baseline. The advantage grows with image resolution, reaching a 5.3-point gain while using 51.8% fewer visual tokens at the highest-resolution setting. The gains are largest on spatially demanding tasks, where prior token-reduction methods degrade most sharply. RVLM adds no model parameters and improves inference throughput by up to 35.9%. On unseen pathology and satellite images and on video benchmarks without any video training, RVLM performs on par with the patch baseline while using 2-3$\times$ fewer visual tokens. These results establish regions as a scalable alternative to patch-based visual representations while preserving the spatial precision required for grounded prediction.

</details>


### [165] [ArborNet: Volume- and skeleton-aware completion of tree quantitative structure models from incomplete point clouds using anchor-conditioned graph learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29148)
*Ge Jiang,Tiangang Yin,Wei Yao,Wai Yeung Yan,Qi Chen,Jinhong Wang,Qihao Weng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ArborNet，在QSM（圆柱图）域补全树木结构，显式保持枝干拓扑、半径与材积，实现体积与骨架感知的补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单站或稀疏TLS采样存在遮挡，导致枝干观测不完整、材积估计有偏；现有点云补全方法只恢复看似合理的几何，不能显式保持枝干拓扑、圆柱半径和木质材积。

Method: 将每棵树表示为TreeQSM圆柱图（编码圆柱几何、枝级、父子拓扑、枝身份）；用多关系图编码器在父、子、枝内连接上传播信息；锚点条件解码器通过末端枝延伸与新枝生成来预测缺失圆柱；训练损失含几何、存在性、枝、半径、轴向平滑与体积损失；推理时在预测的逐级缺失体积预算下筛选生成圆柱。

Result: 在DART模拟TLS树与香港城市TLS树上评估（单站QSM为部分输入、多站QSM为参考）：预算约束推理下，总体积相对误差均值0.081%、逐级体积误差均值0.676%，骨架几何误差低于对照点云补全基线；消融显示联合体积监督对体积控制尤为关键。

Conclusion: QSM域补全可恢复显式枝干结构并约束几何与体积；但所报误差衡量的是与参考QSM的一致性，而非对破坏性实测材积的精度。

Abstract: Terrestrial laser scanning (TLS) enables 3D reconstruction of trees and quantitative structure models (QSMs) for estimating woody volume and structural traits. However, occlusion in single-station or sparsely sampled TLS causes incomplete branch observations and biased volume estimates. Existing point-cloud completion methods recover plausible geometry but do not explicitly preserve branch topology, cylinder radii, or woody volume. We propose ArborNet, a volume- and skeleton-aware framework for completing tree structures in the QSM domain. Each tree is represented as a TreeQSM cylinder graph encoding cylinder geometry, branch level, parent-child topology, and branch identity. A multi-relation graph encoder propagates information across parent, child, and intra-branch connections. An anchor-conditioned decoder generates missing cylinders through terminal-branch continuation and new-branch generation. Geometry, existence, branch, radius, axis-smoothness, and volume losses encourage structural consistency. During inference, generated cylinders are selected under predicted level-wise missing-volume budgets. We evaluate ArborNet on DART-simulated TLS trees and urban TLS trees from Hong Kong, using single-station QSMs as partial inputs and multi-station QSMs as references. Under budget-constrained inference, ArborNet achieves mean total-volume relative error of 0.081% and mean level-wise volume error of 0.676% against reference QSMs, while reducing skeleton geometry error relative to evaluated point-cloud completion baselines. These errors quantify agreement with reference QSMs, not accuracy against destructively measured woody volume. Ablation results show that joint volume supervision is particularly important for volume control. These results demonstrate the potential of QSM-domain completion to recover explicit woody structure while constraining geometry and volume.

</details>


### [166] [Can an Agent Trust Its Own Zoom? Cross-Magnification Provenance Verification for Whole-Slide Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29150)
*Yan Wang,Yiran Song,Zizhuo Gong,Jianxiang Qi,Zhongxing Xu,Lingfei Qian,Minquan Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨放大倍率来源验证任务CMPV，并构建PyraFault基准（306万坐标监督样本）与ZoomTrace验证器，在测试集上取得0.969 AUPRC、0.987 AUROC，显著优于Efficient LoFTR，可降低视觉智能体受污染观测与静默失败的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉智能体在全切片图像中导航时，所获放大观测可能看似合理却并非来自其声称位置，尤其在跨放大倍率及组织相似区域；需要判断视觉证据是否支持所声称的空间关联，并在证据不足时弃权。

Method: 形式化跨放大倍率来源验证（CMPV）；构建PyraFault基准，含5个TCGA队列、2200例、306万坐标监督样本，覆盖上下文内替换、源缺失故障与几何扰动；提出与声明无关的验证器ZoomTrace，先估计观测来源再评估其与声称位置的一致性，融合物理尺度对齐、视觉检索、几何精化，并利用空间不相交竞争匹配进行可靠性估计。

Result: 在503,320个测试样本上，ZoomTrace达0.969 AUPRC与0.987 AUROC，对照Efficient LoFTR为0.789与0.903；进一步评估揭示可靠性估计的权衡，以及视觉语言模型、跨数据集检索与校准方面的局限。

Conclusion: 将ZoomTrace嵌入视觉智能体工具路径可减少对受污染观测的暴露与静默失败，下游精度提升取决于智能体类型与验证阈值。

Abstract: Visual agents navigate whole-slide images through magnified observations associated with claimed locations. However, a visually plausible observation does not necessarily originate from its claimed location, particularly across magnifications and in regions with similar tissue structures. We formulate cross-magnification provenance verification (CMPV) to evaluate whether visual evidence supports a claimed spatial association, recover the source when possible, and abstain when evidence is insufficient. We introduce PyraFault, a benchmark of 3.06 million coordinate-supervised examples from 2,200 cases across five TCGA cohorts, covering within-context substitutions, absent-source faults, and geometric perturbations. We further propose ZoomTrace, a claim-independent verifier that estimates the observation's source before evaluating its agreement with the claimed location. It combines physical-scale alignment, visual retrieval, geometric refinement, and reliability estimation using spatially disjoint competing matches. On 503,320 test examples, ZoomTrace achieves 0.969 AUPRC and 0.987 AUROC, compared with 0.789 and 0.903 for Efficient LoFTR. Further evaluations reveal trade-offs in reliability estimation and limitations in vision-language models, cross-dataset retrieval, and calibration. Integrating ZoomTrace into visual agent tool paths reduces exposure to corrupted observations and silent failures, while downstream accuracy improvements depend on the agent and verification threshold.

</details>


### [167] [Inclusive Hand Pose Estimation via Topology-Aware Distillation and Progressive Kinematic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29163)
*Kanglei Zhou,Zikai Hao,Liyuan Wang,Xiaohui Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文构建了模拟结构缺失的手部基准IncNYU，并提出InsHaPE框架，通过拓扑感知蒸馏与渐进式运动链推理，实现结构不完整条件下的鲁棒手部姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部姿态估计方法普遍假设手部结构完整且关节齐全，当运动链中某些部分持续缺失时，预测会变得不稳定或不合理解，导致性能下降甚至系统失效，而部分观测信息实际上仍可利用。作者因此希望研究结构不完整性下的手部姿态估计问题。

Method: 作者首先构建IncNYU基准，通过对完整手部模型进行形态学修改来模拟结构不完整，并保留完整区域与人工移除区域的参考位置。在此基础上提出InsHaPE框架，基于层次化运动学建模，包含两个组件：（1）拓扑感知蒸馏策略，从完整手部表示中迁移关节级与关系级先验，在结构缺失下提供解剖一致的初始化；（2）渐进式运动学推理模块，沿运动链进行结构化推理，实现严重结构变化下的完整且解剖一致的姿态估计。

Result: 在NYU/IncNYU上的大量实验以及MSRA上的外部验证表明，InsHaPE在结构不完整条件下提升了鲁棒性，同时在完整手部上保持有竞争力的性能。系统级评估进一步显示，InsHaPE提升了需要固定拓扑手部姿态表示的下游应用的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作将结构不完整手部姿态估计作为独立问题提出，通过基准构建、拓扑感知蒸馏与渐进式运动链推理，在缺失结构场景下兼顾鲁棒性与完整手性能，并为依赖固定拓扑表示的下游系统提供支撑。

Abstract: Hand pose estimation aims to recover the spatial configuration of hand joints from visual observations and serves as a fundamental perception module for downstream systems that rely on complete and topologically consistent hand representations. Most existing methods are developed under the assumption of fully articulated hand structures and tend to produce unstable or implausible predictions when parts of the kinematic structure are persistently absent, resulting in degraded performance or even system failure despite available partial observations. To study this challenge, we construct IncNYU, a physiologically constrained benchmark that simulates structural incompleteness through morphological modification of complete hand models and retains reference locations for both intact and synthetically removed regions. Based on this benchmark, we propose InsHaPE, an Inclusive Hand Pose Estimation framework designed to remain robust under structural incompleteness while preserving performance on standard, fully articulated hands. Built on hierarchical kinematic modeling, InsHaPE introduces two complementary components. First, a topology-aware distillation strategy transfers joint-level and relational priors from fully articulated hand representations, providing anatomically consistent initialization under missing structures. Second, a progressive kinematic reasoning module performs structured inference along the kinematic chain, enabling complete and anatomically consistent pose estimation under severe structural variations. Extensive experiments on NYU/IncNYU, together with external validation on MSRA, demonstrate that InsHaPE improves robustness under structural incompleteness while maintaining competitive performance on complete hands. System-level evaluations further show that InsHaPE improves robustness for downstream applications that require fixed-topology hand pose representations.

</details>


### [168] [Training-Free 3D Editing via Feature-Divergence Localization and Trajectory Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29165)
*Tri Ton,Siwoo Lim,Joshua Tian Jin Tee,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FocusFlow，一个无需分割（segmentation-free）的两阶段轨迹调度框架，用于在3D资产编辑中实现局部化结构修改并保持未编辑区域几何不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于概率流模型的无训练编辑方法在高维潜空间中难以约束修改范围：依赖全局速度更新的无反转轨迹会导致空间"溢出"到应保留结构，并出现"轨迹欠承诺"使编辑中途停滞。

Method: 将积分轨迹拆分为两个不相交阶段：早期粗结构形成阶段，采用 Feature-Divergence Localization（FDL）借助内在跨注意力对速度场进行空间门控；粗结构形成后，采用 Reference Generation Guidance（RGG）作为轨迹校正项，将轨迹拉向显式的干净潜变量目标以驱动收敛到目标形状。

Result: 在 Google Scanned Objects 与 PartObjaverse-Tiny 联合基准上，FocusFlow 在对比方法中取得最佳保留分数与最大编辑幅度。

Conclusion: 该方法通过阶段调度实现稳定、局部化的几何控制，无需人工空间标注，缓解了保留—修改之间的传统权衡，降低了合成3D媒体修改的技术门槛。

Abstract: Modifying 3D assets requires enacting targeted structural transformations while strictly preserving the geometric identity of unedited regions. While recent probability flow models enable training-free editing, bounding these modifications within a high-dimensional latent space presents severe mathematical challenges. Current inversion-free trajectories rely on global velocity updates, which often cause spatial bleed into preserved structures and induce trajectory under-commitment, causing edits to stall mid-path. To resolve these dual failures, we introduce FocusFlow, a segmentation-free framework that structures the integration trajectory into two disjoint phases. During early coarse structure formation, FocusFlow applies Feature-Divergence Localization (FDL) to spatially gate the velocity field using intrinsic cross-attention. Once coarse structure has formed, FocusFlow applies Reference Generation Guidance (RGG), a trajectory-correction term that drives convergence to the target shape by pulling the trajectory toward an explicit clean-latent target. Evaluated on the joint Google Scanned Objects and PartObjaverse-Tiny benchmarks, FocusFlow obtains the best preservation scores and the largest edit magnitude among compared methods, suggesting that it can reduce the traditional preservation-modification trade-off. By providing stable, localized geometric control without manual spatial annotations, this phase-scheduled approach lowers technical barriers to the modification of synthetic 3D media.

</details>


### [169] [Decoupling Perception from Generation for Agentic 3D CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29167)
*Chenyu Wang,Mingyu Chen,Wenchao Li,Kayhan Batmanghelich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将胸部CT报告生成重构为大语言模型智能体协调的视觉问答决策集合，提出Rad-VL-JEPA视觉语言联合嵌入模型作为感知模块，实现感知与推理/写作解耦，在细粒度CT理解与报告生成上优于生成式VLM及智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT报告反复评估相同解剖区域的发现及其属性（如结节是否存在、位置），本质是可直接回答的视觉问题，而现有CT报告生成模型多沿用通用视觉-语言建模，在训练数据有限时易漏检细微异常。

Method: 提出Rad-VL-JEPA，通过视觉-语言联合嵌入预测统一报告检索、发现识别与细粒度属性刻画，将感知建模为判别式非生成任务；其上以强化学习训练智能体，结合患者上下文提出相关视觉查询获取患者特异的视觉证据；再由独立编辑器将证据整合为最终报告，从而分离证据获取与报告生成。

Result: Rad-VL-JEPA在细粒度CT理解任务上表现强劲，且摊销后单查询推理时间低于使用相同CT编码器与VQA数据训练的生成式VLM基线；由此构成的报告系统优于强VLM基线与智能体基线。

Conclusion: 将报告生成表述为由LLM智能体协调的VQA决策集合，并把视觉感知与推理、写作解耦，是提升数据受限条件下CT报告生成质量与效率的有效范式。

Abstract: Chest CT reports repeatedly assess the same anatomical regions for findings and their attributes, such as whether a lung nodule is present and where it is located. These assessments suggest learning to answer visual questions directly, rather than coupling perception to open-ended text generation. Yet current CT report generation models largely follow generic vision-language modeling, which may miss subtle abnormalities when training data are limited. We therefore formulate report generation as a set of visual question-answering decisions coordinated by a large language model (LLM) agent. This decouples visual perception from reasoning and report writing: perception can be learned as a discriminative, non-generative task, while the LLM focuses on reasoning over patient context and posing relevant visual queries. We introduce Rad-VL-JEPA, which unifies report retrieval, finding recognition, and fine-grained attribute characterization through vision-language joint-embedding prediction. Building on this perception module, an agent trained with reinforcement learning (RL) acquires patient-specific visual evidence, while a separate editor incorporates the evidence into the final report, further separating evidence acquisition from report realization. Experiments show that Rad-VL-JEPA achieves strong performance on fine-grained CT understanding tasks with lower amortized per-query inference time than a generative VLM baseline trained with the same CT encoder and VQA data. The resulting reporting system outperforms strong VLM-based and agentic baselines.

</details>


### [170] [SpatialDuet: Bayesian task routing between a metric expert and a geometry expert for spatial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29168)
*Duy Tran Thanh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialDuet将一个小型数据驱动空间VLM（度量专家）与一个带3D几何编码器的VLM（几何专家）通过贝叶斯任务路由器组合，冻结权重、按题型动态加权融合，在四个空间基准上超越已发表的小型VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身助手与可穿戴设备需要3D空间推理，但硬件受限只能用小型VLM；而小型空间VLM在训练中习得的能力各异：在大规模空间问答集上训练的模型擅长估计物体与房间尺寸，带3D几何编码器的模型更擅长相对方向与绝对距离，因此单一模型难以覆盖全部空间任务。

Method: 不改动已发表方法"训练单一模型覆盖所有任务"的做法，而是构建一个系统按各冻结模型最擅长的任务来使用。度量专家为数据驱动空间VLM，配三个免训练模块：训练对齐采样、度量感知的最小贝叶斯风险解码、按题型度量校准；几何专家为带3D几何编码器的VLM。贝叶斯任务路由器在带标签的开发问题上按题型估计各专家更优的概率，并以该概率为权重在对数空间（数值答案）或选项分布上（选择题）池化两专家答案。

Result: 在覆盖视频与单图的四个空间基准上验证有效：VSI-Bench上比最佳已发表小型VLM高3.17%；CV-Bench上（排除在CV-Bench类数据上训练的模型）高1.44%；全程未训练任何模型权重。

Conclusion: 按题型路由并融合互补的冻结专家，是一种无需训练即可提升小型空间VLM整体空间推理能力的有效范式，可作为单一模型方案的替代。

Abstract: Embodied assistants and wearable devices need 3D spatial reasoning, and their limited hardware restricts them to small vision-language models (VLMs). Small spatial VLMs acquire different abilities from their training: a model trained on large spatial question sets estimates object and room sizes well, whereas a model with a 3D geometry encoder is better at relative directions and absolute distances. Instead of training a single model for all spatial tasks, as published methods do, we build one system that uses each frozen model for the tasks it handles best. We propose SpatialDuet, which pairs a metric expert with a geometry expert under a Bayesian task router. The metric expert is a data-driven spatial VLM run with three training-free modules: training-aligned sampling, metric-aware minimum Bayes risk decoding and per-type metric calibration.
