The geometry expert is a VLM with a 3D geometry encoder. For each question type, the router estimates from labelled development questions how likely each expert is to be better, and pools their answers with that probability as weight, in log space for numeric answers and over the option distributions for multiple-choice answers. Extensive experiments on four spatial benchmarks covering video and single images validate SpatialDuet; it outperforms the best published small VLMs by 3.17% on VSI-Bench and, excluding a model trained on CV-Bench-style data, by 1.44% on CV-Bench, without training any model weight.

</details>


### [171] [loopxDINO: Dense Visual Prediction via Iterative Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29180)
*Xiangjing An,Zhibin Jiao,Aiting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: loopxDINO用权重绑定的循环交叉注意力解码器替代深层堆叠解码器，以14.85M可训练参数在Pascal VOC 2012上达到85.66% mIoU与65.01% Boundary IoU，参数量比6层基线少72.5%且边界与遮挡鲁棒性显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准稠密视觉预测依赖堆叠6–16层Transformer解码器（50M–200M参数）提升表示容量，但带来三方面代价：反向传播时激活内存开销大、非绑定权重需高片外带宽、计算分配静态无法随图像空间复杂度自适应。

Method: 提出两个解耦组件：(1) 冻结的DINOv2-Base骨干一次前向提取空间特征并将静态Key-Value表示缓存于GPU显存；(2) 紧凑的权重绑定循环交叉注意力解码器（核心7.96M、总可训练14.85M），跨T次迭代循环更新查询。稳定性由步条件adaLN、基于LayerNorm的超椭球流形正则化和可学习残差收缩阻尼保障；中间掩码预测作为空间注意力偏置无参数回馈，引导查询聚焦模糊轮廓。另引入ACT实现自适应迭代深度。

Result: 单张RTX 5060 Ti 16GB上，T=2时mIoU 85.66%、bIoU 65.01%；相较DINO-Trans6L（54.06M）提升+1.09% mIoU、+7.41% bIoU且参数少72.5%；相较DINO-FCN4L（2.43M，83.82% mIoU）边界+6.43% bIoU、60%掩蔽下+6.76% mIoU。结合ACT达87.3 FPS，60%遮挡下保留29.64% mIoU（较DINO-Trans6L +5.83%）。

Conclusion: 以迭代潜空间推理替代物理解码器深度可有效降低参数与内存开销，同时改善边界描绘与遮挡鲁棒性；权重绑定循环结构配合ACT具备动态计算优势，代码与权重以Apache 2.0开源。

Abstract: Standard dense visual prediction architectures scale representation capacity by stacking deep feedforward decoders (6-16 transformer layers with 50M-200M parameters). This explicit layer stacking brings substantial activation memory overhead during backpropagation, requires high off-chip memory bandwidth for non-tied weights, and uses static computational allocation that cannot adapt to spatial image complexity.
  We propose loopxDINO, which replaces physical decoder depth with iterative latent reasoning. It has two decoupled components: (1) a frozen DINOv2-Base backbone extracting spatial features in one forward pass and caching static Key-Value representations in GPU memory; (2) a compact weight-tied Looped Cross-Attention Decoder (7.96M core, 14.85M total trainable parameters) that recurrently updates queries across T iterations. Recurrence is stabilized by step-conditioned adaLN, LayerNorm-based hyper-ellipsoid manifold regularization, and learnable residual contraction damping. Intermediate mask predictions are fed back as spatial attention biases to guide queries toward ambiguous contours with no extra parameters.
  On Pascal VOC 2012 semantic segmentation with a single RTX 5060 Ti 16GB GPU, loopxDINO (T=2) achieves 85.66% mIoU and 65.01% Boundary IoU. It outperforms 6-layer DINO-Trans6L (54.06M params) by +1.09% mIoU and +7.41% Boundary IoU with 72.5% fewer parameters. Compared to 4-layer DINO-FCN4L probe (2.43M params, 83.82% mIoU), loopxDINO improves boundary delineation by +6.43% bIoU and occlusion resilience by +6.76% mIoU (60% patch masking). With Adaptive Computation Time (ACT), it reaches 87.3 FPS and retains 29.64% mIoU under 60% occlusion (+5.83% over DINO-Trans6L).
  Source code, pre-trained weights and evaluation suites are open-sourced under Apache 2.0 at https://github.com/loopxDINO/loopxDINO

</details>


### [172] [PhyCoVE: Benchmarking Physical Counterfactual Video Editing across Diverse Material Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29184)
*Conghan Yue,Yuanjie Chen,Yunyan Xiao,Zeyu Peng,Xingsong Ye,Ya Gao,Yanglin Huang,WeiYao Zhang,Yue Han,Zhineng Chen,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理反事实视频编辑基准 PhyCoVE（110 源视频、533 案例，覆盖刚体/弹性体/流体及五种交互模式），并给出跨材料的反事实保真度指标；七个模型评测显示最佳模型仅复现约 1/7 的物理变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实视频编辑评测主要局限于物体移除或特定刚体运动，而真实场景还包含弹性形变、流体流动以及刚体-弹性体-流体之间的交互，缺乏统一、多材料的评测基准与跨材料可用的度量指标。

Method: 构建 PhyCoVE：533 个案例、110 段源视频，覆盖刚体、弹性与流体动力学及五种交互模式；编辑类型包括移除物体、改变物理量、插入新物体。仿真数据由语言模型生成场景设计、编码智能体用适配各材料的求解器构建并基于源场景状态进行物理渲染与仿真，人工逐阶段校验；真实数据采用单机位连续拍摄。同时提出反事实保真度指标，衡量生成视频复现了源视频到目标视频之间多大比例的物理变化。

Result: 评测 7 个开源与商用视频编辑模型：反事实保真度与人类偏好排序近乎一致，但最佳模型仅复现约七分之一的物理变化。

Conclusion: 当前模型只改变场景外观而非演化过程，物理反事实视频编辑远未解决，PhyCoVE 为该任务的开发与评测提供基础。

Abstract: Physical counterfactual video editing turns a source video and a language-specified physical edit at a given execution frame into a counterfactual video that shows how the scene would evolve after the edit. Existing evaluations with counterfactual target videos mainly cover object removal or specific rigid-body motions, although real scenes also involve elastic deformation, fluid flow, and interactions among rigid bodies, elastic solids, and fluids. We introduce PhyCoVE, a benchmark of 533 cases on 110 source videos that covers rigid, elastic, and fluid dynamics in five interaction patterns, with edits that remove an object, change a physical quantity, or insert a new object. For the simulated cases, a language model proposes scene designs, coding agents build them with solvers suited to each material and physically based rendering and then simulate each edit from the source scene state, and humans check every stage. For the real cases, we record continuously from one camera position before and after each edit. We also propose counterfactual fidelity, a metric that applies across materials and their interactions and measures how much of the physical change from the source video to the target video a generated video reproduces. We evaluate seven open-source and commercial video editing models and find that counterfactual fidelity ranks them nearly as human preference does, but even the best model reproduces only about one seventh of the physical change. The models change how a scene looks but rarely how it evolves, whereas a physical edit requires understanding the dynamics that it changes. Physical counterfactual video editing thus remains far from solved, and PhyCoVE provides a foundation for developing and evaluating methods for it.

</details>


### [173] [Unleashing the Value of Raw Point Clouds for 3D Understanding with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29194)
*Tailai Chen,Jiawei Yu,Yiming Cheng,Ping Nie,Yongyong Chen,Tao Zhou,Zheng Zhu,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointTool：一个免编码器、工具增强的3D点云推理框架，通过AFPT、DPL与PSTR三个设计，使冻结LLM可直接操作原始点云并调用外部工具进行交互式3D推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点云语言模型依赖针对预定义任务优化的预训练点编码器，易形成表征瓶颈，与下游3D理解任务存在显著不匹配；同时模型多为单次"闭卷"生成，无法主动检视任务相关的3D区域或检索外部知识。

Method: 提出PointTool框架：(1) Alignment-Free Patch Tokenization (AFPT)——对原始点云做局部聚合，保留细粒度几何结构；(2) Decoupled Point-Text LoRA (DPL)——在冻结LLM内实现全局聚合与3D知识内化；(3) Program-Supervised Tool Reasoning (PSTR)——借助可执行工具显式操作原始3D数据与外部资源，支持基于空间证据的交互式推理。并构建PointCloud-TR基准（13K物体、39.8K问题、35.6K工具轨迹）。

Result: 在标准与复杂3D理解任务上均取得强劲性能，验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 免编码器+工具增强的范式可有效缓解表征瓶颈与单次生成局限，实现有空间证据支撑的交互式3D推理，并提供配套基准促进后续研究。

Abstract: Standard 3D point cloud language models typically rely on pretrained point encoders to extract 3D features and project them into representations processable by large language models (LLMs). However, these encoders are often originally optimized for pre-defined tasks, resulting in a representation bottleneck that may lead to a substantial mismatch with downstream 3D understanding. Moreover, existing models largely follow a single pass closed-book generation, limiting their ability to actively inspect task-relevant 3D regions or retrieve necessary external knowledge. In this work, we present PointTool, an encoder-free and tool-augmented framework for 3D reasoning. PointTool introduces Alignment-Free Patch Tokenization (AFPT) to locally aggregate raw point clouds while preserving fine-grained geometric structure, and Decoupled Point-Text LoRA (DPL) to facilitate global aggregation and 3D knowledge internalization within a frozen LLM. Building upon these designs, Program-Supervised Tool Reasoning (PSTR) enables the model to explicitly operate on raw 3D data and external resources through executable tools, supporting interactive 3D reasoning grounded in spatial evidence. We further introduce PointCloud-TR, a benchmark comprising 13K 3D objects, 39.8K questions, and 35.6K tool trajectories to facilitate training and evaluation of complex 3D reasoning. Experiments demonstrate that PointTool achieves strong performance on both standard and complex 3D understanding tasks, validating the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [174] [PartPO: Part-Level Policy Optimization for Shape-to-Program Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29213)
*Hannuo Zhang,Zhixiang Chi,Xuanchen Li,Yiqun Zhou,Yang Wang,Xinxin Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PartPO 将"形状程序由语义命名部件代码块组成"的先验引入强化学习，用部件级奖励实现细粒度信用分配，在无需人工标注的情况下提升点云到结构化形状程序的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 点云到形状程序生成模型依赖在程序化生成数据上的监督学习，对分布更丰富的形状泛化差；而基于执行反馈的强化学习对构建多部件的长程序只给单一标量奖励，信用分配困难。

Method: 利用形状程序的"自描述性"：按部件切分生成的程序，仅对该部件对应代码施加部件级奖励，仅对物体名称施加类别奖励，对整体程序施加形状级奖励。部件监督无需人工标注，通过在目标网格上用开放词汇分割模型将部件名称接地获得，且该分割模型在模型自身输出上做了 prompt 微调。以 MeshCoder 为基座应用。

Result: 在 ShapeNetCore 与 ABO 上，相比基座模型、拒绝采样微调（RFT）与轨迹级 RL，重建精度与执行成功率均有提升，并能泛化到未见过的 HSSD 数据集。

Conclusion: 部件级、细粒度奖励结构结合无标注的部件接地，可有效解决长程序生成中的信用分配问题，为程序化形状生成提供更强的强化学习方案。

Abstract: We present PartPO, a reinforcement learning method for generating structured shape programs from 3D point clouds. Existing shape-to-program models are trained with supervised learning on procedurally generated data and generalize poorly to more diverse shapes, while reinforcement learning from execution feedback assigns a single scalar reward to a long program that builds many parts, making credit assignment difficult. PartPO instead builds on a core property of the task: shape programs are self-describing, with each semantically named part produced by its own code block. It segments each generated program by its parts and applies part-level rewards only to the code of the corresponding part, a category reward only to the object name, and a shape-level reward to the whole program. The required part supervision is obtained without manual annotation, by grounding part names on target meshes with an open-vocabulary segmentation model prompt-tuned on the model's own outputs. Applied to MeshCoder, PartPO improves reconstruction accuracy and execution success on ShapeNetCore and ABO over the base model, rejection-sampling fine-tuning and trajectory-level RL, and generalizes to the unseen HSSD dataset. Code will be released at https://github.com/HannuoZ/PartPO.

</details>


### [175] [Ego-HMB: Human Motion Bridging from Egocentric Images via A Motion Bridge Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29218)
*Gaoge Han,Yongkang Cheng,Shaoli Huang,Jing Zhang,Mingming Gong,Fakhri Karray,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“Egocentric Human Motion Bridging”任务，从两张端点第一视角图像生成连贯的3D人体运动序列，并提出统一的扩散框架Ego-HMB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统motion in-betweening依赖结构化的3D端点姿态，而头戴设备/机器人只能提供原始第一视角观测；该设定面临视角变化、身体部分可见、跨模态端点约束下的时间歧义等挑战。

Method: Ego-HMB为单一扩散模型，两阶段训练：①Egocentric-to-Canonical Pretraining，采用时间跨度递增的分层课程学习强化端点姿态先验；②Motion Bridge Diffusion Training，引入端点与物理感知，从相邻姿态出发扩散以增强时间连续性并保持全局端点约束。

Result: 与一阶段方法（直接从两图生成运动）及两阶段流水线（端点姿态估计+运动插值）对比，Ego-HMB在端点精度与运动生成质量上均稳定优于基线。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 将端点姿态估计与时间一致的运动补全整合进统一扩散模型，是解决第一视角运动桥接的有效方案。

Abstract: We introduce Egocentric Human Motion Bridging, a task that generates a coherent 3D human motion sequence from two endpoint egocentric images. Unlike conventional motion in-betweening, which requires structured 3D endpoint poses, this setting uses raw first-person observations available from wearable devices in VR/AR and robotics. It presents challenges due to viewpoint variations, partial body visibility, and temporal ambiguity under cross-modal endpoint constraints. To address these challenges, we propose Ego-HMB, a unified diffusion-based framework that learns endpoint pose estimation and temporally consistent motion infilling within a single model through two training stages. First, Egocentric-to-Canonical Pretraining uses a hierarchical curriculum with progressively increasing temporal spans to strengthen endpoint pose priors. Second, Motion Bridge Diffusion Training incorporates endpoint and physics awareness, diffusing from neighboring poses to promote temporal continuity while preserving global endpoint constraints. Extensive experiments compare our approach with one-stage methods that directly generate motion from two egocentric images and two-stage pipelines that combine endpoint pose estimation with motion interpolation. Ego-HMB consistently outperforms these baselines in endpoint accuracy and motion generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/gaogehan/Ego-HMB.

</details>


### [176] [Why Adaptive Optimizers Struggle with Large Convolution Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29232)
*Tianyu Ding,Jiachen Jiang,Jinxin Zhou,Yong Ma,Zhihui Zhu,Luming Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出大卷积核难以训练源于自适应优化器对核均值的放大效应（k倍于单参数），并证明DCT基是隔离均值、对角化镜像边界下平稳梯度统计的唯一正交基，据此提出DCT-Rep：在DCT系数空间训练大核，训练后转回，几乎零额外代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大卷积核能以较小算力扩大感受野，但极难训练，现有大核网络不得不依赖并行小核分支+BN等昂贵训练技巧。作者希望找出训练困难的根源并给出无需额外参数的简洁解决方案。

Method: 诊断：Adam等自适应优化器逐个归一化权重更新，而输入特征很少均值为零，导致k×k核所有权重共享同一梯度分量，优化器将核均值推进k倍于单参数的距离（mean amplification），使最大学习率须按1/k²下降。理论：证明DCT基隔离均值，且是唯一能在镜像边界下对角化所有平稳梯度统计的正交基。方法DCT-Rep：将大核参数化到DCT系数空间进行训练，训练结束后转回空间域，仅需少量代码，无新增参数、无推理开销、训练开销可忽略。

Result: ImageNet-1K上大核ConvNeXt-T提升+0.79% top-1，ViT-S/16提升+0.43%；COCO检测+1.4 box AP；ADE20K分割+1.2 mIoU；图像去模糊最高+0.4 dB；增益随核尺寸增大而增大。且在图像分类、检测、分割、复原等多任务上一致有效。

Conclusion: 大核训练困难主要归因于自适应优化器下的均值放大，而非架构本身缺陷；通过DCT系数空间训练可系统性缓解该问题，DCT-Rep作为一种即插即用、零参数零推理代价的通用改进，适用于大核ConvNet与ViT。

Abstract: Large convolution kernels enlarge the receptive fields of ConvNets at a modest computational cost, but they are notoriously difficult to train. Existing large-kernel networks therefore rely on costly training techniques, such as parallel small-kernel branches with batch normalization. In this paper, we trace this difficulty to adaptive optimizers such as Adam, which normalize the update of every weight separately. Since input features rarely average to zero, all weights of a kernel share a common gradient, and these optimizers move the mean of a $k\times k$ kernel $k$ times as far as a single parameter. We find this mean amplification in widely used ConvNets and vision transformers, and show that it can force the maximal learning rate to fall as $1/k^2$. We further prove that the basis of the discrete cosine transform (DCT) isolates the mean and is the only orthonormal basis that diagonalizes every stationary gradient statistic with mirrored boundaries. We therefore train large kernels through their DCT coefficients and convert them back after training. The resulting method, DCT-Rep, requires only a few lines of code and adds no parameters, no inference cost and negligible training overhead. Extensive experiments on image classification, object detection, semantic segmentation and image restoration show that DCT-Rep consistently improves large-kernel ConvNets and vision transformers, by $+0.79\%$ top-1 accuracy for a large-kernel ConvNeXt-T and $+0.43\%$ for ViT-S/16 on ImageNet-1K, $+1.4$ box AP on COCO, $+1.2$ mIoU on ADE20K and up to $+0.4$ dB on image deblurring, where the gain grows with the kernel size.

</details>


### [177] [DriveOddsBench: Are Driving Video World Models Aligned with Real-World Odds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29275)
*Jiaxi Liu,Keshu Wu,Bin Ran,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DriveOddsBench预注册基准，用秩统计（概率积分变换）检验视频世界模型采样未来是否符合真实分布，发现11个模型均不对齐且系统性低估不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型的采样未来被用于规划与仿真，隐含假设样本分布与真实世界概率一致；但现有评测仅评估单样本真实性，无法检验分布对齐，且真实数据仅有单一未来、物理量需从视频读取、分析易选择性报告。

Method: 预注册基准：若无概率标签，若样本服从真实分布则真实结果在样本中的秩在每个历史下均匀，可汇总秩做检验；用同一经验证仪器从生成未来与经世界模型自编码器处理的真实未来中读取前车间距、自车运动等物理量，保证读取一致；所有确证性分析在测量任何测试片段前冻结。在200条Argoverse 2保留历史、5类场景、35,200个生成未来上评测11个通用与驾驶世界模型。

Result: 无一模型对齐，且全部低估不确定性；真实结果落在16个样本范围之外的比例为28–61%，而真实分布下期望为11.8%；5个模型显著偏斜且方向相反，误差最大出现在自车运动；9个模型优于气候学基线但未对齐，且模型类型与规模均不能预测谁最接近。

Conclusion: 世界模型的采样未来在概率意义上并不代表真实世界；秩检验可作为无标签的分布对齐评测手段，需将不确定性校准纳入模型开发与仿真使用。

Abstract: Video world models predict how a scene will evolve, and their sampled futures are increasingly used in place of the real one, from planning to simulation. These uses assume that the samples are aligned with real-world odds, with each future appearing among them as often as it would follow the same history in reality, yet current evaluations only score how real each sample looks. Testing this assumption on real driving data is hard for three reasons. Each recorded history has only one real future, so the true probabilities are unknown. Physical quantities must be read from video, where reading errors can look like model errors. Finally, the many possible analyses invite selective reporting. We introduce DriveOddsBench, a pre-registered benchmark that addresses all three. First, if the samples follow the real distribution, the rank of the real outcome among them is uniform in every history, so pooled ranks test them without probability labels. Second, one validated instrument reads the same quantities, such as the lead-vehicle gap and the ego motion, from the generated futures and from the real future, which first passes through the world model's autoencoder so that both are read alike. Third, all confirmatory analyses were frozen before any test clip was measured. We test eleven general and driving world models on 200 held-out Argoverse 2 histories in five scenario families, with 35,200 generated futures. None is aligned, and every model underestimates its uncertainty. The real outcome falls outside the range of all 16 samples in 28-61% of cases instead of the 11.8% expected under the real distribution. Five models are significantly skewed, in opposite directions, and the largest errors are in the ego motion. Nine models beat climatology without being aligned, and among these models neither type nor scale predicts which comes closest.

</details>


### [178] [ContinuLoc: Continuous Pose Inference over Neural Fields for UAV Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29276)
*Weikun Zhou,Jinliang Lin,Yu Zang,Lin Xie,Haiqing Fu,Sheng Ao,Shangshu Yu,Zhiming Luo,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将地理参考地图编码为可连续查询的语义神经场，把地图表示与位姿推断解耦，使跨视角无人机地理定位变为连续4DoF位姿空间（2D位置+航向+尺度）上的显式优化，避免离散候选库的采样分辨率瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流范式把地图组织为离散2DoF候选库，使地图表示与位姿推断耦合，定位精度被候选采样分辨率所限制；且缺乏完整4DoF位姿标注的基准。

Method: 1) 地图表示为连续可查询的语义神经场，解除网格离散化；2) 位姿推断为连续4DoF空间上的显式优化，通过共享语义特征空间中的全局描述子按需评估位姿假设，绕过检索采样瓶颈；3) 在ContinuScenes（多城市、密集近正射无人机影像、完整位姿标注）上以全4DoF监督学习位姿敏感的视觉表示；4) 层级式推断，由宽覆盖逐步自适应收敛到高兼容区域。

Result: 在逐步收紧的4DoF精度准则下，相较代表性定位范式取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 解耦地图表示与位姿推断、以神经场+连续优化替代离散检索，是提升跨视角无人机地理定位精度上限的有效途径。

Abstract: Cross-view UAV geo-localization involves three design choices: how to represent the UAV observation, how to represent the geo-referenced map, and how to infer pose from their interaction. The dominant paradigm organizes the map as a discretized 2DoF candidate library, coupling map representation to pose inference and thus bounding spatial localization precision by candidate sampling resolution. We decouple map representation from pose inference by encoding the geo-referenced map as a continuously queryable semantic neural field, enabling localization to be formulated as explicit optimization over a continuous 4DoF pose space parameterized by 2D ground position, in-plane heading, and viewing scale. Pose hypotheses are evaluated on demand using global descriptors in a shared semantic feature space, bypassing the sampling bottleneck of retrieval. We learn 4DoF pose-sensitive visual representations under full 4DoF supervision provided by ContinuScenes, a multi-city benchmark with dense near-nadir UAV imagery and complete pose annotations. Inference proceeds hierarchically, progressing from broad pose-space coverage to adaptive concentration on high-compatibility regions. Experiments demonstrate substantial gains over representative localization paradigms under progressively stricter 4DoF accuracy criteria.
  Code is available at https://github.com/ZhouWeikun/ContinuLoc/

</details>


### [179] [RadioWorld: Transferring World Models to Radio-Frequency Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29282)
*Hu Zhou,Jiazhou Zhou,Shuowen Zhang,Ying-Chang Liang,Liang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RadioWorld——一种将世界模型适配于射频辐射场合成的通用框架，仅凭物理场景几何与收发机位置即可直接生成 RF 辐射场，推理阶段无需任何参考 RF 测量，并构建 UrbanRF-Bench 大规模射线追踪基准验证其跨城市泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经射频场方法存在三点根本局限：依赖逐场景优化、无法泛化到未见场景；每个目标场景需昂贵的参考 RF 测量；在参考测量邻域之外外推时性能严重退化。作者据此提出无需参考测量、可跨场景泛化的 RF 场合成方案。

Method: 采用双流 Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）架构耦合两个专用模块：Scene-Understanding Transformer 编码 3D 场景几何与收发机位置，提取环境感知的全局电磁传播特征；Field-Generation Transformer 以该特征为条件、结合局部查询条件，合成 RF 辐射场。同时构建 UrbanRF-Bench 基准，覆盖 7 座全球城市、657 个城市场景、32,850 个 RF 辐射场。

Result: 大量实验表明，RadioWorld 显著优于各类专用基线方法，并能在未见城市中任意位置准确合成 RF 场。

Conclusion: 将世界模型思想引入射频辐射场建模，可在零参考测量的前提下实现跨场景、跨城市的可泛化 RF 场合成，为 6G 通信与感知提供新的建模范式。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) wave propagation in the physical world constitutes a Radio-Frequency (RF) radiance field, providing rich information for 6G communication and sensing. However, existing neural RF field methods suffer from three fundamental limitations: (1) they predominantly rely on per-scene optimization and cannot generalize to unseen scenes; (2) they require costly reference RF measurements in each target scene; and (3) they degrade severely when extrapolating beyond the vicinity of reference measurements. To overcome these limitations, we present RadioWorld, a general framework that adapts a world model to synthesize RF radiance fields directly from physical scene geometry and transceiver locations, without requiring any reference RF measurements at inference. Specifically, RadioWorld couples two specialized modules within a dual-stream Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture: a Scene-Understanding Transformer that encodes 3D scene geometry and transceiver locations to extract environment-aware global EM propagation features, and a Field-Generation Transformer that attends to these features to synthesize the RF radiance field with the local query conditions. We also establish UrbanRF-Bench, a large-scale ray-traced benchmark spanning 657 urban scenes and 32,850 RF radiance fields across seven global cities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RadioWorld significantly outperforms specialized baselines and accurately synthesizes RF fields at arbitrary locations in unseen cities. Code, datasets, and checkpoints will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [180] [Robust Concept Erasure in Text-to-Image Models via Simulating Brain Functional Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29288)
*Manyi Li,Yufan Liu,Bing Li,Xia Wu,Ziyu Li,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出初始潜变量优化攻击IVO，揭示概念擦除模型的潜在空间盲点，并据此提出基于单元神经元功能定位的概念擦除方法CeFlune。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型中的概念擦除可被攻击重新激活，而既有攻击主要搜索文本或图像输入空间，初始潜空间这一环节未被充分探索，导致擦除鲁棒性评估存在盲区。

Method: 先在初始潜空间优化生成起点构造再激活攻击IVO；再将脑功能定位思想迁移到扩散模型，将线性模块定义为"单元神经元"，通过比较任务态与静息态的响应轨迹定位概念相关单元，并更新其响应以约束模型预测，形成CeFlune。

Result: 在8种擦除方法上，IVO的重激活效果优于基线攻击；CeFlune在U-Net扩散与流匹配Transformer架构上，相对最多15种基线方法在擦除效果与通用生成能力间取得较好平衡，并在IVO等5种攻击下表现出更低的重激活率。

Conclusion: 初始潜空间是被忽视的攻击面，应纳入鲁棒性评估；基于功能定位的神经元级干预可同时提升擦除效果与抗攻击鲁棒性。

Abstract: Concept erasure in text-to-image models aims to prevent a target concept from appearing in generated images while preserving general image generation capability. However, erased concepts can still be reactivated by attacks. Prior attacks mainly search text or image input spaces, leaving the initial latent space underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce IVO (Initial latent Variable Optimization), a new concept reactivation attack that changes the starting point of image generation by optimizing the initial latent. Comprehensive experiments across 8 erasure methods show that IVO reactivates erased concepts more effectively than the attack baselines, revealing a blind spot in erasure robustness evaluation. This motivates us to localize model components associated with the target concept and then treat them as intervention sites for more robust concept erasure. We propose CeFlune (Concept Erasure via Functional Localization of Unit Neurons), which adapts brain functional localization to text-to-image models. CeFlune defines linear modules as Unit Neurons and localizes concept-responsive ones by comparing their response trajectories in the model's task and resting states. It then updates the localized Unit Neurons to constrain their responses and the erased model's predictions. Across U-Net diffusion and flow-matching transformer architectures, CeFlune achieves a favorable balance between concept erasure and general image generation capability in comparisons with up to 15 baseline methods. Under IVO and 4 other evaluated attacks, CeFlune also shows lower concept reactivation.

</details>


### [181] [CT-Navigator: Towards Interpretable and Generalizable CT Understanding through Agentic Perception and Tool-Invocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29305)
*Yibing Fu,Huidong Xie,Yihao Niu,Wei Ji,Heming Dong,Ziyue Wang,Jingyu Chen,Jun Zhao,Haoyuan Hu,Hongming Shan,Lai Jiang,Mai Xu,Yueming Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"代理感知"（agentic perception）范式与CT-Navigator框架，利用通用MLLM的2D视觉与智能体能力，通过分层视觉探索与影像工具调用实现可追溯的CT解读，并在新建CTNavi-Bench上达79.50%准确率，超最强基线17.59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床CT解读本质是渐进式视觉探索：从整张扫描到单层再到感兴趣区域，并按需调用外部工具。现有3D医学MLLM通常将专用3D视觉编码器接到语言模型上，未充分利用通用MLLM的2D视觉与智能体能力；且仅在大规模"一跳"CT指令数据上训练，迭代推理与视觉证据定位能力不足，难以支撑可追溯的CT解读。

Method: 1) 以放射科医生工作流为灵感提出agentic perception范式；2) 构建CT-Navigator框架，借助通用MLLM的2D视觉与工具调用能力，进行分层视觉探索与放射工具调用来逐步显式定位视觉证据；3) 构建8,904条视觉思维链（V-CoT）轨迹用于冷启动的智能体监督微调；4) 在33,219个问题上用强化学习优化策略，并通过数据筛选与动态rollout采样优先选择信息量大的学习信号；5) 提出CTNavi-Bench，含7,000个覆盖全身解剖区域的3D CT视觉问答题目。

Result: CT-Navigator在CTNavi-Bench上取得79.50%准确率，比最强基线高17.59%，并在三个外部CT基准上表现出较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将通用MLLM的2D视觉与智能体能力用于3D CT解读是可行且有效的，分层探索与工具调用可提升准确率并提供可追溯的视觉证据；作者将开源代码、模型与基准。

Abstract: Clinical computed tomography (CT) interpretation inherently requires progressive visual exploration, where radiologists navigate from whole scans to individual slices and regions of interest and interact with external tools when necessary. However, existing 3D medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) typically connect a specialized 3D visual encoder to a language model, without fully leveraging the native 2D visual and agentic capabilities of generalist MLLMs. Trained on large-scale one-hop CT instruction-following data, these models also have limited ability to perform iterative reasoning and explicitly ground their conclusions in visual evidence, limiting their support for traceable CT interpretation. To solve these problems, we propose \textbf{agentic perception} as an alternative paradigm inspired by radiologists' interpretation workflows. We introduce \textbf{CT-Navigator}, a framework that harnesses generalist MLLMs' 2D visual and agentic capabilities to progressively ground CT interpretation in explicit, traceable visual evidence through hierarchical visual exploration and radiology tool invocation. To build these capabilities, we curate 8,904 visual chain-of-thought (V-CoT) trajectories for cold-start agentic supervised fine-tuning. We then optimize the policy through reinforcement learning on 33,219 questions, with data selection and dynamic rollout sampling prioritizing informative learning signals. Finally, we introduce CTNavi-Bench, a 3D CT visual question answering benchmark comprising 7,000 questions spanning anatomical regions throughout the human body. CT-Navigator achieves 79.50\% accuracy on CTNavi-Bench, surpassing the strongest baseline by 17.59\%, and demonstrates strong generalization across three external CT benchmarks. The code, model, and benchmark will be released on \href{https://github.com/fyb99/ct_navigator}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [182] [Where Does Personalization Reside in Photo Editing? Evidence from One Photographer's Lightroom History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29307)
*Dongwu Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Personalized photo-editing systems must infer both a photographer's recurring preferences and the intent of a particular shoot, yet these signals are rarely evaluated separately. We study interpretable Lightroom adjustments in a longitudinal, private dataset of 3,193 edited photographs from 41 catalogs by one professional photographer, with holdouts formed at the batch level. We compare absolute and prior-relative targets, image-conditioned predictors, and estimators that receive a small set of edits from the target batch. Residual prediction improves validation error, but its advantage on held-out batches is small and not significant under a batch-clustered bootstrap (0.592 vs 0.582 EV exposure MAE). In a batch-shared protocol, closed-form shrinkage using 4, 8, or 16 same-batch anchors reaches 0.466, 0.457, or 0.453 EV with the best estimator at each k, compared with 0.586-0.592 EV for the prior-only baseline; the paired reductions are significant under a batch-clustered bootstrap. Learned anchor-conditioned heads do not improve on this closed-form estimator, and a negative-trained prior already yields a low cold-image false-edit rate, leaving little benefit for an additional gate. The recommended estimator is closed-form and auditable, and it requires only a handful of same-batch edits. A render-space check against 383 native Lightroom exports reproduces the ranking: at k=8, online EB lowers Delta E00 against the prior by 0.41 (p=0.042), and the photographer's true parameters lower it by 0.78 (p=0.034). An appendix extends the action space from six to thirteen global controls: a same-batch median look lowers rendered chroma toward the export, while a per-photograph residual head again fails to transfer. The study is limited to one photographer and does not claim that slider accuracy measures aesthetic preference.

</details>


### [183] [Counting Symbols on Technical Drawings: A Benchmark and Verified Rewards for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29309)
*Junxian Li,Ruixuan Yang,Zhenhua Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出技术图纸计数基准CountDrawBench（涵盖平面图、电气原理图、机械图纸、分子结构，1,574 个计数问题）及Code-Verified RL框架，通过计数奖励、实例匹配奖励与缩放一致性奖励的GRPO统一目标提升MLLM在技术图纸上的计数能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型（MLLM）在自然图像计数上表现良好，但在机械图纸的螺栓孔、化学结构中的氮原子等技术图纸场景中计数困难：目标尺度小、分布密集，甚至仅由制图约定隐含表示。该能力缺口缺乏系统性评测与针对性训练方法。

Method: (1) 构建CountDrawBench基准，覆盖平面图、电气原理图、机械图纸与分子结构四类图纸，含1,574个计数问题及部分实例级标注；(2) 提出Code-Verified RL，以可执行反馈实现强化学习，融合三类互补奖励——最终答案正确性奖励、基于实例匹配的位置验证奖励、基于局部区域缩放的一致性奖励，统一整合进GRPO目标函数进行优化。

Result: 领先MLLM在CountDrawBench上普遍表现不佳，最佳评测模型准确率仅59.3%；所提方法将Qwen3.5-9B从49.3%提升至54.3%，优于所有评测的开源基线。

Conclusion: CountDrawBench可作为技术图纸理解的高难度测试平台，验证了代码验证式奖励在该类任务中的有效性；代码与数据将在论文接收后公开。

Abstract: Today's MLLMs can count objects in natural images. However, when asking them how many bolt holes appear in a mechanical drawing or how many nitrogen atoms are in a chemical structure, they often struggle. Counting in technical drawings remains challenging, as objects can be small, densely packed, or even implied by drawing conventions. To study this problem, we introduce CountDrawBench, a benchmark including floor plans, electrical schematics, mechanical drawings, and molecular structures, with 1,574 counting questions and instance-level annotations where available. Besides evaluation, we introduce Code-Verified RL, a reinforcement learning framework that teaches MLLMs to count with executable feedback. Our method combines three complementary rewards: a count reward that checks the final answer, an instance-matching reward that verifies object locations, and a zoom-based reward that checks counting consistency across local image regions. All three are integrated into a unified GRPO objective. Extensive experiments reveal that even leading MLLMs struggle on CountDrawBench, with the best evaluated model achieving only 59.3% accuracy. Our method improves Qwen3.5-9B from 49.3% to 54.3% accuracy, outperforming all evaluated open-source baselines. These results establish CountDrawBench as a challenging testbed for technical drawing understanding and demonstrate the value of code-verified rewards in these tasks. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [184] [PrimiTok: Learning to Diversify and Allocate Primitives for Visual Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29311)
*Yizhu Chen,Chen Ju,Donghao Zhou,Ziyu Guo,Shuai Xiao,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Ying Chen,Haodong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PrimiTok: 一种组合式视觉 tokenizer，将码字视为可复用的视觉基元，对每个 token 自适应分配并加权组合基元，兼顾重建保真与语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一视觉理解与生成需要同时保留语义信息与细粒度细节。连续 tokenizer 偏重语义对齐而丢失重建细节；离散 tokenizer 受码本量化信息损失影响；且传统多码本设计对所有 token 分配相同数量码字，忽视局部内容差异。

Method: 提出 PrimiTok，含两个互补组件：Diverse Quantitative Primitives (DQP) 通过促进子码本质心间的多样性降低冗余；Dynamic Primitive Allocator (DPA) 依据 token 复杂度选择基元数量，以嵌入量化误差监督各子码本选取的基元数目，并按输入相关权重聚合基元。训练上联合重建损失与图文对比学习。

Result: 在重建质量与语义对齐上优于 SOTA 基线，并在多模态理解与视觉生成任务上进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: 以"可复用基元 + 自适应分配与加权组合"替代固定码字分配，可有效统一细粒度重建与语义对齐，适用于理解与生成双重下游任务。

Abstract: Unifying visual understanding and generation requires representations that preserve both semantic information and fine-grained image details. Continuous tokenizers trained primarily for semantic alignment may omit fine-grained reconstruction details, whereas discrete tokenizers face information loss from codebook quantization. Conventional multi-codebook designs assign every token the same number of codewords regardless of local content. We instead view codewords as reusable visual primitives whose allocation and composition adapt to each token. We introduce PrimiTok, a compositional visual tokenizer that represents each visual token through adaptive allocation and weighted composition of learned codebook primitives. PrimiTok integrates two complementary components: Diverse Quantitative Primitives (DQP), which encourages diversity among sub-codebook centroids to reduce redundancy, and a Dynamic Primitive Allocator (DPA), which selects primitives according to token complexity. Embedding quantization error supervises how many primitives are selected from each sub-codebook, and DPA aggregates them with input-dependent weights. Joint reconstruction and image--text contrastive learning encourage the resulting representations to retain visual fidelity and semantic alignment. Experiments demonstrate improved reconstruction and semantic alignment over SOTA baselines, with further evaluations on multimodal understanding and visual generation.

</details>


### [185] [Prompt-Consistent Cross-View Object Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29312)
*Lingyun Xing,Wenning Hao,Kai Jiang,Dawei Jin,Xiaohan Zhang,Xiaoxiong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示现有跨视角目标地理定位(CVOGL)方法存在"违背提示"的过拟合问题，通过构建多目标数据集CVOGL-MO与跨视角同场景多目标对比损失(CVMCL)两个即插即用组件，并设计提示敏感性评测协议，显著提升模型的提示跟随能力与敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化提示敏感性评测发现，现有CVOGL模型在点击点移动或移除时预测几乎不变，说明其依赖虚假特征相关性而非真正遵循提示语义。根因在于基准数据规模有限（每任务仅4,343个提示-目标对），模型倾向于记忆场景对应目标而非学习提示到目标的真实映射。

Method: 提出两个架构无关、即插即用的组件：(1) 构建CVOGL-MO数据集，在现有训练/测试集上扩展多样化多目标标注，缓解导致捷径学习的记忆效应；(2) 提出跨视角同场景多目标对比损失CVMCL，对同一场景内不同点击点强制产生差异化特征表示，迫使模型遵循提示指令。此外设计带预测偏移指标的提示敏感性评测协议。

Result: 在新多目标测试集上，三个代表性骨干网络的实验表明，所提组件大幅提升提示跟随准确率，并显著增强提示敏感性；同时首次实现对CVOGL提示跟随能力的定量度量。

Conclusion: 现有CVOGL的性能瓶颈源于数据规模不足导致的捷径学习与提示违背过拟合，而非架构本身；通过数据扩展与对比损失即可在无需修改架构的前提下有效解决，数据集与代码将开源。

Abstract: Cross-view object geo-localization (CVOGL) aims to locate an object indicated by a click point in a ground or drone query image within a satellite reference image. Through a quantitative prompt-sensitivity evaluation, we find that existing CVOGL methods suffer from a prompt-disobeying overfitting problem: when the click point is moved or removed, their predictions barely respond, indicating that the models rely on spurious feature correlations rather than genuinely following the prompt semantics. We trace this behavior to the limited scale of the benchmark dataset, which contains only 4,343 prompt-object pairs per task, so that the models simply memorize the object associated with each scene instead of learning the true prompt-to-object correspondence. To address this, we propose two plug-and-play components. First, we construct the CVOGL-MO dataset by expanding the existing training and test sets with diverse multi-object annotations, mitigating the memorization that induces shortcut learning. Second, we propose the Cross-View Co-Scene Multi-Object Contrastive Loss (CVMCL), which enforces distinct feature representations for different click points within the same scene, compelling the model to follow prompt instructions. We further design a prompt-sensitivity evaluation protocol with prediction-shift metrics, providing the first quantitative measurement of prompt-following capability for CVOGL. Both components are architecture-agnostic and can be integrated into existing methods without any architectural modification. Experiments on three representative backbones show that our components substantially improve prompt-following accuracy on the new multi-object test sets and markedly increase prompt sensitivity. Our dataset and code will be publicly released at https://github.com/Sunny-Fresher/CVOGL-MO.

</details>


### [186] [FP-Prune: Rethinking Visual Token Pruning from an Information Gain Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29327)
*Haozhe Zhao,Junxiang Zhang,Junxin Wang,Yuan Ge,Xiangchen Song,Dingyang Zhang,JingBo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的视觉token剪枝方法FP-Prune，以互信息度量候选token的增量信息，并从理论上证明frame potential可作为互信息的有效代理，迭代剔除信息增益低的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉token剪枝可降低VLM推理成本，但效果取决于在有限token预算下保留互补的视觉信息。核心难题在于量化候选token相对已保留集合的额外贡献，而基于注意力或相似度的传统打分难以直接刻画这种增量信息。

Method: 从信息增益视角重新审视视觉token剪枝，用互信息刻画候选token的增量价值；理论证明frame potential是互信息的有效替代目标；据此设计FP-Prune：免训练、迭代式地移除信息增益低的token，以最大化保留互补视觉信息。

Result: 在3个常用VLM和8个数据集上，FP-Prune在准确率与推理效率上均优于基线方法；进一步分析表明该代理目标与模型性能高度一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为视觉token剪枝提供了理论依据与有效方法，说明以frame potential近似互信息的信息增益准则可行且高效。

Abstract: Visual token pruning can reduce the inference cost of vision language models, but its effectiveness depends on preserving complementary visual information under a limited token budget. A central challenge is to quantify the additional information a candidate token contributes beyond the retained set, a contribution that conventional scores based on attention or similarity struggle to capture directly. In this work, we revisit visual token pruning from the perspective of information gain and use mutual information to characterize the incremental value of candidate tokens. We further establish theoretically that frame potential serves as an effective surrogate for mutual information. Building on this observation, we propose FP-Prune, a training-free visual token pruning method that iteratively removes tokens with low information gain to preserve as much complementary visual information as possible. Experiments on three widely used VLMs and eight datasets demonstrate that FP-Prune outperforms baseline methods in both accuracy and inference efficiency. Further analysis shows that the surrogate objective closely aligns with model performance. Our work provides theoretical grounding and an effective method for visual token pruning.

</details>


### [187] [SKIA: Spatial-Knowledge Informed Attention for Robust Remote Photoplethysmography under Lower-Face Motion Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29330)
*Van-Truong Le,Viet-Tham Huynh,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SKIA：一种轻量的掩膜引导框架，通过抑制易受运动干扰的下半脸区域、保留额头与脸颊的生理信号，提升rPPG在面部运动下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程光电容积描记（rPPG）可从面部视频实现非接触式生理监测，但认知与交流任务中的嘴部、下颌运动会严重劣化信号重建质量，需针对性抑制运动干扰区域。

Method: 先在UBFC-rPPG上验证经典CHROM流程中的静态下半脸掩膜效果；在此基础上提出SKIA-Net，紧凑时空架构融合原始面部帧、静态掩膜表征与时间运动差分，重建BVP波形；并通过消融对比单流变体与基于光流的动态掩膜策略。

Result: 静态掩膜使波形Pearson相关从0.3507升至0.4166（p<0.001），心率MAE从33.50降至23.44 bpm；完整SKIA-Net达Pearson 0.7522、心率MAE 3.73 bpm，优于单流变体；静态解剖先验优于所测动态光流掩膜。

Conclusion: 空间先验明确、时间上稳定的掩膜能改善运动鲁棒性rPPG估计；静态掩膜与原始外观信息互补。局限在于仅在UBFC-rPPG单一基准验证，且静态掩膜牺牲了下半脸信息，真实大幅运动场景下泛化性仍待检验。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables contactless physiological monitoring from facial videos, but facial motion, particularly mouth and jaw movements during cognitive and communicative tasks, can severely degrade signal reconstruction. We present SKIA, a lightweight, mask-guided framework that suppresses motion-prone lower-face regions while preserving physiological cues from the forehead and cheeks. Using the UBFC-rPPG benchmark, we first investigate static lower-face suppression in the classical CHROM pipeline. Static masking improves waveform Pearson correlation from 0.3507 to 0.4166 (p < 0.001) and reduces heart-rate mean absolute error (MAE) from 33.50 to 23.44 bpm. Building on this finding, we introduce SKIA-Net, a compact spatiotemporal architecture that combines raw facial frames, statically masked representations, and temporal motion differences to reconstruct blood volume pulse (BVP) waveforms. The full model achieves a Pearson correlation of 0.7522 and a heart-rate MAE of 3.73 bpm on UBFC-rPPG, outperforming single-stream variants. Ablation studies show that the static mask provides complementary information to raw appearance and that static anatomical priors outperform the tested dynamic optical-flow-based masking strategy. These results suggest that spatially informed, temporally stable masking can improve motion-robust rPPG estimation. Code: https://github.com/lee-vtruong/SKIA-rPPG

</details>


### [188] [Probe Before You Learn: Allocating Prompts from Initial Gradients under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29336)
*Jiazhe Zhang,Zibin Geng,Qiang Ma,Xuefeng Jiang,Yijun Lin,Jingyi Wang,Yu Yan,Xingzhou Zhang,Min Liu,Yuwei Wang,Sheng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的GPrompt，仅用初始化时的损失梯度几何推导文本/视觉提示的参数分配，在固定总预算下显著提升鲁棒性，最高超越最强基线7.75%，探测耗时降低99.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据中的噪声标签会偏置提示更新，削弱提示学习适配效果；已有鲁棒提示学习工作集中于抑制不可靠更新，而"提示分配"（文本提示与视觉提示之间的参数预算划分）这一关键因素被忽视。合适的分配随任务而异且先验未知，穷举候选配置训练代价高昂。

Method: GPrompt为免训练方法：在提示优化之前、于初始化阶段，用小样本图像与生成的伪标签作为共享监督进行初始化探测，提取配对的文本与视觉损失梯度；通过trace归一化Gram矩阵刻画其几何性质，包括模态内集中度与模态间重叠度；据此推导闭式分配解，并将其投影到精确参数预算下的可行提示数量上，从而实现任务特定的配置选择而无需训练候选配置。

Result: 在十一个数据集上，GPrompt最高超过最强基线7.75%，所选配置接近穷举训练找到的最优分配；梯度探测的平均运行时间比穷举候选训练低99.9%。

Conclusion: 提示分配是提升鲁棒提示学习的一个关键且此前被忽视的维度；GPrompt表明仅凭初始化梯度信息即可高效、准确地确定任务特定的文本—视觉提示分配，兼顾鲁棒性与计算效率。

Abstract: Prompt learning provides a parameter-efficient way to adapt pretrained vision--language models through text and visual prompts, but noisy labels prevalent in real-world data inevitably bias prompt updates and thereby undermine adaptation effectiveness. Prior work on robust prompt learning focuses on suppressing unreliable updates under noisy supervision. We uncover a pivotal yet overlooked aspect--prompt allocation: shifting the parameter budget between text and visual prompts can substantially enhance robustness while keeping the training objective and total budget fixed. However, suitable prompt allocations vary across tasks and are unknown a priori, rendering brute-force candidate training computationally prohibitive. We propose GPrompt, a training-free method that derives allocation between text and visual prompts using only loss gradients at initialization before prompt optimization. Using a small sample of images and generated pseudo-labels as shared supervision, GPrompt performs an initialization probe to extract paired text and visual loss gradients, and characterizes the geometry of their trace-normalized Gram matrices through both concentration within each modality and overlap between modalities. GPrompt then derives a closed-form allocation, and projects it onto feasible prompt counts under the exact parameter budget, enabling task-specific selection without training candidate configurations. Experiments across eleven datasets show that GPrompt outperforms the strongest baseline by up to 7.75% while selecting configurations close to the best allocation identified through exhaustive training. The average runtime of gradient probe across datasets is 99.9% lower than that of exhaustive candidate training.

</details>


### [189] [MIMORPHEUS: Rethinking Cross-Modal Supervision for Human Pose Estimation from Radar Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29341)
*Shizhao Zhang,Yanyan Zhang,Deshui Yu,Jun Pan,Guangyou Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIMORPHEUS：以条件扩散从3D人体姿态生成MIMO雷达回波，缓解穿墙雷达人体姿态估计的数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穿墙MIMO雷达人体姿态估计依赖成对的雷达-姿态数据，采集耗时费力；大规模视觉姿态数据集无对应回波；物理仿真需散射建模专业知识且存在仿真-真实域差。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：MIMO-VAE依据通道与快时间采样结构压缩回波并构建隐空间；SkeleCoder通过对比对齐学习与回波特征对齐的姿态特征；以姿态特征为条件在MIMO-VAE隐空间做条件扩散生成，再由VAE解码器重建回波。

Result: 数据增强实验中，四个雷达姿态估计器的MPJPE降低6.8%–11.2%；在三个公开姿态数据集上的迁移实验表明视觉姿态标注可提供有效雷达训练数据。

Conclusion: 生成式回波合成可同时用于雷达数据增强与视觉数据集姿态标注的雷达模态补全，训练完成后可对同一记录姿态生成多样化回波。

Abstract: Deep learning has shown promise for estimating human pose with multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) radar through walls. Building paired radar and pose datasets demands considerable time and manual effort, while large visual pose datasets provide no corresponding radar echoes. Physical simulation can supply synthetic signals, but requires expertise in scattering models and can leave a gap between simulated and real data. To address this challenge, we introduce MIMORPHEUS, a framework that generates radar echoes from 3D human poses through conditional diffusion. To prepare echo and pose representations, MIMO-VAE compresses the echoes according to their channel and fast time sample structure, while SkeleCoder learns pose features through contrastive alignment with echo features. These pose features then guide diffusion in the MIMO-VAE latent space, and the VAE decoder reconstructs echoes from the generated latents. Once trained, the generator augments radar data by producing different echoes for a recorded pose and supplies the radar modality for pose annotations in existing visual datasets. In data augmentation experiments, our method reduces MPJPE by 6.8%-11.2% across four radar pose estimators. Transfer experiments with three public pose datasets also show that visual pose annotations can supply useful radar training data. Code will be made publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [190] [A Multi-Criteria Evaluation of Color Normalization Methods in Histopathology: Nuclei Preservation, Artifact Sensitivity, Background Fidelity, and Downstream Performanc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29348)
*Falah Jabar,Aria Torkpour,Pasquale Lombardi,Masoud Tafavvoghi,Per Niklas Benzler Waaler,Sigve Andersen,Ilona Urbarova,David J. Pinato,Mehrdad Rakaee,Tom Donnem,Lill-Tove Rasmussen Busund,Elin Richardsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统基准测试11种颜色归一化方法与7种输入色彩空间，指出无单一最优方法、图像级指标难以预测下游性能，并发布WSI-QCN流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习细胞核检测与分类模型因染色和扫描差异在不同医疗中心间泛化性差；颜色归一化虽旨在减小这些差异，但其对核结构、背景保留、伪影敏感性及下游检测/分类性能的影响尚未被系统评估。

Method: 在7个公开与私有细胞核数据集上，对11种经典与深度学习归一化方法进行基准测试；同时考察7种输入色彩空间的影响；评估维度涵盖核结构、背景与伪影敏感性，以及检测与分类性能。

Result: 各归一化方法在核结构、背景及伪影敏感性上表现不同；但检测与分类性能在数据集间的差异大于归一化方法间的差异，输入色彩空间影响有限；无单一方法在所有指标上最优，图像级指标无法可靠预测下游性能。

Conclusion: 需发展面向细胞核与结构的归一化方法；作者提出并公开WSI-QCN流水线，用于全切片图像的质量评估与颜色归一化，源码已在GitHub发布。

Abstract: Deep learning models for nuclei detection and classification may generalize poorly across medical centers because of variations in staining and scanning. Although color normalization aims to reduce these variations, its effects on nuclear structure, background preservation, artifact sensitivity, and nuclei detection and classification performance have not been systematically evaluated. We present a procedure to benchmark 11 classical and deep learning normalization methods using 7 public and private nuclei datasets. Additionally, we investigate the effect of 7 input color spaces. The normalization methods differed in their effects on nuclear structure, background, and sensitivity to artifacts. However, detection and classification performance varied more across datasets than across normalization methods, and the input color space had limited influence. No single method performed best on all criteria, and image-level metrics did not reliably predict downstream performance, highlighting the need for nuclei- and structure-aware normalization methods. To support this work, we introduce WSI Quality Control and Normalization (WSI-QCN), a publicly available pipeline for robust whole-slide image quality assessment and color normalization; the source code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [191] [Polytropos: What Do Vision-Language Model Descriptions Make of a Cultural Object?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29355)
*Yuechen Jiang,Enze Zhang,Yuyan Wang,Mohsinul Kabir,Chenying Lin,Sijia Li,Qianqian Xie,Stavroula Golfomitsou,Konstantinos Arvanitis,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Polytropos 基准，通过将文化物品的模型描述"回译为图像"并与原照片比对，量化描述保留了多少"具体性"。在 374 件博物馆藏品、1,870 条模型描述与 230 条策展人描述的对比中，重建图像更易被识别为所属文化（54.55%–59.09% vs. 照片 52.14%），但仅 0.49% 的重建被认出是原始具体物件，且 16%–21% 的物件发生类别判定翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型已成为普遍的"描述者"，其描述反过来塑造人和模型对文化的认知。文化描述倾向于把"具体个别之物"抽象为"类型化特征"，而描述的缺失恰恰暴露描述者的局限。作者希望建立可量化的评测方式，衡量描述究竟保留了多少对象自身的特殊性。

Method: 构建 Polytropos 基准：收集大都会艺术博物馆与盖蒂的 374 件藏品，获得 5 个模型共 1,870 条描述及 230 条策展人描述；对每条描述用图像生成模型"重建"为图像，再与原照片进行识别/比对实验（文化归属判定、具体物件辨识），统计重建是否更易被当作该文化的代表，以及类别判定是否被改变。

Result: 重建图像被识别为所记录文化的比例（54.55%–59.09%）高于原照片（52.14%）；16%–21% 的物件在重建后判定结果发生翻转；仅 0.49% 的重建被识别为具体的那一件物件。与策展人相比，模型在"丢失所记录文化"上的比例相近，但"新增/泛化文化特征"更多。模型描述给每个物件赋予多重"面孔"，每一个都典型于其文化，却几乎没有一个属于物件本身。

Conclusion: 模型描述系统性地将个别文化物件类型化、原型化：它们能很好地代表"文化类别"，却几乎不保留"这一件"的独特性，甚至使物件更趋刻板。该基准为评估描述保真性与文化描述的"丢失—增益"结构提供了可复用的测量框架。

Abstract: Vision-language models have become a new kind of describer, writing about almost anything people see, and their descriptions now shape how cultures are known by people and by other models. Descriptions of cultures tend to turn particular things into types, and what a description loses shows what its describer lacks. We introduce Polytropos, a benchmark that measures what model descriptions of cultural objects keep of the particular object by turning each description back into an image and comparing it with the original photograph. On 374 objects from the Metropolitan Museum of Art and the Getty, with 1,870 descriptions from five models and 230 written by curators, reconstructions are recognised as the recorded culture more often than the photographs, at 54.55% to 59.09% against 52.14%, yet 16% to 21% of objects change outcome and only 0.49% of reconstructions are identified as the specific object. On the same objects, models lose recorded cultures at a rate similar to curators but gain more. Model descriptions give each object many faces, each typical of its culture and almost none the object itself.

</details>


### [192] [Beyond Attention Maps: Understanding and Improving Medical MLLMs via Visual Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29361)
*Yichen Zhao,Fenghe Tang,Wenxin Ma,Rongsheng Wang,Zihao Wan,Shaohua Kevin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文反思了多模态大模型（MLLM）中以空间注意力图为视觉感知关键隐含假设的不足，指出经由 value 检索到的视觉内容才是更关键的要素，并提出 VIVID 蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 方法大多关注模型是否注意到正确的视觉区域，并隐含地将空间注意力图视为有效视觉感知的首要关键。作者希望检验这一假设是否完整。

Method: 构建了一个医学 VQA 数据集，包含解剖结构对应但线索不同的成对平扫（NC）与增强（CE）图像，用于对比分析。通过成对图像上的注意力模式观察，以及受控的双向干预（分别干预视觉 value 与 attention weight）比较预测变化幅度，定位关键因素。据此提出 VIVID（VIsual Value Intervention Distillation），利用增强视觉 value 识别并筛选与有用视觉证据对齐的教师纠正，改进从 CE 到 NC 的 on-policy 蒸馏。

Result: 成对 NC/CE 图像上，MLLM 常表现出相似的注意力模式却给出相反的答案；受控双向干预下，干预视觉 value 引起的预测变化显著大于干预注意力权重。在六个内部与外部测试集上，VIVID 在不同模型规模与架构下均持续优于现有 on-policy 蒸馏方法，推理时仅需 NC 图像，并在通用领域 VQA 基准上保持较强的零样本性能。

Conclusion: 空间注意力并非有效视觉感知的唯一关键，经 value 检索的视觉内容才是超越空间注意力的重要因素；基于该发现设计的 VIVID 提供了一种有效的视觉价值引导蒸馏范式。

Abstract: Current MLLM methods largely focus on whether models attend to the right visual regions, implicitly treating spatial attention map as the primary key of effective visual perception. To examine this assumption, we introduce a medical VQA dataset with paired non-contrast (NC) and contrast-enhanced (CE) images that share corresponding anatomy while providing different cues. We show that this view is incomplete: across paired NC and CE medical images, MLLMs often exhibit similar attention patterns despite producing contrasting answers, while controlled bidirectional interventions induce substantially larger prediction changes on visual values than attention weights. These results identify the visual content retrieved through values as a critical factor in improving perception beyond spatial attention alone. Guided by this finding, we propose VIVID (VIsual Value Intervention Distillation), which uses enhanced visual values to identify and refine teacher corrections aligned with useful visual evidence to improve on-policy distillation from CE to NC images. Across six internal and external test sets, VIVID consistently outperforms existing on-policy distillation methods across different model scales and architectures, requires only NC images at inference, and maintains strong zero-shot performance on general-domain VQA benchmarks. The project page is available at https://qiuzyc.github.io/VIVID.

</details>


### [193] [Scenario Compliant Indoor Pedestrian Trajectory Reconstruction from Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29366)
*Qi Dang,Luis Quintero,Shibo Zhu,Oskar Juhlin,Xiaodan Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于条件扩散模型的场景图条件生成框架，将密集室内地图作为结构化输入并进行端点嵌入，以重建物理可行的室内行人轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率与生成式方法在室外GPS轨迹插补上有效，但在室内因墙体、走廊等密集几何约束而失效；标准模型将轨迹视为无约束坐标序列，产生高碰撞率的物理不可行路径。

Method: 基于条件扩散模型的生成框架：用CNN编码器将密集场景地图作为结构化空间输入，引导迭代去噪过程；并采用端点感知机制，将起止观测点直接嵌入场景图，通过图像级特征提供空间锚点。

Result: 在模拟室内轨迹与真实Shopper数据集、3个训练种子上取得对比生成方法中最低误差；模拟数据上场景条件使碰撞率与无效率分别降低19%和46%，真实数据增益一致但较小（模型很少学会绕障路径）；相比从不碰撞的最短路径规划器，本方法更好地刻画真实运动变异性（CRPS低31%），但停留时序仅部分重建。

Conclusion: 显式环境上下文可提升物理一致性，但在真实室内空间中重建逼真人体运动仍具挑战。

Abstract: Pedestrian trajectory reconstruction is vital for applications ranging from security surveillance to indoor navigation. While existing probabilistic and generative frameworks have shown success in outdoor GPS-based trajectory imputation, they often fail in indoor settings where movement is strictly governed by dense geometric constraints such as walls and corridors. Standard models typically treat trajectories as unconstrained coordinate sequences, leading to physically infeasible paths with high collision rates. To address this, we propose a map-conditioned generative framework based on conditional diffusion models. Our approach explicitly integrates dense scenario maps as structured spatial inputs via a CNN-based encoder, allowing environmental geometry to guide the iterative denoising process. Furthermore, we employ an endpoint-aware mechanism by embedding observations (start and end points) directly into the scenario map, providing critical spatial anchors through image-level features. Experiments on simulated indoor trajectories and the real-world Shopper trajectory dataset, with three training seeds, show that our method achieves the lowest errors among the compared generative methods. Scene conditioning reduces the collision and invalid rates by 19\% and 46\% on the simulated dataset, whereas on real shopper data the gains are consistent but small, mainly because the model rarely learns to route around obstacles between the endpoints. Compared with a shortest-path planner, which never collides, our method better captures the variability of real motion (31\% lower CRPS), but the timing of stops is reconstructed only partially. These results show that explicit environmental context improves physical consistency, while reconstructing realistic human motion in real indoor spaces remains challenging. Our code is available at https://github.com/dang-qi/trajectory_reconstruction

</details>


### [194] [PINE: Point, Instance and Normal Estimation in One-Step Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29389)
*Zeyu Ren,Jie Ni,Rui Qian,Jiahao Wei,Hao Tang,Yongtai Liu,Zeyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PINE 是一步式 Omni Diffusion Transformer，单次前向即同时预测点图、表面法向与实例图，实现几何与语义的统一建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景理解需要几何（空间结构）与语义（物体分割）两方面信息，现有模型依赖分离的网络或多次推理，效率低且缺乏统一性。

Method: 将三种图视为 token 流，用流掩码控制流间交互；提出相机感知的射线编码替代像素位置编码；在注意力 logits 中引入预训练 DINOv3 特征的亲和度（Grounding attention）。

Result: 仅用 139K 合成图像训练几何部分，在有/无内参条件下均取得 SOTA 点图效果，法向取得最佳平均排名；以不足 SAM 1% 的训练图像在大物体上超过 SAM；预测三图比 MoGe-3+SAM 快 5.7 倍。

Conclusion: 统一的一步式扩散 Transformer 可在单次前向中高效输出几何与语义多任务结果，兼顾精度与速度。

Abstract: Understanding a scene requires both geometry and semantics: geometry recovers its spatial structure, and semantics separates it into objects. Current models obtain the two from separate networks or separate passes. We present PINE, a one-step Omni Diffusion Transformer that predicts the point map, the surface normals, and the instance map of an image in one forward pass. PINE treats each map as a token stream, and a stream mask decides which streams interact. A camera-aware ray encoding positions every token by its camera ray rather than its pixel. Grounding attention adds the affinity of pretrained DINOv3 features to the attention logits. Trained on only 139K synthetic images for geometry, PINE achieves state-of-the-art point maps whether or not intrinsics are given and the best average rank on surface normals. It also exceeds SAM on large objects with less than 1 percent of its training images, and it predicts all three maps 5.7x faster than MoGe-3 with SAM.

</details>


### [195] [AgroVG+: Generalized Visual Grounding in Agriculture with Region-Supervised Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29396)
*Haocheng Li,Juepeng Zheng,Guilong Xiao,Kaiqi Du,Gengmeng Pu,Haohuan Fu,Jianxi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AgroVG+农业广义视觉定位数据集（专家撰写指代表达，含单目标/多目标/无目标情形）与ReSAG框架（区域证据监督+显式BOX/END生成终止协议）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业场景的广义视觉定位需同时定位所有匹配自然语言描述的物体，并识别无匹配目标的情形；现有数据与建模方法对此支持不足。

Method: 数据集由农业专家基于图像内容与目标集合撰写并审核指代表达，含7,404张图像、10,081条查询，覆盖六类农业场景族及单目标、多目标、目标缺失三类情形。模型ReSAG在答案生成前计算共享空间证据分布，对每个目标施加等权重监督；采用BOX/END协议在框边界处选择继续生成框或终止，坐标使用原始语言词表生成；区域与序列目标联合训练共享语言解码器。

Result: 在AgroVG+上，ReSAG在单目标与多目标精确集合准确率上优于仅坐标微调；消融实验显示其总体准确率高于单独使用任一组件。

Conclusion: 专家标注的广义定位数据与区域监督加显式生成终止的联合建模可有效提升农业视觉定位的集合级准确性。

Abstract: Generalized visual grounding in agriculture requires localizing all objects that match a natural-language description and recognizing when no matching target is present. We introduce AgroVG+, a dataset whose referring expressions are all written and reviewed by agricultural experts based on image content and corresponding target sets. The dataset comprises 7,404 images and 10,081 query records across six agricultural scene families, covering single-target, multi-target, and target-absent cases. We also propose ReSAG (Region-Supervised Action Generation), a framework that couples regional evidence supervision with explicit box-generation and termination decisions. ReSAG assigns each target an equally weighted supervision term on a shared spatial evidence distribution computed before answer generation. The BOX/END protocol exposes a choice between generating another box and terminating the response at box boundaries, while coordinates are generated using the original language vocabulary. Regional and sequence objectives jointly train a shared language decoder. On AgroVG+, ReSAG outperforms coordinate-only fine-tuning on single- and multi-target exact-set accuracy, and achieves higher aggregate accuracy than either component alone in ablations.

</details>


### [196] [MSBench: Benchmarking Script-Controlled Multi-Shot Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29417)
*Tao Huang,ZiHao Liang,Junhao Liu,Junzhe Li,Yuancheng Wei,Sifan Zuo,Beiming Yu,Taojin Liu,Zhuoyang Li,Bing Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MSBench基准，围绕Scene-Entity-Shot结构化剧本评测文本驱动多镜头视频生成的可控性，含四维11子指标，14个系统评测显示多镜头控制是主要区分点，系统排名与人类偏好Spearman相关0.87。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V模型能生成逼真的单镜头短片，但多镜头生成需要切分时序、转场风格、镜头语言与跨镜头连续性等额外能力；现有基准要么只评测单镜头，要么仅检查所请求镜头是否出现，而不评测镜头何时切换、如何衔接，因此文本对多镜头输出的可控程度仍未被量化。

Method: 构建MSBench基准，基于Scene-Entity-Shot结构化迷你剧本，明确规定每个镜头的时间跨度、相机状态、内容与出向转场；设计涵盖视觉质量、动态质量、语义对齐、跨镜头一致性四个维度、共11个子指标的混合评测框架，并在14个商用与开源系统上开展实验，辅以人工评估验证。

Result: 视觉质量在各系统间区分度很低，而多镜头控制能力成为主要区分因素：许多模型主要在场景变化处切换，并将请求的渐变转场渲染为硬切；人工评估验证MSBench的有效性，其系统排名与人类偏好的Spearman相关系数达0.87。

Conclusion: MSBench填补了多镜头视频生成可控性评测的空白，表明当前系统的短板集中在切分时序与转场控制而非画质，为后续模型在镜头语言层面的改进提供了可量化、与人类判断高度一致的评测依据。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) models can now generate visually plausible short clips, but multi-shot generation requires a different set of skills: composing multiple shots with intended cut timing, transition style, camera language, and cross-shot continuity. How far this multi-shot output can be controlled from text remains unmeasured: existing benchmarks either evaluate single shots or, when they provide shot-level scripts, typically check whether the requested shots appear but not when they change or how they are joined. We introduce MSBench, a benchmark for controllable text-driven multi-shot video generation built around structured Scene-Entity-Shot mini-scripts that specify each shot's time span, camera state, content, and outgoing transition. MSBench defines a four-dimensional hybrid evaluation framework spanning Visual Quality, Dynamic Quality, Semantic Alignment, and Cross-Shot Consistency with 11 sub-metrics. Experiments on 14 commercial and open-source systems show that visual quality does little to separate the systems, whereas multi-shot control does: many models cut mainly on scene changes and render requested gradual transitions as hard cuts. Human evaluations further validate MSBench, with its system ranking reaching a Spearman correlation of 0.87 with human preferences. The project page is available at https://totalhuang.github.io/MSBench-page/.

</details>


### [197] [A colour-marker tracker for pose estimation of slender surgical instruments: vision-language-model-guided adaptive colour calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29421)
*Jaesung Hong,Dong Won Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 细长手术器械需要小尺寸窄标记，彩色条纹标记虽合适但图像颜色会因光照、打印与相机设置漂移。作者提出基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自适应颜色校准，并与两种规则方法（初始颜色更新、颜色自适应）比较；VLM引导在严重颜色偏移场景下显著优于规则方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细长手术器械的相机位姿估计需要小而窄的标记，彩色条纹标记满足尺寸要求，但其图像颜色相对设计值发生漂移；而颜色只能在标记被找到之后才能校正，导致依赖设计颜色的方法陷入循环依赖问题。因此需要一种不依赖设计颜色即可定位标记并建立颜色模型的自适应校准方案。

Method: 提出VLM引导的自适应颜色校准：用微调后的视觉语言模型在不依赖设计颜色的情况下寻找标记，并由其像素构建颜色剖面。对比两类规则方法：初始颜色更新（跟踪开始后一次性重测颜色）与颜色自适应（跟踪过程中持续更新）；规则方法仍以设计颜色完成初始检测后再重测颜色。另用仿真评估色相边界对曝光增益的鲁棒性，并与固定亮度阈值方法比较。

Result: Recall/F1(%)：672对合成图像上，初始颜色更新92.0/95.7、颜色自适应87.8/93.3、VLM引导98.2/99.1；八段退化实验室视频上分别为51.3/67.2、48.8/65.0、83.8/87.0；在颜色强烈偏移的da Vinci手术机器人视频上分别为38.9/55.2、19.7/29.7、100/100，VLM引导找到全部234个标记且无误检，规则方法从未找到紫/青色标记。仿真中曝光增益0.5–2.0时两条纹色相边界移动小于0.1像素，固定亮度阈值方法为2.9–4.0像素。

Conclusion: 退化轻微时，无需训练、无需GPU的规则方法已足够；颜色变化剧烈时，性能增益主要来自不依赖设计颜色的VLM引导候选搜索。将彩色条纹标记与自适应颜色校准结合，可实现小标记对细长手术器械的跟踪。

Abstract: Slender surgical instruments need small, narrow markers for camera-based pose estimation. A colour-stripe marker fits this requirement, but its colours in the image drift from the design values with lighting, printing and camera settings. Because the colours can only be corrected after the marker has been found, methods that rely on the design colours face a circular problem. We propose vision-language-model (VLM)-based adaptive colour calibration and compare it with two rule-based methods, which re-measure the colours from detections made with the design colours: Initial colour update does so once after tracking starts, and Colour adaptation continuously during tracking. VLM guidance uses a fine-tuned VLM to find the markers without relying on the design colours and builds the colour profile from their pixels. Recall/F1 (%) for Initial colour update, Colour adaptation and VLM guidance were 92.0/95.7, 87.8/93.3 and 98.2/99.1 on 672 synthetic image pairs; 51.3/67.2, 48.8/65.0 and 83.8/87.0 on eight degraded laboratory videos; and 38.9/55.2, 19.7/29.7 and 100/100 on a surgical-robot (da Vinci) video with strongly shifted colours. In that video the rule-based methods never found the purple/cyan marker, whereas VLM guidance found all 234 markers with no false positives. In a simulation, the hue boundary between the two stripes moved by less than 0.1 pixel for exposure gains of 0.5-2.0, compared with 2.9-4.0 pixels for a boundary found with a fixed brightness threshold. When the degradation is mild, the rule-based methods, which need no training and no GPU, are sufficient. When the colours change greatly, the gain comes from a VLM-guided candidate search that does not rely on the design colours. Combining colour-stripe markers with adaptive colour calibration enables tracking of slender surgical instruments with small markers.

</details>


### [198] [HorizonFlow: Few-Step Video Distillation in 600 Iterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29431)
*Xianglong Guo,Haobo Xu,Zanyi Wang,Zhen Lei,Chang Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HorizonFlow 将 DMD 视频蒸馏中的运动校正与布局校正解耦，通过在 DMD 损失上仅修改回归目标，实现仅 600 步、8.4k 样本的高效少步视频生成蒸馏，性能超越现有 Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 少步模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 DMD 的视频蒸馏存在动作不自然与训练代价高的问题。作者将其归因于 DMD 梯度联合优化外观、布局与运动而不区分其学习动态：携带外观与布局的静态分量信噪比远高于运动分量，导致逐帧质量数百步即收敛而运动严重滞后；同时梯度缺乏两类信号——(1) 视频 VAE 将多帧编码为单个 latent 帧（Wan2.1 为 4 帧），梯度对运动在这些帧间均匀分布缺乏引导，造成周期性卡顿；(2) DMD 未约束最终采样步保持先前步骤设定的布局，导致物体在运动时融合与漂移。

Method: HorizonFlow 保留 DMD 损失，仅平移其回归目标，分别在运动分量与正交的布局分量上施加训练期校正。Chunk Evenness 将每个 latent 块的运动在其对应帧间均匀分配以解决周期性卡顿；Semantic Commitment 将粗粒度布局锚定到教师模型对学生最后一步的补全结果上，以解决布局漂移。

Result: 仅 600 次迭代、8.4k 训练样本，即将 Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 蒸馏为 4 步模型，样本量较先前工作少 4.6–304 倍。在 VBench 上优于所有已公开的少步 Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 模型；同一方案迁移至 Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B，学生模型超过英伟达的蒸馏模型。

Conclusion: 通过解耦运动与布局的校正目标，HorizonFlow 在极低训练成本下实现了高质量少步视频生成，并具备良好的跨模型可迁移性。

Abstract: Distribution matching distillation (DMD) enables fast video generation by distilling a multi-step teacher into a few-step student. However, DMD-based methods often suffer from unnatural motion and costly training. We trace these limitations to the DMD gradient, which jointly optimizes appearance, layout, and motion without distinguishing their learning dynamics. Its static component, which carries appearance and layout, has a much higher signal-to-noise ratio than its motion component. As a result, per-frame quality converges within a few hundred iterations, whereas motion lags far behind. Beyond being noisy, the gradient also lacks two signals. (1) Video VAEs encode several frames into one latent frame (four in Wan2.1), and the gradient provides little guidance for spreading motion over these frames, causing periodic stutter. (2) DMD does not constrain the final sampling step to preserve the layout set by earlier steps, so objects merge and drift once the video moves. We propose HorizonFlow, which decouples the correction of motion from that of layout. It keeps the DMD loss and only shifts its regression target, with one training-only correction in the motion component and one in the orthogonal layout component. Chunk Evenness addresses (1) by spreading the motion of each latent chunk evenly over its frames, and Semantic Commitment addresses (2) by anchoring the coarse layout to the teacher's completion of the student's last step. In only 600 iterations, HorizonFlow distills Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B into a 4-step model using 8.4k training samples, 4.6-304$\times$ fewer than prior work. On VBench, HorizonFlow outperforms all released few-step Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B models. The same recipe transfers to Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B, where our student outperforms NVIDIA's distilled model. Code: https://github.com/XIANGLONGGUO/HorizonFlow-repo

</details>


### [199] [Ride on Anything: Contact-Coupled Monocular 4D Reconstruction of a Rider and Horse in Camera and World Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29435)
*Zehang Weng,Ci Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的RoA方法，将骑手与坐骑两个冻结估计器在测试时耦合，修正沿相机轴的交互误差，使骑手真正"坐"在马背上并与马同步运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立分别估计人与马时，二者各自拟合轮廓良好，但骑手会悬浮于马侧、穿模、沿马背滑动并相对抖动；这些误差沿相机轴方向，在输入视角下不可见，因此需要显式建模人-动物交互。

Method: 免训练通用框架：SAM 3D Body（骑手）+ 4DEquine（马）。统一帧索引；用由马估计器初始化的像素各向同性相机投影两主体；在马体坐标系下定义交互项（含基于广义绕数的碰撞项，适用于非水密马网格），联合优化有界修正；再用背景相机旋转与MoGe-2视场角提升到水平世界坐标系。

Result: 骨盆-马背间距由15 cm降至1 cm；沿/横跨脊线座位漂移分别降低64%与89%；90分位穿透深度降57%；骑手表面嵌入马体比例由12.1%→7.9%；相对加速度降94%，马的jerk降80%；骑手轮廓IoU由0.59降至0.52（略降）。定性上骑手稳定坐于马背并随马运动。

Conclusion: 交互约束可有效修复不可见的深度方向错位，代价是轻微2D轮廓精度损失；尚缺标定数据做3D精度定量评估；另测试了非本任务的牵马片段并分析失败模式。

Abstract: We address the reconstruction of a horse and its rider from a single video. When a human estimator and a horse estimator are applied independently, both meshes can fit their silhouettes well while the rider hovers beside or passes through the horse, slides along its back and jitters relative to it. These errors lie along the camera axis and are not visible in the input view. We present Ride on Anything (RoA), a general, training-free methodology for reconstructing a person riding an animal, which couples frozen estimators of the rider and the mount at test time. We instantiate it for horseback riding with SAM 3D Body and 4DEquine. RoA aligns both estimators on a common frame index, projects both subjects through one pixel-isotropic camera initialized from the horse estimator, and jointly optimizes bounded corrections under interaction terms defined in the horse's body frame, including a collision term based on generalized winding numbers that remains valid on the non-watertight horse mesh. The result is lifted to a levelled world frame using the camera rotation from the background and the field of view from MoGe-2. On our riding clips, the raw outputs of the two estimators leave the rider's pelvis 15 cm from the horse's back on average. RoA reduces this seat gap to 1 cm, the drift of the seat along and across the spine by 64% and 89%, the 90th-percentile penetration depth by 57%, and the share of rider surface inside the horse from 12.1% to 7.9%. The motion is markedly smoother (94% lower acceleration of the rider relative to the horse, 80% lower horse jerk), while the rider's silhouette IoU decreases from 0.59 to 0.52. Qualitatively, the rider stays seated on the horse and moves together with it. A quantitative evaluation of 3D accuracy awaits calibrated recordings of ridden animals. We also test a clip outside the task, a horse led on foot, and analyse the failure modes.

</details>


### [200] [Seeing Through Preferences: Recovering Hidden Local Preference Neighborhoods for Text-to-Image Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29436)
*Pulkit Bansal,Vivek Srivastava,Shirish Karande*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PrefLens:从稀疏人类偏好标注中恢复"局部偏好邻域",通过围绕偏好图像生成受控感知变化并在奖励空间筛选信息量大的邻居,将单条标注转化为结构化比较集合,无需额外人工标注即可增强对齐监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I偏好对齐依赖大规模成对标注,代价高且监督稀疏;同时偏好比较并非等价,语义差异大的图像对提供的细粒度感知信息有限。作者认为有价值的信息来自人类偏好图像周围的局部邻域——语义对齐的替代样本能暴露有意义的感知与结构差异。

Method: 提出PrefLens框架:(1)围绕人类偏好图像生成受控的感知变化,大体保留语义身份;(2)在预训练奖励模型诱导的"奖励定义偏好空间"中,于各偏好图像周围选取紧凑且信息量大的邻居集合;(3)将每条人类偏好标注转换为结构化邻域比较,扩充监督信号而不需新增人工标注。

Result: 实验与诊断评估表明,恢复出的邻域能捕捉与人类相关的偏好特征,并在多种设置下为偏好对齐提供有效监督。

Conclusion: 局部偏好邻域可从稀疏反馈中被有效恢复,作为低成本、密集且细粒度的偏好监督来源,提升T2I扩散模型的偏好对齐效果。

Abstract: Aligning text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models with human preferences typically relies on large-scale pairwise annotations, which are expensive to obtain and provide only sparse supervision. Moreover, not all preference comparisons contribute equally to learning: comparisons between semantically distant images often reveal little about the fine-grained perceptual factors underlying human judgments. We argue that informative supervision can arise from local preference neighborhoods surrounding human-preferred images, where semantically aligned alternatives expose meaningful perceptual and structural differences. Motivated by this observation, we propose PrefLens, a framework that recovers local preference neighborhoods from sparse human feedback. Specifically, PrefLens generates controlled perceptual variations around preferred images that largely retain semantic identity and selects compact sets of informative neighbors in a reward-defined preference space induced by pretrained reward models. By transforming each human preference annotation into a structured neighborhood of informative comparisons, PrefLens enriches supervision without requiring additional human labeling. Our experiments and diagnostic evaluations show that the recovered neighborhoods capture human-relevant preference characteristics and provide effective supervision for preference alignment across diverse settings.

</details>


### [201] [Fill in the Blanks: Non-Autoregressive Schema-Parallel Key Information Extraction with CTC Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29460)
*Shaked Perek,Avihu Dekel,Ben Wiesel,Sivan Harary,Eli Schwartz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种非自回归抽取器：直接从schema写出结构、用计数pass确定列表长度、并以CTC在固定槽位块中并行解码所有值，从而在KIE任务上实现与文档长度无关的固定少次前向，速度更快且精度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM通过逐token生成JSON做文档结构化抽取，解码步数随答案长度线性增长，效率低。而KIE任务的schema在解码前已确定键、嵌套与列表结构，仅值未知，因此自回归式顺序生成存在大量冗余。

Method: 非自回归抽取器：（1）依据schema直接写出答案结构；（2）通过一次计数pass为每个列表确定长度；（3）并行预测所有值，前向pass数与文档长度无关；（4）每个值用CTC在其专属槽位块中解码，无需预测长度；（5）块结束时仍开放的值分配更大token预算。另将该抽取器作为投机解码草稿模型，配合自身自回归基座。

Result: 在VAREX、CORD、FUNSD零样本设置下，4B模型平均比扩散VLM基线快3.5倍（最高5.6倍）且更准确，每文档仅需4–6次前向（块扩散需12–74次）。投机解码下比自回归解码快8.3倍（最高10.4倍），精度差距0.4个百分点内；比投机扩散解码快3.3倍且高13.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 利用KIE的schema先验可有效去除自回归冗余，实现固定少次前向的高效高精度抽取，并可作为强草稿模型兼容投机解码。模型与代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) extract structured data from documents by generating the requested JSON one token at a time, so their sequential steps grow with the length of the answer. Yet in key information extraction (KIE) the schema already fixes the answer's structure: keys, nesting and lists are known before decoding, and only the values are unknown. We exploit this with a non-autoregressive extractor that writes the structure from the schema, sizes every list with a count pass, and predicts all values in parallel, in a fixed number of passes that does not grow with the document. Each value is decoded with connectionist temporal classification (CTC) in its own block of slots, so its length never has to be predicted, and a value that is still open after its block gets a larger token budget. Zero-shot on VAREX, CORD and FUNSD, our 4B extractor is 3.5$\times$ faster than the diffusion-VLM competition on average (up to 5.6$\times$) and more accurate, with 4--6 forward passes per document where block diffusion needs 12--74. The same extractor also drafts for speculative decoding with its own autoregressive base: it runs 8.3$\times$ faster than autoregressive decoding (up to 10.4$\times$) within 0.4 points of its accuracy, and 3.3$\times$ faster than speculative diffusion decoding while 13.0 points more accurate. We release the model and code.

</details>


### [202] [Separating Action Allocation from Image Matching in Visual Token Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29477)
*Chenxi Wang,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将自适应视觉token路由器的增益分解为"分配匹配度"与"高分辨率使用频率"两部分，发现匹配带来的收益被任务层面动作频率选择不当导致的损失抵消，且以任务标签驱动的查表策略优于离散路由器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应视觉token路由器追求在节省计算的同时保留回答所需图像细节，但更高的平均分可能源于对单图做出更好选择，也可能仅源于在需要高分辨率的任务上更频繁地启用了高分辨率。原文动机即在于区分这两种来源，从而判断路由器的真实有效性。

Method: 采用控制变量式的反事实对比：在每个任务内固定动作计数，将路由器的分配结果与"相同动作、相同图像"的随机配对进行比较，从而剥离动作频率的贡献、单独衡量匹配质量。同时在等权重的ChartQA与TextVQA上评测Qwen2.5-VL-3B与7B，并与外部选择器CARES所选连续分辨率的互换实验、以及带任务标签的查表策略进行对照。

Result: 在ChartQA与TextVQA等权重设置下，两个模型均从匹配中获益（7B提升1.71分），但相对任务表因动作频率不当损失更大（7B损失3.95分），净效应为负。交换CARES选择的连续分辨率同样导致7B质量下降。当请求自带任务标签时，任务表比离散路由器高1.92分，并在完整7B请求上平均节省31.76 ms。

Conclusion: 路由器表面上的平均分提升主要来自动作频率而非精细的逐图匹配，其分配能力尚不足以弥补频率层面的偏差；在任务标签可得时，简单的任务级查表在质量与延迟上均优于自适应离散路由器。这表明应以固定动作预算的对照实验评估路由器，并重新审视其设计价值。

Abstract: Adaptive visual token routers aim to save computation while preserving the image detail needed to answer each question. A higher average score could reflect better choices for individual images or more frequent use of high resolution on tasks that need it. We distinguish the two by keeping action counts fixed within each task and comparing the router's assignments with random pairings of the same actions and images. On equally weighted ChartQA and TextVQA, both Qwen2.5-VL-3B and -7B gain from matching but lose more from their action frequencies relative to a task table, with a 1.71-point gain and a 3.95-point loss on 7B. Exchanging the continuous resolutions chosen by the external selector CARES also lowers quality on 7B. When requests arrive with task labels, the table outscores the discrete router by 1.92 points and saves 31.76 ms per complete 7B request on average.

</details>


### [203] [Neutron Imaging Enhancement for Lithium Batteries Based on Swin-Transformer and Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29479)
*Hansheng Ding,Fanxu Meng,Tian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出改进Swin-Transformer结合空域图像融合的锂电池中子图像增强方法，兼顾去噪与边缘/对比度增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子成像对锂等轻元素敏感，适合锂电池无损检测与内部结构演化分析，但中子源通量有限、探测器性能受限，导致图像存在混合噪声、低对比度和边缘模糊，妨碍电池内部状态准确评估。

Method: 1）改进Swin-Transformer，引入MDTA与GRN提取并重建特征，抑制高密度白斑及高斯-泊松混合噪声；2）引导滤波结合梯度自适应掩膜进行选择性边缘增强，CLAHE提升局部对比度；3）基于空间频率(SF)的局部分块加权融合机制，自适应融合边缘增强图与对比度均衡图。

Result: 与BM3D、DnCNN、GAN相比，在全参考与无参考评价上均表现优异：最佳PSNR 33.0517 dB、SSIM 0.9637，NR-IQA低至0.1794、NIQE低至4.0974；减少伪影与过平滑。

Conclusion: 该方法提升了锂电池中子图像的视觉保真度与结构可辨识性，在去噪与细节保留之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Neutron imaging is highly sensitive to light elements such as lithium and is therefore valuable for non-destructive testing (NDT) and analyzing internal structural evolution in lithium batteries. However, limited neutron-source flux and detector performance often result in images with mixed noise, low contrast, and blurred edges, hindering accurate assessment of battery internal states. To address these issues, this paper proposes a lithium-battery neutron image enhancement method combining an improved Swin-Transformer with spatial-domain image fusion. First, an improved Swin-Transformer incorporating Multi-Dconv Head Transposed Attention (MDTA) and Global Response Normalization (GRN) extracts and reconstructs image features to suppress high-density white spots and Gaussian-Poisson mixed noise. Second, guided filtering and a gradient-adaptive mask are used for selective edge enhancement, while Contrast Limited Adaptive Histogram Equalization (CLAHE) improves local contrast. Finally, a spatial-frequency (SF)-based local block-weighted fusion mechanism adaptively combines the edge-enhanced and contrast-equalized images to preserve microscopic details while suppressing noise. Experiments show that, compared with BM3D, DnCNN, and GAN, the proposed method achieves strong performance in full-reference and no-reference evaluations. Its best PSNR and SSIM reach 33.0517 dB and 0.9637, respectively, while the NR-IQA and Natural Image Quality Evaluator (NIQE) scores are as low as 0.1794 and 4.0974. The method reduces artifacts and over smoothing, improving the visual fidelity and structural identifiability of lithium-battery neutron images.

</details>


### [204] [CAR: Reducing Decoder Output Shift in Visual Token Pruning via Coverage Selection and Attention Reallocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29489)
