*Xixi Jiang,Pingcheng Dong,Chen Yang,Xin Yang,Kwang-Ting Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAR框架，从"输出偏移"角度将视觉token剪枝带来的性能下降分解为图像摘要误差与注意力分配误差，分别用覆盖度选token和闭式logit偏置重分配注意力来缓解，在90%剪枝率下VQA保留93.0%、VG保留77.9%精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法在激进剪枝下性能显著下降，尤其视觉定位(VG)任务；作者希望从解码器层输出偏移的角度阐明性能退化的来源，从而有针对性地设计剪枝策略。

Method: 将剪枝引起的解码器输出偏移闭式分解为两项：图像摘要误差（视觉信息丢失）与注意力分配误差（视觉token注意力份额下降）。对应提出CAR：(1) 覆盖度选择——保留既显著又非冗余的token以收紧摘要误差上界，显著性来自经无标签探针挑选的编码器注意力头；(2) 注意力重分配——用闭式logit偏置把被丢弃token的注意力转移给保留token，恢复注意力份额。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上剪除90%视觉token时，CAR在VQA上保留93.0%的无剪枝精度，VG上保留77.9%，而最佳现有方法在VG上仅26.5%；仅logit偏置一项即可将现有方法的VG精度最高提升24个百分点。多MLLM上VQA、VG与视频理解实验显示良好泛化性与一致性优势。

Conclusion: 视觉token剪枝的性能退化可被闭式归因为摘要误差与注意力分配误差两部分，针对性地做覆盖度选择与注意力重分配能显著缓解激进剪枝下的退化，尤其大幅改善视觉定位，且方法具跨模型、跨任务的通用性。

Abstract: Visual token pruning is an effective way to accelerate multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing methods degrade substantially under aggressive pruning, especially on visual grounding (VG). We analyze the sources of this degradation from the perspective of the output shift that pruning causes in decoder layers, and decompose this shift in closed form into two terms, an image summary error and an attention allocation error. The summary error stems from the loss of visual information, and the allocation error from the drop in the share of attention assigned to visual tokens. To reduce both errors, we propose CAR (Coverage selection and Attention Reallocation), a token pruning framework that restores attention share while maintaining information. Specifically, to minimize the summary error, whose upper bound tightens as the retained tokens better cover all visual tokens, our coverage selection retains tokens that are both salient and non-redundant, with saliency from encoder heads chosen by a label-free probe. To minimize the allocation error, our attention reallocation reassigns the attention of the discarded tokens to the retained ones through a closed-form logit bias. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B with $90\%$ of the visual tokens pruned, CAR retains $93.0\%$ of the unpruned accuracy on visual question answering (VQA) and $77.9\%$ on VG, against $26.5\%$ for the best existing method on VG; the logit bias alone raises the VG accuracy of existing methods by up to $24$ points. Experiments on multiple MLLMs across VQA, VG and video understanding show that CAR generalizes well and consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [205] [STAGE: Structured Test-Time Adaptation with Global Entropic Soft-coupling for Cross-Subject Brain-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29538)
*Yihang Zou,Ruixi Liang,Chunmei Qing,Junpeng Tan,Haichuan Wei,Wanxiang Luo,Gao Wu,Haodong Li,Xiangmin Xu,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAGE 是一种无标签测试时自适应框架，通过矩校准、耦合加权初始化、耦合引导自适应与软耦合排序，恢复跨被试脑-图像对齐，在三个公开数据集上提升跨被试脑到图像检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试脑到图像检索需从未见被试的脑信号中识别其观看的图像，但被试间差异破坏了脑-图像表征的对齐；同时独立查询排序会使预测集中于少数候选，在无目标标签时难以准确检索。

Method: 提出 STAGE（结构化测试时自适应与全局熵软耦合）：1）矩校准减小特征间统计差异；2）耦合加权初始化建立初始对齐；3）耦合引导自适应进一步修正残余特征失配；4）软耦合排序协调各查询间的候选竞争，避免匹配分配过度集中。仅使用无标签目标批次，冻结两个编码器，保持从特征校准到图像检索的一致匹配。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上验证检索性能。THINGS-EEG2 上达 68.25% Top-1、90.47% Top-5，在相同冻结 NICE 特征下较 SCORE recovery 与 CSLS 提升 Top-1 18.52 个百分点；以 200 个查询及覆盖其身份的 200 张图像库校准后，冻结变换在 1,854 张图像中排序仍保持 42.05% Top-1。

Conclusion: STAGE 通过结构化测试时自适应与软耦合排序，在无需目标标签和编码器更新的条件下有效恢复跨被试脑-图像对齐，并缓解候选过度集中的问题，具备良好检索性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Cross-subject brain-to-image retrieval aims to identify viewed images from unseen subjects' brain signals. However, inter-subject variability hinders alignment of brain-image representations, while independent query ranking tends to concentrate predictions on a few candidates, making accurate retrieval difficult without target labels. We propose STAGE (Structured Test-Time Adaptation with Global Entropic Soft-coupling), a structured test-time adaptation framework for recovering cross-subject alignment from uncertain brain-image correspondences. Moment calibration reduces statistical discrepancies between features, while coupling-weighted initialization establishes initial alignment. Coupling-guided adaptation further refines residual feature mismatches, and soft-coupling ranking coordinates candidate competition across queries to prevent overly concentrated matching assignments. Using only unlabeled target batches, STAGE maintains consistent matching from feature calibration to image retrieval while keeping both encoders frozen. Experiments on three public datasets validate STAGE's retrieval performance. On THINGS-EEG2, STAGE achieves 68.25% Top-1 and 90.47% Top-5, improving Top-1 by 18.52 percentage points over SCORE recovery and CSLS on identical frozen NICE features. Calibrated on 200 queries and a 200-image gallery covering their identities, STAGE retains 42.05% Top-1 when ranking 1,854 images with the transform frozen.

</details>


### [206] [Seeing vs. Knowing: Counterfactual Supervision and Correction for 3D-Aware Object Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29542)
*Jian Zahng,Zhijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实状态监督（counterfactual state supervision）与对称反事实校正（symmetric counterfactual correction），训练照片编辑器在生成新视角/遮挡区域时遵循3D状态引导、同时保留照片可见外观，且仅在训练阶段生效、不改变架构与推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对照片中的物体进行旋转、平移或缩放需补全未观测表面、保持可见外观并更新阴影与反射。以目标姿态渲染的重建3D资产虽提供显式几何引导，但其纹理不完美、易与照片冲突；标准成对训练未显式教编辑器"该信谁"。

Method: 1) 反事实状态监督：通过隐藏面重绘、隐藏几何+重渲染阴影反射、可见面纹理替换，构造受控的"照片—状态"冲突样本，教会模型对未见内容服从3D状态、对可见外观服从照片。2) 对称反事实校正：构造共享照片/共享状态的孪生样本，制造指向条件不一致目标的定向潜空间扰动，并训练模型按当前条件回归目标，以在采样过程中维持上述区分能力。均仅用于训练，无需改架构或增加推理步数。

Result: 在OBJect与SpatialEdit-Bench上取得较强的空间编辑性能与内容保持效果；受控消融显示源选择能力提升，且采样过程中对条件信号的响应性得以持续。

Conclusion: 通过训练期反事实监督与对称校正，可在不增加推理成本的前提下使照片编辑器在几何引导与照片外观之间正确取舍，实现高质量空间编辑。

Abstract: Rotating, translating, or rescaling an object in a photograph requires revealing unseen surfaces, preserving visible appearance, and updating shadows and reflections. Reconstructed 3D assets, rendered at the target pose as a 3D state, provide explicit geometric guidance, but their imperfect textures may conflict with the photograph, while standard paired training does not explicitly teach the editor which source to trust. To address this, we propose counterfactual state supervision, which constructs controlled photo--state conflicts through hidden-surface repainting, hidden geometry with re-rendered shadows and reflections, and visible-surface texture replacement. This teaches the editor to follow the 3D state for unseen content while preserving photograph-visible appearance. However, learning this distinction does not ensure that it is maintained during sampling. We therefore introduce symmetric counterfactual correction, which uses state-side and photo-side twins, which share the photograph and the state respectively, to construct directed latent perturbations toward condition-inconsistent targets and train recovery toward the target specified by the current condition. Both strategies operate only during training, requiring no architectural changes or additional inference steps. Experiments on OBJect and SpatialEdit-Bench demonstrate strong spatial editing performance and content preservation, while controlled ablations show improved source selection and sustained responsiveness to conditioning during sampling.

</details>


### [207] [SCRA-CLIP: Spherical-Contextual Representation Adaptation for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29545)
*Peng Chen,Chao Huang,Wenqiang Wang,Mingbo Yang,Yannan Chen,Yunkang Cao,Wei Wang,Li Shen,Fangjun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCRA-CLIP框架，通过球面原型条件提示与上下文偏差引导的视觉自适应，联合增强文本语义与视觉表示，实现零样本细粒度异常检测与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型虽支持零样本异常检测（ZSAD），但对多样化异常模式及其空间上下文建模不足，导致细粒度异常检测仍具挑战。

Method: 1) 球面原型条件提示（SPCP）：用von Mises–Fisher混合模型对正常/异常patch特征的方向分布建模，将状态特定原型注入文本上下文以学习更具判别性的文本表示；2) 上下文偏差引导的视觉自适应（CGVA）：捕捉patch相对多尺度邻域上下文的局部特征偏差，并通过自适应门控精炼patch表示。

Result: 在11个工业与医学基准上的大量实验表明，SCRA-CLIP在多种未见类别与跨域场景下具有有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将分布感知的语义建模与上下文敏感的视觉自适应相结合，可提升跨模态匹配的判别性，有效支持细粒度异常检测与定位。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models have facilitated zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) through transferable visual-semantic representations. However, detecting fine-grained anomalies remains challenging due to inadequate modeling of diverse anomaly patterns and their spatial context. To address these challenges, we propose SCRA-CLIP, a Spherical-Contextual Representation Adaptation framework that jointly enhances anomaly-aware textual semantics and context-sensitive visual representations. Specifically, we introduce Spherical Prototype-Conditioned Prompting (SPCP), which employs von Mises--Fisher mixtures to model the directional distributions of normal and abnormal patch features. These state-specific prototypes are incorporated into textual contexts to guide the learning of more discriminative textual representations. Furthermore, we develop Context-Deviation Guided Visual Adaptation (CGVA), which captures local feature deviations relative to multi-scale neighborhood contexts and refines patch representations through adaptive gating. By integrating distribution-aware semantic modeling with context-sensitive visual adaptation, SCRA-CLIP facilitates more discriminative cross-modal matching for fine-grained anomaly detection and localization. Extensive experiments on 11 industrial and medical benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization capability of SCRA-CLIP across diverse unseen categories and domains. The code will be publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [208] [Align the Pair, Not the Sample: Representation Alignment for Faster and Better One-Step Generato](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29547)
*Mingxiao Li,Xuantuo Huang,Encheng Su,Aoran Wang,Han Zhang,Jinouwen Zhang,Shixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究一步生成器表征对齐中"哪个训练路径应接收损失"的问题：对齐生成路径会损害召回，而对齐配对重建路径（冻结DINOv2）在所有设置下均有效，据此提出方法同时加速两阶段训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征对齐可显著加速扩散训练，但对一步生成器存在扩散模型不存在的新问题：一步生成器有两类输入——带干净对应图像（实例级配对目标）的损坏真实图像，以及无对应真实图像的纯噪声采样（最终模型实际工作场景），因此需确定损失应施加在哪条路径上。

Method: 以Priming为研究对象（从零训练一步生成器：先重建损坏图像，再用最优传输匹配特征分布）。对比两种对齐选择：对齐生成路径（看似自然的选择）与对齐配对重建路径（使用冻结DINOv2编码器）。并通过理论分析与对照实验解释不对称性，进一步考察生成器内部机制。

Result: 对齐生成路径在17种配置（含5种目标构造、损失权重低至0.05）中全部导致召回崩溃（0.45→0.15–0.25）；对齐配对路径在所有设置下均有提升。分析表明：目标不含输入实例信息时，最优对齐特征在类内为常数，损失将样本拉向类原型；对照实验（对齐至同类随机图像而非自身图像）消除了大部分增益，证实该解释。生成器内部，配对路径对齐形成接口模块，训练后噪声经此路由，其携带的图像语义可跨16个模型预测样本质量。方法在ImageNet 256×256上同时加速两阶段：以2×更少priming轮次匹配160轮基线，以4×更少后训练更新超越其最终质量，标准预算下FID降至1.30（B）和1.155（L）；单阶段联合变体以115M参数训练原生一步生成器达FID 1.22。

Conclusion: 一步生成器的表征对齐应作用于配对重建路径而非生成路径；配对路径经由接口模块承载语义并决定噪声到图像的路由，从而同时加速两阶段训练并提升质量，为一步生成器的对齐式训练提供了有效配方。

Abstract: Representation alignment markedly accelerates diffusion training, but one-step generators raise a question that diffusion does not: \emph{which training path should receive the loss?} A one-step generator sees two kinds of inputs. Corrupted real images come with their clean counterpart, which provides an instance-paired target. Samples generated from pure noise have no corresponding real image, yet this is where the final model operates. We study this question on Priming, which trains one-step generators from scratch by reconstructing corrupted images and then matching feature distributions with optimal transport. Aligning the generative path, the natural choice, collapses recall ($0.45\!\to\!0.15$--$0.25$) in all 17 configurations we test, including five target constructions and loss weights down to $0.05$. Aligning the paired reconstruction path with a frozen DINOv2 encoder instead helps in every setting we test. A short analysis explains this asymmetry: when the target carries no instance information about the input, the optimal aligned feature is constant within each class, so the loss pulls samples toward class prototypes. A control confirms this: aligning the paired path to a random same-class image instead of the input's own image removes most of the gain. Inside the generator, paired-path alignment creates an interface block through which post-training routes noise, and the image semantics carried through it predict sample quality across 16 models. The resulting recipe, \method, accelerates \emph{both} training stages on ImageNet $256\!\times\!256$: it matches the 160-epoch baseline with $2\times$ fewer priming epochs, surpasses its final quality with $4\times$ fewer post-training updates, and lowers FID to 1.30 (B) and 1.155 (L) at the standard budget. A joint single-stage variant trains native one-step generators and reaches FID 1.22 with 115M parameters.

</details>


### [209] [Inspect Before You Decide: Evidence-Guided Visual Search for Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29551)
*Peng Chen,Chao Huang,Yangyang Liu,Maodong Wu,Jinyi Xu,Mingbo Yang,Yunkang Cao,Fangjun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EVAD框架，让多模态大模型通过证据引导的主动视觉搜索（全局评估+MCTS探索）获取细粒度视觉证据，实现可靠的工业异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在工业异常检测中难以获取并验证超越全局观测的细粒度视觉证据，导致细微异常识别不可靠。

Method: EVAD：先做全局视觉评估定位可疑异常区域；再用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）引导的探索策略，通过语义精化（细粒度检查）与空间扩展（上下文验证）两种互补操作逐步获取视觉证据；随后对证据进行评估与整合以支持异常判定。

Result: 在多个工业异常检测任务的广泛实验中，EVAD在多数评测设置下取得SOTA性能，验证了主动证据探索对细粒度异常理解与推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 以主动证据搜索替代单一全局观测，可显著提升MLLM对细微异常的定位与推理可靠性；代码将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable potential for industrial anomaly detection and understanding. However, reliably identifying subtle anomalies remains challenging, as existing approaches often struggle to acquire and verify fine-grained visual evidence beyond global observations. To address this challenge, we propose EVAD, an evidence-guided visual search framework that enables MLLMs to actively seek and examine diagnostically informative visual evidence for reliable anomaly reasoning. Specifically, EVAD begins with a global visual assessment to identify potentially anomalous regions for further inspection. Building upon this observation, we introduce a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-guided exploration strategy that progressively acquires visual evidence through two complementary operations: semantic refinement for fine-grained inspection and spatial expansion for contextual verification. The acquired evidence is subsequently evaluated and integrated to support more informed and reliable anomaly decisions. Extensive experiments across multiple industrial anomaly detection tasks demonstrate that EVAD achieves state-of-the-art performance in most evaluated settings, highlighting the effectiveness of active evidence exploration for fine-grained anomaly understanding and reasoning. The code will be publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [210] [GravHMR: Coupling SLAM and Human Mesh Recovery in a Shared Gravity Frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29614)
*Hanglei Jin,Chenglu Sun,Junchen Ge,Yuchao Wang,Siqi Chen,Yuzhi Huang,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GravHMR：以共享重力对齐参考系耦合SLAM与HMR，用YAOO修正朝向、GATO锚定轨迹，实现免训练即插即用的单目视频全局一致3D人体运动恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态相机运动下的单目视频全局一致3D人体运动恢复仍具挑战。SLAM与人体网格恢复（HMR）分别提供互补的相机与人体估计，但直接融合会导致朝向不一致与轨迹漂移累积。

Method: 提出免训练、即插即用的GravHMR框架，通过共享的重力对齐参考系耦合SLAM与HMR；重力估计独立于HMR骨干，兼容带或不带显式重力视角表示的世界接地HMR模型。具体包含两部分：偏航感知朝向优化（YAOO），绕共享重力轴校正人体与速度朝向以消除航向不一致；重力感知轨迹优化（GATO），以校正后的朝向为条件，联合利用2D重投影、人体隐含的度量尺度及人体自身运动，将根轨迹锚定在SLAM的度量世界中。

Result: 在EMDB-2上，使用ViPE相机估计达154.0 mm W-MPJPE100，使用masked DROID-SLAM达189.8 mm，均取得最优性能；相同masked DROID-SLAM条件下优于TRAM（222.4 mm）；相同HMR骨干下较GVHMR（276.5 mm）降低44.3%，较GVHMR + HTD-Refine（192.4 mm）降低20.0%。

Conclusion: 以重力为共享参考系的解耦式后处理优化可有效缓解朝向不一致与轨迹漂移，且无需训练、可即插即用地适配多种SLAM与HMR组合。

Abstract: Recovering globally consistent 3D human motion from monocular videos remains challenging under dynamic camera motion. Although SLAM and human mesh recovery (HMR) provide complementary camera and body estimates, directly combining them often leads to inconsistent orientations and accumulated trajectory drift. We present GravHMR, a training-free, plug-and-play framework that couples SLAM and HMR through a shared gravity-aligned reference. By allowing gravity to be estimated independently of the HMR backbone, GravHMR accommodates world-grounded HMR models with or without explicit gravity-view representations. Specifically, Yaw-Aware Orientation Optimization (YAOO) resolves heading inconsistencies by correcting body and velocity orientations about the shared gravity axis. Conditioned on these corrected orientations, Gravity-Aware Trajectory Optimization (GATO) anchors root trajectories in the metric SLAM world by jointly leveraging 2D reprojection, the metric scale implicit in the human body, and the body's own motion. On EMDB-2, GravHMR achieves state-of-the-art performance with 154.0 mm W-MPJPE100 using ViPE and 189.8 mm using masked DROID-SLAM, outperforming TRAM (222.4 mm) under the same masked DROID-SLAM camera estimates. It further reduces W-MPJPE100 by 44.3% over GVHMR (276.5 mm) and by 20.0% over GVHMR + HTD-Refine (192.4 mm) with the same HMR backbone.

</details>


### [211] [Mass Forcing: On-Policy Mass-Aware Distillation for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29618)
*Yiyu Li,Fei Wei,Yong Wang,Zaiquan Yang,Gerhard Petrus Hancke,Xiangxiang Chu,Rynson W. H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mass Forcing：将on-policy DMD监督从整段视频细化到单个状态，用可测的概率质量估计重平衡条件更新并做跨rollout对齐，缓解模式崩塌，保持视频动态性与多样性且无额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMD的reverse-KL目标具有模式寻求性，会把学生的条件视频分布塌缩到少数模式，导致样本多样性下降、时间动态减弱和色彩过饱和。已有方法靠覆盖导向的学生初始化缓解，但作者观察到这种覆盖是暂时的：三种初始化下，动态视频比例在后续DMD训练中都急剧下降，说明退化由模式寻求目标本身而非初始化驱动。

Method: 提出Mass Forcing框架，将on-policy DMD监督从整视频粒度下移到状态粒度：每次更新从共享的学生生成前缀分支出若干续写，使该状态下学生的概率质量可对教师度量。其一，通过潜空间score integration估计这些续写间教师与学生的相对密度比，据此重平衡条件DMD更新，放大相对教师而言被低估的rollout；其二，由于模式概率失配未必引发强局部score修正，进一步将生成rollout集合与由同一质量估计定义的加权参考分布对齐，提供跨rollout监督。

Result: 在MovieGen与VBench上的实验表明，Mass Forcing提升视频保真度并在训练全程维持时间动态，在对比方法中取得最高Total分数；1,000次生成器更新后Dynamic Degree达83%，基线为12%–32%，且无额外推理时开销。

Conclusion: 模式崩塌源于DMD目标而非初始化，状态级概率质量感知监督能有效纠正学生的条件概率质量分配，兼顾少步实时合成的效率与动态性、多样性。

Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for real-time video synthesis, distilling pretrained diffusion models into few-step autoregressive generators. However, its reverse-KL objective is mode-seeking and can collapse the student's conditional video distribution onto a narrow subset of modes, leading to reduced sample diversity, diminished temporal dynamics, and color oversaturation. Existing methods mitigate this issue mainly via coverage-oriented student initialization, but we observe that such coverage is transient: across three initializations, the fraction of dynamic videos drops sharply during subsequent DMD training, indicating that the mode-seeking objective, not the initialization, drives this degradation. We propose Mass Forcing, a probability-mass-aware framework that moves on-policy DMD supervision from whole videos to individual states. Each update branches several continuations from a shared student-generated prefix, making the student's probability mass at that state measurable against the teacher. First, Mass Forcing estimates relative teacher-to-student density ratios among these continuations through latent-space score integration and uses them to rebalance conditional DMD updates, amplifying rollouts underrepresented relative to the teacher. Second, since mismatched mode probabilities do not necessarily induce strong local score corrections, it aligns generated rollout sets with a reweighted reference distribution defined by the same mass estimates, providing cross-rollout supervision. Experiments on MovieGen and VBench show that Mass Forcing improves video fidelity and sustains temporal dynamics throughout training, achieving the highest Total scores among compared methods and a Dynamic Degree of 83% after 1,000 generator updates, versus 12-32% for the baselines, with no additional inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [212] [Contamination at the Operating Point: Train-Test Identity Overlap Inflates Low-FMR Face Recognition Accuracy by an Order of Magnitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29629)
*Fu Dai,Kamal Taha,Naoufel Werghi,Maregu Assefa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示人脸验证中训练-测试身份重叠导致的性能偏差并非小且恒定的加性误差，而是随FMR下降而放大的乘性误差；在FMR=$10^{-6}$处，去除污染身份使FNMR上升13.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署系统实际工作在FMR≤$10^{-6}$，但常用基准可能与训练语料共享身份，导致性能被高估。已有工作（Wu et al., 2024）仅将偏差量化为10折准确率的小幅加性变化（≤1.01个百分点），低估了在低FMR区间的真实影响，需要重新审视重叠审计方法与偏差的量级和性质。

Method: 1）用基于零分布校准的重叠检测器替代手工阈值，审计MS1MV3、IJB-C与WebFace42M的重叠，并对100个被标记IJB-C受试者进行人工复核；2）固定模型，仅移除测试集中受污染身份，对比随机移除同样数量身份的结果，测量不同FMR下FNMR变化，并归因于冒名者尾部变短导致阈值降低；3）首次直接估计IJB-C在FMR=$10^{-6}$下的有效独立冒名比对数量；4）构建经审计零重叠、可解析至FMR=$10^{-8}$的真实人脸协议。

Result: 检测器标记MS1MV3中88.6%的身份、IJB-C中14.3%的受试者存在于WebFace42M（尽管WebFace260M的公开去重叠流程明确列出IJB-C）；人工复核确认69.2%，残余重叠约9.9%。移除污染身份后，FMR=$10^{-4}$/$10^{-5}$/$10^{-6}$处FNMR分别上升3.6×/6.3×/13.9×，同规模随机移除无此效应。偏差幅度依赖训练语料，且在模型与公开基线间不相互抵消，可使两者差距缩小一半。IJB-C在FMR=$10^{-6}$下仅有约270万次有效独立比对，低于常用报告规则要求。

Conclusion: 训练-测试重叠导致的乐观偏差是随FMR降低而增长的乘性误差，在部署工作点可严重歪曲模型排名，并侵蚀基准的有效比对样本量；应使用零分布校准的重叠审计和零重叠的真实人脸协议进行评测。

Abstract: Face verification systems are deployed by selecting a decision threshold that achieves a target false match rate (FMR); performance is then reported as the corresponding false non-match rate (FNMR). In practice, deployed systems operate at FMR $\le 10^{-6}$, but widely used benchmarks can share identities with the models' training corpora. Prior work (Wu et al., 2024) measures the resulting optimistic bias as a small additive change in 10-fold accuracy (up to 1.01 percentage points). We show that the bias is neither small nor constant: it is a multiplicative error that grows as FMR falls. First, we audit train-test overlap with a detector calibrated against a null distribution (rather than a hand-tuned threshold). Relative to WebFace42M, we flag 88.6% of MS1MV3 identities and 14.3% of IJB-C subjects as present, despite WebFace260M's published de-overlap explicitly naming IJB-C. Manual review of 100 flagged IJB-C subjects confirms 69.2%, implying a residual overlap of roughly 9.9%. Second, holding the model fixed, removing only the contaminated identities from the test set increases FNMR by 3.6$\times$ at FMR=$10^{-4}$, 6.3$\times$ at FMR=$10^{-5}$, and 13.9$\times$ at FMR=$10^{-6}$, while a same-size random removal does not. The effect is traced to a shorter impostor tail that lowers the threshold. The magnitude of the shift depends on the training corpus and does not cancel betweent margin between our model and a publicbaseline by half. Third, we give the first direct measurement of the effective number of independent impostor comparisons in IJB-C: only 2.7 million effectively independent comparisons at FMR=$10^{-6}$, which is below all commonly accepted reporting rules. Finallyth a known training corpus, we build areal-face protocol with audited zero overlap that resolves FMR=$10^{-8}$.

</details>


### [213] [SuperFine3D: Efficient Global-Local Recursive Refinement for Extremely Detailed 3D Geometry Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29630)
*Lihan Li,Junwei Zhou,Yu-wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SuperFine3D 提出全局-局部递归细化框架，借由可复用的共享细化算子将粗到细几何生成外推到训练分辨率之外，在 4096^3 解码网格上生成极精细几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像蕴含丰富细节，忠实重建需要精细几何结构；但从图像生成极细3D几何需大量算力与训练数据，现有SOTA模型难以胜任高分辨率几何生成。

Method: 从成对粗-细表示中学习共享细化算子并递归复用，逐步合成超出训练分辨率的更细几何，无需目标分辨率的额外配对监督；引入空间单元注意力（spatial-cell attention）将高分辨率交互限制在局部邻域、保持跨单元有界通信以提升效率；提出空间锚定的跨尺度引导，用全局上下文化的粗几何引导局部细节合成并保持结构一致；并采用几何感知训练目标稳定几何结构、引入细粒度表面细节。

Result: 在4096^3解码网格（对应稀疏256^3潜格）上生成几何，细化算子仅在512^3–1024^3表示上训练；相较全注意力递归基线，端到端推理加速9.4倍，峰值显存降低23.0%，同时提升跨尺度结构一致性并生成更干净、细节更精细的表面。

Conclusion: SuperFine3D 通过递归复用共享细化算子与局部化注意力，在不增加目标分辨率监督的前提下实现了高效、极精细且结构一致的3D几何生成，兼顾质量与计算可行性。

Abstract: High-resolution images contain rich visual details that require fine geometric structures for faithful 3D reconstruction. However, generating extremely detailed 3D geometry from images requires substantial computational resources and training data, making high-resolution geometry generation challenging for existing state-of-the-art models. In this paper, we present SuperFine3D, an efficient framework for generating extremely detailed 3D geometry through global-local recursive refinement. SuperFine3D learns a shared refinement operator from paired coarse-to-fine representations and recursively reuses it to progressively synthesize finer geometry beyond the training resolutions, without requiring additional paired supervision at target resolutions. To make recursive refinement computationally efficient, we introduce spatial-cell attention, which restricts high-resolution interactions to local spatial neighborhoods while maintaining bounded communication across neighboring cells. We further propose spatially anchored cross-scale guidance, which leverages globally contextualized coarse geometry to guide local detail synthesis and preserve structural consistency. In addition, geometry-aware training objectives encourage the refinement process to preserve stable geometric structures while introducing fine-grained surface details. Experiments demonstrate that SuperFine3D generates geometry at a $4096^3$ decoder-grid resolution, corresponding to a sparse $256^3$ latent lattice, using a shared refinement operator trained only on $512^3$--$1024^3$ representations. Compared with a full-attention recursive baseline, SuperFine3D achieves a $9.4\times$ speedup in end-to-end inference and reduces peak GPU memory usage by $23.0\%$, while improving cross-scale structural consistency and producing cleaner surfaces with finer geometric details.

</details>


### [214] [PROPS: Can Visual Generators Keep the Current Look of a Prop?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29665)
*Xiangzhao Hao,Dianyi Wang,Keyan Zhou,Yiqian Zhang,Zefeng Zhang,Bailin Li,Kun Zhan,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出并定义了视觉生成中的"道具漂移"（prop drift）失败模式，构建了含2,257对双胞胎历史的PROPS基准以隔离该问题，测试的34个开闭源系统均出现漂移，并发布PROPS-Train训练集，微调后四类模型性能均明显提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故事板、连环画、多轮编辑等生成任务中，新请求通常只描述"接下来发生什么"，而不重述早期物体的外观。生成器往往仅依据当前请求重绘物体，或回退到过时外观，且错误会累积到后续步骤。现有评测难以隔离该失败：与历史相似度会奖励"复制"，而只看单一历史的评判器无法区分"从历史中恢复的外观"与"生成器本身偏好的外观"。

Method: 提出PROPS基准，包含2,257对"双胞胎历史"：两侧历史仅在某一道具的外观上不同，但以相同的请求结尾。一对样本仅在生成器两侧分别画出对应外观且执行了请求时才通过，因此生成器或评判器的固有偏好至多只能通过一侧。此外构建PROPS-Train，含四条轨道共80,250条训练链，目标为恢复早期状态或将脚本化的变化向前传递。

Result: 所测34个开闭源系统全部表现出道具漂移；即便在多数样本上能正确执行动作的系统，也常丢失道具的当前外观。通过交换智能体的规划器与编辑器，表明历史理解与图像生成二者均限制智能体表现。在PROPS-Train上微调后，四个受测模型均获得明显提升。

Conclusion: 道具漂移是视觉历史生成中普遍存在且此前未被单独评测的失败模式；PROPS以双胞胎历史设计提供了无偏诊断手段，PROPS-Train则给出可行的训练缓解路径。两个数据集将公开发布。

Abstract: Many generation tasks draw the next image from a visual history, such as storyboards, illustrated stories and multi-turn editing. A new request often states what happens next without restating how earlier objects look. Instead of recovering an object's current look from the history, generators often redraw it from the request alone or bring back an outdated look, and the error can carry into later steps. We call this failure prop drift. Existing evaluations rarely isolate it, because similarity to the history rewards copying and a judge that sees a single history cannot tell a look recovered from that history from one the generator happens to prefer. We introduce PROPS, a benchmark of 2,257 pairs of twin histories that end on the same request but show one prop in two different looks. A pair passes only if the generator draws the matching look on each side and carries out the request, so a look preferred by the generator or the judge passes at most one side. All 34 open and closed systems we test show prop drift, and even systems that carry out the requested action on most sides often lose the current look of the prop. Swapping the planners and editors of agents further shows that both history understanding and image generation limit agent performance. We also build PROPS-Train, 80,250 training chains in four tracks whose targets recover an earlier state or carry scripted changes forward, and fine-tuning on it clearly improves all four tested models. Both datasets will be publicly released.

</details>


### [215] [DeclareViT: Decoupling Early Classification from Feature Refinement with Exit Query for Efficient ViTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29674)
*Xinrui Gong,Yao Rong,Chuangtao Chen,Daniel Ochs,Ulf Schlichtmann,Bing Li,Kristian Kersting,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeclareViT通过将早期分类与特征精炼解耦，在冻结主干的前提下用专用query经单向cross-attention读取中间特征，从而在ViT/DeiT/Swin上实现1.58–2.57倍MAC加速且精度仅下降0.39–1.91个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT推理计算开销大，早期退出可让简单样本在中间层终止；但现有方法将早期分类与共享主干耦合，中间表示需同时服务早期分类与后续特征精炼，联合训练时优化目标共同更新共享主干参数，易产生梯度方向冲突。

Method: 提出DeclareViT框架，解耦早期分类与特征精炼：每个退出使用专用query通过单向cross-attention读取中间特征，不修改主干token流；仅优化query分支用于早期分类，主干与最终分类器保持冻结。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、ImageNet-1K、Food-101上，针对ViT、DeiT、Swin均取得最优精度-效率权衡：MAC加速1.58–2.57倍，精度仅下降0.39–1.91个百分点；CPU与GPU的wall-clock实测亦验证了实际推理加速。

Conclusion: 通过解耦早期分类与特征精炼、避免梯度冲突，可在不改动预训练主干的情况下有效实现ViT的早期退出加速，并获得稳定的精度-效率优势。

Abstract: Vision Transformers achieve remarkable recognition performance but incur substantial computational cost during inference, limiting their use in latency- and compute-constrained deployment scenarios. Early-exit inference offers a promising solution by allowing easy samples to terminate at intermediate layers. However, existing approaches typically couple early classification with the shared backbone, requiring intermediate representations to support both early classification and subsequent feature refinement. Under joint training, the corresponding optimization objectives jointly update shared backbone parameters, which can lead to conflicting gradient directions. Motivated by this observation, we introduce DeclareViT, a framework that decouples early classification from feature refinement. Each exit uses a dedicated query to read intermediate features through one-way cross-attention without modifying the backbone token stream. The query branches are optimized for early classification while the backbone and final classifier remain frozen. Across ViT, DeiT, and Swin on CIFAR-100, ImageNet-1K, and Food-101, DeclareViT consistently establishes a state-of-the-art accuracy-efficiency trade-off: It achieves 1.58 times to 2.57 times multiply-accumulate (MAC)-based speed-up with only a 0.39-1.91 percentage-point accuracy drop. Wall-clock measurements on CPU and GPU further demonstrate practical inference acceleration. Code is available at https://github.com/TUDa-HWAI/DeclareViT.

</details>


### [216] [HandiVid: A High-Quality Dataset for Hand-Driven Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29693)
*Chaoyang Zhang,Shaohao Rui,Xiaofeng Mao,Yuanxun Xu,Haoyuan Xia,Xiaohong Liu,Qinghe Du,Weijie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 HandiVid 数据集（20 万条精选标注的第一人称手部交互视频），通过统一数据处理流水线融合公开数据集、网络视频与合成视频，提升场景多样性、手-物交互有效性与运动标注可靠性，并验证其对手驱动交互视频生成的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手驱动第一人称交互视频生成需要以手部运动和相机运动为条件生成具有真实交互响应的动态场景，但现有第一人称视频数据集难以同时满足场景多样性、有意义的抓握/操作交互以及可靠的运动标注三项要求。

Method: 构建统一数据处理流水线：整合公开数据集、网络视频与合成视频；依次进行视频质量评估、手部检测过滤，以及基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的多阶段交互验证，筛选出具有有意义手-物交互的高质量片段；由于 3D 手部重建依赖初始手部检测，将 SAM 3 引入手部检测环节以减少漏检、提升 3D 手部标注完整性；每条片段均附带高质量手部渲染、相机轨迹与交互描述。

Result: 数据集规模为 200K 条经筛选与标注的第一人称交互视频片段；全面的数据分析与下游视频生成实验表明，HandiVid 在数据多样性、标注质量以及可控视频生成方面均表现有效。

Conclusion: HandiVid 通过多源数据整合与 VLM 驱动的多阶段筛选及 SAM 3 增强的手部检测，缓解了现有第一人称交互数据集的瓶颈，可作为手驱动交互视频生成的高质量数据基础。

Abstract: Hand-driven egocentric interactive video generation aims to generate dynamic scenes with realistic interaction responses conditioned on hand movements and camera motion. However, existing egocentric video datasets struggle to provide scene diversity, meaningful hand-object interactions, and reliable motion annotations simultaneously. To address these limitations, we introduce HandiVid, a high-quality dataset comprising 200K curated and annotated egocentric interaction video clips for hand-driven interactive video generation. We develop a unified data processing pipeline that integrates public datasets, online videos and synthetic videos. Through video quality assessment, hand detection filtering, and multi-stage interaction validation using vision-language models (VLMs), we select high-quality video clips with meaningful hand-object interactions. Since 3D hand reconstruction relies on initial hand detections, we integrate SAM 3 into the hand detection pipeline to reduce missed detections and improve the completeness of 3D hand annotations. Each video clip is provided with high-quality hand renderings, camera trajectories, and interaction descriptions. Comprehensive dataset analysis and downstream video generation experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of HandiVid in terms of data diversity, annotation quality, and controllable video generation.

</details>


### [217] [UniDR: Learning To Generate Anatomical Deformations From Images via Unified Diffusion Modeling and Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29712)
*Swakshar Deb,Tonmoy Hossain,Tom Fletcher,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniDR框架，用条件扩散模型学习形变场，并以配准过程联合优化，实现拓扑保持的解剖形变生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将生成过程与基于配准的形变估计分离建模，导致配准阶段的误差被传播到生成结果中。

Method: 构建基于形变场的条件扩散模型，与配准过程联合优化；设计配准引导的score-matching目标函数，使扩散过程对齐配准诱导的形变分布，从而在生成与配准之间建立显式联系：扩散模型探索合理解剖形变空间，配准提供与观测图像一致的结构化监督。

Result: 训练后可以以单张输入图像为条件采样多样且解剖上合理的形变；在多类真实应用中相比SOTA生成更高保真度的图像样本，且生成样本可提升下游医学图像分析与视觉任务性能。

Conclusion: 生成与配准的统一建模范式可有效避免误差传播，并兼具形变多样性、拓扑保持与下游任务增益。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework, Unified Diffusion modeling and Registration (UniDR), which learns to generate arbitrary anatomical deformations from images with preserved topology. Existing approaches often model the generative process separately from registration-based deformation estimation; hence making them prone to propagated errors from the registration stage. To address this limitation, we propose a unified paradigm based on a conditional diffusion model over deformation fields that are jointly optimized from the registration process. A principled registration-guided score-matching objective function is then introduced to align the diffusion process with the deformation distribution induced by registration. As a result, our UniDR establishes an explicit connection between generation and registration: the diffusion model enables exploration of the space of plausible anatomical deformations, whereas registration provides structured supervision for learning transformations consistent with observed images. Once trained, UniDR is able to sample diverse, anatomically plausible transformations conditioned on any single input image. We evaluate UniDR across multiple real-world applications and demonstrate its effectiveness in generating high-fidelity image samples compared to the state-of-the-art. Additionally, we demonstrate that these generated samples improve performance in downstream medical image analysis and vision tasks.

</details>


### [218] [ApexID: Hierarchical Learning for Multi-Species Animal Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29723)
*Seifeldin Elkerdany,Mohamed ElBassat,Gamal Abouelhamd,Assem Elkady,Jana Ghoneim,Matin Mahmood,Abigail Allen Martin,Gerard de Melo,Antonio Rueda-Toicen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ApexID——一种基于SphereFace2的分层损失，将全局身份判别与均衡的动物类别监督结合，在大规模多数据集上验证其提升跨数据集、跨物种迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物重识别需跨物种、跨观测条件区分个体。现有方法多聚焦单一物种或数据集，缺乏类别层级的结构化监督，导致向未见数据集与未见物种的迁移能力有限。

Method: 构建132,533张图像、来自19个公开数据集的训练集；设计ApexID分层损失，在SphereFace2框架下联合优化全局身份判别与均衡化的动物类别监督；对3类模型家族与4种目标函数进行对比，并在4个外部分数据集上采用观测感知的检索协议评估。

Result: ConvNeXt-Small配ApexID取得数据集平均Rank-1 64.89%；MIEW-ID取得mAP 32.54%，均优于各自预训练参考模型。在大象（训练集中未涉及的类别）上，ConvNeXt-Small变体在14种配置中检索性能最高。性能随骨干与数据集存在差异，开集评估仍暴露发现性挑战。

Conclusion: 分层类别-身份学习可有效增强动物Re-ID对未见数据集与物种的迁移能力，但开集场景下的新个体发现仍是待解决问题。

Abstract: Animal re-identification (Re-ID) must distinguish individuals across species and capture conditions. We introduce ApexID, a hierarchical SphereFace2-based loss that integrates global identity discrimination with balanced animal-category supervision. Using 132,533 images curated from 19 public datasets, we evaluate three model families and four objectives on four external datasets with observation-aware retrieval protocols. ApexID achieves a dataset-averaged Rank-1 accuracy of 64.89% with ConvNeXt-Small and an mAP of 32.54% with MIEW-ID, outperforming their pretrained reference models. On elephants, a category absent from animal Re-ID training, our ConvNeXt-Small variant achieves the highest retrieval performance among the fourteen evaluated configurations. Scores vary by backbone and dataset, and open-set evaluation reveals remaining discovery challenges. These findings support hierarchical category-identity learning to improve transfer to unseen datasets and species.

</details>


### [219] [Reasoning Beyond Imitation: Observation Conditioned Distillation for Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29728)
*Junzhou Chen,Jindong Wang,Gang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将视频推理蒸馏中师生视频采样差异导致的轨迹不可执行问题形式化为“条件目标失配”，提出“获取—执行”两阶段框架并配合执行感知参考正则，在多骨干与六基准上优于监督蒸馏与标准RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师与学生Video-LLM采用不同的视频采样管线，对同一视频构建的时序观测不同；教师观测下有效的推理轨迹可能依赖学生无法获得的证据，因而无法在学生观测下直接执行，形成“推理获取—执行鸿沟”。

Method: 将上述问题形式化为条件目标失配（conditional target mismatch）。提出获取到执行框架：第一阶段把教师轨迹蒸馏为学生推理先验；第二阶段在多种观测条件下优化该先验的执行能力。并引入执行感知参考正则（Execution-Aware Reference Regularization）：当先验在当前观测下可执行时保留其约束，不可执行时放宽约束以允许更大自适应调整。

Result: 在多个Video-LLM骨干与六个视频推理基准上，显式优化观测条件化执行一致优于监督蒸馏与标准基于RL的优化，且增益可迁移至留出的域外基准。

Conclusion: 推理蒸馏需将“推理的获取”与“推理在具体观测下的执行”解耦并分别优化；观测条件化的执行优化是弥合师生观测差异、提升视频时序推理迁移性的关键。

Abstract: Reasoning distillation offers a promising approach to transfer temporal reasoning capabilities of powerful Video-LLMs to open-source student models. However, teacher and student Video-LLMs may construct different temporal observations from the same video due to the adoption of distinct video sampling pipelines. A reasoning trajectory valid under the teacher's observations may rely on evidence inaccessible to the student and thus cannot be directly executed based on the student's observations. We formalize this as a conditional target mismatch, thereby revealing the Reasoning Acquisition-Execution Gap. To bridge this gap, we propose an acquisition-to-execution framework that first distills teacher trajectories into a student reasoning prior and then further optimizes the execution capability of this prior across multiple observations. We further introduce Execution-Aware Reference Regularization, which retains the existing prior when it is executable under current observations, while allowing a greater degree of adaptive adjustment when it is not. Experiments across multiple Video-LLM backbones and six video reasoning benchmarks show that explicitly optimizing observation-conditioned execution consistently outperforms supervised distillation and standard RL-based optimization, with gains extending to held-out out-of-domain benchmarks.

</details>


### [220] [Seeing Beyond Names: Image-Grounded Concept Linking for Robust Vision-Language Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29735)
*Behraj Khan,Shabir Ahmad,Tahir Qasim Syed,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 零样本视觉-语言分类器对类名词汇高度敏感；作者提出免训练的"图像 grounding 概念链接"方法来缓解词汇漂移带来的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 等零样本分类器即使图像不变，仅将熟悉标签替换为同义词、缩写或本体术语也会显著改变预测，这种"词汇漂移"（vocabulary shift）问题的成因与缓解手段尚缺乏无泄漏的系统评估。

Method: 在 Tiny ImageNet、ImageNet-R、ImageNet-A、ImageNetV2 上采用无数据泄漏的评估协议；提出 image-grounded concept linking——一种免训练方法，将类名映射到视觉概念，并通过可靠性门控（reliability gate）修正预测；与模板集成基线对比，并在 5 个视觉-语言骨干网络上验证；另测试更强的文本链接与直推式（transductive）精炼。

Result: CLIP ViT-B/16 在替代类名下 top-1 精度下降 14.8–17.6 个百分点；所提方法在四个数据集上均优于模板集成，合并增益 1.8 点，在 20 个骨干-数据集组合中 16 个提升且无显著负结果，并在全部 5 个骨干上降低合并 NLL。

Conclusion: 更强的文本链接与直推式精炼无一致收益，说明准确的视觉-类名链接仍是核心挑战；第二个 CLIP ViT-B/16 随机种子在 ImageNetV2 上复现提升，但在 ImageNet-A 上未复现。

Abstract: A zero-shot vision-language classifier is highly sensitive to the class names it receives. Even when images remain unchanged, replacing familiar labels with synonyms, abbreviations, or ontology terms can significantly alter predictions. We investigate this \emph{vocabulary shift} using a leakage-free evaluation protocol across Tiny ImageNet, ImageNet-R, ImageNet-A, and ImageNetV2. With CLIP ViT-B/16, alternative class names reduce top-1 accuracy by $14.8$--$17.6$ percentage points. To address this issue, we propose \emph{image-grounded concept linking}, a training-free method that maps class names to visual concepts and uses a reliability gate to improve predictions. The method outperforms a template ensemble across all four datasets, achieving a pooled gain of $1.8$ points. We further evaluate five vision-language backbones across four datasets. Our method improves performance in $16$ of $20$ backbone--dataset combinations, with no significantly negative results. It also reduces pooled negative log-likelihood across all five backbones. Stronger text linking and transductive refinement provide no consistent improvements, suggesting that accurate visual class-name linking remains the main challenge. A second CLIP ViT-B/16 seed confirms the improvement on ImageNetV2, but not on ImageNet-A.

</details>


### [221] [DREAM: Dual-Memory Reasoning for Anticipatory 3D Perception in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29753)
*Sriram Mandalika,Athira Nambiar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DREAM是一个面向在线持续LiDAR语义分割的双记忆推理框架，通过潜在语义记忆与错误记忆的检索调制，在不回放历史帧、冻结主干的前提下提升动态场景鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR语义分割方法多为帧中心式，逐帧独立处理并丢弃历史上下文经验，难以应对遮挡、稀疏观测和重复性失败模式等感知挑战，在动态场景变化下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 在严格在线设定下维护两个互补记忆库：潜在记忆存储既往场景的紧凑语义抽象，错误记忆记录与不确定预测相关的表示。每个时间步以余弦相似度检索相关记忆条目，并通过轻量调制机制将其融入特征空间，从而强化一致的语义模式、抑制重复失败模式。主干网络冻结，且不回放历史扫描，保证计算效率与记忆有界增长。

Result: 在多个大规模LiDAR基准上的大量实验表明，DREAM取得state-of-the-art性能，并在动态目标与小尺度目标上取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 持久且错误感知的记忆机制对稳健的长时程感知有效，将感知重构为经验驱动、具备预见性的过程具有实际价值。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems must not only interpret the current scene but also anticipate perceptual challenges such as occlusions, sparse observations, and recurring failure patterns. However, most existing LiDAR semantic segmentation methods operate in a frame-centric manner, processing each scan independently and discarding past contextual experience, which limits robustness under dynamic scene changes. We propose DREAM, a Dual-memory REAsoning framework for continual LiDAR seMantic segmentation that reframes perception as an experience-driven and anticipatory process. DREAM maintains two complementary memory banks in a strictly online setting: a latent memory that stores compact semantic abstractions of previously observed scenes, and an error memory that records representations associated with uncertain predictions. At each timestep, relevant memory entries are retrieved via cosine similarity and integrated into the feature space through a lightweight modulation mechanism, enabling the model to reinforce consistent semantic patterns while suppressing recurring failure modes. The backbone remains frozen and no past scans are replayed, ensuring computational efficiency and bounded memory growth. Extensive experiments on multiple large-scale LiDAR benchmarks demonstrate that DREAM achieves state-of-the-art performance, with consistent improvements on dynamic and small-scale objects, highlighting the effectiveness of persistent and error-aware memory for robust long-horizon perception.

</details>


### [222] [DictaRashi: A State-of-the-Art OCR Model For Rashi Script](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29760)
*Ometz Shmidman,Yonah Taurog,Aryeh Sanders,Shlomo Bluth,Moshe Koppel,Joshua Geudalia,Avi Shmidman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 发布 DictaRashi——一个针对"Rashi"字体希伯来语古籍扫描件的 Tesseract OCR 模型，CER 0.86%、WER 3.38%，刷新公开 Tesseract 模型最优水平，并优于当前前沿大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "Rashi"字体印刷的希伯来语古籍扫描件识别困难，现有公开希伯来语 Tesseract 模型在该字体上精度不足，缺少专用模型与公开评测基准。

Method: 基于 Tesseract 框架训练，语料为来自 100 余部拉比及后拉比文献的逾一百万行人工校正文本；在 1,120 行人工转写、取自扫描 Rashi 字体页面的留出集上评测，并与另外 31 个公开希伯来语 Tesseract 模型及前沿 LLM 进行对比。

Result: DictaRashi 达到 CER 0.86%、WER 3.38%；对比模型中最优者为 CER 2.98%、WER 11.86%；在同类 OCR 任务上性能超过当前前沿 LLM。

Conclusion: DictaRashi 为 Rashi 字体希伯来语 OCR 的公开最优 Tesseract 模型；模型与测试集以 CC BY NC 4.0 许可发布，测试集可作为该领域后续研究的基准。

Abstract: We release DictaRashi, a Tesseract OCR model for scanned Hebrew books printed in the so-called "Rashi" font. We trained DictaRashi on over one million manually corrected lines of text from over 100 different books of Rabbinic and post-Rabbinic literature. On a held-out set of 1,120 manually transcribed lines from scanned Rashi-script pages, DictaRashi reaches a character error rate (CER) of 0.86% and a word error rate (WER) of 3.38%, setting a new state-of-the-art for publicly-available Tesseract models. We provide a full comparison of our results to those of the 31 other publicly available Hebrew Tesseract models; the best of those models achieves a 2.98% CER and 11.86% WER. We further demonstrate that DictaRashi outperforms the current frontier LLMs on this OCR task. We release the test set of Rashi-script documents so that it may be used as a benchmark for future work in this area. We are pleased to release the model and test set to the community under a CC BY NC 4.0 license.

</details>


### [223] [CivicBench: Robust Detection of AI-Generated Political Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29766)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Elham Buxton,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 CivicBench 基准（10,800 个虚构政治人物生成视频 + 2,632 个真实片段，且均可被对抗攻击），揭示现有检测器在攻击下性能骤降，并提出 Tau-Anchor 方法达到 .941 AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治类 AI 生成视频既可冒充真实素材，也可使真实素材被诬为伪造；而上传者掌握像素控制权，可施加对抗扰动。现有政治视频数据集规模小、仅含真实政治人物、且不测试攻击，无法评估检测器的真实鲁棒性。

Method: 构建 CivicBench：10,800 条来自 8 个开源生成器的虚构政治人物片段，加 2,632 条公共领域/开放许可真实片段，每条均可攻击（隐藏伪造或诬陷真实）。提出"攻击触及范围（reach）"概念刻画攻击可移动分数的幅度，并据此提出 Tau-Anchor：在受攻击图像上预训练编码器后微调，训练中对真实与生成片段均施加攻击，并在部署阈值处加入 margin 项。

Result: 通用基准上训练的已发表检测器在 CivicBench 上 AUC 最高 .781；每像素 1 灰度级的扰动即可让几乎所有非抗攻击检测器漏检。Tau-Anchor 达 .941 AUC；2 灰度级预算下仍将 .668 的生成片段判为伪造（已发表方法最多 .289）；同等条件下真实片段被诬陷时误报仅从 8% 升至 14%，而四个已发表检测器在 1 灰度级下几乎flag 所有被诬陷真实片段。外部政治素材与两个通用基准呈现同样规律。

Conclusion: 政治视频取证需在数据集层面纳入对抗攻击，并采用阈值感知的鲁棒训练；Tau-Anchor 显著提升攻击下的检测与抗诬陷能力。代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: AI-generated video of politicians can pass for real footage, and once such fakes are common, real footage can be dismissed as fake. Platforms address both problems with detectors, but whoever uploads a clip controls its pixels. Existing datasets of political video are small, show real politicians, and test no attacks. We introduce CivicBench, with 10,800 generated clips of invented politicians from eight open generators and 2,632 public-domain or openly licensed real clips. Each clip can be attacked, to hide a fake or to frame real footage. Published detectors trained on a general benchmark reach at most .781 AUC on CivicBench. Adversarial changes of 1 gray level per pixel hide nearly every generated clip from the detectors not built to resist attacks. An attack's reach is how far it can move a clip's score. A clip keeps its label when its margin from the threshold exceeds this reach, and we find that the reach grows almost linearly with the budget. On this basis, we propose Tau-Anchor, which fine-tunes an encoder pretrained on attacked images. It attacks real and generated clips during training and adds a margin term at its deployed threshold. It reaches .941 AUC on CivicBench. It also keeps .668 of generated clips flagged at 2 gray levels, against at most .289 for published detectors. When real clips are framed at the same 2 gray levels, Tau-Anchor's false alarms rise only from 8% to 14%, whereas four published detectors flag nearly every framed real clip at just 1 gray level. External political footage and two general benchmarks show the same pattern under attack. Code and data are available at https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/CivicBench.

</details>


### [224] [S1Arena-MM: Which Multimodal Decisions Can Be Left to System 1?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29773)
*Lang Lin,Tianyu Chen,Yi Gu,Yang Zhao,Xinran Song,Xinyang Liu,Jianpeng Cheng,Mingyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 S1Arena-MM 基准，用统一的决策请求与概率打分方式，在 51,060 题、94 个任务（图像/视频/音频）及 102 个闭环游戏环境中对比 System-1 模型与 MLLM 的离散感知决策能力。结果显示：静态任务上最佳 System-1 模型在图像上领先最佳 MLLM 4.9 个百分点，视频/音频差距在 1 个百分点内；决策后训练主要改善概率校准（Brier 分数）；闭环游戏显著拉开差距，System-1 模型进展 22.0–33.3%，其余模型（关闭思考）不超过 9.7%；实时场景下每步 1.1 s 延迟对 System-1 模型无显著进展损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知系统常需做离散决策（如判定仇恨模因、预测行人过街、检测伪造语音、选择游戏动作）。System-1 模型单次前向即输出选项概率，MLLM 则以生成文本作答。二者在何种多模态决策上各有优势缺乏统一、可比的评测，本文旨在填补这一空白：以相同决策请求、相同概率打分口径系统对比两类模型。

Method: 构建 S1Arena-MM 基准：51,060 个问题、94 个任务，覆盖图像、视频、音频输入，源自 94 个公开数据集与 3 个程序生成数据集；另含 102 个闭环游戏环境（模型从截图操作，实时模式下模型思考时游戏继续推进）。评测 33 个开源模型（12 个 System-1 模型、21 个 MLLM），MLLM 在答案 token 处读取概率；使用准确率与 Brier 分数衡量，并对比决策后训练与思考（最多 2,048 个 thought token）的影响。

Result: 静态决策上，最佳 System-1 模型在图像上领先最佳 MLLM 4.9 个百分点，视频与音频差距不足 1 个百分点。决策后训练主要提升概率质量：视频上，12 对中有 10 对 System-1 模型的 Brier 分数优于其源自的 MLLM。启用最多 2,048 思考 token 后，最准确 MLLM 仅略微超过最佳 System-1 模型，且思考使多数 MLLM 的 Brier 分数变差。闭环环境差距明显：三个基于 27B MLLM 的 System-1 模型在 BALROG 中进展 22.0–33.3%，其余模型在关闭思考时不超过 9.7%；实时 GameWorld 中每步最高 1.1 s 延迟对 System-1 模型的进展无可测损失。

Conclusion: 在静态离散决策上，System-1 模型与 MLLM 精度接近（图像略优），核心差异在于概率校准：面向决策的后训练比生成式思考更能产出可靠的决策概率，思考反而常损害校准。闭环与实时交互场景才是真正的分水岭，System-1 模型兼顾低延迟与高进展，更适合实时感知决策系统。

Abstract: Perception systems often make discrete decisions: flag a hateful meme, predict whether a pedestrian will cross, detect a spoofed voice, or select the next move in a game. System-1 models, such as OneJev and Jev-Omni, read the media and return probabilities over the options in a single forward pass, while multimodal large language models (MLLMs) generate text. Which multimodal decisions can System-1 models handle, and where do MLLMs perform better? We introduce S1Arena-MM, a benchmark that gives every model the same decision requests and scores the probabilities it returns. It contains 51,060 questions across 94 tasks with image, video, and audio input, drawn from 94 public datasets and three program-generated ones. It also includes 102 closed-loop game environments that a model plays from screenshots; in real-time mode, the game continues while the model decides. We evaluate 33 open models: 12 System-1 models and 21 MLLMs read at the answer token. On static decisions, the best System-1 model leads the best MLLM by 4.9 percentage points on images and is within one point of it on video and audio. Post-training for decisions mainly improves the probabilities: on video, a System-1 model has a lower Brier score than the MLLM that it is post-trained from in 10 of 12 pairs. With up to 2,048 thought tokens per question, the most accurate MLLMs pass the best System-1 models only slightly, and thinking raises the Brier score of most MLLMs. Closed loops separate the models: three System-1 models built on a 27B MLLM reach 22.0-33.3% progression in BALROG, and no other model exceeds 9.7% with thinking off. In real-time GameWorld, latencies of up to 1.1 s per decision cause no measurable loss of progress for System-1 models. Code and data: https://github.com/zhaoyang02/S1Arena-MM.

</details>


### [225] [Beyond Language Optimization: Enhancing Visual Representations for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29783)
*Rui Zuo,Qinyue Tong,Ziqian Lu,Zheming Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出选择性区域视觉蒸馏框架，结合区域感知教师与空间门控残差适配器（SGRA），在保持原有视觉能力的前提下提升难识别异常的视觉表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO虽提升生成报告的临床准确性，但分析发现其对异常识别的视觉表征改进有限，难识别异常的区域视觉表征仍是瓶颈。

Method: 1) 以针对性监督训练区域感知教师，获取难解剖区域的互补视觉知识；2) 通过区域特征替换验证教师特征效用，仅选择能提升原始学生模型性能的特征进行迁移；3) 引入空间门控残差适配器（SGRA）蒸馏已验证特征，减少对学生既有视觉表征的干扰。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR与IU-Xray上，跨多种多模态大模型骨干均提升临床报告准确性与区域级异常识别能力，同时基本保持非目标发现上的性能。

Conclusion: 选择性、可验证的区域视觉蒸馏可在不牺牲既有能力的情况下增强难异常识别，是提升胸片报告生成临床可靠性的有效途径。

Abstract: Accurate chest X-ray radiology report generation (RRG) requires recognizing clinically relevant abnormalities and accurately describing them. Although Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) improves the clinical accuracy of generated reports, our analyses suggest limited improvements in visual representations for abnormality recognition. To address this limitation, we propose a selective regional visual distillation framework that enhances visual representations of challenging abnormalities while preserving existing visual capabilities. Specifically, we train a region-aware teacher with targeted supervision on challenging anatomical regions to acquire complementary visual knowledge. Rather than transferring all teacher features, we verify their utility through regional feature replacement and select only those that improve the original student's performance. We further introduce a Spatially Gated Residual Adapter (SGRA) to distill these verified features while minimizing interference with the student's existing visual representations. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR and IU-Xray demonstrate improved clinical report accuracy and region-level abnormality recognition across different multimodal large language model backbones, while largely preserving performance on non-target findings.

</details>


### [226] [A Cascaded Framework with a Cross-Attention-Guided Mamba-CNN Model for Pre- and Post-Treatment Glioma Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29792)
*Siavash Taremi,Mahmoud Heidari,Seyed Mohammad Hossein Hashemi,Mohsen Hooshmand*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出级联框架下的交叉注意力引导 Mamba-CNN 模型 CMC-Seg，用于治疗前后弥漫性胶质瘤分割，在 BraTS 2025 mpMRI 上取得最高平均 Dice（81.20%）、RC Dice（78.63%）、最低 HD95（4.39 mm）与最少推理 GFLOPs（381.49）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质瘤分割对诊断与随访至关重要，但人工分割耗时、主观且标注者间差异大；肿瘤异质性强。CNN 擅长局部特征但长程依赖建模不足，ViT 全局建模好但计算复杂度高；且已有研究多集中于治疗前病例，含切除腔（RC）的治疗后病例研究不足。因此需在精度与计算成本间取得平衡。

Method: 在级联框架中提出 Cross-Attention-Guided Mamba-CNN（CMC-Seg）。数据为 BraTS 2025 弥漫性胶质瘤 mpMRI；预处理后由粗分割模型提取以肿瘤为中心的 patch，再输入 CMC-Seg 进行精细分割。与 3D U-Net、SegResNet、SwinUNETR、UNETR、nnFormer、SegMamba 对比，并进行消融与 Grad-CAM 可解释性实验。

Result: 测试集上平均 Dice 最高（81.20%），NETC Dice 77.09%，RC Dice 78.63%，HD95 最低（4.39 mm）。性能与 CNN、Mamba 基线相当，显著优于 Transformer 基线（P < 0.05）；推理 GFLOPs 最少（381.49）。消融显示多方向扫描与交叉注意力带来提升，尤其在 RC 上显著（P < 0.05）。Grad-CAM 显示模型关注具有诊断意义的肿瘤区域。

Conclusion: CMC-Seg 在治疗前后弥漫性胶质瘤分割中实现了精度与计算效率的良好平衡，交叉注意力与多方向扫描是性能提升的关键，可用于临床分割与随访场景，治疗后含 RC 病例同样适用。

Abstract: Accurate glioma segmentation is vital for diagnosis and follow-up, but manual segmentation is time-consuming, subjective, and variable across interpreters. Gliomas are heterogeneous in size, shape, and location. Deep learning has therefore emerged as a potential solution. CNNs excel at local features but may struggle with long-range dependencies, whereas Vision Transformers capture global context efficiently but incur higher computational complexity. Most previous studies focused on pre-treatment gliomas; post-treatment cases with resection cavities (RCs) remain less explored. This study proposes a Cross-Attention-Guided Mamba-CNN (CMC-Seg) model within a cascaded framework for pre- and post-treatment diffuse glioma segmentation, aiming to balance accuracy and computational cost. Experiments used BraTS 2025 mpMRI of diffuse glioma patients. After preprocessing, a coarse segmentation model extracted tumor-centered patches, which were fed to CMC-Seg for fine-grained segmentation. CMC-Seg was compared with 3D U-Net, SegResNet, SwinUNETR, UNETR, nnFormer, and SegMamba. On the test set, CMC-Seg achieved the highest mean Dice (81.20%) and non-enhancing tumor core (NETC) Dice (77.09%). It also achieved an RC Dice of 78.63% and the lowest 95% Hausdorff distance (HD95; 4.39 mm). Results were comparable to CNN and Mamba baselines and significantly better than Transformer-based baselines (P < 0.05). CMC-Seg required the fewest inference GFLOPs (381.49). Ablation experiments indicated that multi-direction scanning and cross-attention improved performance, particularly for RC (P < 0.05). Grad-CAM explainability experiments showed that CMC-Seg focuses on diagnostically relevant tumor regions.

</details>


### [227] [Revisiting RadioGalaxyNET: modern detectors for radio galaxy detection and host identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29798)
*Argo Saakyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在RadioGalaxyNET基准上训练D-FINE-seg、YOLO26-pose、RF-DETR三个当前检测器，全部超越2023年发表结果：D-FINE-seg M达67.1 box AP、72.4 host AP（分别+12.6、+24.3），并揭示基准评分协议与标注的多处特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RadioGalaxyNET是EMU先导巡天图像中寻找延展射电星系、进行分类并定位红外宿主的公开基准，但其最佳公开结果停留在2023年（Gal-YOLOv8 54.5 box AP、Gal-DINO 48.1 host keypoint AP），缺少对当前检测器能力的重新评估，也缺少对基准自身评分协议与标注质量的审计。

Method: 在相同数据上训练D-FINE-seg、YOLO26-pose、RF-DETR三个当代检测器，并使用基准自带评测代码打分（该代码可精确复现Gal-DINO原始数值），同时系统分析基准的评分协议、宿主标注位置、框标注偏移以及图像可重建性。

Result: 所有模型均超越公开结果：D-FINE-seg M达67.1 box AP与72.4 host AP，box性能与YOLO26持平，host AP为所测模型最高；其host优势主要来自占基准多数的宿主居中图像。端到端速度上D-FINE-seg S最快（1.9 ms/图像），同尺寸引擎对引擎时YOLO26更快。基准分析发现：评分协议虚增5–10 AP、host标签位于图像中心、box标签相对图像偏移1.5像素、图像可由公开巡天数据重建（可恢复天球坐标并作额外训练数据）。建议采用host识别F1：D-FINE-seg M 71.8、YOLO26-pose M 70.5、RF-DETR 63.8、Gal-DINO 59.2。

Conclusion: 2023年的基准最优结果已被显著超越，D-FINE-seg M在精度上领先；同时基准本身存在评分偏高、标注中心化与像素偏移等问题，未来工作应报告更贴近星表使用场景的host识别F1。

Abstract: RadioGalaxyNET is the public benchmark for finding extended radio galaxies in EMU pilot-survey images, classifying them and locating their infrared hosts. Its best published results date from 2023: 54.5 box AP (Gal-YOLOv8) and 48.1 host keypoint AP (Gal-DINO). We train three current detectors, D-FINE-seg, YOLO26-pose and RF-DETR, on the same data and score them with the benchmark's own evaluation code, which reproduces the published Gal-DINO numbers exactly. All exceed the published results. D-FINE-seg M reaches 67.1 box AP and 72.4 host AP, 12.6 and 24.3 points above the best published results, and the highest host AP of the detectors we tested; on boxes it is level with YOLO26. Its host lead over YOLO26 comes from host-centered images, which make up most of the benchmark. End to end, D-FINE-seg S is the fastest model at 1.9 ms per image; engine against engine, YOLO26 is faster at the same size. We also report what matters for anyone using the benchmark: its scoring protocol adds 5 to 10 AP, its host labels sit at the image center, its box labels are offset from the image by 1.5 pixels, and its images can be rebuilt from the public survey data, which recovers sky positions and doubles as extra training data. We suggest that future work report host identification F1, as a measure closer to catalog use: D-FINE-seg M 71.8, YOLO26-pose M 70.5, RF-DETR 63.8, Gal-DINO 59.2.

</details>


### [228] [Hierarchical Reasoning Action Head for Tiny Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29813)
*Aditya Makineni,Amitesh Sahoo,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分层推理动作头HR-AH，通过双潜状态异频更新实现轻量级VLA模型的推理能力增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时机载机器人控制需要轻量VLA模型，但缩小骨干网络会削弱推理时 deliberative 容量；CoT解码、双系统架构和更大骨干可恢复推理能力但会重新引入延迟与内存开销。循环动作头虽轻量，但现有设计仅精炼单一无区分状态，该状态需同时编码缓慢演化的任务上下文与快速变化的运动指令，存在表征冲突。

Method: 提出分层推理动作头HR-AH，维护两个以不同频率更新的潜状态：低级状态快速更新，以动作token和本体感知为grounding；高级状态缓慢更新，以视觉理解和受指令影响的低级状态为grounding。两状态通过嵌套推理循环交换信息。全部迭代在单次骨干前向传播后的紧凑头部内完成，不增加骨干规模。

Result: 22.5M参数的头（同骨干下比RD-VLA和VLA-Adapter小3-4.3倍），LIBERO达97.0%成功率，CALVIN ABC->D平均链长4.40，在对比方法中延迟最低。分层结构优于参数匹配的单状态头；将不同模态路由至不同层级可进一步提升精度并降低推理成本。

Conclusion: 在紧凑动作头内以分层异频潜状态迭代实现推理增强，可在不扩大骨干的前提下兼顾性能与实时性，并验证了模态分层路由的有效性。

Abstract: Real-time onboard robot control calls for a lightweight vision-language-action (VLA) model, yet shrinking the backbone leaves less capacity for deliberation at inference time. Chain-of-thought decoding, dual-system architectures, and larger backbones can restore deliberation but reintroduce the latency and memory costs that motivated going small. Recurrent action heads offer a lighter alternative by iterating in latent space before committing to an action, but existing designs refine a single undifferentiated state that must simultaneously encode slowly evolving task context and rapidly changing motor commands. We propose the Hierarchical Reasoning Action Head (HR-AH), which maintains two latent states updated at different frequencies: a low-level state that updates fast, grounded in action tokens and proprioception, and a high-level state that updates slowly, grounded in visual understanding and the instruction-informed low-level state. The two states exchange information through nested reasoning cycles. Because all iteration occurs in the compact head after a single backbone pass, HR-AH adds hierarchical reasoning without increasing the backbone size. With a 22.5M-parameter head, 3-4.3x smaller than those of RD-VLA and VLA-Adapter on the same backbone, HR-AH reaches 97.0% success rate on LIBERO and a 4.40 average chain length on CALVIN ABC->D, at the lowest latency among compared methods. We show that this hierarchy improves performance over a parameter-matched single-state head, and that routing distinct modalities to each level yields further gains in both accuracy and inference cost.

</details>


### [229] [Data-Quality-Aware Fundus Classification with Anatomy-Guided Glaucoma Assessment and Cross-Dataset Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29834)
*Imdadul Haque Sourav,Marjana Jannat Miti,Md. Jehadul Islam Mony*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在数据审计基础上，将解剖引导的专科模型与九分类眼底分类器融合，在不损失内部精度的前提下提升了跨队列迁移性能，但阈值迁移不佳，说明内部指标不足以证明可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习眼底分类器内部表现良好，但重复图像、标签不一致、采集差异与阈值迁移性差会削弱其可靠性，需要超越内部准确率进行多队列评估。

Method: 对5,318张图像审计重复与跨标签冲突，剔除464个冲突组（942张）及6张同标签副本，余4,370张；以三个随机种子训练ConvNeXt-Small、EfficientNetV2-Small、DenseNet169九分类模型；构建解剖引导的青光眼专科模型，读取视盘中心裁剪图并结合血管与视盘/视杯分割图及杯盘比特征；在RFMiD、JSIEC上做冻结模型外部评估，并在REFUGE上做预设比较，于ORIGA、G1020、PAPILA复现。

Result: ConvNeXt-Small内部准确率0.910、macro-F1 0.917、AUC 0.982，视盘与视杯Dice分别为0.966与0.915；固定解剖引导混合较等权软投票内部准确率变化-0.001，RFMiD外部闭集准确率提升4.9个百分点（三种子中两种子为正），JSIEC提升2.3；REFUGE（800张）平衡准确率提升7.4个百分点（95% CI 5.1–9.5），ORIGA提升2.1，G1020与PAPILA亦有正向增益；阈值迁移差（REFUGE敏感度0.950、特异度0.411）。

Conclusion: 视网膜分类器应以数据质量审计、解剖信息建模与多队列评估为标准；解剖引导融合在不增加内部代价下改善迁移，但增益因队列而异且阈值不可迁移，仅凭内部指标无法确立可靠性能。

Abstract: Background: Deep learning fundus classifiers often perform well internally, but duplicated images, inconsistent labels, acquisition differences and poor threshold transfer can undermine their reliability. We assess nine-class classifiers beyond internal accuracy, using a data audit, frozen-model external evaluation and an anatomy-guided specialist. Methods: Nine-class experts (ConvNeXt-Small, EfficientNetV2-Small, DenseNet169) were trained with three seeds. Auditing 5,318 images for exact duplicates and cross-label conflicts removed 464 conflict groups (942 images) and six same-label copies, leaving 4,370 images. An anatomy-guided glaucoma specialist read disc-centred crops with vessel and disc/cup segmentation maps, plus cup-to-disc features. Frozen models were evaluated on RFMiD and JSIEC, and a prespecified comparison on REFUGE was replicated on ORIGA, G1020 and PAPILA. Results: ConvNeXt-Small reached an internal accuracy of 0.910, macro-F1 of 0.917 and AUC of 0.982; disc and cup Dice scores were 0.966 and 0.915. Across seeds, the fixed anatomy-guided mix left internal accuracy unchanged relative to equal soft voting (-0.001) but raised mean external closed-set accuracy by 4.9 percentage points on RFMiD (positive in two of three seeds) and 2.3 on JSIEC. On REFUGE (800 photographs) it improved balanced accuracy by 7.4 percentage points (95% CI 5.1 to 9.5), and by 2.1 on ORIGA, with positive gains on G1020 and PAPILA. Thresholds transferred poorly (REFUGE sensitivity 0.950, specificity 0.411). Conclusions: Retinal classifiers should be judged by data-quality auditing, anatomy-informed modelling and multi-cohort evaluation. Anatomy-guided fusion improved transfer at no internal cost, but gains varied by cohort and thresholds did not transfer, so internal metrics alone do not establish reliable performance.

</details>


### [230] [Continuous-time 4D Reconstruction via Event-guided Latent Feature Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29838)
*Gwangtak Bae,Seunggu Kang,Junho Kim,Eun Sun Lee,Changwoon Choi,Young Min Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种连续时间4D重建方法，将视频帧输入几何基础模型后，在隐特征空间中插值并引入事件相机微秒级测量作为辅助监督，从而实现任意时刻的3D结构与相机位姿估计；配合基于伪真值与模拟事件的自监督训练框架，性能优于对RGB或几何结果做时间插值的基线，并对运动模糊与低光照具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何基础模型可将视频序列转换为已配准点云帧，但受限于观测帧率，无法预测观测时间分辨率之外（帧间）的瞬间。事件相机能提供微秒级连续证据填补时间空隙，但如何将异步事件流融入基于RGB的几何基础模型仍具挑战，且缺乏成对的RGB–事件–几何训练数据。

Method: 将连续时间重建形式化为隐特征空间中的插值问题：在基础模型的潜在特征上做时间插值以复用其几何先验，同时在图像空间施加事件损失作为辅助任务提供物理约束；为此设计自监督训练框架，利用高帧率RGB视频估计的几何作为伪真值并模拟生成事件流，以规避成对真实数据缺失。

Result: 大量实验表明，该流程优于在输入RGB视频上或已估计几何上做时间插值的基线方法，并可泛化到未见过的真实场景；受控的合成退化实验显示其在运动模糊与低光照条件下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 在基础模型的隐空间中进行连续时间插值并辅以事件域监督，是突破帧率限制、实现任意时刻4D重建的有效途径，结合自监督伪标签+模拟事件的训练策略可在无成对数据的条件下取得优于插值基线的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent geometric foundation models can successfully convert a video sequence into frames of registered point clouds, but cannot predict moments beyond the observed temporal resolution. We propose a continuous-time 4D reconstruction method that extracts 3D structure and camera poses at any time instant, with additional guidance from event-camera measurements. While event cameras provide evidence at microsecond resolution to continuously fill the temporal gap, integrating them into existing RGB-based foundation models remains challenging. We formulate the problem as interpolation in the latent feature space and augment event-based losses in the image space as an auxiliary task to boost accuracy. Operating in the latent space leverages the powerful geometric priors of foundation models, while the event loss provides physically grounded guidance. To effectively address the lack of paired training data, we further propose a self-supervised training framework that uses pseudo-ground-truth labels of estimated geometry and simulated events generated from high-frame-rate RGB videos. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that the proposed pipeline outperforms baselines that temporally interpolate either the input RGB videos or the estimated geometry, and that it generalizes to unseen real-world scenarios. Controlled experiments with synthetic corruptions show that it remains robust under motion-blur and low-light conditions.

</details>


### [231] [Dynamics Forcing: Mitigating Error Accumulation in Causal Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29839)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Dynamics Forcing 训练方案，仅用真实视频监督、通过三种方式扰动历史（随机加噪、自采样重生成、联合去噪）并将历史引导与首帧锚引导由推理移入训练，缓解因果视频世界模型的误差累积与"复制上一帧"捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果视频世界模型逐块自回归生成，早期块的微小误差作为历史被反馈并放大，最终导致场景与机器人结构崩坏。Teacher forcing 在干净真值历史上训练，模型从未见过推理时真正条件的历史，且易学得复制前一帧的捷径，而不去预测场景变化。

Method: 三种历史扰动：随机加噪、模型自身重采样（self-resampling）、联合去噪（预测下一块时最近块仍带部分噪声）；并将推理阶段使用的历史引导与首帧锚引导迁移到训练中，迫使模型依赖历史与参考帧。无需教师模型、无需蒸馏、推理时无额外模型。

Result: 在单 GPU 上以 LoRA 微调 5B 视频模型、使用 77 小时机器人操作视频：在未见过的真实视频延续上匹配度更高，并降低误差累积。

Conclusion: Dynamics Forcing 以真实视频为唯一监督，通过重塑历史呈现方式与训练期引导注入，有效抑制自回归误差累积并减少捷径行为，为因果视频世界模型提供轻量、无需额外推理开销的训练范式。

Abstract: Causal video world models generate block by block from their own previous outputs. Small errors in early blocks are fed back as history and grow until the scene and the robot fall apart. Teacher forcing trains on clean ground-truth history, so the model never sees the history it will actually condition on, and it also finds a shortcut: copying the previous frame instead of predicting how the scene changes. We present Dynamics Forcing, a training scheme that keeps real video as the only supervision and reshapes how history is presented. History is perturbed in three ways: random noising, self-resampling by the model itself, and joint denoising, in which the most recent blocks are still partly noisy when the next block is predicted. On top of this we move history guidance and first-frame anchor guidance from inference into training, so that the model is pushed to use its history and its reference frame rather than ignore them. Dynamics Forcing needs no teacher model, no distillation, and no additional model at inference. Applied to a 5B video model with LoRA on 77 hours of manipulation video on a single GPU, it better matches unseen real continuations and reduces error accumulation.

</details>


### [232] [Ego2Wrist: Bootstrapping Wrist-View Synthesis from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29850)
*Ziyao Zeng,Xiatao Sun,Hao Wang,Ziyu Guo,Youheng Yao,Jingcheng Ni,Shenyuan Gao,Fengyu Yang,Renrui Zhang,Bangya Liu,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ego2Wrist 从人类头戴式第一视角视频与任务描述中合成同步的手腕相机视角，并提出手部中心跨视角一致性（HCC）指标实现无真值评估与递归自改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手腕相机视角对机器人操作极具价值，但大规模采集人类手腕视频成本高、装置笨重且干扰自然手部动作；相比之下头戴式第一视角视频易于规模化，因此需要从头部视角合成手腕视角。

Method: 基于约 940 小时配对头-腕相机数据训练，Ego2Wrist 联合生成三个视角；提出 HCC 指标，度量生成手腕视图与输入头视图中对应手部区域的一致性；并将 HCC 用于在野外第一视角视频上筛选生成结果以进行迭代自训练。

Result: HCC 的模型检查点排序效果与基于真值的 LPIPS 相当，人工评估中 77% 偏好 HCC 选出的生成结果；在四个数据集上 LPIPS 较微调的 WorldWander 降低 28%，人类偏好率 76%；递归自改进三轮后生成质量持续提升。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可扩展的真实世界手腕视角人类操作视频生成方案，可用于机器人策略训练。

Abstract: Wrist cameras provide an action-centric view that is directly relevant to robot manipulation. However, collecting human wrist-camera videos at scale is challenging due to cumbersome setups that can interfere with natural hand movements. In contrast, head-mounted human egocentric videos are much easier to scale. We introduce Ego2Wrist, which synthesizes synchronized wrist-camera views from human egocentric videos and task descriptions. Trained on approximately 940 hours of paired head- and wrist-camera recordings, Ego2Wrist jointly generates three views. To evaluate generated wrist views without ground truth, we propose hand-centric cross-view consistency (HCC), which measures consistency between generated wrist views and corresponding hand regions in the input head view. HCC ranks model checkpoints comparably to ground-truth-based LPIPS, and human evaluators prefer HCC-selected generations in 77% of responses. Evaluated on four datasets, Ego2Wrist outperforms fine-tuned WorldWander, reducing LPIPS by 28% and receiving human preference in 76% of responses. More importantly, HCC facilitates recursive self-improvement on in-the-wild egocentric videos by selecting generated wrist views for further training. Generation quality progressively improves over three rounds. These results demonstrate a scalable approach to generating real-world wrist-view human manipulation videos for robot policy training.

</details>


### [233] [LexiDet-OVD: Benchmarking Lexical Sensitivity in Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29851)
*Amit Thakur,Mukesh Singhal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LexiDet-OVD基准与指标套件，系统研究开放词汇目标检测的词汇敏感性（提示词敏感性），发现平均精度与词汇稳定性是两种独立属性，且不存在主导性的提示聚合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇检测器的可靠性高度依赖用户对目标的文本描述方式，但标准评测通常只使用固定类别名，导致提示词诱发的失效模式研究不足，亟需一个量化词汇敏感性的基准与指标体系。

Method: 构建LexiDet-OVD基准：将提示词集划分为等价、条件、宽泛三类语义范围；在固定COCO（800张留出图像）与LVIS（1,000张图像）样本上各覆盖30类，评测四类检测器家族。除均值与最差提示AP50外，还提出P10、CVaR@20%、留一提示影响度、检测一致性、框一致性、置信度方差等指标，均基于一对一多实例匹配计算。并对比最大分数选择、加权框融合、Prompt Consensus及一种探索性融合规则。

Result: 等价提示词的最佳-最差差距在COCO1000上为5.3–23.4点、在LVIS1000-30c上为5.1–29.9点，表明高平均精度与词汇稳定性为相互独立的属性；聚合方法在部分设置下可恢复性能，但没有任何方法在所有模型-数据集组合上占优；Detic/SHiNe受控对比显示语义层级增强本身并不能消除词汇敏感性。

Conclusion: 建议在报告常规开放词汇检测精度的同时，一并报告提示词分布鲁棒性指标；词汇敏感性是当前开放词汇检测器不可忽视的独立评价维度。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detectors localize objects from free-form text prompts, but their reliability depends on how users describe the target. Standard evaluations usually test a fixed class name, leaving prompt-induced failure modes underexplored. We introduce LexiDet-OVD, a benchmark and metric suite for studying lexical sensitivity in open-vocabulary object detection. We organize prompt sets into equivalent, conditional, and broad semantic scopes, and evaluate four detector families on fixed COCO and LVIS samples, each covering 30 classes (800 held-out COCO images and 1,000 LVIS images). Beyond mean and worst-prompt AP50, we report P10, CVaR at 20%, leave-one-prompt-out influence, detection agreement, box consistency, and confidence variance using one-to-one multi-instance matching. Equivalent-prompt best-to-worst gaps range from 5.3 to 23.4 points on COCO1000 and from 5.1 to 29.9 points on LVIS1000-30c, showing that strong average accuracy and lexical stability are distinct properties. We compare maximum-score selection, weighted-box fusion, Prompt Consensus, and an exploratory fusion rule. Aggregation recovers performance in several settings, but no method dominates all model-dataset pairs. A controlled Detic/SHiNe comparison further shows that semantic hierarchy enrichment does not by itself eliminate lexical sensitivity. These results support reporting prompt-distribution robustness alongside conventional open-vocabulary detection accuracy.

</details>


### [234] [Towards Agentic Video Corpus Moment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29877)
*Chenwei Xu,Shang Wu,Zhenyu Pan,Jianshu Zhang,Pranav Maneriker,Lie Lu,Fan Du,Manling Li,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 RELAY，通过查询库、视频笔记与大模型重排、视频 prompt 缓存三阶段实现视频语料库时刻检索（VCMR），在 VERIFIED 基准的视频检索 R@1 上超越训练基线；经单一边际校准后，VCMR 在 IoU 0.5 上也超越训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VCMR 需在视频语料库中同时定位匹配文本查询的视频与时间区间，现有方法依赖训练。作者希望免训练完成该任务，并利用同一批次查询间的共享信息（跨查询记忆）提升效率与准确性，同时解决预测区间与标注约定不一致的问题。

Method: RELAY 分三阶段处理一批查询：(1) 搜索：嵌入模型以最佳匹配帧为每个视频打分，查询库对在批次中多个其他查询上同样高分的视频施加惩罚；(2) 排序：VLM 对候选视频的场景、人物、物体、动作一次性撰写笔记，再由 LLM 基于笔记重排候选；(3) 定位：VLM 在排名最高视频中预测时刻区间，当多个查询选中同一视频时用 prompt caching 复用帧。最后引入一个在少量标注查询上校准的单一 margin，将预测区间扩展到基准标注约定。

Result: 在三个 VERIFIED 基准上，RELAY 无需训练即在视频检索 R@1 上优于训练基线。无标注时刻时预测区间短于标注区间：VCMR 在一个基准上与 VideoSearch-R1 相当，另两个基准在 IoU 0.5 上落后；人类标注者亦标出比原标注更短的时刻。加入校准 margin 后，RELAY 在 VCMR 的 IoU 0.5 上超越训练基线。

Conclusion: RELAY 证明免训练、跨查询共享记忆的三阶段流程可有效完成 VCMR，并揭示基准标注约定与人类感知的短时刻之间存在系统性偏差；单一边际校准即可弥合该差距并使性能超越训练基线。

Abstract: Video corpus moment retrieval (VCMR) requires finding the video and time interval that match a text query in a corpus of videos. We present RELAY, a training-free framework for VCMR that shares memory across queries. RELAY processes a batch of queries in three stages: search with a query bank, ranking with video notes, and localization with video prompt caching. In the search stage, an embedding model scores each video by its best-matching frame. The query bank penalizes videos that score highly for many other queries in the batch. In the ranking stage, a vision-language model (VLM) writes notes on each candidate video's scenes, people, objects, and actions once. Then, a large language model (LLM) reranks the candidates from these notes. In the localization stage, a VLM predicts the time interval of the moment in the top-ranked video. When multiple queries select the same video, prompt caching lets the VLM reuse its frames. On the three VERIFIED benchmarks, RELAY outperforms the trained baselines in video retrieval R@1 without training. Without any labeled moment, its predicted intervals are shorter than the annotated ones. In VCMR, it achieves similar performance to VideoSearch-R1 on one benchmark and trails it at IoU 0.5 on the other two. Human annotators also mark shorter moments than the original annotations of the benchmarks. A single margin, calibrated on a few labeled queries, widens the predicted interval to the annotation convention of the benchmarks. With it, RELAY outperforms the trained baselines in VCMR at IoU 0.5.

</details>


### [235] [Reparameterizing Your Object Detectors for Weight Space Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29882)
*Chien-Yao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出逆重参数化目标检测权重空间编程 IR-ODWSP，通过保函数算子在解耦子空间内编辑权重，零参数/零FLOPs增量下提升多种检测器与跨域性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接对已收敛目标检测器做权重空间编程（WSP）会因集合预测将重复框判为假阳性而导致停滞或崩溃，同时紧凑参数化使权重陷于各向同性欧氏极小值，难以逃逸。

Method: 用精确保函数算子（E, C）将检测器层提升至解耦的矩、二维DCT、PCA 与 Stiefel-Procrustes 子空间；求解赢者独占更新并折叠回原空间（ΔParams=0%、ΔFLOPs=0%），由此诱导各向异性黎曼度量；并给出桥接收缩、1.00x 折叠、动态（IR-ODWSP-DYN）与静态（IR-ODWSP-SG）图等多种结构。

Result: 在 17 个 YOLO26、EdgeCrafter、RF-DETR 检测器的 26/26 MS COCO 设置上全部改善（最高 +0.31 APs）；COCO-O 跨盆地 +1.42 mAP；零图像修复 +2.95 mAP；无标签天气自适应 +2.79 AP。延迟预算 -100%~+100%：-10% 仍保持 51.62 AP / 33.48 mAP，+10% 提升 +0.30 AP，RF-DETR-N +1.12，+100% 达 54.83 AP，IR-ODWSP+ 达 55.91 AP / 35.44 mAP。

Conclusion: IR-ODWSP 在不重训练、零参数与零计算开销的前提下实现可靠的检测器权重编辑，并支持可控延迟预算与跨域/退化场景修复。

Abstract: Weight-Space Programming (WSP) edits network weights without backpropagation, yet directly editing converged object detectors stalls or collapses because set prediction penalizes duplicate boxes as false positives while compact parameterizations trap weights in an isotropic Euclidean minimum. We propose Inverse-Reparameterized Object-Detection WSP (IR-ODWSP), lifting detector layers via exact function-preserving operators (E, C) into decoupled moment, 2D-DCT, PCA, and Stiefel-Procrustes subspaces. Solving winner-exclusive updates and folding back (Delta Params=0%, Delta FLOPs=0%) induces an anisotropic Riemannian metric, improving all 26/26 MS COCO settings across 17 YOLO26, EdgeCrafter, and RF-DETR detectors (up to +0.31 APs), +1.42 mAP cross-basin COCO-O, zeroimage repair (+2.95 mAP), and label-free weather adaptation (+2.79 AP). Without retraining, IR-ODWSP supports -100% to +100% latency budgets across bridged contraction, 1.00x folding, dynamic (IR-ODWSP-DYN), and static (IR-ODWSP-SG) graphs: -10% retains 51.62 AP / 33.48 mAP, while positive budgets scale monotonically (+0.30 AP at +10%, +1.12 on RF-DETR-N; 54.83 AP at +100%; 55.91 AP / 35.44 mAP in IR-ODWSP+). Source code is released at https://github.com/WongKinYiu/ODWSP.

</details>


### [236] [Segment Anything In-Context via Visual Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29896)
*Yuzhu Wang,Manni Duan,Hongliang Ye,Shu Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAYD是一个基于视觉示例的上下文分割基础模型，通过提示图像及其掩码与查询图像建立视觉对应来分割连贯区域，在24个域外基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数分割模型依赖语言提示或固定类别定义，难以处理需要默会视觉知识、或用语言难以明确表述的概念的分割任务，因此需要一种基于视觉示例的通用上下文分割基础模型。

Method: 输入为提示图像及其分割掩码，通过在提示与查询图像间建立视觉对应来分割查询图像中的连贯区域；在12个原本用于全景分割的公开数据集上训练，并提出两项技术：用于与提示无关的掩码生成的轻量级掩码解码器，以及识别提示中未出现的新查询类别的学习机制。

Result: 在涵盖自然、生物、生态、宇宙学、医学、病理及卫星影像的24个域外基准上，SAYD显著优于现有上下文分割与开放词汇分割方法。

Conclusion: 视觉示例驱动的上下文分割可泛化至未见任务，轻量解码器与新类识别机制同时提升了计算效率与域外泛化能力，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: We present the Segment Anything Model via Visual Demonstrations (SAYD), a foundation model for in-context segmentation. Given visual demos comprising prompt images and their segmentation masks, SAYD segments coherent regions (e.g., objects or their parts) in a query image by establishing visual correspondences between the prompts and the query. SAYD generalizes to previously-unseen segmentation tasks, including those requiring tacit visual knowledge or involving concepts that are difficult to articulate unambiguously in language. We train SAYD on 12 public datasets originally curated for panoptic segmentation using our proposed techniques, including a lightweight mask decoder for prompt-agnostic mask generation and a learning mechanism for recognizing novel query classes absent in the prompts. These innovations enhance both computational efficiency and out-of-domain generalization. Consequently, SAYD significantly outperforms existing methods of in-context segmentation and open-vocabulary segmentation across 24 out-of-domain benchmarks, spanning natural, biological, ecological, cosmological, medical, pathological, and satellite imagery.

</details>


### [237] [SpectralRoute: Adaptive Spectral Routing for Training-Free Open-Vocabulary Hyperspectral Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29919)
*Qingmei Li,Xiaoya Fan,Juepeng Zheng,Jianxi Huang,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV
