### [271] [Multimodal View Training: Learning More Through Different Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30165)
*Devang Kulshreshtha,Priyanka Kaswan,Minh Pham,Chinmay Hegde,Niv Cohen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态视图训练（MVT）：将同一信息以文本、语音、图像三种视图呈现并训练模型输出一致目标，可在不引入新事实或监督的前提下提升模型学习效果，且在推理时保持纯文本架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型能否仅通过改变同一信息的呈现形式（而非增加信息量）学到更多，并希望把多模态收益迁移到纯文本大模型。

Method: 从文本样本生成对应音频与渲染文本图像视图，训练多模态语言模型在三种视图下产生相同目标输出；为目标纯文本LLM训练轻量连接器模块接入预训练音频与视觉编码器，使LLM在多模态输入下学习但推理时仍保持纯文本架构；并提出自适应MVT，与标准数据增强结合；辅以理论分析说明跨模态训练如何将权重更新导向共享事实内容、降低对视图特定特征的依赖。

Result: 在问答、机器遗忘、知识编辑任务上，自适应MVT相对纯文本训练分别提升QA召回+15.2个百分点、遗忘质量+40.7分、编辑泛化性+25.2分；MVT与标准增强结合进一步提升QA与编辑泛化性。

Conclusion: 同一信息的多视图呈现本身即可带来额外学习收益；MVT可有效增强纯文本LLM的问答、遗忘与知识编辑能力，且不改变推理时的文本架构，理论分析支持其通过促进共享事实表征发挥作用。

Abstract: Can a language model learn more from the same information simply by encountering it in different forms? We answer this in the affirmative by introducing Multimodal View Training (MVT), a simple training approach that presents identical information through text, speech, and images, without requiring new facts or supervision. Starting from a text sample, MVT generates corresponding audio and rendered-text image views, and trains a multimodal language model to produce the same target output across all three views. To further bring these benefits to text-only large language models (LLMs), we train lightweight connector modules to pretrained audio and vision encoders, allowing the LLM to learn from multimodal inputs while retaining its text-only architecture at inference. Across question answering, machine unlearning, and knowledge editing, adaptive MVT improves QA recall by +15.2 percentage points, forget quality by +40.7 points, and editing generality by +25.2 points over text-only training. Combining MVT with standard augmentation further improves QA and editing generality. Our theoretical analysis illustrates how training across modalities can shift weight updates toward shared factual content and reduce their reliance on features specific to each view.

</details>


### [272] [PathStream: Towards Streaming Diagnostic Copilots for Whole-Slide Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30167)
*Dankai Liao,YuFeng Wu,Ziyue Wang,Guanxi Liu,Tianyi Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Linghan Cai,Yueming Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出病理学家主导的流式诊断任务PathStreamBench（1,791张TCGA WSI、十器官、五名病理学家观看轨迹），并设计PathStream模型以两阶段训练联合学习流式响应与诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理AI多从选定区域预测、在固定视觉上下文中答题或自主导航切片，未反映病理学家沿观看轨迹整合跨区域、跨倍率形态证据的真实诊断过程。

Method: 形式化“病理学家主导的流式诊断”：病理学家控制导航，copilot选择性描述进入视野的视图并将累积证据综合为最终诊断。采集五名病理学家在1,791张TCGA全切片图像（十器官）上的观看轨迹，配以响应标签、形态描述及会话级发现与诊断，构建PathStreamBench评测响应时序、形态描述与会话级诊断（辅以视频理解）。模型PathStream采用两阶段训练联合学习流式响应与诊断，并用诊断注意力损失引导诊断时关注随检查累积的相关视觉与文本证据。

Result: PathStream在核心任务上优于14个视觉语言模型和3个领域微调基线，轨迹WSI相关性得分77.87，显著高于最强领域微调基线的62.76；无需额外训练即可泛化到新的教学病理视频、三个外部中心及一个未见器官。

Conclusion: 流式、病理学家在环的建模范式可有效对齐真实诊断路径，并在多源数据上展现良好泛化性。

Abstract: Pathologists reach a diagnosis by integrating morphological evidence across regions and magnifications along viewing trajectories. Existing pathology AI rarely reflects this process: models predict from selected image regions, answer questions within fixed visual contexts, or navigate slides autonomously. We formalize pathologist-led streaming diagnosis, in which the pathologist controls navigation while a copilot selectively describes incoming views and synthesizes accumulated evidence into a final diagnosis. We record five pathologists' viewing trajectories on 1,791 TCGA whole-slide images (WSIs) spanning ten organs, with response labels, morphological descriptions, and session-level findings and diagnoses. We construct PathStreamBench to assess response timing, morphological description, and session-level diagnosis, alongside auxiliary video understanding. We introduce PathStream, which jointly learns streaming responses and diagnosis through two-stage training, with a diagnosis-attention loss guiding attention during diagnosis to relevant visual and textual evidence accumulated as the examination unfolds. PathStream outperforms 14 vision-language models and three domain-fine-tuned baselines on the core tasks, achieving a trajectory WSI Relevance score of 77.87, surpassing the strongest domain-fine-tuned baseline's 62.76. Without additional training, its diagnostic capabilities generalize to new educational pathology videos, three external centers, and an unseen organ.

</details>


### [273] [GUI-Q: Task-Sensitive Granularity and Relation-Preserving Quantization for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30170)
*Tianqi Li,Yang Wang,Xin He,Le Zhang,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GUI-Q 是一个面向 GUI 智能体的训练后量化（PTQ）框架，采用 W4A16 配置，通过任务敏感粒度分配与关系保持的对称性网格选择，在保留响应精度与指令-视觉/局部空间关系的同时大幅降低显存占用并加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 智能体显存占用大、重复调用模型次数多，难以部署在资源受限设备上，需要免全量重训的 PTQ。但量化会破坏 GUI 响应准确率，并扭曲目标选择所需的指令-视觉亲和度与局部空间关系；坐标小误差导致点击失败，早期动作误差会改变后续界面状态并中断任务，而仅用局部重建误差无法直接评估这些响应与关系是否保持。

Method: 提出 GUI-Q：在 4-bit 权重/16-bit 激活（W4A16）下以“响应与关系保持”为目标。包含 (1) TSGA（任务敏感粒度分配）：在尺度字节预算约束下，结合响应敏感度与实测的激活加权失真下降量，为更敏感的层/组分配更细粒度分组；(2) RPSS（关系保持对称性选择）：选择对称或非对称量化网格，以保持 GUI 中的指令-视觉亲和度与局部空间关系。

Result: 在三个具备 GUI 能力的模型上，GUI-Q 相较最强 4-bit 基线配置将平均 grounding TargetHit 提升 8.04–12.55 点；静态峰值 GPU 显存相比 BF16 降低 49.9%–56.2%；采用打包 4-bit 执行的固定输出回放带来 1.7–2.8 倍的模型调用加速。代码将开源。

Conclusion: GUI-Q 表明以响应与关系保持为目标的 PTQ 可在 W4A16 下有效维持 GUI 任务精度，同时显著降低显存并提升推理速度，为资源受限设备上的 GUI 智能体部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents enable automated interactions with mobile, web, and desktop interfaces. However, their large memory footprint and repeated model calls hinder deployment on resource-constrained devices, motivating post-training quantization (PTQ) without full retraining. Quantization can degrade GUI response accuracy and distort the instruction-vision affinities and local spatial relations needed for GUI target selection. Small coordinate errors can cause missed clicks, while early action errors can alter subsequent interface states and disrupt task execution. Local reconstruction error alone does not directly assess whether these responses and relations are preserved. We propose GUI-Q, a PTQ framework that targets response and relation preservation with 4-bit weights and 16-bit activations (W4A16). Task-Sensitive Granularity Allocation (TSGA) allocates finer groups under a scale-byte budget by combining response sensitivity with the measured reduction in activation-weighted distortion. Relation-Preserving Symmetry Selection (RPSS) then selects symmetric or asymmetric grids to preserve instruction-vision affinities and local spatial relations in GUIs. Across three GUI-capable models, GUI-Q improves mean grounding TargetHit by 8.04 to 12.55 points over the strongest evaluated 4-bit configurations, while reducing static peak graphics processing unit (GPU) memory by 49.9% to 56.2% relative to bfloat16 (BF16). Fixed-output replay with packed 4-bit execution provides 1.7 to 2.8 times model-call speedups. The code will be released.

</details>


### [274] [OmniSenMo: A Unified Multisensory Language Model for Generalizable Human Motion Estimation, Understanding, and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30176)
*Cheng Guo,Qiming Cao,Pu Perry Wang,Pu Wang,Hongfei Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniSenMo 是一个以人体骨架为统一参照的多感官语言模型，将 RGB、深度、LiDAR、mmWave、WiFi 等异构传感器统一为逐关节 token，实现任意传感器子集下的融合与运动理解，在跨动作划分与跨数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 主要依赖 RGB 相机估计和理解人体运动；深度、LiDAR、mmWave、WiFi 可在弱光、遮挡和隐私敏感场景下补充 RGB，但已有多感官模型仅覆盖部分感知任务。困难在于：异构传感器 token 语义不一致且空间未对齐、配对多感官数据稀缺、各任务所需时间粒度不同。

Method: 提出 OmniSenMo：以人体骨架作为所有传感器的公共参照，构建 skeleton-grounded motion representation，把每个传感器转化为关节级 token（每一身体关节一个 token），从而可按关节逐一融合任意传感器子集，并表达为语言模型从动作捕捉与文本中学到的运动词表；使用多感官模拟器生成五种传感器数据做预训练；采用 Multi-Granularity Observation 为不同任务提供所需帧数。

Result: 在 MM-Fi 的跨动作划分上，五传感器全用的同一模型在各任务上均优于对比模型：位姿估计 MPJPE 84.1 mm vs. LEO-Fuse 的 89.0 mm；未见动作识别 69.2% vs. HoloLLM 的 42.8%。即使不使用 RGB，仍优于两种使用 RGB 的方法；在 XRF55 跨数据集验证中，未见动作识别显著优于 HoloLLM。

Conclusion: 以骨架为公共参照的关节级 token 化表示，可有效解决异构多感官语义不一致、空间不对齐与数据稀缺问题，使单一模型统一覆盖多传感器、多任务并支持任意传感器子集输入，在跨动作与跨数据集设置下展现强泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) estimate, understand, and predict human motion, but mainly from RGB cameras. Depth, LiDAR, mmWave, and WiFi complement RGB under poor lighting, through occlusions, and in privacy-sensitive applications, but existing multisensory models cover only some of these sensing tasks. Covering all of them from any sensor subset is challenging, since the tokens of heterogeneous sensors are semantically inconsistent and spatially misaligned, paired multisensory data are scarce, and the tasks need different temporal granularities. We present OmniSenMo, a multisensory language model that takes the human skeleton as a common reference for all sensors. Its skeleton-grounded motion representation turns each sensor into joint tokens, one per body joint, so that any sensor subset is fused joint by joint and expressed in the motion vocabulary that the language model learned from motion capture and text. We pretrain it on all five sensors generated by our multisensory simulator, and Multi-Granularity Observation gives each task the frames it needs. On a cross-action split of MM-Fi, the same model with all five sensors outperforms the models compared on each task, including LEO-Fuse in pose estimation (84.1 vs. 89.0 mm MPJPE) and HoloLLM in unseen-action recognition (69.2% vs. 42.8%). Even without RGB, it outperforms both methods that use RGB, and in a cross-dataset validation on XRF55, it recognizes unseen actions far better than HoloLLM.

</details>


### [275] [Visual-RFT++: Extending Visual Reinforcement Fine-Tuning to Multimodal Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30193)
*Ziyu Liu,Zeyi Sun,Xiaoyi Dong,Yuhang Cao,Haodong Duan,Dahua Lin,Jiaqi Wang,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Visual-RFT++：将强化微调统一扩展到视觉感知任务与多模态智能体工作流，实现「推理图像、操作图像、自评输出」，并显著超越SFT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RFT已成为大型推理模型的核心范式（如o3、R1系列），但如何用RFT把视觉感知、逐步推理与工具化智能体行为统一进单一训练范式仍是开放问题。

Method: 提出Visual-RFT++统一框架：以可验证奖励（verifiable reward）驱动RFT，同时覆盖视觉感知任务与多模态智能体工作流，使LVLM能够基于图像推理、对图像执行动作（网络搜索、代码化图像处理）并自评输出；同时构建Multimodal Agentic Tool Bench（MAT，含MAT-Search与MAT-Coding）用于评测智能体行为。

Result: 视觉感知上超越SFT：单样本细粒度分类+24.3pp，COCO 4-shot +21.0pp，LVIS（数百样本）约+15.4pp；智能体任务MAT-Coding +18.6 F1 / +13.0 EM，MAT-Search +10.3 F1 / +8.7 EM；多跳QA（2Wiki、HotpotQA）+29.3 F1 / +25.9 EM，显示跨模态泛化。

Conclusion: Visual-RFT++是一种数据高效、奖励驱动的框架，可同时增强视觉推理能力与智能体适应性，为LVLM的统一训练提供可行范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) has recently become a central paradigm for advancing Large Reasoning Models (LRMs), as demonstrated by systems such as OpenAI's o3 and the R1 series. Despite significant progress in LLMs, how to leverage RFT to unify visual perception, step-by-step reasoning, and agentic tool use into one coherent training paradigm remains an open challenge. In this work, we present Visual Reinforcement Fine-Tuning++ (Visual-RFT++), a unified framework that extends RFT to both visual perception tasks and multimodal agentic workflows, enabling LVLMs to reason with images, act upon images, and evaluate their own outputs using verifiable reward. With Visual-RFT++, open-source LVLMs not only show significant gains on diverse visual perception tasks, but also acquire agentic abilities such as web searching for real-time information and code-based image manipulation (e.g., cropping, rotation, and analysis). Across visual perception tasks, including fine-grained classification, few-shot detection, reasoning grounding, and open-vocabulary detection, Visual-RFT++ delivers strong gains over SFT, including a +24.3 percentage points improvement in one-shot fine-grained classification and +21.0 percentage points on COCO's 4-shot setting and +15.4 percentage points on LVIS with around several hundred samples. To evaluate agentic behaviors, we introduce the Multimodal Agentic Tool Bench (MAT) with MAT-Search and MAT-Coding. Visual-RFT++ surpasses its baseline by +18.6 F1 / +13.0 EM percentage points on MAT-Coding and +10.3 F1 / +8.7 EM percentage points on MAT-Search. It also shows gains of 29.3 F1 and 25.9 EM percentage points on multi-hop QA benchmarks such as 2Wiki and HotpotQA, demonstrating strong cross-modal generalization. Visual-RFT++ introduces a data-efficient and reward-driven framework that strengthens both visual reasoning and agentic adaptability.

</details>


### [276] [Quantization for Action, Execution for Deployment: Joint Precision and Execution Selection for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30195)
*Tianqi Li,Xin He,Xue Geng,Le Zhang,Ming-Ming Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PTQ4WAM：面向世界动作模型的量化与部署联合优化框架，在显存、延迟和任务评测预算约束下逐层选择位宽与执行路径，15 个工作负载中位任务分数达 FP32 的 99.2%，中位加速 2.36×、峰值显存降低 71.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型需反复读取需驻留设备内存的权重以比较上百条候选未来，部署受显存限制；训练后量化虽减小模型，但临时张量可能超出 GPU 显存，且微小预测误差即可改变所选动作。故需同时解决"保持任务性能的位宽分配"与"满足真实执行约束"两个耦合问题，且不能耗尽昂贵的任务试验。

Method: 提出 PTQ4WAM 框架：以校准测量引导候选位宽提案（可选引入语言模型智能体）；先在 GPU 上对全模型执行路径做性能剖析，再对可行分配投入任务试验；通过融合分组重缩放与原地读取视觉历史，将原本不可行的分配变为可部署。

Result: 在 4 个模型家族的 15 个工作负载上，中位任务分数为对应 FP32 分数的 99.2%；固定 GPU 复现中位加速 2.36 倍，峰值已分配显存中位降低 71.5%。

Conclusion: 将位宽分配与执行路径选择置于统一的资源预算框架下，可在几乎不损失任务性能的前提下显著降低显存占用与延迟，实现量化模型的可部署性。代码即将发布。

Abstract: World action models guide robots by predicting the effects of actions or generating commands from observations. A model may compare hundreds of candidate futures before acting, repeatedly reading weights that must fit in device memory. Post-training quantization reduces the number of bits used to store pretrained weights, but a smaller model is not necessarily a deployable one: temporary tensors can exceed graphics processing unit (GPU) memory limits, while even small prediction errors can change the selected action. This creates two coupled challenges: identifying bit assignments that preserve task performance and determining which of them meet real execution constraints, without exhausting costly task trials. We present PTQ4WAM, a framework for choosing layer-wise bit assignments and execution routes under memory, latency and task-evaluation budgets. Calibration measurements guide candidate proposals, optionally with a language model agent. We profile full-model routes on the GPU before spending task trials on feasible assignments. Fusing group rescaling and reading visual history in place can make an otherwise infeasible assignment deployable. Across 15 workloads from four model families, the median task score is 99.2% of the corresponding 32-bit floating-point (FP32) score. On fixed GPU replays, the median speedup over FP32 is 2.36 times and the median reduction in peak allocated memory is 71.5%. The code will be released soon.

</details>


### [277] [ToWo-VTON: Take-Off Wear-On Virtual Try-On via Topology-aware Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30203)
*Zhenchen Wan,Tianxi Chen,Runqi Lin,Tianyi Bo,Ce Chen,Jiaxin Huang,Yanwu Xu,Tongliang Liu,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ToWo两阶段范式（Take-Off生成紧身身体代理，Wear-On据此合成服装），并构建BFDC数据集与ToWo-VTON统一框架，提升下装/全身复杂场景虚拟试衣效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VTON方法在上装表现良好，但在下装与全身场景中面对复杂遮挡和大幅轮廓变化仍表现不佳；源服装信息干扰导致服装定位困难。作者希望将身体结构估计与服装合成解耦以缓解该问题。

Method: 提出ToWo两阶段范式：Take-Off先生成紧身身体代理作为结构先验，Wear-On在该先验条件下合成目标服装。基于此构建统一拓扑感知框架ToWo-VTON，并加入结构感知监督。通过身体感知扩散与VLM过滤构建BFDC数据集，提供上/下/全身的身体代理监督。分别在扩散骨干与MLLM引导骨干上实例化：扩散模型以身体代理作为人物输入在BFDC上微调；MLLM模型采用原生骨干条件输入，并提出Garment Boosting Attention（GBA）保留服装细节。

Result: 在VITON-HD与DressCode上均优于所评估基线；扩散实现与MLLM引导实现的KID分别降低26.1%与58.6%。BFDC训练与Take-Off/Wear-On策略结合后，在各服装类别上生成更连贯的肢体布局与服装边界。

Conclusion: 结构估计与服装合成的解耦范式可有效支持大幅轮廓变化下的服装定位，并可跨扩散与MLLM骨干泛化，为下装和全身虚拟试衣提供可复用方案。

Abstract: Virtual Try-On (VTON) aims to synthesize garment transfer without physical trials. Although recent methods perform well on upper-body garments, they still struggle in lower-body and full-body scenarios with complex occlusions and large silhouette changes. To address this limitation, we introduce ToWo (Take-Off Wear-On), a two-stage paradigm that decouples body structure estimation from garment synthesis. Take-Off first generates a tight-fitting body proxy that provides estimated structural guidance, and Wear-On then synthesizes the target garment conditioned on this prior. This decoupling reduces source-garment interference, supporting garment placement under substantial silhouette variations. Built on this paradigm, we propose ToWo-VTON, a unified topology-aware framework with structure-aware supervision. We construct the Body-Fitting DressCode (BFDC) dataset via body-aware diffusion and VLM-based filtering to provide body-proxy supervision across upper-, lower-, and full-body cases. We instantiate ToWo-VTON on both diffusion-based and MLLM-guided backbones. For diffusion-based models, we fine-tune on BFDC using the body proxy as the person input. For the MLLM-guided model, we use native backbone conditioning for the body proxy and target garment, and add Garment Boosting Attention (GBA) to preserve garment details. Experiments on VITON-HD and DressCode demonstrate improvements over the evaluated baselines, with KID reductions of 26.1% and 58.6% for diffusion-based and MLLM-guided implementations, respectively. The combined BFDC-training and Take-Off/Wear-On strategy also produces more coherent limb layouts and garment boundaries across the evaluated garment categories.

</details>


### [278] [Benchmarking Geospatial Foundation Models under Calibrated Label Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30215)
*Mollah Md Saif,Showrin Rahman,Anindya Majumder,Sowad Rahman,Md. Golam Rabiul Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一套在地球观测标签不完美条件下微调土基模型的基准评测协议：将四类空间结构化错误校准到相同标签mIoU并同等施加于所有模型与种子，采用配对比较与±3点等效边界，在9个编码器、6个解码头、10个数据集上评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实地球观测标签（来自地图、旧产品、仓促标注）常存在错位、粗糙、轮廓松散或不完整等问题，但地理空间基础模型仍以"正确标签"为基准被评测，导致实践者在标签不完美时无法判断应选哪个编码器。需要一套公平、可控、可复现的噪声标签微调基准协议。

Method: 设计基准协议：定义四类空间结构化错误，校准使其标签mIoU相等，并完全一致地施加于每个模型与随机种子；所有对比采用配对设计，以±3点等效边界与最小可检测效应为判读标准。实验覆盖9个编码器、6种解码头、10个数据集，并额外检验真实标签对（5对中4对）。

Result: 在匹配学习率的探索性结果中，卫星预训练与网络预训练的DINOv3编码器在9个数据集中的8个上处于等效边界内；多光谱GFM与ImageNet ResNet-50约在一半数据集上等效；相同标签质量下随机偏移型标签代价最小；其他解码头下结论在FLAIR-2上基本保持，在HLS Burn Scars上依赖解码头；5对真实标签中有4对，真实标签的代价高于其匹配的模拟错误类型。

Conclusion: 没有任何预训练数据选择能让编码器持续更鲁棒，因此应依据"不完美标签下的精度"而非预训练来源来选择编码器；评测需以配对、等效边界与标签质量校准为规范。

Abstract: Earth-observation labels from maps, older products or hurried annotation arrive misaligned, coarse, loosely outlined or incomplete. Geospatial foundation models (GFMs) are nevertheless benchmarked with labels taken as correct. A practitioner therefore cannot tell which encoder to choose when the labels are imperfect. We present a benchmark protocol for fine-tuning under such labels. Four spatially structured error types are calibrated to equal label mIoU and applied identically to every model and seed. Every contrast is paired and read against an equivalence margin of $\pm 3$ points and a minimum detectable effect. We apply it to nine encoders, six decoder heads and ten datasets. In an exploratory reading at a matched learning rate the satellite-pretrained and web-pretrained DINOv3 encoders are equivalent within the margin on 8 of 9 datasets. Multispectral GFMs and an ImageNet ResNet-50 are equivalent within the margin on about half of them. Labels with random offsets cost the least at equal label quality. Under other decoder heads the verdicts mostly hold on FLAIR-2 and are decoder dependent on HLS Burn Scars. On four of five real label pairs the real label costs more than its matched simulated type. No choice of pretraining data makes an encoder consistently more robust, so the encoder is better chosen by its accuracy under imperfect labels.

</details>


### [279] [RoomTokens: Masked Diffusion over Structured Tokens for Indoor Scene Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30219)
*Xiaohao Sun,Angel X. Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RoomTokens——一种切片式结构化token表示，每token编码语义类别、实例颜色身份与朝向场，配合掩码离散扩散模型MDIRT联合去噪三通道，在3D-FRONT上提升布局保真度与物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D室内场景生成需要同时捕捉物体语义、实例身份、高度结构与朝向，并遵守房间几何约束。现有序列化物体向量方法隐式学习空间约束，碰撞与越界依赖损失或后处理；语义地图方法显式表达占用，却丢失实例与方向结构。

Method: 1) RoomTokens：切片式结构化token表示，每个token存储语义类别、实例颜色身份与facing field（朝向场），使场景可表示为离散网格，同时保留实例分组、朝向估计与3D范围预测线索；2) MDIRT：基于RoomTokens的掩码离散扩散模型，以共享跨切片骨干网络联合去噪语义/实例颜色/朝向三个通道；3) 将建筑元素token作为显式floor-plan约束进行clamp，采样时保证布局尊重房间几何。

Result: 在3D-FRONT数据集上，RoomTokens+MDIRT相较序列/向量方法与地图方法在布局保真度和物理一致性（碰撞、越界）上均有提升。

Conclusion: 结构化空间token是离散3D场景合成的实用方案，兼顾显式占用表达与实例、朝向属性结构。

Abstract: Generating 3D indoor scenes requires a representation that captures object semantics, instance identity, height structure, and orientation while respecting room geometry. Methods that model scenes as sequences of object vectors learn spatial constraints implicitly, leaving collisions and boundary violations to losses or post-processing; semantic-map methods make occupancy explicit, but collapse instance and directional structure. We introduce RoomTokens, a sliced structured-token representation in which each token stores semantic class, instance-color identity, and facing field. This representation allows a scene to be generated as a discrete grid while preserving cues for instance grouping, orientation estimation, and 3D extent prediction. To learn the RoomToken distribution, we propose MDIRT (Masked Diffusion over RoomTokens), a masked discrete diffusion model that denoises semantic/instance-color/facing channels jointly with a shared cross-slice backbone. Architectural tokens are clamped as explicit floor- plan constraints, so the model samples furniture layouts that respect room geometry while retaining instance and object-attribute structure. Experiments on 3D-FRONT show that RoomTokens with MDIRT improve layout fidelity and physical consistency over sequence/vector- and map-based methods, suggesting structured spatial tokens as a practical choice for discrete 3D scene synthesis.

</details>


### [280] [EditGRN: Inversion-Free Image Editing by Source-Target Generative Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30224)
*Jiahuan Wang,Jianan Chen,Jian Han,Rui Li,Guangming Lu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditGRN：一种免训练、免反演、在离散空间中实现全轨迹源-目标生成同步的文本引导图像编辑框架，通过双分支设计在模型内部融合源上下文，兼顾背景保真与目标语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导图像编辑长期面临源内容保持与目标语义实现之间的权衡。现有基于离散生成模型的免反演编辑方法依赖事后概率校正，导致局部 token 级调整与全局语义生成之间脱节。

Method: 提出 EditGRN 双分支框架：源分支利用 GRN（生成精炼网络）从任意二值损坏中恢复干净 token 的能力，在每个精炼步直接构建损坏的源状态以提取编辑特征，无需恢复特定源生成轨迹；目标分支采用相同损坏调度与源分支同步，在模型内部将对应源上下文与可编辑表示融合。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 上，与连续型及离散型基线相比，EditGRN 在背景保持与文本对齐两方面均取得最先进性能，覆盖多种编辑任务。

Conclusion: 沿整条轨迹同步源上下文与目标生成，可在模型内部实现融合，从而忠实保留背景并生成与场景无缝衔接的高质量编辑结果。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing faces a persistent trade-off between preserving source content and realizing coherent target semantics. We explore this challenge through training- and inversion-free image editing based on discrete generative models. Existing inversion-free discrete editors primarily rely on post-hoc probability correction, creating a disconnect between local token-level adjustments and globally coherent semantic generation. To bridge this gap, we introduce EditGRN, a training- and inversion-free framework with trajectory-wide source-target generative synchronization in discrete space. EditGRN adopts a dual-branch design---the source branch constructs source references and the target branch generates the target image-enabling trajectory-wide synchronization between the two. Exploiting GRN's ability to recover clean tokens from arbitrary binary corruptions, the source branch directly constructs corrupted source states at each refinement step to extract useful editing features, without recovering a specific source-generation trajectory. The target branch synchronizes with the source branch through the same corruption schedule, fusing the corresponding source context with editable representations intra-model. By synchronizing source context with target generation along the entire trajectory, our approach faithfully preserves the background while producing high-quality edits that blend seamlessly with the surrounding scene through intra-model fusion. PIE-Bench show state-of-the-art performance on background preservation and text alignment across diverse editing tasks against continuous and discrete baselines.

</details>


### [281] [PixelOne: From Options to Pixels in a Single Forward for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30235)
*Kanghui Tian,Shuai Dong,Yuhan Zhu,Congcong Wang,Ji Zhao,Tianxiang Jiang,Zhixiong Zhang,Shengyuan Ding,Yi Wang,Jiaqi Wang,Yu Qiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixelOne 通过将 search teacher 的空间知识蒸馏到原生分辨率学生模型，实现在单次前向中以 0.43 s 平均延迟达到 83.8% 的 GUI grounding 平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI grounding 需低延迟的精确点击。现有方法（如 GUI-Decisions、GUI-Swift）虽用单次前向预测坐标，但依赖固定全局 bins 加局部细化：截图下采样会丢失定位小控件所需的细节，而固定 bins 在高分辨率下覆盖的像素范围过大，形成精度与分辨率的矛盾。

Method: 提出 PixelOne：(1) teacher 借助 VideoJEV 的 text-option scorer 选择行与列并渐进放大候选区域；(2) student 继承 teacher 参数，通过空间蒸馏匹配其在被探索区域上的分布，并结合 box 监督；(3) 推理时由同一 decision head 选择视觉 token，轻量 pixel head 在该 token 内预测点击，全部在一次前向中完成；(4) 引入 click-aligned loss，使目标框内像素得分高于框外像素。

Result: 在四个 benchmark 上，PixelOne-Pro-35B-A3B 取得 83.8% 平均准确率，为所评估的单次前向 grounder 中最高，平均每目标延迟 0.43 s。

Conclusion: 以搜索式 teacher 蒸馏替代固定 bins 细化，可在原生分辨率下实现高精度、低延迟的单次前向 GUI grounding，兼顾小控件定位细节与推理效率。

Abstract: GUI grounding requires precise clicks at low latency. Recent models such as GUI-Decisions and GUI-Swift predict coordinates in one forward pass using fixed global bins with local refinement. However, screenshot downsampling removes detail needed to locate small controls, while fixed bins cover larger pixel ranges at higher resolutions. We introduce PixelOne, which transfers spatial knowledge from a search teacher to a student that predicts clicks directly from screenshots at native resolution. The teacher uses VideoJEV's text-option scorer to select rows and columns and progressively zoom into candidate regions. The student inherits the teacher's parameters and matches its distributions over explored regions through spatial distillation alongside box supervision. At inference, the same decision head selects a visual token, and a lightweight pixel head predicts the click within it, all in one forward pass. A click-aligned loss encourages pixels inside the target box to score above those outside. Across four benchmarks, PixelOne-Pro-35B-A3B achieves 83.8% mean accuracy, the highest among the evaluated single-forward grounders, with a mean latency of 0.43 s per target.

</details>


### [282] [AIGI-26: A Benchmark for AI-Generated Image Detection Across Diverse Modern Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30236)
*Qishuai Ding,Yifeng Wang,Shichuang Xie,Yanli Ren,Guorui Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AIGI-26基准（253,200图、26个最新生成器）与多视角取证检测网络MAFDNet，在240,540图测试集上达88.5%准确率、94.9% AP，参数量仅1.04M、1.39 GFLOPs/256²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGI检测基准多基于早期合成系统，内容多样性有限且伪影明显，无法反映当代高保真生成图像的检测难度，缺乏对现代生成器进行系统评估的基础。

Method: 构建AIGI-26基准（26个近期开源/闭源生成器，253,200图）。MAFDNet包含两分支：固定取证算子（暴露纹理、局部预测、重采样、色度响应）经层次化编码器以多尺度均值与标准差汇总；频谱分支提取径向频带与方向轴上的傅里叶对数幅值及邻频相位变化。融合局部不一致性与全局频谱组织的描述子用于真伪判别。

Result: 以12,660张AIGI-26图像训练，在240,540张测试集上取得88.5%准确率与94.9%平均精度；仅1.04M参数、每张256×256输入1.39 GFLOPs；在DailyBench与Treasure-37上具备有效的跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 高保真现代生成图像需要更具代表性的基准与互补的多视角取证特征；轻量化的局部+频谱融合网络可在新基准上实现强检测性能并保持跨数据集泛化。

Abstract: Existing AI-generated image (AIGI) detection benchmarks predominantly rely on earlier synthesis systems with limited content diversity and conspicuous artifacts, failing to fully reflect the detection challenges posed by high-fidelity images from contemporary models. We introduce AIGI-26, a benchmark of 253,200 images covering 26 recent open- and closed-source generators. Its novel, diverse, and heterogeneous sources provide a basis for systematically evaluating detection methods on modern generated imagery. Additionally, to capture subtle statistical discrepancies across complementary forensic views, we propose the multi-aspect forensic detection network (MAFDNet). Its fixed forensic operators expose complementary texture, local prediction, resampling, and chromatic responses, summarized by a hierarchical encoder using multiscale means and standard deviations. In parallel, a spectral branch captures Fourier log-magnitude and neighboring-frequency phase variation across radial bands and directional axes. Fusing these descriptors combines local inconsistencies with global spectral organization for authenticity detection. Trained on 12,660 AIGI-26 images, MAFDNet achieves 88.5% accuracy and 94.9% average precision on the 240,540-image test set, with only 1.04M parameters and 1.39 GFLOPs per $256\times256$ input. MAFDNet also demonstrates effective cross-dataset generalization on DailyBench and Treasure-37.

</details>


### [283] [TriSegAgent: Context-Efficient 3D Annotation with MLLMs via Tri-Planes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30240)
*Qingqiao Hu,Jialin Li,Meilong Xu,Kehan Qi,Yipeng Zhang,Lin Tian,Hongwei Bran Li,Klaus Mueller,Xiaoling Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TriSegAgent：以三平面（tri-planes）为MLLM的紧凑2D观测，让MLLM智能体在3D体数据上迭代修正交互式分割预测，Dice较最强基线高约10点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式分割需人工反复"看预测—点击修正"，这一视觉判断循环适合多模态大模型智能体；但MLLM仅接受2D图像输入，且循环中需多次调用，故需一种token紧凑又能显现3D预测错误区域的2D观测表示。

Method: 以过同一体素点的三张正交切面（tri-planes）作为观测，理论推导与实验验证其上下文效率与细节保真；构建TriSegAgent，MLLM仅在tri-planes上做判断，由visual harness将判断转为精确到体素坐标的3D点击代码动作，并判定是否接受3D预测；基于35个公开医学数据集、三种模态构建690个ROI的评测基准。

Result: 使用前沿MLLM时整体Dice较最强基线高约10点，在小于3cm的靶标上领先更多；使用flash级MLLM时仍优于基于前沿MLLM的近期3D分割智能体。

Conclusion: tri-planes是兼顾token效率与错误可见性的有效3D—2D观测方式，配合精确落点的harness可构建可靠的3D交互式分割智能体，并具备以更小MLLM替代大模型的性价比优势。

Abstract: Interactive segmentation models turn a few clicks into a segmentation prediction across domains, so annotators only decide where the prediction still needs correction. Annotation thus becomes a loop of inspecting the prediction and clicking again. Each step of this loop is a visual judgment, which suits general-purpose multimodal large language model (MLLM) agents. In this work, we focus on letting these agents run this loop on 3D volumes. However, the MLLM in such an agent takes only 2D images as input, so the agent must observe the 3D volume in 2D. Since the loop calls the MLLM many times, this observation must be token-compact enough to keep every call cheap, yet informative enough to show error regions in the 3D prediction. We meet both needs with tri-planes, the three orthogonal planes that cross at one point in the volume. We show, both theoretically and empirically, that tri-planes are context-efficient yet keep sufficient detail to show error regions in the 3D prediction. Building on tri-planes, we introduce TriSegAgent, an MLLM agent that refines the 3D prediction iteratively. Within the agent, the MLLM only judges the prediction on tri-planes, while a visual harness turns each judgment into a 3D action in code. To make the loop reliable, the harness places each click at an exact voxel in the volume and checks whether to accept the 3D prediction. To evaluate the agent, we build a diverse benchmark of 690 regions of interest (ROIs) from 35 public medical datasets across three imaging modalities. With frontier MLLMs, TriSegAgent reaches an overall Dice about 10 points above the strongest baseline, and its lead is even larger on targets below 3 cm. With flash-level MLLMs, it outperforms a recent 3D segmentation agent built on a frontier MLLM.

</details>


### [284] [OCCA-Seg: Object and Channel Context Aggregation for Semantic Segmentation of Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30267)
*Gang Yang,Si Chen,Quan Zhou,Jiangchuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出对象与通道上下文聚合方案OCCA（含OCAM、CCAM、AM三个模块），用于遥感图像语义分割，通过对象级上下文与通道级上下文建模并加权调制，在LoveDA、UAVid、ISPRS Potsdam、Vaihingen等数据集上取得优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像语义分割面临复杂场景与多尺度目标的挑战，导致分割精度受限。

Method: 提出OCCA上下文聚合方案，包含三部分：OCAM基于"每个像素共享其所属对象的类别标签"这一观察，通过聚合其对应对象类别的上下文来表示每个像素；CCAM基于特征通道与对象类别相关的特性，以对象类别为条件建模通道间上下文依赖；AM接收OCAM与CCAM的上下文信息，用于对对象上下文进行加权与调制，增强模型判别能力。

Result: 在LoveDA、UAVid、ISPRS Potsdam、Vaihingen等多个公开遥感数据集上的实验表明，相较现有方法具有竞争力提升。

Conclusion: 通过对象级与通道级上下文聚合及加权调制，可有效缓解遥感图像复杂场景与多尺度问题，提升语义分割精度。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation, which assigns a class label to each pixel, is a key task in computer vision. However, semantic segmentation of remote sensing images (RSIs) remains challenging because of complex scenes and objects at multiple scales, which limits segmentation accuracy. To address these challenges, we propose a context aggregation scheme called object and channel context aggregation (OCCA), consisting of three components: object contextual attention module (OCAM), channel contextual attention module (CCAM), and aggregation module (AM). Firstly, motivated by the observation that each pixel shares the class label of the object to which it belongs, object-level context represents each pixel through aggregating context from its corresponding object class. Secondly, because feature channels are correlated with object categories, channel-wise context models channel-wise contextual dependencies conditioned on object categories. Finally, the context information from OCAM and CCAM are fed into AM and used to weight and modulate the object context, enhancing the discriminative ability of the segmentation model. The experiments on several public remote sensing datasets including LoveDA, UAVid, ISPRS Potsdam, and Vaihingen datasets demonstrate competitive improvements over existing methods.

</details>


### [285] [RigidAnchor: Cross-View Geo-Localization for UAV via Iterative Planar Rigid Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30278)
*Bo Wang,Dekang Zhu,Jian Li,Zhipeng Meng,Bo Wang,Dewen Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将无人机-卫星跨视角地理定位(U-S CVGL)重构为求解两幅异尺寸图像间的平面刚体变换（2D平移+平面内旋转），提出迭代式RigidAnchor，位置与航向不再受瓦片网格限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有U-S CVGL多按图像检索处理，输出只能落在预定义网格上，精度受瓦片尺寸限制且无法给出航向；单次回归虽可去除网格，但缺少后验修正机会。作者观察到卫星瓦片按固定间距、尺度与朝向铺放，相邻瓦片构成度量一致的连续卫星块，而垂直下视无人机视图与该块仅相差一个平面刚体变换。

Method: RigidAnchor迭代求解该变换：网络编码两幅图像并构建多层级相关金字塔；每次迭代用当前估计将每个空中格元索引到金字塔，将全部格元描述子池化为单一隐状态；两个解耦头分别预测平移增量与旋转增量，其中旋转以复合（compose）而非相加方式更新。

Result: 在Bearing-UAV-90K上，跨视角位置平均误差较Bearing-UAV降低78.6%，航向降低69.7%，HSR@15提升19.06个点。

Conclusion: 把位置与航向统一为一个几何量并迭代精化，可突破瓦片网格的精度瓶颈并同时输出朝向，验证了刚体变换建模式U-S CVGL的有效性。

Abstract: A UAV that flies without GNSS has to estimate its absolute position and heading without any external signal. Most existing methods for UAV-to-satellite cross-view geo-localization (U-S CVGL) treat the task as image retrieval: they compress the UAV image and each satellite tile into separate descriptors, rank the tiles by their similarity, and return the center of the best tile. The output can therefore only lie on a pre-defined grid, its accuracy is limited by the tile size, and no heading is produced. Regressing the pose in a single pass removes the grid, but it leaves no chance to correct the estimate afterwards. We observe that satellite tiles are not independent samples. They are laid on the map at a fixed spacing, scale and orientation, so neighboring tiles join into one continuous satellite block with a consistent metric, and a nadir UAV view of that block differs from it by a planar rigid transform only, i.e. a 2D translation plus an in-plane rotation. We therefore reformulate U-S CVGL as solving for this transform between two images of different sizes. Position and heading then become parts of one geometric quantity, and the accuracy is no longer limited by the tile grid. We propose RigidAnchor, which solves the transform iteratively. The network encodes the two images and builds a multi-level correlation pyramid between them. At each iteration, it indexes the pyramid with every aerial cell carried through the current estimate, pools the descriptors of all cells into one hidden state, and lets two decoupled heads predict a translation increment and a rotation increment, the latter being composed rather than added. On Bearing-UAV-90K, the cross-view mean errors are 78.6% lower in position and 69.7% lower in heading than Bearing-UAV, and HSR@15 increases by 19.06 points. Code is available at https://github.com/wwsource/RigidAnchor.

</details>


### [286] [AdvMTO: Multimodal and Temporal Adversarial Object Attacks via 2D Gaussian Splatting for BEV-Based 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30283)
*Zhiyang Cui,Hao Tan,Ruonan Li,Shibin Wu,Junjian Zhang,Hong'an Lin,Zhaoquan Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdvMTO，一个基于 2D 高斯泼溅（2DGS）的统一框架，从单一物体状态同时生成当前与历史时刻的相机图像与扫描线 LiDAR 回波，构造“检测器原生”的当前/历史输入，从而持续抑制 BEV 3D 检测器对对抗物体的检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗物体攻击多针对单一模态、仅在当前时间戳协调相机与 LiDAR，或只在相机流中建模时间持续性；缺乏对当前与历史观测的统一构造，导致检测器累积证据中仍有部分未被抑制，攻击效果受限。

Method: 1) 用有向面元（oriented surfels）将外观、几何与不透明度编码于同一物体状态，并由此生成相机图像与扫描线式 LiDAR 回波；2) 通过检测器原生处理流程构造当前与历史输入，对融合检测器在各原生扫描帧中重新生成 LiDAR 回波；3) 针对时间相机检测器，优化有界外观扰动并将梯度通过历史 BEV 记忆反传；4) 针对融合检测器，沿局部法线进行有界几何细化，同时扰动两种模态。

Result: 在 nuScenes 上对三种 BEV 检测器有效抑制了交通锥的检测；时间维度消融实验表明，检测器原生的历史建模对攻击构建与优化均至关重要。

Conclusion: 统一建模当前与历史、相机与 LiDAR 的对抗物体状态，并保持检测器原生输入构造，是提升时序 BEV 检测器对抗攻击持续性与有效性的关键。

Abstract: Adversarial object attacks pose a threat to BEV-based 3D object detectors in autonomous driving. Existing attacks typically target a single modality, coordinate camera and LiDAR observations at the current timestamp, or model temporal persistence mainly in camera streams. Without a unified construction of current and historical observations, attacks can leave part of the detector's accumulated evidence unsuppressed, limiting their effectiveness. In this paper, we propose AdvMTO, a 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) framework for suppressing detection of persistent adversarial objects. Its oriented surfels encode appearance, geometry, and opacity in one object state, from which camera images and scanline LiDAR returns are generated. AdvMTO then constructs current and historical inputs through detector-native processing, regenerating LiDAR returns in each native source sweep for fusion detectors. We optimize bounded appearance perturbations for each detector, propagating gradients through historical BEV memory for temporal camera detectors. For fusion detectors, bounded geometry refinement along local normals further perturbs both sensing modalities. Experiments on nuScenes demonstrate effective suppression of traffic-cone detection across three BEV detectors. Temporal ablations further highlight the importance of detector-native history modeling for both attack construction and optimization.

</details>


### [287] [Uncovering Separable and Composable Skill Mechanisms in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30290)
*Miaoyu Li,Boyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多技能可解释性框架DISCO，以"可分离性"和"可组合性"为技能定位的两大设计要求，通过Separable Head Selection选取技能专属注意力头，并首次给出度量这两性质的评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型视觉推理技能的内部机制定位工作，在机制识别与评估两方面均忽视了可分离性与可组合性，导致定位不精确、难以诊断与干预，也无法说明少量机制能否支撑更广泛能力。

Method: 将可分离性（机制主要贡献于目标技能、对其他技能影响有限）与可组合性（技能机制可组合以支持复杂任务）作为技能定位的核心准则；构建多技能可解释性框架DISCO：识别阶段提出Separable Head Selection，依据注意力头在各技能上的贡献差异选择技能特定头；评估阶段首次设计用于度量所识别头可分离性与可组合性的指标。

Result: 在3个VLM与4项基础视觉推理技能上开展实验，DISCO取得最高可分离性，分别以24.80、18.38、13.75个百分点的优势超过最强基线，并在全部三个模型上取得最高可组合性。

Conclusion: 可分离性与可组合性应作为技能定位的关键准则；DISCO在机制识别与评估两方面同时落实二者，实证表明其能更精准地定位技能机制并支持机制组合。

Abstract: Recent work has sought to localize the internal mechanisms underlying visual reasoning skills in vision-language models. These methods largely overlook separability and composability in both mechanism identification and evaluation. We propose separability and composability as two important desiderata for skill localization. Separability requires identified mechanisms to contribute primarily to their target skills while having limited effects on other skills, enabling precise diagnosis and targeted intervention. Composability requires skill mechanisms to be combinable to support complex tasks, allowing a small set of mechanisms to account for broader capabilities. In this work, we propose a multi-skill interpretability framework DISCO, built around these two properties. For identification, we propose Separable Head Selection, which selects skill-specific attention heads based on their contributions across skills. For evaluation, we are the first to design metrics for measuring separability and composability of identified heads. Extensive experiments on three VLMs and four fundamental visual reasoning skills demonstrate the effectiveness of DISCO. It achieves the highest separability, outperforming the strongest baseline by 24.80, 18.38, and 13.75 points across the three models, respectively, while also achieving the highest composability on all three models.

</details>


### [288] [PinGround: Pinning Queries to Points for Parallel Grounding in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30304)
*Shuxuan Li,Jialiang Kang,Yanghao Zhou,Ziye Zhu,Zhilin Zhao,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出锚点式并行解码方法，将每个查询写为单点空间锚点并让并行流各自检索最近实例，在不改架构的前提下实现稠密场景最高21.9×加速，F1最多仅比顺序解码低5.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM通过文本接口做视觉定位时把实例框写成单一序列，但同一查询的实例本质是无序集合，解码步数随实例数线性增长，稠密场景代价最高；而朴素并行解码的流之间互不可见，会重复或漏检实例。

Method: 在查询中写入单点作为空间锚点，要求每条流返回局部半径内距该点最近的匹配实例（或为空）；锚定查询彼此独立，作为共享图像与查询前缀树的叶子并行解码，训练也用同一树结构；配合由粗到细的锚点仅在检出区域细化、去重抑制保证一实例一框、以及集合级强化学习优化集合F1；仅改动prompt与attention mask。

Result: 在3种VLM、12个覆盖检测/OCR/版面/指代任务的基准上，F1最多比同数据训练的序列解码低5.5，加速比随实例密度增长，稠密场景达21.9×。

Conclusion: 并行集合式解码可在大幅降低稠密场景延迟的同时保持接近顺序解码的定位精度，且无需修改模型架构，具备良好的通用性与可移植性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can localize through their text interface, grounding open-vocabulary objects, complex referring expressions, text lines and layout regions with strong semantic understanding. They write the boxes one after another as a single sequence, although the instances of a query, unlike the words of a sentence, form an unordered set with no sequential dependence. The number of decoding steps therefore grows with the number of instances, which makes dense scenes the most expensive. Decoding the instances in parallel avoids this growth, but streams that cannot see each other duplicate some instances and miss others. We propose \method, which writes a single point into each query as a spatial anchor and asks for the matching instance nearest to that point within a local radius, or \none. Given the image, the anchored queries are independent, so they are decoded in parallel as leaves of a prefix tree that shares the image and the query, and the model is trained on the same tree. Coarse-to-fine anchors refine only the regions where instances are found, duplicate suppression keeps one box per instance, and set-level reinforcement learning optimizes the set F1. Only the prompt and the attention mask change, not the architecture. On three different VLMs and 12 benchmarks covering detection, OCR, layout and referring, \method is at most 5.5 F1 below sequential decoding trained on the same data, while its speedup grows with instance density and reaches 21.9$\times$ on dense scenes.

</details>


### [289] [SafeRank-ReID: Candidate-Conditioned Re-Ranking under Identity Conflicts for Multimodal Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30314)
*Yizhi Wang,Zixuan Li,Zhuwen Chen,Feiwei Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SafeRank-ReID，一种以候选为条件的多模态行人重识别安全重排框架；在由单模态证据、输入质量与跨模态一致性估计的模态-候选对权重基础上，结合候选集自注意力精排与查询级回退门控，提升身份冲突下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态ReID通常在查询级别估计模态可靠性，无法识别视觉质量正常但身份错误的冲突输入；这类输入会获得高置信度、提升无关候选，导致负向融合。

Method: 1) 候选条件证据仲裁：为每个模态-候选对整合单模态检索证据、输入质量与跨模态一致性，估计信任与冲突logit，并归一化为模态权重，使支持强度随模态与候选变化；2) 候选集精化模块：以自注意力联合建模候选表示，由候选间竞争关系预测残差分数以修正初始融合排序；3) 查询级回退门控：对精化后仍不确定的结果，依据整体冲突水平与跨模态不确定性估计插值系数，将融合分数与最可靠单模态分支分数自适应组合。

Result: 在RGBNT201与MSVR310上，跨行人与车辆基准均保持有竞争力的干净输入检索性能，并提升对身份冲突的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 候选条件化的证据仲裁与重排可有效抑制不可靠模态证据的负向融合，配合回退机制在干净与冲突场景间取得兼顾。

Abstract: Multimodal Person Re-Identification (ReID) exploits complementary identity cues from different sensing modalities to mitigate the performance degradation caused by challenging imaging conditions. Existing methods typically estimate Modality Reliability at the query level, making it difficult to identify conflicting inputs that retain normal visual quality but correspond to an incorrect identity. Such inputs may receive high confidence, promote irrelevant gallery candidates, and lead to negative fusion. To address this issue, we propose SafeRank-ReID, a candidate-conditioned safe re-ranking framework for Multimodal Person Re-Identification (ReID). To capture the varying support of the same modality for different candidates, we introduce a candidate-conditioned evidence arbitration mechanism that integrates unimodal retrieval evidence, input quality, and cross-modal consistency to estimate trust and conflict logits for each modality-candidate pair. Together, these logits contribute to the normalized modality weights, allowing support strength to vary across modalities and candidates while reducing the contribution of potentially unreliable evidence. A candidate-set refinement module subsequently applies self-attention to jointly model candidate representations and predicts residual scores from their competitive relations, thereby refining the initial fused ranking. For fusion results that remain uncertain after refinement, a query-level fallback gate estimates an interpolation coefficient from the overall conflict level and cross-modal uncertainty, adaptively combining the fused scores with those of the most reliable unimodal branch. Experiments on RGBNT201 and MSVR310 show that SafeRank-ReID maintains competitive clean-input retrieval while improving robustness to Identity Conflicts across person and vehicle benchmarks.

</details>


### [290] [Video Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30316)
*Bardia Safaei,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VideoGCD，将广义类别发现（GCD）扩展到视频动作识别，通过分区混合专家、新类平衡损失与时间最优传输损失提升新类识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GCD 方法在静态图像上有效，但在视频中因时间动态、类内差异更大、缺乏时间一致性而性能下降，缺少针对视频场景的 GCD 框架。

Method: 三项设计：(i) 分区式 Mixture-of-Experts 模块，促使专家分别专精于已知类与新类；(ii) 新类平衡损失，缓解对已知类的偏置；(iii) 时间最优传输损失（temporal optimal transport），促进跨帧路由的一致性。

Result: 在三个视频基准上，VideoGCD 显著优于现有 GCD 方法，尤其在新类上提升明显。

Conclusion: 结合时间建模与专家专精对视频 GCD 至关重要。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) aims to categorize unlabeled data from both known and unknown classes using labeled data from only the known classes. While recent GCD methods have shown promise on static images, their performance degrades in videos due to temporal dynamics, larger intra-class variation, and the lack of temporal consistency in existing approaches. We introduce VideoGCD, a framework tailored for video action recognition under the GCD setting. Our method integrates (i) a partitioned Mixture-of-Experts module that encourages specialization for known and novel categories, (ii) a novel-class balancing loss to reduce bias toward known classes, and (iii) a temporal optimal transport loss that promotes consistent routing across frames. Experiments on three video benchmarks demonstrate that VideoGCD significantly improves performance over existing GCD methods, particularly on novel categories. Our findings highlight the importance of combining temporal modeling and expert specialization for video GCD.

</details>


### [291] [Accuracy Is Not Evidence: Measuring and Training Evidence Dependence in Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30320)
*Zijie Jin,Shuai Zhao,Haoran Tao,Chaomin Huang,Liwen Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"证据依赖"(evidence dependence)度量，通过固定答案审计比较编辑标注区间内帧与等量区间外帧(安慰剂)导致的答案似然下降差异，揭示现有视频时序定位模型(含SFT与IoU奖励RL)准确率提升主要来自"盲答"和"拟合单一标注风格"而非依赖事件帧；并以该似然差为可训练目标，配合安慰剂对比与界限约束，在五个基座模型上提升证据依赖且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时序定位以Recall@IoU评价，正确区间可能来自真正找到事件、盲答或拟合单一标注风格，IoU分数无法区分，导致模型看似进步实则未依赖事件证据。

Method: 固定答案审计：分别编辑标注区间内帧与同视频等量其他帧(安慰剂)，比较标注答案似然的两类下降之差，定义为证据依赖；该量为模型自身似然差，故可直接作为训练目标，并加入安慰剂对比与安慰剂下降上界约束，防止模型学习对任意编辑反应。

Result: SFT与三个IoU奖励RL公开checkpoint使Qwen2.5-VL-7B精度提升9–20点，但证据依赖不高于基座；在五个基座模型上训练该目标均可提升证据依赖，权重由干净监督比例控制；与IoU奖励训练联合时保持精度增益；掩蔽事件帧时训练模型更常拒答或漏检，掩蔽等量其他帧则不然；查询命名其他事件时依赖随之转移，并在第二数据集与训练未见的patch scrambling下成立。

Conclusion: IoU类指标掩盖了证据依赖的缺失，所提审计可量化且可作为训练目标，在提升证据依赖的同时保持定位精度，并具备跨数据与跨扰动泛化性。

Abstract: In video temporal grounding, a correct interval can come from finding the event, answering blind or fitting one annotation style, and Recall at IoU scores them alike. We measure evidence dependence, how much the answer rests on the event's frames. Our fixed-answer audit edits either the frames inside the annotated interval or, as a placebo, as many frames elsewhere in the same video, and compares the two drops in the annotated answer's likelihood. Supervised fine-tuning and three public checkpoints trained with IoU-reward reinforcement learning raise the accuracy of Qwen2.5-VL-7B by 9 to 20 points, partly by answering blind and by fitting one annotation. Yet their answers rest on the event no more than the base model's, and the same holds for IoU-reward training on our data at the audit's frame rate. This comparison is a difference of the model's own likelihoods, so we can train on it. A placebo contrast and a bound on the placebo drop rule out learning to react to any edit. With them, evidence dependence rises in all five base models we train, and the weight on clean supervision controls how much. Added to IoU-reward training, the objective raises evidence dependence while keeping the accuracy gain. Masking the event's frames makes the trained models decline or miss the event more often than their base; masking as many other frames does not. The dependence moves to another event when the query names it, and holds on a second dataset and under patch scrambling, an edit never used in training.

</details>


### [292] [TAED: Trajectory-Aligned Encoder--Decoder Distillation for Detection Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30326)
*Zilong He,Zhangchi Hu,Hebei Li,Yijun Pan,Yifan Zhao,Xiangyu Yu,Yansong Peng,Xinghao Wang,Dongsheng Jiang,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轨迹对齐的编码器-解码器蒸馏框架TAED，解决基于编码器查询选择的DETR中学生与教师同排名查询空间不对应问题，在COCO上提升DINO-R18 2.5 AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在采用编码器查询选择的DETR中，教师与学生独立选择top-K候选，同序号查询可能源自不同空间位置并对应不同目标，导致基于排名的逐层监督不可靠，因此需要建立稳定的师生查询对应关系。

Method: TAED包含三部分：(1) 编码器特征蒸馏，对齐归一化多尺度特征，增强学生表示并增加源自同一编码器位置的师生查询对；(2) 源索引查询对齐，配对来自同一编码器位置的原生查询，仅保留对应同一目标且教师最终定位可靠且更精确的查询对；(3) 轨迹对齐定位蒸馏，保持上述对应关系，利用教师候选框及对应解码层框预测引导学生细化，且不引入额外查询。

Result: 在COCO上，TAED使DINO-R18与两阶段Deformable DETR-R18分别提升2.5 AP和1.7 AP；在D-FINE与ECDet上的额外增益表明该方法适用于近期实时DETR，且无额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 以空间来源（编码器位置）而非排名建立师生查询对应，是DETR蒸馏中更可靠的监督方式，可无缝迁移到多种检测器结构且不增加推理成本。

Abstract: Distilling localization knowledge into compact detection transformers (DETRs) requires consistent teacher-student query correspondence across decoder layers. However, in DETRs with encoder-based query selection, the teacher and student select top-K proposals independently. Same-rank queries can originate from different spatial locations and correspond to different objects, making rank-based layer-wise supervision unreliable. To address this problem, we propose Trajectory-Aligned Encoder-Decoder Distillation (TAED), a framework for knowledge transfer between DETRs of different capacities. Encoder feature distillation matches normalized multi-scale features to strengthen student representations and increase the number of teacher-student query pairs from the same encoder locations. Source-indexed query alignment pairs native queries from the same encoder locations and retains pairs associated with the same object for which the teacher provides reliable and more accurate final localization than the student. Trajectory-aligned localization distillation maintains these correspondences and uses teacher proposals and corresponding decoder-layer box predictions to guide student refinement, without introducing additional queries. On COCO, TAED improves DINO-R18 and two-stage Deformable DETR-R18 by 2.5 and 1.7 AP, respectively. Further gains on D-FINE and ECDet demonstrate applicability to recent real-time DETRs at no additional inference cost.

</details>


### [293] [Draft Forcing: Target-guided Speculative Drafting and Learned Verifier for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30328)
*Chenkai Xu,Yuji Ren,Zihui Liu,Yijie Jin,Yuhao Shen,Xin Wang,Yajing Chen,Keyang Luo,Shengju Qian,Litong Gong,Tiezheng Ge,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Draft Forcing 框架，通过目标引导的特征级起草（drafting）与在线策略训练的验证器，解决自回归视频扩散模型分块推测解码中的误差累积与验证缺失问题，在 14B Self-Forcing 上实现约 1.6 倍加速且质量损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型支持逐块流式合成，但每块都调用大模型代价高昂。分块级推测解码可将部分块交给轻量草稿模型，但现有方法存在两点缺陷：(1) 现成草稿模型与目标模型轨迹不一致，历史误差会在自回归过程中累积放大；(2) 要么省略验证，要么依赖代价高昂的解码后美学评分，而非与目标模型进行一致性校验。

Method: 提出 Draft Forcing，将目标引导起草与在线策略训练验证器相耦合。起草端采用特征级（而非像素级）起草，以从已提交分块中持久化的多层目标隐藏状态为条件，并用多方面损失将输出拉向目标轨迹以提升接受率。验证端设计特征空间验证器，预测经 CDF 校准的逐帧相对 RMSE（相对于目标），在推测 rollout 上以从随机混合逐渐退火至推理对齐混合的上下文进行在线策略训练，从而无需 VAE 解码即可门控提案。

Result: 在 14B Self-Forcing 目标模型上，Draft Forcing 在 VBench 上取得 1.63× 的 DiT 加速与 1.57× 端到端加速，质量损失极小；与正交的 KV 压缩结合时，DiT 加速可达 1.93×（端到端 1.82×）。该方法还可推广至加速 MiniMax-H3 的流式变体，生成超过 50% 的草稿帧且质量具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 特征级目标引导起草与在线策略训练的轻量验证器相结合，可有效缓解自回归推测解码的轨迹偏移与误差累积，实现无需解码的可靠门控，在不牺牲视频质量的前提下获得显著推理加速，并具备跨模型泛化性与与其他加速技术正交叠加的能力。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models enable streaming synthesis chunk by chunk, yet invoking the large model for every chunk remains costly. While chunk-level speculative decoding can offload chunks to a lightweight draft model, existing approaches suffer from two limitations: (1) an off-the-shelf draft diverges from the target's trajectory, compounding errors in history; and (2) they either omit verification or rely on costly post-decode aesthetic scores rather than verifying consistency against the target. We address both with Draft Forcing, a framework coupling target-guided drafting with an on-policy trained verifier. Specifically, to align the draft for high acceptance, we opt for feature-level drafting, conditioning it on multi-layer target hidden states persisted from committed chunks, whose outputs are pushed to match target trajectories with multi-aspect losses. For verification, we propose an on-policy trained feature-space verifier predicting CDF-calibrated frame-wise relative RMSE against the target. It is trained on speculative rollouts as contexts anneal from random to inference-aligned mixtures, and gates proposals without VAE decoding. On the 14B Self-Forcing target, Draft Forcing achieves $1.63\times$ DiT and $1.57\times$ end-to-end speedups on VBench with minimal quality loss, and up to $1.93\times$ DiT ($1.82\times$ end-to-end) when combined with orthogonal KV compression. It generalizes to accelerate a streaming variant of MiniMax-H3, generating over $50\%$ draft frames with competitive quality.

</details>


### [294] [AirVT-15k: A Query-Diverse UAV Video-Text Dataset and Query-Adaptive Evidence Alignment Network for Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30339)
*Xi Chen,Xu Chen,Xiangyang Jia,Ting Gan,Xu Zhang,Shuquan Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UAV视频-文本检索基准AirVT-15k（15,504视频/155,040双语描述）与方法AirFocus-CST，在AirVT-15k与CapERA上平均平均召回率分别达63.67%和24.41%，较最强基线提升2.96和1.91个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV-TVR需同时匹配场景上下文与细粒度物体变化：如"港口布局"类查询依赖全局场景，而"船只离港"类查询依赖局部功能线索与运动状态。现有基准虽提供多样航拍描述，但未区分同一视频内不同证据需求。

Method: 构建AirVT-15k：每个视频配10条互补的双语描述，覆盖场景、物体状态与时间变化；提出AirFocus-CST，包含查询相关的帧与图像块选择、时序特征差分编码，以及由粗到细交互（CFI）结合最优传输对齐（OTA）的匹配机制。

Result: AirVT-15k上平均平均召回率63.67%，CapERA上24.41%，分别较最强基线提升+2.96与+1.91个百分点；数据集与代码已公开。

Conclusion: 细粒度证据感知的选择与对齐对UAV-TVR有效，新基准可支撑区分全局场景与局部动态证据的评测。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle video-text retrieval (UAV-TVR) requires matching language to both scene context and subtle object changes. A harbor-layout query relies on global scene context, whereas a departing-vessel query requires local functional cues and motion states. Existing benchmarks provide diverse aerial descriptions but give limited attention to distinct evidence needs within the same video. We introduce AirVT-15k, a benchmark of 15,504 UAV videos and 155,040 captions, with ten complementary bilingual captions per video describing scenes, object states, and temporal changes. We also propose AirFocus-CST, which selects query-relevant frames and patches, encodes temporal feature differences, and combines coarse-to-fine interaction (CFI) with optimal-transport alignment (OTA). Average mean recall reaches 63.67% on AirVT-15k and 24.41% on CapERA, improving over the strongest baselines by 2.96 and 1.91 percentage points, respectively. The dataset and code are available at https://chenxi000233.github.io/AirVT-15k-site/.

</details>


### [295] [Shift by Analogy for Compositional Zero-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30342)
*Zanwu Liu,Bo Li,Guanglin Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Shift by Analogy (SBA)，通过"平移-匹配"流程在视觉空间中构造未见组合的代理特征，实现组合零样本识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合零样本学习（CZSL）需识别训练中未出现的属性-物体组合对；未见组合仅有语义描述而缺乏可观测视觉参照，导致视觉中心对应关系不足。

Method: 核心为加减-加（add-subtract-add）规则：将两个已见图文残差间的属性变化迁移到源物体上，转移至目标物体的参考残差，从而把目标自身文本特征平移到其视觉中心；再经学习式精化生成代理用于匹配查询图像。训练时排除目标自身作为支撑样本，用其他组合构建参考；并引入类比库与同物体注意力扩展候选构造，联合学习整合代理匹配、语义证据与基元证据。

Result: 视觉中心分析显示未见组合的对应关系显著改善；在三个标准基准上，封闭世界与开放世界评测中均稳定优于强CZSL基线。

Conclusion: 通过类比平移生成未见组合的视觉代理，可有效弥补其视觉参照缺失，SBA在多种评测设定下取得一致提升。

Abstract: Compositional zero-shot learning recognizes attribute-object pairs absent from training. An unseen pair has a semantic description but lacks an observed visual reference. We propose Shift by Analogy (SBA) to make unseen compositions visually seen through a shift-then-match process. Its core add-subtract-add rule transfers an attribute change between two seen image-text residuals on a source object to a reference residual on the target object. This shifts the target's own text feature toward its visual center; learned refinement produces a proxy for matching query images. During training, each target is excluded from its supports, and recognition is learned using references constructed from other pairs. An analogy library and same-object attention extend this construction across candidates, while joint learning integrates proxy matching with semantic and primitive evidence. Visual-center analysis confirms substantially improved correspondence for unseen compositions. Experiments across three standard benchmarks show that SBA consistently outperforms strong CZSL baselines under both closed- and open-world evaluation.

</details>


### [296] [SceneCast: Learning Indoor Scene Synthesis from Curated Synthetic Videos with LLM Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30345)
*Ziyang Zong,Canming Xia,Haoran Xu,Guang Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无标注框架 SceneCast，基于大规模无标注视频训练 3D 室内场景合成模型，通过 RL+LLM 空间常识评估与视频数据筛选策略，在场景质量上超越有监督 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 室内场景合成方法高度依赖人工标注数据集，成本高且泛化性受限。直接使用无标注视频面临两大挑战：视频仅提供场景局部视角，需模型外推未见 3D 区域，易出错；在线原始视频未经筛选，条件不可控、数据质量参差。

Method: 1) 设计基于 Transformer 的模型，顺序预测物体，并用强化学习微调，其中 LLM 充当空间常识评估器，通过思维链推理与可执行代码的几何校验提供奖励信号，以优化物体配置；2) 提出 \trim 数据筛选方法，利用可控视频生成模型合成训练视频，并通过多准则质量评估与基于熵的加权策略，仅保留高质量的前缀片段。

Result: 在筛选后的合成视频集上评估，该无标注方法在场景质量上优于当前最先进的有监督方法。

Conclusion: 无需人工标注、仅依赖视频监督即可实现高质量 3D 室内场景合成，LLM 推理奖励与可控视频生成的数据筛选是突破标注依赖的关键。

Abstract: 3D indoor scene synthesis requires generating objects to form a complete and coherent scene. Existing approaches rely heavily on manually annotated datasets, leading to high costs and limited generalization. We present SceneCast, an annotation-free framework that trains on large-scale, unlabeled videos, addressing two key challenges: (1) Videos provide only partial views of a scene, requiring the model to extrapolate unseen 3D regions, which is inherently error-prone; (2) Raw videos available online are uncurated, suffering from uncontrolled conditions and variable data quality. First, we design a Transformer-based model that predicts objects sequentially and is fine-tuned with reinforcement learning (RL), where an LLM acts as a spatial common-sense evaluator. The LLM provides reward signals through chain-of-thought reasoning and code-executable geometric checks to refine object configurations. Second, we introduce \trim, a data curation approach that generates training videos with controllable video generation models. Crucially, it retains only high-quality prefix clips via multi-criteria quality assessment with an entropy-based weighting strategy. Evaluated on curated synthetic video sets, our annotation-free approach outperforms state-of-the-art supervised methods in scene quality.

</details>


### [297] [NNS-GS: Non-Local Neural Scattering for Relightable Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30351)
*Shiyu Liang,Yang Gao,Lili Wang,Aimin Hao,Beibei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NNS-GS，用非局部神经散射与几何引导局部反射学习可重光照的半透明3D高斯资产，PSNR在合成与真实场景分别提升2.47 dB和0.81 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半透明物体的可重光照3D资产生成困难：改变某一表面位置的光照会通过次表面散射影响其他区域外观。现有基于高斯的散射模型通常由局部特征预测散射，难以刻画这种非局部依赖关系。

Method: 方法包含两个模块：(1) 散射模块在光空间学习可见光照表面对视空间着色点的贡献，并以区域漫反射响应和空间关系为条件，实现非局部散射；(2) 反射模块提供这些漫反射响应，并利用额外几何引导捕捉高光与锐利阴影边界。训练上采用基于锚点的渐进策略，先建立漫反射-散射基座，再引入镜面反射，并对各分量分别监督，以减少分量混叠并支持可控散射外观。

Result: 在校准点光源下，NNS-GS相比现有方法在合成与真实半透明场景上的平均PSNR分别提升2.47 dB和0.81 dB；额外合成对比显示，调整散射衰减时NNS-GS能更好地匹配参考结果。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模光空间到视空间的非局部散射并结合几何引导的局部反射与渐进分离训练，可有效提升半透明高斯资产的重光照真实感与散射可控性。

Abstract: Creating relightable 3D assets of translucent objects is challenging because changing the illumination at one surface location can affect the appearance elsewhere through subsurface scattering. Existing Gaussian-based scattering models typically predict scattering from local features, making this dependence difficult to capture. We present Non-Local Neural Scattering for Gaussian Splatting (NNS-GS), which learns non-local scattering together with geometry-guided local reflection. The scattering module learns how illumination from light-visible surface regions in light space contributes to the shading point in view space, conditioned on regional diffuse responses and spatial relationships. The reflection module supplies these diffuse responses and uses additional geometric guidance to better capture specular highlights and sharp shadow boundaries. To reduce component mixing and support controllable scattering appearance, anchor-based progressive training establishes the diffuse-scattering base before introducing specular reflection and supervises the components separately. Under calibrated point lighting, NNS-GS improves mean PSNR over existing methods by 2.47 dB and 0.81 dB on the synthetic and real translucent scenes, respectively. Additional synthetic comparisons further show that NNS-GS better matches the reference when adjusting scattering attenuation.

</details>


### [298] [STAR: Structure-Aware Temporal Action Recovery for Point Supervised Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30354)
*Yawar Rehman,Min Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAR：面向点监督时序动作定位的结构感知时序动作恢复框架，通过时序差分交互注意力、start/core/end角色建模、全点提案排序与RoleBank结构重排提升伪标签质量，并结合检测器反馈的软目标进一步精化伪标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点监督时序动作定位在视频数据快速增长的背景下需求迫切。现有方法主要依赖片段级动作响应生成伪标签，但这类标签存在边界模糊、提案过度延长、时序结构弱等问题，限制了定位精度。

Method: 提出STAR框架：(1) 在基础S模型上引入带注意力模块的时序差分交互，捕捉前向与后向片段变化；(2) 学习start、core、end三种角色以建模动作内部时序结构；(3) 通过全点提案排序(All point proposal ranking)与基于RoleBank的结构重排抑制相邻交叉及结构不一致的提案；(4) 构建时序动作检测器向STAR反馈监督，以软start/core/end/action/background目标将高质量检测预测回传，进一步精化伪标签。

Result: 在四个标准基准上验证有效性。使用I3D特征时：THUMOS'14上AVG@0.1:0.7达67.2%，GTEA达58.1%，BEOID达74.6%，ActivityNet-1.3上AVG@0.5:0.95达31.7%，在多个基准上优于此前的点监督方法。

Conclusion: 通过结构感知的伪标签生成与检测器反馈闭环，STAR有效缓解了点监督下的边界模糊与结构弱化问题，显著提升了时序动作定位性能，证明时序结构建模与伪标签精化在弱监督定位中的重要性。

Abstract: Point supervised temporal action localization is becoming the need of time in this era of ever growing and non-stop video data. Existing method mainly rely on snippet level action response to generate pseudo labels. However, these labels often suffer from boundary ambiguity, over extended proposals, and weak temporal structure. To address these issues, we propose STAR, a structure-aware Temporal Action Recovery framework for point supervised temporal action localization. STAR works by enhancing the base S model with temporal difference interaction with attention module to capture forward and backward snippet changes, and introduces the learning of start, core, and end roles to model the internal temporal structure of actions. In addition, STAR further improves the pseudo label generation through All point proposal ranking and Rolebank based structural re-ranking, which suppress neighbor crossing and structurally inconsistent proposals. Finally, temporal action detector to STAR feedback supervision transfers high quality detector prediction back to the STAR model through soft start, core, end, action, and background targets. This helps to further refine the pseudo labels. Extensive experiments on four standard benchmarks shows the effectiveness of STAR. By using I3D features, STAR achieves 67.2% at AVG@0.1:0.7 on THUMOS'14, 58.1% on GTEA, 74.6% on BEOID, and 31.7% at AVG@0.5:0.95 on ActivityNet-1.3, outperforming previous point supervised methods across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [299] [Boosting zero-shot learning through neuro-symbolic integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30358)
*Francesco Manigrasso,Fabrizio Lamberti,Lia Morra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLPN将零样本学习建模为视觉-语义嵌入空间中的原型匹配，并用Logic Tensor Network把逻辑公理作为可微损失注入先验知识，在GZSL的AWA2与SUN上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号（NeSy）方法很少被用于在训练中注入先验知识以提升零样本学习（ZSL）能力；ZSL还需缓解对已见类的过早过拟合与类级属性中的例外问题。

Method: 提出Fuzzy Logic Prototypical Network（FLPN）：在视觉-语义嵌入空间中做原型匹配分类；借助LTN框架，将一阶逻辑语言落地为真实张量间的可微运算，从而把类层级（类与宏类）及鲁棒的高层归纳偏置作为逻辑公理纳入NeSy损失；同时学习类级原型与属性级原型，属性级原型由面向CNN或Transformer骨干的注意力机制生成。

Result: 在广义零样本学习基准AWA2和SUN上达到SOTA，性能匹配或超过对比算法，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: NeSy式逻辑先验注入（含类层级与高层归纳偏置）能有效约束原型学习，抑制对已见类的过早过拟合并提升整体ZSL性能；方法通用、轻量，代码已开源。

Abstract: Zero-shot learning (ZSL) aims to train deep neural networks to recognize objects from unseen classes, starting from a semantic description of the concepts. Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) integration refers to a class of techniques that incorporate symbolic knowledge representation and reasoning with the learning capabilities of deep neural networks. However, to date, few studies have explored how to leverage NeSy techniques to inject prior knowledge during the training process to boost ZSL capabilities. Here, we present Fuzzy Logic Prototypical Network (FLPN) that formulates the classification task as prototype matching in a visual-semantic embedding space, which is trained by optimizing a NeSy loss. Specifically, FLPN exploits the Logic Tensor Network (LTN) framework to incorporate background knowledge in the form of logical axioms by grounding a first-order logic language as differentiable operations between real tensors. This prior knowledge includes class hierarchies (classes and macroclasses) along with robust high-level inductive biases. The latter allow, for instance, to handle exceptions in class-level attributes and to enforce similarity between images of the same class, preventing premature overfitting to seen classes and improving overall performance. % CHECK: original sentence had no verb ("Both class-level and attribute-level prototypes through an attention mechanism ...") FLPN learns both class-level and attribute-level prototypes, the latter through an attention mechanism specialized for either convolutional- or transformer-based backbones. FLPN achieves state-of-the-art performance on the GZSL benchmarks AWA2 and SUN, matching or exceeding the performance of competing algorithms with minimal computational overhead. The code is available at https://github.com/FrancescoManigrass/FLPN

</details>


### [300] [REACT: Response-based Expert Assembly from Contextual Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30366)
*Hesen Yang,Yuxue Liu,Yanzhe Chen,Weibo Zhao,Junwei Su,Dan Hu,Shenggan Cheng,Xuanlei Zhao,Wenchao Zhou,Junhua Wang,Lansong Diao,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出REACT方法，按神经元对相似输入的响应进行分组（upcycling），将稠密FFN转换为稀疏混合专家，仅激活37.5%的FFN参数即获得更优生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密FFN中每个神经元都被计算，神经元如何分组无关紧要；但稀疏执行下每个输入只能访问少数专家，任意划分虽保留预训练权重，却会把同一输入需要协同的计算拆散。因此作者主张upcycling应按神经元对相似输入的响应来组织神经元。

Method: 提出情境引导的upcycling方法REACT（Response-based Expert Assembly from Contextual Trajectories）：利用冻结的去噪轨迹将输入情境与预训练神经元响应关联。先经过逐时间步中心化以消除不同噪声水平带来的分布偏移，再识别情境，并用其对应响应将完整神经元分配给路由专家；同一情境用于初始化对应路由器条目，使专家内容与目标输入对齐；同时用全局激活统计选择常激活的共享专家；最后通过逐层重构与端到端适配精炼该初始化以适配稀疏执行。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，该方法在仅激活原FFN参数37.5%的情况下取得更好的生成质量，并将每token总激活参数减少27.6%。

Conclusion: 以响应为依据的专家组装（REACT）能有效将稠密FFN转化为稀疏专家结构，在显著降低激活计算量的同时提升生成质量，验证了按输入响应分组神经元的合理性。

Abstract: Dense feed-forward networks are unaffected by how their neurons are grouped: every neuron is evaluated. Sparse execution makes this grouping consequential, because each input can access only a few experts. An arbitrary partition can therefore retain pretrained weights while separating computations that an input needs together. We argue that upcycling should organize neurons according to their responses to similar inputs. To establish this correspondence, we introduce a context-guided upcycling approach REACT, Response-based Expert Assembly from Contextual Trajectories, that uses frozen denoising trajectories to connect input contexts with pretrained neuron responses. We first identify contexts after timestep-wise centering to account for distribution shifts across noise levels, then use their associated responses to allocate intact neurons to routed experts. The same contexts initialize the corresponding router entries, aligning expert contents with their intended inputs, while global activation statistics select an always-active shared expert. Layer-wise reconstruction and end-to-end adaptation refine this initialization for sparse execution. On ImageNet-1K, our approach achieves better generation quality while activating only 37.5% of the original FFN parameters and reducing total per-token activated parameters by 27.6%.

</details>


### [301] [Mind the Gaps: State-Guided Supervision Allocation for Object Detection Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30369)
*Jiehui Xie,Zhengxun Jin,Jiahao Zhang,Yuxuan Luan,Dongshen Han,Caiyan Qin,Sung-Ho Bae,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SGSA框架，将目标检测数据集蒸馏建模为数据集层面的渐进式监督配额分配问题，在COCO和VOC上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测数据集蒸馏方法强调单图信息量，未显式考虑整份蒸馏集累积的监督，易导致监督冗余与类-尺度覆盖不均。

Method: 设定类-尺度监督配额，维护追踪已有合成图累计检测器预测的全局状态；以目标配额与累积监督的剩余差距指导自适应目标采样、检测器引导的画布精修，以及基于边际增益的候选筛选，促进跨图互补监督。

Result: MS COCO上三种图像预算（0.25%–1.0%）下较匹配OD3基线提升3.73–4.06 mAP；PASCAL VOC三种预算下较最强基线提升4.53–6.90 mAP50。

Conclusion: 有限的图像预算下，由累积检测器反馈引导的数据集级监督分配是有效的。

Abstract: Recent advances in dataset distillation have enabled large-scale training datasets to be compressed into compact synthetic sets. However, dataset distillation for object detection remains challenging, as it requires preserving diverse classification and localization signals under limited image budgets. Existing approaches often emphasize individual-image informativeness without explicitly accounting for supervision accumulated across the distilled dataset, potentially leading to redundant supervision and uneven class-scale coverage. To address this limitation, we propose State-Guided Supervision Allocation (SGSA), a framework that formulates object detection dataset distillation as a dataset-level progressive supervision allocation problem. SGSA establishes class-scale supervision quotas and maintains a global state that tracks detector predictions accumulated from previously selected synthetic images. The remaining gaps between the target quotas and accumulated supervision guide adaptive object sampling, detector-guided canvas refinement, and marginal-gain-based candidate selection, encouraging complementary supervision across images. On MS COCO, SGSA outperforms a matched OD3 baseline by 3.73-4.06 mAP points across three image budgets (0.25%-1.0%). On PASCAL VOC, SGSA outperforms the strongest evaluated baseline by 4.53-6.90 mAP50 points across three image budgets. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of dataset-level supervision allocation guided by accumulated detector feedback under limited image budgets.

</details>


### [302] [ColonoWorld: Camera-Controlled Full-Procedure Colonoscopy Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30372)
*Jihwan Hong,Sunghwan Steve Cho,Ping Shu Ho,Joo Seong Kim,Bokyung Kim,Nam-Joon Kim,Simon See,Ji Won Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ColonoWorld——一个以相机轨迹和结肠3D管状骨架为条件、在活体视频上训练的结肠镜世界模型，可从盲肠到直肠生成完整检查过程，并按指定位置与时间放置息肉、器械和冲洗事件，相较SOTA视频生成器轨迹误差降低32%、FVD降低29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜导航系统正从"引导医师"走向"自主操控镜体"，错误动作会伤及患者，因此亟需一个能随指令运动、保持结肠解剖一致、并能呈现病变与操作事件的结肠镜世界模型用于开发与评估。现有方法存在两类缺陷：通用场景训练的相机控制生成器在运动下会丢失结肠结构；内窥镜专用生成器则缺乏相机控制能力。

Method: 以相机轨迹（commanded camera trajectory）和结肠的粗粒度3D管状结构（coarse 3D tube）为双重条件，在in vivo活体结肠镜视频上训练视频生成模型；支持从未见过的结肠形状出发、由整条结肠管生成全流程视频，并可在指定空间位置与时间注入息肉、器械与冲洗等事件。

Result: 生成质量与可控性优于现有SOTA视频生成器：相机轨迹误差降低32%，FVD降低29%；可对未见结肠形状生成完整的盲肠至直肠全过程，并实现病变与操作事件的时空可控放置。

Conclusion: ColonoWorld 首次将显式相机控制与结肠解剖一致性结合，为自主结肠镜导航系统的开发与安全评估提供了可控的世界模型基础；代码与标注将开源。

Abstract: Navigation systems in colonoscopy are advancing from guiding the endoscopist to steering the scope autonomously. Since an incorrect motion can injure the patient, developing and evaluating these systems calls for a world model of colonoscopy. Such a model must follow a commanded scope motion, keep the colon anatomy consistent, and show lesions and procedural events. Yet camera-controlled generators trained on general scenes lose the colon's structure under motion, and endoscopic generators offer no camera control. We present ColonoWorld, a colonoscopy world model conditioned on a camera trajectory and a coarse 3D tube of the colon, trained on in vivo videos. From a whole-colon tube, it generates a full cecum-to-rectum procedure, even for unseen colon shapes. It also places polyps, instruments, and irrigation at specified locations and times. ColonoWorld outperforms state-of-the-art video generators, reducing camera trajectory error by 32% and FVD by 29%. Our code and annotations will be released at https://aidaslab.github.io/ColonoWorld.

</details>


### [303] [BASE: A Backend-Agnostic Streaming Embodiment Layer for Full-Duplex Conversational Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30388)
*Chenye Yang,Jin Jiang,Shengcai Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 BASE——一种与对话模型后端无关的流式具身层，仅以对话模型输出的语音为接口，实现全身体态、倾听反馈与轮流对话的实时 3D 虚拟人驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将动作生成器与某一对话模型紧耦合（读取内部隐状态或联合训练语音与动作），导致对话模型更新即需重训动作模块、无法接入仅暴露音频的云 API，并纠缠角色、声音与动作风格。

Method: 1) 仅由音频驱动对话状态管理器，在统一时间线上处理说话、倾听、空闲、打断与主动轮次，角色/声音/动作风格/3D 外观独立配置；2) 将离线 2 s 流匹配手势模型改为滑窗续接推理，配合重叠前瞻集成消除接缝抖动；3) 提出 Drift-Aware History Perturbation (DAHP)，向真实训练历史注入推理式漂移，弥补自生成历史误差累积带来的流式差距。

Result: 在 BEAT2 上流式 BASE 取得 FGD 0.342，与紧耦合的 MIBURI（0.333）相当；85%→82% 强制选择中被试更偏好其身体动作；无需重训即可实时驱动级联流水线、三个开放全双工模型及一个云 API。DAHP 在单卡约 50 分钟内缩小流式差距，并可迁移至第二个生成器。

Conclusion: 以语音为唯一接口的解耦式流式具身层可在质量上媲美紧耦合方案，并具备良好的后端通用性与即插即用能力。

Abstract: Many end-to-end spoken dialogue models can now converse in real time and full duplex, and turning them into 3D avatars requires full-body gestures, listening feedback, and turn-taking. Existing methods couple the motion generator tightly to one dialogue model by reading its internal states or training speech and motion jointly. The motion module must then be retrained whenever the dialogue model changes, cannot be attached to cloud APIs that expose only audio, and entangles role, voice, and motion style. We propose BASE, a backend-agnostic streaming embodiment layer whose only interface to the dialogue model is its emitted speech. First, a conversation state manager driven solely by audio handles speaking, listening, idling, barge-in, and proactive turns on one timeline, while role, voice, motion style, and 3D appearance are set independently. Second, to run an offline 2 s flow-matching gesture model on live audio, we reformulate inference as sliding-window continuation with overlapped look-ahead ensembling, which removes seam jitter. Third, we show that the remaining streaming gap comes from errors accumulating in the self-generated motion history rather than missing future speech; our Drift-Aware History Perturbation (DAHP) reduces this gap in about 50 min on one GPU by injecting inference-like drift into ground-truth training histories, and transfers to a second generator. On BEAT2, streaming BASE reaches an FGD of 0.342, on par with the tightly coupled MIBURI (0.333), and viewers prefer its body motion over MIBURI's in 82% of forced-choice judgments. Without retraining, the same layer drives a cascaded pipeline, three open full-duplex models, and a cloud API in real time.

</details>


### [304] [LiquidGazeNet: Towards Event-Based Near-Eye Gaze Estimation under Low-Light Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30392)
*Himanshu Kumar,Sanjay Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiquidGazeNet，一个仅依赖事件相机的注视估计框架，结合极性感知脉冲延迟编码与LTC循环模块，在Gaze-FELL低光夜间数据集上跨被试误差3.75°、跨场景2.41°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光夜间条件下，传统强度帧的质量与有效时间分辨率下降，难以支撑近眼注视估计；事件相机具备高时间分辨率和低光鲁棒性，但缺少适配的表示与建模方法。

Method: 极性分离的双通道事件表示 + 指数衰减时间面（保留逐像素时间新近性）；共享卷积编码器逐窗口提取空间特征；两层LTC（液体时间常数）启发的循环单元对短时序列做因果整合；混合损失：屏幕空间坐标回归 + 以中心为参考的方向一致性约束。

Result: 在Gaze-FELL上跨被试平均误差3.75°，跨场景2.41°；并通过事件表示、时序模块、窗口时长与计算量消融验证各组件有效性。

Conclusion: 极性感知的时间新近性编码与循环时序整合相结合，在评估的低光夜间条件下对纯事件注视估计有效。

Abstract: Near-eye gaze estimation under low-light nighttime conditions is challenging because the quality and effective temporal resolution of conventional intensity frames can be reduced. This paper presents LiquidGazeNet, an event-only gaze-estimation framework that combines polarity-aware spike-latency encoding with a custom LTC-based recurrent module. Positive- and negative-polarity events are represented in separate channels, while exponentially decayed time surfaces retain per-pixel temporal recency across successive event windows. A shared convolutional encoder extracts spatial features from each window, and a two-layer recurrent module composed of LTC-inspired cells integrates them causally over short temporal sequences. The network is trained using a hybrid objective that combines screen-space coordinate regression with centre-referenced directional consistency. The framework is evaluated on Gaze-FELL under cross-subject and cross-scene protocols and is further assessed through event-representation, temporal-module, window-duration, and computational ablations. LiquidGazeNet achieves mean errors of 3.75 degrees and 2.41 degrees under cross-subject and cross-scene evaluation, respectively. The results indicate that polarity-aware temporal-recency encoding, together with recurrent integration, is effective for event-only gaze estimation under the evaluated low-light nighttime conditions.

</details>


### [305] [GeLiFiSH: Recursive LiDAR Keypoint Estimation with Ground and Moving Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30402)
*Nikolaos Stathoulopoulos,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无学习的关键点滤波框架GeLiFiSH，在滚动体素图上以序贯假设检验递归追踪持久关键点，实现扫描率下的可重复关键点提取，并在五个LiDAR、多会话数据上取得最高平均重复率与次高跨会话配准召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR配准与回环依赖稀疏关键点，其需在跨会话、视角与传感器间保持可重复性。但现有方法存在三方面缺陷：(1) 为适配不同扫描模式通常在累积子图上检测，带来延迟且逐子图独立处理、重复观测未被融合；(2) 基于高度的检测器以估计地面为结构参考，地面跨会话不一致会传播至关键点；(3) 动态物体留下"鬼影"轨迹被误判为静态结构。

Method: GeLiFiSH为无学习滤波流程，包含：(1) 高度—结构检测器运行于滚动体素地图，检测结果以位置、协方差与存在概率初始化轨迹；(2) 采用序贯概率比检验(SPRT)对轨迹进行接受/拒绝，递归估计持久关键点；(3) 地面分割提供传感器无关的高度参考，避免依赖先验传感器高度；(4) 运动目标分割在地图插入前剔除动态点。整流程以扫描率运行，仅需单一参数组。
