Result: 在SemanticKITTI、HeLiMOS与HeLiPR（覆盖5种LiDAR、多次会话）上，GeLiFiSH在对比检测器中取得最高平均关键点重复率；仅用关键点的跨会话配准召回率排名第二，仅次于ISS，而ISS提取的关键点数量为其4倍。地面分割与运动目标分割模块在平均意义上均优于各自基线。

Conclusion: 通过将持久性建模为序贯假设检验问题并结合地面/动态点预处理，可在无需学习、无需传感器高度先验且单一参数配置下获得高可重复性的扫描率关键点，为跨会话、跨传感器的LiDAR配准与回环提供更高效的前端特征，同时显著降低算力与关键点冗余。

Abstract: LiDAR registration and loop closure often rely on sparse keypoints, which must remain repeatable across sessions, viewpoints, and sensors. To handle differing scan patterns, detectors typically operate on accumulated submaps, which introduces latency and processes each submap independently, so repeated observations are never fused. Height-based detectors also reference structure to an estimated ground, so its inconsistency across sessions propagates into the keypoints. Moreover, dynamic objects leave ghost trails that can be mistaken for static structure. We present GeLiFiSH (Geometric LiDAR Filtering with Sequential Hypothesis testing), a learning-free filter that recursively estimates persistent keypoints at scan rate. A height-structure detector runs on a rolling voxel map, and detections initialize tracks with a position, covariance, and existence probability, accepted or rejected by a sequential probability ratio test. Ground segmentation provides a sensor-agnostic height reference, and moving object segmentation removes dynamic points before map insertion. On SemanticKITTI, HeLiMOS, and HeLiPR, spanning five LiDARs and multiple sessions, GeLiFiSH attains the highest average keypoint repeatability among the compared detectors and the second-highest cross-session registration recall from keypoints alone, behind ISS which extracts four times as many keypoints. With one parameter set and no sensor-height prior, both ground and moving object segmentation modules outperform their baselines on average.

</details>


### [306] [Spatial-Zero: Self-Evolving Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models from Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30424)
*Yutong Li,Haoyu Zhao,Daoguo Dong,Ziyi Ye,Yugang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Spatial-Zero，一种零数据、自演化的 VLM 空间推理训练框架，通过自主空间探索生成任务并自动标注监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理需要模型理解度量距离、三维关系与视角相关几何，而现有能力获取通常依赖外部人工整理训练数据，成本高且难以扩展。作者希望实现无需外部数据的自演化训练。

Method: 框架由多角色闭环构成：Proposer 指定物体配置与问题；Coder 将其转为可执行场景程序，生成多视角图像与显式场景几何；Geometric Oracle 校验空间约束、可见性与答案唯一性，并从执行几何中推导真值标签。这些标签用于监督 Solver 并支撑难度反馈，验证结果反过来引导后续任务生成，形成迭代自演化。

Result: 在六个空间推理基准、三种 VLM 主干上，经三轮迭代自演化均取得一致提升；Qwen3.5-9B 平均准确率提升 3.23 个百分点；在 Qwen3.5-4B 上也优于现有 VLM 自演化方法。

Conclusion: 自演化+几何验证的零数据范式可有效提升 VLM 空间推理能力，验证了用可执行场景程序提供可靠监督的可行性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning requires a vision-language model~(VLM) to understand metric distances, three-dimensional relationships, and viewpoint-dependent geometry. Learning these capabilities typically requires externally curated training data. This paper introduces Spatial-Zero, a VLMs' training framework that can self-evolve through autonomous spatial exploration with zero data. Spatial-Zero addresses two key challenges in autonomous spatial exploration: reliably generating valid spatial interaction tasks and providing accurate supervision for learning from these tasks. Specifically, a proposer specifies object configurations and questions, and a Coder turns them into executable scene programs that produce multi-view images and explicit scene geometry. A Geometric Oracle checks spatial constraints, visibility, and answer uniqueness and derives ground-truth labels from executed geometry; these labels supervise the Solver and ground difficulty feedback, while validation outcomes guide subsequent task generation. Across six spatial reasoning benchmarks and three VLM backbones, Spatial-Zero yields consistent gains through iterative self-evolution. After three iterations, Qwen3.5-9B improves average accuracy by 3.23 percentage points; with Qwen3.5-4B, Spatial-Zero also outperforms existing VLM self-evolution methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/YutongLi2024/Spatial-Zero

</details>


### [307] [Decompose and Describe: Narrowing the Diagnosis-to-Report Gap in 3D CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30458)
*Zhisong Wang,Wanxing Chang,Weiwei Cao,Fenghe Tang,Bowen Shi,Ruifeng Yuan,Yong Xia,Jianpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出医学视觉语言模型存在"诊断到报告差距"（diagnosis-to-report gap）：同一CT研究的诊断VQA表现优于其生成报告在相同疾病类别上的诊断表现。为此提出DeCAD，从诊断、区域文本、区域视觉三个层级逐步分解CT报告生成任务，显著提升报告级诊断性能，并在CT-RATE与Merlin上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化3D CT报告生成可支持放射科工作流，但MedVLM生成的报告常遗漏诊断发现。核心问题是：模型的诊断能力能否迁移到自由形式的报告生成中？实验发现诊断VQA的macro-F1普遍高于同一CT研究生成报告在相同疾病类别上的表现，即存在"诊断到报告差距"，且常规Report Adaptation虽能改善多数模型的报告质量，却无法弥合该差距。

Method: 提出DeCAD（Decompose CT Analysis and Describe），将CT报告生成在三个层级上渐进分解：1）诊断层级——在撰写报告前先识别阳性发现；2）区域文本层级——将诊断与描述组织为区域性子任务；3）区域视觉层级——将每个子任务的视觉token限制在对应解剖区域。方法在五个MedVLM上进行验证，并进一步在无医学VQA预训练的标准VLM上做消融，以检验各层级的通用性。

Result: 诊断层级与区域文本层级的分解在五个MedVLM上均提升了报告级诊断性能；区域视觉分解带来的额外增益有限，但配合区域级对比预训练编码器时可进一步提升。在无医学VQA预训练的标准VLM上，前两个层级仍有效。完整DeCAD在CT-RATE与Merlin数据集上取得SOTA。分析显示，相比Report Adaptation，DeCAD在诊断阶段对CT token的注意力更高，且诊断输出与最终报告对参考阳性发现的召回率更高。

Conclusion: 显式地组织诊断流程（先诊断、后描述，并按区域分解任务与视觉输入）能够改善诊断性报告质量，有助于缩小诊断到报告差距。该结果揭示了任务形式对MedVLM诊断能力迁移的影响，为3D CT报告生成提供了有效的任务分解范式。

Abstract: Automated 3D CT report generation could support radiology workflows, yet medical vision-language models (MedVLMs) often omit diagnostic findings from generated reports. We ask whether the diagnostic ability of these models carries over to free-form reporting. Across five MedVLMs, diagnostic VQA achieves higher macro-F1 than reports generated from the same CT studies and evaluated on the same disease categories. We call this task-dependent discrepancy the diagnosis-to-report gap. Conventional Report Adaptation improves reporting for most models but does not close the gap. To investigate and narrow it, we introduce DeCAD (Decompose CT Analysis and Describe), which progressively decomposes CT report generation at diagnostic, regional textual, and regional visual levels. The model identifies positive findings before writing a report, organizes diagnosis and description into regional tasks, and restricts each task to visual tokens from the corresponding region. Diagnostic and regional textual decomposition improve report-level diagnostic performance across the five MedVLMs, whereas regional visual decomposition provides limited additional gains. In a standard VLM without medical VQA pretraining, the first two levels remain effective; regional visual decomposition also provides further gains with a region-level contrastively pretrained encoder. Full DeCAD achieves state-of-the-art performance on CT-RATE and Merlin. Analyses show greater CT-token attention during diagnosis and higher recall of reference-positive findings in both the diagnostic output and final report than with Report Adaptation. These results show that explicitly organizing diagnosis improves diagnostic reporting and helps narrow the diagnosis-to-report gap.

</details>


### [308] [LiveMirror: Real-Time Diffusion Virtual Try-On on a Consumer GPU and Its Self-Supervised Adaptation to Indian Ethnic Wear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30461)
*Jigyansha Jeevan Rout,Sukanta Kumar Sabut*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveMirror 在单张 16GB 消费级 GPU 上实现实时摄像头虚拟试衣镜像：显示端以 25 FPS／720p 运行，生成端每 370–490 ms 更新关键帧，并通过异步关键帧试衣 + 光流插值 + 交叉淡入消除卡顿与闪烁；同时用大腿覆盖掩码、自监督配对与 3.2 GPU 小时 attention-only 微调修复南亚民族服饰被误渲染为短上衣或连衣裙的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式虚拟试衣（VTO）模型效果可信，但单图推理需数百毫秒至数秒，部署只能离线运行或退化为需要每件商品配套绑定 3D 资产的 AR 镜；同时基于 VITON-HD、DressCode 训练的模型存在文化盲区，把及膝 kurta／sherwani 错误渲染为及腰短上衣或裸露双腿的连衣裙。

Method: 1）异步关键帧试衣：生成不阻塞显示，每帧由最新关键帧经光流 warp 得到，关键帧仅交叉淡入替换；2）逐阶段延迟预算与加速策略评估（步数、一致性 LoRA、小型解码器、编译），并测量残余闪烁；3）用大腿覆盖掩码 + 从 24.9k 电商模特图自动构建的自监督配对 + CatVTON 的 3.2 GPU 小时 attention-only 微调；4）两遍下摆感知掩码解决下摆以下裤子被重着色问题，镜像中下摆直接复用上一关键帧。

Result: 显示 25 FPS@720p，关键帧每 370–490 ms 到达；民族服饰感知误差减半：IndoFashion 上 LPIPS 0.137→0.077，Atlas 上 0.135→0.073（各 200 对留出配对），西方上衣无退化；两遍下摆感知掩码在零训练成本下将下摆下裤子重着色失败率降至 3–4%；聚合民族服饰指标全面优于 FASHN VTON 1.5，推理成本约低 20 倍。

Conclusion: 通过异步关键帧架构与针对性数据/掩码适配，扩散式试衣可在消费级 GPU 上达到实时镜像可用性，并显著改善非西方服饰的渲染正确性；适配权重可直接部署到镜像系统。

Abstract: Diffusion virtual try-on (VTO) models produce convincing garment transfers from a single product photograph, but at hundreds of milliseconds to seconds per image every deployment today is offline or falls back to AR mirrors that need a rigged 3D asset per product. We present LiveMirror, a live-camera try-on mirror that renders a flat garment photograph onto a moving person with a diffusion model on one 16 GB consumer GPU. Its core is asynchronous keyframe try-on: generation never blocks the display, every displayed frame is an optical-flow warp of the latest generated keyframe, and keyframes are replaced only through a cross-fade. The display runs at camera rate (25 FPS at 720p) while a new try-on arrives every 370-490 ms; we measure the residual flicker, give a stage-by-stage latency budget, and report which accelerations (step count, consistency LoRA, tiny decoder, compilation) keep garment prints intact. We then show that the same GPU fixes the model's biggest cultural blind spot: try-on models trained on VITON-HD and DressCode render a knee-length kurta or sherwani as a torso-length top or a dress over bare legs. A thigh-covering mask, self-supervised pairs built automatically from 24.9k on-model e-commerce photographs, and 3.2 GPU-hours of attention-only fine-tuning of CatVTON halve the perceptual error on ethnic wear (LPIPS 0.137 to 0.077 on IndoFashion, 0.135 to 0.073 on Atlas; 200 held-out pairs per source) with no regression on Western tops. A two-pass hem-aware mask then removes the remaining failure, recoloured trousers below the hem, on all but 3-4% of pairs at no training cost; in the mirror the hem comes from the previous keyframe for free. The adapted model beats FASHN VTON 1.5 on every aggregate ethnic-wear metric at about $20\times$ lower inference cost, and its weights drop straight into the mirror.

</details>


### [309] [Struct2Det: Grounding Category Structure in Visual Evidence for Few-Shot Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30469)
*Kaixuan Chang,Huabing Zhou,Yanduo Zhang,Tao Lu,Liwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Struct2Det 利用类别结构（部件与关系图）引导少样本目标检测，通过关系结构编码、部件接地检测与验证引导精炼三阶段，并构建 InduSym 数据集，少样本类别 AP 提升 6.02/9.68 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本检测器难以区分外观相似、仅在局部部件上有差异的类别，因为少量样本难以揭示决定类别身份的关键差异。类别描述虽给出部件与关系，却无法确认其在图像中的存在。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 关系结构编码将类别图映射为部件嵌入与全局条件；(2) 部件接地检测用部件查询检索多尺度图像证据，并与全局条件共同调制类别文本嵌入以完成初始检测；(3) 验证引导精炼将候选区域与其最高分类别所期望的部件匹配，当类别分数接近且匹配可靠时，用匹配对修正区域特征后由共享检测器再预测。文本嵌入融合全图部件证据，区域特征融合候选特异的匹配结果。

Result: 在 InduSym 上少样本类别 AP 较最强基线提升 6.02 点，Digitize-PID 上提升 9.68 点，且两者基类 AP 均最优。消融显示增益来自正确的类别结构、图像级检索与候选验证。在少样本自然物体检测与零样本汉字识别上亦有提升。同时构建 InduSym：674 张工程图纸、275 类、88,912 个符号实例及每类结构图。

Conclusion: 显式引入类别结构可有效增强少样本检测的判别力，且该结构先验在工程图符号、自然物体与汉字等跨域任务上具有可迁移性。

Abstract: Few-shot detectors struggle to distinguish categories that look alike but differ in local components, because a few examples rarely reveal which differences determine category identity. Category descriptions specify components and relations but do not establish their presence in an image. We propose Struct2Det, which uses category structure to guide detection and verify candidates in three stages. Relational Structure Encoding maps category graphs to component embeddings and a global condition. Component-Grounded Detection uses component queries to retrieve multi-scale image evidence that, with the global condition, modulates category text embeddings for initial detection. Verification-Guided Refinement matches candidate regions with components expected by their top-scoring categories; when class scores are close and matches are reliable, matched pairs correct region features before the shared detector predicts again. Category structure informs both classification inputs: text embeddings integrate image-wide component evidence, while region features incorporate candidate-specific matches. We also construct InduSym, with 88,912 symbol instances across 275 categories from 674 engineering drawings and a structural graph per category. Struct2Det improves few-shot-category AP over the strongest baseline by 6.02 points on InduSym and 9.68 on Digitize-PID, with the highest base-category AP on both. InduSym ablations show few-shot gains from correct category structure, image-level retrieval, and candidate verification. Further tests show gains in few-shot natural-object detection and zero-shot Chinese character recognition.

</details>


### [310] [Inclusion of obstacles in ground point classification in ALS data for terrain models used for analysis of vehicle traversability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30471)
*Mikael Lundbäck,Erik Wallin,Sima Mohtasami,Martin Servin,Mattias Nyström,Henrik Persson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估多种ALS点云地面分类算法在含障碍物地面类场景下的性能，CSF算法RMSE最优（0.08 m），参数敏感性显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林场景中地形类型、传感器、飞行参数与点密度差异大，使ALS地面点分类困难；近年点密度显著提升，为低密度设计的算法可能失效。现有算法多无法在分类障碍物（如巨石）的同时区分非障碍物（灌木、下层植被），而越野机械路径规划与导航需要将障碍物归入地面类以构建可用地形模型。

Method: 将障碍物纳入地面类，评估不同ALS地面分类算法；在点云层面用Precision、Recall、F1-score，在地形模型层面用RMSE与差异图作为评价指标。

Result: 多种算法表现良好，但性能高度依赖参数设置；就地形模型RMSE而言，CSF算法最优，平均RMSE为0.08米。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，现有算法可经调整后将障碍物纳入地面类，从而生成适用于越野机械路径规划与导航的地形模型。

Abstract: Classification of ALS point clouds has been studied for decades, but it remains challenging in forest contexts due to the great variety of terrain types, sensor equipment, flight parameters, and resulting point densities. Point cloud density has increased significantly in recent years, and existing algorithms, developed for lower density point clouds, may no longer be effective. It is beyond the scope of most existing algorithms to classify obstacles, such as boulders, while also distinguishing non-obstacles, such as bushes and undergrowth. We evaluate the performance of different algorithms for ground point classification in ALS data in a context where obstacles are included in the ground class, resulting in terrain models useful for offroad machinery route planning and navigation. The evaluation is performed using metrics directly on the point clouds (Precision, Recall, $F_1$-score) and on resulting terrain models (RMSE and maps of differences). The results show that there are several algorithms that perform well, but performance is highly affected by the parameters used. The best results in terms of terrain model RMSE are obtained with the CSF-algorithm, with an average RMSE of 0.08 meters. The results indicate that existing algorithms can be adapted to include obstacles in the ground class under the tested conditions.

</details>


### [311] [Target-Part Preference Optimization for Part-Aware 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30472)
*Zishan Qin,Rui Li,Zeyu Zhang,Sevvandi Kandanaarachchi,Lina Yao,Dong Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于FlowDPO的参考相对偏好微调方法，仅更新DiT即可从少量部件标注数据中提升预训练部件感知3D生成器的部件完整性与分离度，中等训练集规模下在Objaverse上取得最佳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部件感知3D生成需要全局合理且语义部件完整、彼此分离，但高质量部件分解数据稀缺，难以支撑大规模有监督微调；因此探索能否在无架构改动下用DPO偏好微调让预训练生成器适应有限的部件标注数据。

Method: 用不同随机种子生成候选，并按GT几何指标排序，选取跨种子质量差距最大的(object, target-part)样本；每个样本存储一对共享同一干净GT组合隐变量、仅高斯噪声不同的流轨迹。基于FlowDPO推导参考相对的目标块偏好损失，将两条轨迹与冻结参考模型比较；同时对目标块与完整部件集施加GT流匹配损失作为绝对监督，训练中仅更新DiT。并设置人工排序对照与合成裁剪（构造性不完整负样本）作为对照。

Result: 在三种训练集规模中，中等规模在留出Objaverse上效果最强，在多部件聚合指标上优于预训练生成器及已报告的直接有监督微调基线；消融显示对配对顺序、目标部件覆盖度及成对监督与GT监督的平衡较敏感。跨数据集实验表明分布偏移下增益不够一致。

Conclusion: DPO式偏好微调可作为低数据条件下适配部件感知3D生成器的有效补充手段，但收益依赖于数据规模、配对构造与监督平衡，且在分布偏移下鲁棒性有限。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation requires not only globally plausible objects but also complete, well-separated semantic parts, yet high-quality part decompositions remain too scarce to support large-scale supervised fine-tuning. We study whether preference fine-tuning based on Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) can adapt a pretrained part-aware generator from limited part-annotated data without architectural changes. We rank candidates generated with different random seeds against ground-truth (GT) geometric metrics, and select the (object, target-part) examples with the largest across-seed quality gap. Each selected example stores a seed pair whose two flow trajectories share one clean GT compositional latent and differ only in Gaussian noise. We derive a reference-relative target-block preference loss from FlowDPO that compares the two trajectories against a frozen reference model. GT flow-matching losses on the target block and complete part set provide absolute supervision, while only the diffusion transformer (DiT) is updated. Human-ranked controls test an alternative ordering source, and synthetic cropping provides controlled loser targets that are incomplete by construction. Among the three training-set scales, the medium-scale setting gives the strongest result on held-out Objaverse and outperforms both the pretrained generator and the reported direct supervised fine-tuning baseline on the multi-part aggregate. Further analysis shows sensitivity to pair ordering, target-part coverage, and the balance between pairwise and GT supervision. Cross-dataset experiments show less consistent gains under distribution shift.

</details>


### [312] [Test-Time Latent Computation to Improve Visual Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30480)
*Chen Yang,Zheng Fang,Wenqi Fan,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练解码方法 Lobals，通过在潜在空间重访视觉证据（重前向注意力最强的连续层窗口）来提升多模态大模型在测试时的视觉感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明 LLM 可在测试时通过潜在空间额外计算增强推理，由此引出问题：MLLM 能否在测试时利用潜在空间额外计算改善视觉感知。

Method: 首先验证 MLLM 中存在稳定、可复用的层，可在推理时重复前向而不破坏正常生成；据此提出免训练解码方法 Lobals，识别对视觉 token 注意力最强的连续层窗口，并对其重新前向，使模型在潜在空间中重访视觉证据。

Result: 该潜在空间重访策略在多种多模态设置下均带来性能提升，包括通用视觉-语言任务、视觉幻觉任务与以视觉为中心的任务。

Conclusion: 测试时的潜在空间额外计算（层重前向）是提升 MLLM 视觉感知的有效且无需训练的手段。

Abstract: Recent work on Large Language Models (LLMs) suggests that reasoning capabilities can be enhanced at test time not only through longer token-level reasoning traces, but also through additional computation in latent space. This raises a natural question for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs): can additional computation in latent space improve visual perception at test time? In this work, we first verify that MLLMs contain stable reusable layers that can be re-forwarded at inference time without disrupting normal generation. Based on this observation, we propose \textbf{Lo}ok \textbf{Ba}ck in the \textbf{L}atent \textbf{S}pace (\textbf{Lobals}), a training-free decoding method that identifies the contiguous layer window with the strongest visual-token attention and re-forwards through it, allowing the model to revisit visual evidence in latent space. We demonstrate that this latent-space revisit strategy can improve MLLMs' performance across diverse multimodal settings, including general vision-language tasks, visual hallucination tasks, and vision-centric tasks.

</details>


### [313] [Do learned image captioning metrics generalize? Auditing duplicated samples in human-judgment benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30488)
*Xiaochen Zhang,Jin Li,Chunlong Xia,Longjiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文审计了近年图像描述（image captioning）学习型评价指标与人类判断相关性所宣称的"泛化"性质，发现测试样本在训练集中重复、Kendall τ主要由区分最差样本这一近乎饱和项贡献等问题，并提出更严格的评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型图像描述指标以与人类判断的相关性作为泛化声明，但若测试样本泄漏进训练集或相关性主要由平凡项贡献，该声明便不成立，需系统审计 Polaris、Nebula、Spica 三个最新人类判断数据集。

Method: （1）像素级匹配检测训练/测试集重复；（2）控制变量重训练，考察重复样本对指标（Pearl、DENEB）得分的膨胀效应及其随 epoch、学习器与重复频率的依赖；（3）将 Kendall τ 分解为"识别人类评分最低的caption"与"对其余样本排序"两项；（4）用冻结特征上的岭回归作为基线对比；（5）提出新协议：仅用不含测试样本训练的模型来排名指标、同时报告两项 τ、并用配对检验比较指标。

Result: Spica 训练集约含 Polaris 测试集的三分之二，且人类评分完全相同；Pearl 的分数增益仅局限于重复样本并随 epoch 增长，DENEB 增益不足 1 分；Kendall τ 的第一项（识别最低分caption）接近饱和并贡献大部分相关性；近三个测试集无法一致排序 Polos、DENEB、Pearl，而冻结特征上的岭回归可持平或超越三者。

Conclusion: 在所提协议下，零样本视觉-语言评判模型在三个最新测试集上排名第一，微调评判模型在三个更早基准上排名第一；该分析可推广至任何从人类判断中学习的评价器，过滤列表与代码已开源。

Abstract: Learned image captioning metrics are trained and tested on human judgments, so their reported correlation is a claim about generalization. We audit this claim for the three most recent collections of such judgments, Polaris, Nebula, and Spica, matching images at the pixel level. First, test samples reappear in training sets: the Spica training set contains about two-thirds of the Polaris test set, with identical human ratings. Second, controlled retraining shows that duplicates can inflate a metric: for Pearl, the gain is confined to the duplicated samples and grows with every epoch; for DENEB, it stays below one point. The gain depends on the learner and on how often a training set repeats a sample. Third, Kendall's $τ$ over all samples is the sum of two terms, detecting captions with the lowest human score and ranking the others; the first supplies most of the reported correlation and is nearly saturated. The recent test sets cannot order Polos, DENEB and Pearl consistently, and a ridge regression on frozen features matches or exceeds all three. Fourth, we propose a protocol that ranks a learned metric only with a model trained without test samples, reports both terms, and compares metrics by paired tests. Under it, zero-shot vision--language judges rank first on the three recent test sets, and a fine-tuned judge ranks first on the three earlier benchmarks. The same analysis applies to any evaluator learned from human judgments; filter lists and code are released at https://github.com/morninghut/ic-dataset-audit .

</details>


### [314] [When Do Spatial Plans Help Text-to-Image Generation? Causal Control, System Utility, and Text Responsiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30514)
*Lu Han,Jingyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统比较了"显式空间布局条件"与"纯文本条件"对生成器空间可控性的增益：布局的收益取决于模型从文本中已能实现的能力，且在区域间距受限时布局精度提升有限，提示指令在纯文本路径上更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往工作常假设提供空间计划（位置、尺寸等布局条件）必然优于文本提示，但缺乏在匹配训练算力下的受控对比；本文旨在厘清布局条件的真实增量收益来源及其边界条件。

Method: 采用18组配对训练种子的受控实验比较位置/尺寸校正与直接文本；在预训练生成器上比较固定规则布局与纯文本适配器；再在288个新任务族上引入最小间距（minimum-gap）指令，分别考察纯文本适配器、宽间距区域与紧间距区域三种条件。

Result: 校正预测位置与尺寸使空间IoU提升0.030，但在匹配训练算力下直接文本不劣于布局条件；固定规则布局使双物体任务成功率较纯文本适配器提升12.50个百分点，主要来自将已按正确顺序出现的物体分离；加入最小间距指令后，纯文本适配器提升37.15个百分点，宽间距区域至多提升0.69个百分点，紧间距区域提升6.94个百分点；紧间距条件下288张基线图中有230张物体与方向正确但未满足间距要求。

Conclusion: 布局条件的收益并非普遍存在，而是取决于模型从文本已能实现的能力与条件路径的精度上限；精确指令在文本路径上可替代布局实现空间分离，而基于布局的方法在区域间距受限时收益显著衰减。基线成功率低并非文本响应弱的唯一解释。

Abstract: A generator can follow a spatial plan, but the benefit of supplying that plan depends on what the model can already do from text. In a controlled study with 18 paired training seeds, correcting predicted positions and sizes improves spatial intersection-over-union by 0.030, yet direct text is non-inferior at matched training compute. In a pretrained generator, a fixed rule layout improves two-object task success over the same text-only adapter by 12.50 percentage points, mostly by separating objects that already appear in the correct order. We then ask whether a more precise instruction can achieve the same separation across conditioning paths. On 288 new task families, adding a minimum-gap instruction improves success by 37.15 points with the text-only adapter, by at most 0.69 points with widely spaced regions, and by 6.94 points with tightly spaced regions. Under tight regions, 230 of 288 baseline images have the correct objects and direction yet miss the required gap, ruling out a high baseline success rate as the sole explanation for the weak text response.

</details>


### [315] [Field-Level Crop Classification via Similarity-Induced Supervision from Imperfect Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30515)
*Raul Lomonte Figueiredo,João Rebouças Dorea,Dário Augusto Borges Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种无需新地面调查或人工纠错、直接利用有偏公共土地覆盖产品构建大尺度热带作物分类模型的方法：通过“样本级→组级”相似性聚类提升标签语义一致性，并用田块分割+多数投票提升预测空间一致性。在巴西两市上平均类别精度较噪声基线最高提升10.14个百分点，且州级泛化稳定、面积与官方统计一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带地区作物制图受持续云覆盖限制，SAR可全天候观测；但第二障碍是标签质量——国家级土地覆盖产品存在系统性偏差，低估稀有作物，直接在其上训练会继承并放大误差。现有工作通常依赖新野外调查或人工纠错，成本高、难以规模化。

Method: 两个机制：(1) 降低标签语义不一致性——把监督从样本级转为组级：基于相似性的聚类模型将SAR时间序列组织为一致性组，噪声标签仅用于为每组分配最具代表性的类别；(2) 降低预测空间不一致性——用光学影像训练分割模型勾绘田块边界，块级预测在每个田块内以多数投票汇总，得到空间连贯的图。整个流程无需野外调查、地面实测或人工标签修正。

Result: 两个研究区域聚类相对参考类别的纯度分别为89.0%和91.2%；在Campo Verde（马托格罗索）与Luís Eduardo Magalhães（巴伊亚）两市，平均类别精度持续优于噪声基线，最高提升10.14个百分点，少数作物受益最明显；在马托格罗索和巴伊亚全州范围分析中无需再调参即表现稳定，且制得的市域作物面积与模型从未观测的IBGE/PAM官方统计一致。

Conclusion: 公共土地覆盖产品虽有系统偏差，但经“组级监督+田块级投票”两步去噪后可用于训练可扩展的SAR作物分类模型，具备跨区泛化能力，并能产出与独立官方统计相符的区域作物面积估计。

Abstract: Regional-scale crop mapping is critical for food production monitoring, yet remains challenging in tropical regions, where persistent cloud cover limits optical imagery. Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) enables continuous observation throughout the agricultural cycle, but a second obstacle is label quality: national-scale land cover products exhibit systematic biases that underestimate less prevalent crops, and training directly on them inherits and amplifies their errors. This work proposes a methodology to build large-scale crop classification models from imperfect public land cover products, requiring no new field campaign, ground survey, or manual label correction. The approach combines two mechanisms. The first reduces the semantic inconsistency of the labels by shifting supervision from the sample level to the group level: a similarity-based clustering model organizes SAR time series into coherent groups, and noisy labels are used only to assign each group its most representative class. The resulting clusters are highly pure with respect to the reference classes (89.0% and 91.2% in the two study regions). The second reduces the spatial inconsistency of the predictions: a segmentation model delineates crop field boundaries from optical imagery, and patch-level predictions are consolidated by majority voting within each field, yielding spatially coherent maps. Evaluated in two Brazilian municipalities, Campo Verde (Mato Grosso) and Luís Eduardo Magalhães (Bahia), the approach consistently outperforms the noisy baseline in mean class accuracy, with gains of up to 10.14 percentage points, especially for minority crops. A state-scale analysis across Mato Grosso and Bahia shows stable behavior without further model adjustment, and the derived municipal crop areas are consistent with official IBGE/PAM statistics, an independent estimate the model never observes.

</details>


### [316] [GeoRead3D: Grounding Part Descriptions for 3D Pointing and Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30519)
*Zhangcheng Hou,Kevin Tonkich,Tomoaki Ohtsuki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 语言常通过部件指代物体，指向与再定向任务需要的是物体中心到部件中心的方向而非掩码重叠。作者提出几何读出监督（GRS）与GeoRead3D系统，直接学习点证据权重以加权质心定位部件，在两数据集上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导的部件分割以掩码重叠训练与评估，但当部件相对物体中心分散时，高掩码重叠并不能很好预测指向方向；分割产出与任务实际使用的部件几何之间存在鸿沟，需闭合该鸿沟。

Method: 提出GRS：学习观测点上的权重（点证据），其加权质心即部件位置，通过质心位置监督权重，并对再定向命令涉及的部件施加方向监督；系统GeoRead3D用语言模型将描述映射到已知部件名，双路部件证据结合细化分割证据与直接预测的点证据定位部件。

Result: 在PartNeXt与Artiverse上，22.5°内指向准确率为74.1%与60.0%，分别超出最强基线5.8与7.3个百分点；再定向成功率超出最强基线10.4与7.8个百分点；单一冻结编码器与单一输入位姿下仍有效。

Conclusion: 加权质心式的点证据监督比掩码重叠更契合指向与再定向任务，能有效弥合分割评估与部件几何使用之间的差距，GeoRead3D在两项任务上均取得一致提升。

Abstract: Language often refers to an object by its parts, as in asking to point to a part or to turn an arbitrarily posed object so that two named parts lie in given directions. Both tasks need the direction from the object's center to the part's center, not the part's own orientation. A natural approach segments the part and takes the centroid of its mask. However, high mask overlap predicts the pointing direction poorly when a part is spread out relative to its distance from the object center. Language-guided part segmentation is trained and evaluated by mask overlap, which leaves a gap between the masks it produces and the part geometry that these tasks use. We close this gap by learning point evidence, weights over the observed points whose weighted centroid gives the part's location. Geometric Readout Supervision (GRS) trains these weights through the centroid's position and adds direction supervision on the parts used in reorientation commands. GRS improves both tasks even with a single frozen encoder and a single input pose. We implement this supervision in GeoRead3D, a system for pointing and reorientation from part descriptions. A language model maps each description to a known part name. Dual-Route Part Evidence combines refined segmentation evidence with directly predicted point evidence to locate each part. On PartNeXt and Artiverse, GeoRead3D points within $22.5^\circ$ for 74.1% and 60.0% of queries, 5.8 and 7.3 percentage points above the strongest baselines. Its reorientation success exceeds the strongest baselines by 10.4 and 7.8 points.

</details>


### [317] [VeriOrigin: A Proactive Image Protection Framework for Provenance, Tamper Localization, and Lossless Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30523)
*Jiale Chen,Wei William Wang,Junnan Yin,Chongyang Shi,Li Dong,Yuanman Li,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VeriOrigin统一图像保护框架，基于CNN可逆编码器XOnet实现来源认证、篡改定位与无损恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方案难以同时兼顾来源溯源与篡改检测；且常用流式（flow-based）可逆架构在复杂失真下鲁棒性与恢复能力不足，缺少统一且无损的框架。

Method: 设计CNN可逆编码器XOnet，由两个标准CNN水印子网络Xnet与Onet组成，通过交叉残差预测实现可逆性；采用两阶段训练策略，先优化水印分支再优化篡改定位分支；推理时将载体图像依次进行水印嵌入与篡改残差嵌入。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明，VeriOrigin实现精确篡改定位与强鲁棒性，并在无损信道下保证载体图像的无损恢复以支持完整性认证。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了溯源与篡改检测，验证了CNN可逆编码器替代流式架构的可行性，兼顾鲁棒性、定位精度与无损恢复。

Abstract: Versatile watermarking, which seamlessly integrates provenance and tamper detection, has recently attracted widespread attention. In this paper, we propose \textbf{VeriOrigin}, a comprehensive and unified image protection framework that not only robustly achieves provenance and highly precise tamper localization under various complex distortions, but also guarantees perfect recovery of the original cover in lossless channels. To realize this framework, we depart from conventional flow based architectures. Instead, we design \textbf{XOnet}, a CNN based invertible encoder. XOnet comprises two standard CNN based watermarking sub networks, Xnet and Onet, which achieve reversibility through cross residual prediction. Based on XOnet, we train the proactive protection scheme via a two stage strategy: first optimizing the watermarking branch, followed by the tamper localization branch. During inference, the cover image undergoes sequential watermarking and tamper residual embedding. Extensive experimental results on four datasets demonstrate that VeriOrigin achieves precise tamper localization and strong robustness, while guaranteeing lossless cover recovery for integrity authentication. Project page is available at https://veriorigin.github.io/

</details>


### [318] [Assembly-JEPA: Using World Models for Fine-Grained Industrial Image Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30530)
*Michael Hollmer,Andreas Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于JEPA自监督世界模型的工业装配验证框架，将装配验证建模为前后观测的预测式世界建模任务，在自建数据集上AUROC达86.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业装配自动验证面临视觉变化大、缺陷标注数据稀缺、错误粒度细等挑战；现有基于目标检测、CAD模型或监督分类的方法难以跨产品与装配配置泛化。

Method: 以JEPA世界模型LeWorldModel为基础，将其适配为细粒度装配验证任务：把装配正确性判定表述为基于装配过程"前后"观测的预测式世界建模，进行自监督联合嵌入预测学习，并分析架构需做哪些调整。

Result: 在新引入的装配验证数据集上，方法可可靠检测细粒度错误装配状态及动作-状态不匹配，AUROC为86.9%。

Conclusion: JEPA式预测表征可作为鲁棒、数据高效的工业装配验证基础；自监督世界模型路线优于依赖标注与CAD的既有流程。

Abstract: Automated verification of industrial assembly processes remains a challenging problem due to substantial visual variability, limited availability of labeled defect data, and the fine-grained nature of many assembly errors. Existing approaches often rely on object detection, CAD models, or supervised classification pipelines, which can struggle to generalize across changing products and assembly configurations. In this work, we propose a novel self-supervised assembly verification framework based on Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs). Starting from the JEPA-based world model LeWorldModel, we investigate how the architecture must be adapted to verify the correctness of fine-grained assembly operations. To this end, we formulate assembly verification as a predictive world modeling task operating on before-and-after observations of assembly processes. Experimental results on industrial assembly tasks demonstrate that the proposed approach reliably detects both fine-grained incorrect assembly states and action-state mismatches, achieving an AUROC of 86.9% on a newly introduced assembly verification dataset. These results indicate that JEPA-based predictive representations constitute a promising foundation for robust and data-efficient industrial assembly verification.

</details>


### [319] [ReDiffDet: Rotation-equivariant Diffusion Model for Oriented Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30531)
*Zeyu Ding,Yong Zhou,Jiaqi Zhao,Hancheng Zhu,Wen-Liang Du,Rui Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReDiffDet，将旋转目标检测建模为旋转等变去噪扩散过程，用2D高斯分布表示定向框，并设计条件编码器与对齐解码器，显著优于扩散模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型用于定向目标检测存在三个问题：任意旋转目标需编码朝向信息；随机框不可控，难以准确定位密集排列和极端长宽比目标；定向框与图像特征之间存在错位。

Method: 1) 将定向框表示为2D高斯分布作为去噪范式基础，并证明在该表示与模型框架下反向过程具有旋转等变性；2) 设计带条件框的条件编码器，避免框在整幅图像中随机放置；3) 提出对齐解码器，实现定向框与图像特征的对齐。

Result: 大量实验表明 ReDiffDet 取得有前景的性能，并显著优于扩散模型基线。

Conclusion: 通过旋转等变去噪扩散建模、条件编码与特征对齐设计，ReDiffDet 有效缓解了扩散模型在定向目标检测中的朝向编码、随机框不可控与特征错位问题。

Abstract: The diffusion model has been successfully applied to various detection tasks. However, it still faces several challenges when used for oriented object detection: objects that are arbitrarily rotated require the diffusion model to encode their orientation information; uncontrollable random boxes inaccurately locate objects with dense arrangements and extreme aspect ratios; oriented boxes result in the misalignment between them and image features. To overcome these limitations, we propose ReDiffDet, a framework that formulates oriented object detection as a rotation-equivariant denoising diffusion process. First, we represent an oriented box as a 2D Gaussian distribution, forming the basis of the denoising paradigm. The reverse process can be proven to be rotation-equivariant within this representation and model framework. Second, we design a conditional encoder with conditional boxes to prevent boxes from being randomly placed across the entire image. Third, we propose an aligned decoder for alignment between oriented boxes and image features. The extensive experiments demonstrate ReDiffDet achieves promising performance and significantly outperforms the diffusion model baseline.

</details>


### [320] [BEND: Spectral Bregman Losses for Multi-Image Unlearning in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30532)
*Mohamed-Hicham Leghettas,Markus Püschel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEND 首次在扩散模型的遗忘任务中搜索 Bregman 散度族，通过双层优化拟合谱域 Bregman 散度，并采用向保留集均值重定向的策略，在 Stable Diffusion 上单模型一次性遗忘 48 张被记忆图像，遗忘更彻底且生成质量更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型会记忆训练图像并在提示下近乎逐字复现，带来版权与隐私风险。数据遗忘需同时满足"彻底忘记目标"与"保持其余生成质量"的双向要求。已有方法仅使用平方 L2 扩散损失（至多加上人工设定频域掩码），而理论上所有 Bregman 散度均为合法选择，平方 L2 只是最简单的特例，这一族损失空间未被探索。

Method: 提出 BEND：在 Bregman 散度族上通过双层循环（bilevel loop）搜索，将谱域 Bregman 散度拟合到遗忘任务，使损失函数本身被"塑形"以兼顾彻底遗忘与质量保护；同时将记忆图像的重定向目标设为其保留图像的均值（而非 SOTA 方法 ReTrack 所采用的重定向到单张近似副本）。

Result: 在 Stable Diffusion 上，单个 BEND 模型一次性遗忘全部 48 张经验证被记忆的基准图像；遗忘程度不逊于逐目标、串行及联合训练的 ReTrack（SSCD 0.084 vs. 0.094–0.120），同时达到遗忘前模型的生成质量（CLIP-IQA 0.81 vs. 0.64–0.69）。在 CelebA-HQ 上三项指标均优于 SOTA，在 MNIST 与 CIFAR-10 上持平或超越。

Conclusion: BEND 证明损失函数本身的搜索（Bregman 散度族）是扩散模型数据遗忘的有效维度，配合均值重定向策略可实现更强的遗忘-质量权衡，并在多数据集与多基准上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Diffusion models memorize training images and reproduce them almost verbatim when prompted, creating copyright and privacy liabilities. Data unlearning erases the influence of specific training samples from a trained model without retraining, and is two-sided: it must forget the targets while preserving generation quality on everything else. Prior methods use the squared-L2 diffusion loss, at most with a hand-set frequency mask; however, it is known that every Bregman divergence is a mathematically valid choice and the squared-L2 is only the simplest example. BEND is the first to search this family: it fits a spectral Bregman divergence to the unlearning task through a bilevel loop, so the loss itself is shaped to forget thoroughly while protecting quality. It also redirects a memorized image to the mean of its retain images, where ReTrack, the state of the art, redirects it to a single near-copy. On Stable Diffusion, a single BEND model erases all 48 verifiably memorized benchmark images at once. It forgets at least as much as per-target, sequential, and jointly trained ReTrack (SSCD 0.084 versus 0.094-0.120), at the quality of the model before unlearning (CLIP-IQA 0.81 versus 0.64-0.69). BEND also outperforms the state of the art on CelebA-HQ in all three measures of the benchmark, and matches or exceeds it on MNIST and CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [321] [Where Compositionality Hides: Shared-Content Interference in Vision-Language Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30538)
*Chimata Anudeep,Harsh Vardhan Poddar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mean-erasure通过将冻结图像嵌入投影去除两张候选caption均值方向，证明其保持图内caption排序并使Group Score等于基线Text Score；在Winoground上将CLIP ViT-B/32的Group Score从9%提升至31%，五个CLIP/SigLIP检查点提升21–27个百分点。分数分解显示约60%的Image失败仅源于跨图偏移而非caption判断错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对组合基准（如Winoground）的Group失败可能源于caption偏好错误，也可能仅源于跨图比较，而Group Score将两者混在一起，无法区分"重训编码器"与"更改造型评分规则"两条路线。

Method: 提出mean-erasure：将每张冻结图像嵌入对两张归一化候选caption的均值做正交投影剔除；理论上证明该投影保持每张图内部的caption相对排序，并使Group Score等于基线Text Score。辅以分数分解诊断、固定修正与学习式双线性打分器对比，以及基于检索caption估计方向的变体。

Result: Winoground上CLIP ViT-B/32的Group Score由9%升至31%，五个CLIP/SigLIP检查点提升21–27个百分点；ColorSwap颜色-物体绑定上有类似增益。分解表明约60%的Image失败中正确联合分配已高于交换版本，仅因两图偏移而失败。固定修正与学习式双线性打分器增益不足1个百分点；检索得到的方向在2个caption时提升约5点，16个时降至基线以下。

Conclusion: 在为成对检索重训编码器之前，应先测量mean-erasure可回收的Group损失；超出基线Text Score的增益必须来自改变caption判断本身，而非仅修正跨图偏移。

Abstract: Paired compositional benchmarks such as Winoground ask each image to prefer its matching caption and each caption to prefer its matching image. A failed Group decision can come from a wrong caption preference or from the cross-image comparison alone, and Group Score merges the two. Choosing between retraining the encoder and changing the scoring rule therefore requires separating them. We study mean-erasure, which projects each frozen image embedding away from the mean of the two normalized candidate captions. We prove that the projection preserves every within-image caption ranking and makes Group Score equal baseline Text Score. On Winoground, mean-erasure raises CLIP ViT-B/32 Group Score from 9% to 31%, and five CLIP and SigLIP checkpoints gain 21 to 27 percentage points. ColorSwap shows a similar gain for color-object binding. A score decomposition shows that about 60% of Image failures already score the correct joint assignment above the swapped one and fail only on an offset between the two images. Fixed corrections and a learned bilinear scorer gain under one point. A direction estimated from retrieved captions gains about five points with two captions and falls below baseline with sixteen. Before retraining an encoder for paired retrieval, measure the Group loss that mean-erasure recovers: gains beyond baseline Text Score require changing the caption decisions themselves.

</details>


### [322] [SVUBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Proactive Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30541)
*Yuxuan Liang,Bohan Zhang,Haiyang Yu,Bocheng Hu,Ke Niu,Xu Li,Zhengtao Yao,Yan Wen,Haotian Chen,Xiaolei Chen,Jiacong Wang,Zhe Liu,Jingqun Tang,Benlei Cui,Fan Shi,Xiangyang Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SVUBench，一个包含12,131个问题、62,881个答案点、覆盖感知/理解/推理三大类共十项主动任务的全面的主动流式视频理解基准，并提出Proactive Response Score (PRS) 综合评估答案正确性、响应及时性、历史一致性与内容质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动流式视频理解要求模型实时解读正在发生的事件、及时响应并检索相关历史证据，但缺乏系统、全面的评估手段。

Method: 构建SVUBench：模型辅助标注+人工校验；设计两种互补交互模式——Basic（从视频起始进行持续主动响应）与Trajectory（流式过程中任务激活后的历史回溯与持续响应更新）；引入Duration-Scaled Challenge，在四个视频时长层级上评估；提出带任务自适应答案窗口的Proactive Response Score (PRS)。

Result: 通过大量实验与分析，揭示了现有模型在主动流式视频理解上的优势与局限，并指明改进方向。

Conclusion: SVUBench为主动流式视频理解提供了系统化评估框架，PRS指标可多维度衡量模型能力，为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: Proactive streaming video understanding requires models to interpret unfolding events, respond timely, and retrieve relevant historical evidence. To systematically evaluate these capabilities, we introduce SVUBench, a comprehensive proactive streaming video benchmark comprising 12,131 questions and 62,881 answer points across ten proactive tasks spanning Perception, Understanding, and Reasoning. Constructed through model-assisted annotation and human verification, SVUBench introduces two complementary interaction modes. Basic evaluates sustained proactive responses from video onset, while Trajectory requires historical recovery and continuous response updates following task activation during streaming. A Duration-Scaled Challenge further evaluates these capabilities across four video duration tiers. We develop the Proactive Response Score (PRS) with task-adaptive answer windows to jointly assess answer correctness, response timeliness, historical consistency, and content quality. Extensive experiments and analysis reveal the strengths and limitations of existing models, highlighting directions for advancing proactive streaming video understanding.

</details>


### [323] [JONES-19: A Cultural Image Dataset Based on The Grammar of Ornament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30558)
*Linh Pham,Stuart Shieber,David Alvarez-Melis,Alexandros Haridis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布 JONES-19 图像数据集（基于 Owen Jones《装饰法则》的 1,901 件装饰图案，涵盖 19 种文化），并给出分类基线基准与错分模式分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个面向计算机视觉与艺术/设计交叉研究的装饰图案数据集，以填补"小样本、人造设计类图案、细粒度"图像资源与基准的空白，并支撑视觉识别、数据高效学习、艺术史、建筑风格与文化遗�产等研究。

Method: 以《装饰法则》（伦敦，1856）开放获取档案为来源，整理并标注 1,901 幅装饰设计图像，形成 19 类文化标签的数据集；随后训练并评估基线分类器，通过分析错分错误的模式揭示类间关系（尤其是文化相似性）。

Result: 数据集具备四类挑战性特征：样本量小、图像为人造装饰性图案而非自然场景或语境中的常见物体、类别分布不均衡、类别区分依赖线条纹样/浮雕/色彩等细节。基线的分类结果验证了该数据集用于训练分类器的可行性，并显示错分集中体现文化间的相似性关联。

Conclusion: JONES-19 可作为多领域通用基准，尤适用于小样本与细粒度识别、艺术史与文化遗产研究；错分模式分析可作为一种揭示文化/风格间相似度的有效手段。

Abstract: We introduce JONES-19, a high-quality image dataset documenting 1,901 ornament designs belonging to nineteen human cultures. The images and their annotations are based on an open access archive of The Grammar of Ornament (London, 1856), by Owen Jones. The dataset poses numerous challenges as a benchmark for computer vision classification tasks and for research at the intersection of machine learning and art and design: a small sample size, image samples of human-designed artifacts rather than common objects in-context or natural scenes, imbalanced class distribution, and image distinctions based on fine details involving line patterns, reliefs, and colors. As a design-inspired dataset, JONES-19 can serve as a benchmark for various research fields, such as visual recognition, data-efficient learning, art-historical research, architectural style analysis, and cultural heritage. This paper describes the curation of the JONES-19 dataset and reports a baseline classification benchmark that evaluates the suitability of the dataset for training classifiers and exposes insights into inter-class relationships--particularly cultural similarities--by examining patterns in misclassification errors. The dataset and its accompanying documentation are available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/harvardseas-cultural-ornaments/JONES-19.

</details>


### [324] [Seeing Is Locating: Spatial Awareness and Geographic Bias in Decision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30564)
*Wilson Wongso,Hada Melino Muhammad,Xiachong Lin,Hao Xue,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoDecisions 是首个评估决策模型（输出标签概率分布）空间感知与地理偏差的多模态基准，含地理感知图像分类与位置评分两项任务；发现空间感知依赖视觉、坐标文本不足，且决策模型存在显著地理偏差，并提出免训练的位置先验融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型被越来越广泛地用作分类器和评估器，处理与现实地点相关的输入，但其能否利用空间上下文、预测是否存在地理偏差尚不明确，缺乏系统评测手段。

Method: 构建多模态基准 GeoDecisions，包含两项任务：(1) 地理感知图像分类；(2) 针对客观与主观主题的位置评分。将 6 个决策模型与监督式任务专用模型对比，并提出一种免训练的"决策模型+位置先验"融合方法。

Result: 空间感知需依赖视觉：仅提供文本坐标不足以对日常照片和卫星图像分类；存在显著地理偏差——日常照片上开放权重决策模型准确率更高但错误在空间上更聚集；全部 5 个主观主题上，所有决策模型都给社会经济条件较低的地点更低评分。

Conclusion: 决策模型在空间上下文利用上存在局限且带有地理偏差；所提免训练位置先验融合可在不显著加剧性能偏差的前提下提升空间感知能力。

Abstract: Decision models, which output probability distributions over candidate labels instead of generating text, are increasingly used as classifiers and evaluators for inputs associated with real-world locations. However, it remains unclear whether they can leverage spatial context and whether their predictions are geographically biased. We introduce GeoDecisions, a multimodal benchmark for evaluating the spatial awareness and geographic bias of decision models. GeoDecisions comprises two tasks: (1) geo-aware image classification, and (2) location rating on objective and subjective topics. Evaluating six decision models against supervised task-specific models, we find that spatial awareness requires vision: textual coordinates alone provide insufficient context for classifying everyday photos and satellite images. We also find substantial geographic bias. On everyday photos, despite achieving higher accuracy, open-weight decision models exhibit more spatially clustered errors than supervised models. Moreover, across all five subjective topics, every decision model gives lower ratings to locations with lower socioeconomic conditions. Finally, we propose a training-free method that combines decision models with a location prior to improve their spatial awareness without substantially increasing performance bias. Our code is available at https://github.com/cruiseresearchgroup/GeoDecisions.

</details>


### [325] [Reliable Recursive Memory for Causal Multi-scale Video Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30568)
*Tong Wang,Wenbo Peng,Yaolei Qi,Yuting He,Yutong Xie,Guanyu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STREAM 是面向视频息肉分割（VPS）的流式框架，将因果多尺度聚合（CMA）从固定片段扩展到在线流，通过递归槽记忆（RSM）以恒定代价提供长程历史，并用可靠性感知槽写入（RASW）抑制退化区域写入记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VPS 中息肉与黏膜相似且尺度快速变化，需要超出短片段的长程历史；同时模糊、镜面高光与遮挡往往只损坏帧的局部。现有方法以整帧保存历史，代价随历史长度增长，且局部退化内容会被传播到后续预测。

Method: 在会议工作 CMA 基础上提出 STREAM 流式框架：(1) Recursive Slot Memory（RSM）将多尺度特征及软前景/背景线索压缩为恒定数量的槽，当前特征读取这些槽并送入 CMA，实现恒定代价的长程历史建模；(2) Reliability-Aware Slot Writing（RASW）由当前帧估计像素级质量图，以局部预测误差作为监督信号训练，并用其控制哪些位置写入 RSM 及每个槽的更新幅度。

Result: 在 SUN-SEG 与 CVC-ClinicDB 上达到 SOTA；在最困难子集上 Dice 由会议模型的 81.3% 提升至 85.7%，运行速度 48 FPS，状态占用恒定为 0.32 MB。

Conclusion: 以“槽式递归记忆 + 可靠性加权写入”替代整帧历史，可在恒定内存与实时速度下获得长程时序信息并缓解局部退化传播，验证了流式记忆设计对 VPS 的有效性。

Abstract: Video polyp segmentation (VPS) is essential for computer-aided colonoscopy, as it localizes and tracks polyps frame by frame. However, VPS remains challenging because polyps resemble the mucosa and change quickly in scale, which calls for history beyond a short clip, and because blur, specular highlights, and occlusion often degrade only part of a frame. Existing methods keep history as whole frames, so their cost grows with the history length, and a partly degraded frame carries its degraded content to later predictions. To address these issues, we propose STREAM, a streaming framework for VPS that extends the causal multi-scale aggregation (CMA) of our conference work from a fixed clip to an online stream. STREAM introduces a Recursive Slot Memory (RSM) that summarizes multi-scale features and soft foreground and background cues into a constant number of slots, which the current features read and pass to CMA, supplying long-range history at a constant cost. Furthermore, we design Reliability-Aware Slot Writing (RASW), which estimates a pixel-level quality map from the current frame, supervised by its local error, and uses it to control which locations are written into RSM and how much each slot is updated. Experiments on SUN-SEG and CVC-ClinicDB demonstrate that STREAM achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving the Dice of the conference model from 81.3% to 85.7% on the hardest subset while running at 48 FPS with a constant state of 0.32 MB. Code will be released at https://github.com/wangtong627/STREAM.

</details>


### [326] [Write, Evolve, Read: Streaming Cameras into a Persistent World State for 4D Occupancy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30573)
*ZiAn Wang,Tianyi Wang,Zhihao Chen,Manni Cui,Junkai Chen,ZhiJin Chen,Ruiqi Liu,Junfei Wu,Huanyu Zhang,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PersistWorld将已观测空间显式作为持久化3D世界状态，通过流式写入而非逐帧重建，在Occ3D-nuScenes上将相机方法1–3 s的IoU提升19点、8 s内提升18–24点，且速度提升逾2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D占据预测中，车辆短时运动导致大部分未来空间已被观测。作者将未来空间划分为不变、变化与未观测三类，发现现有基于相机的世界模型的主要短板在"不变空间"——无法稳定保持已观测内容，而非预测变化本身。

Method: 提出以"已见即状态"为核心的PersistWorld：将相机视频流式写入一个锚定于场景的持久化3D世界状态，每帧只写入一次，避免从历史帧反复重建；该状态与预测任务端到端联合学习，并由一个evolver模块沿自车轨迹在任意查询时刻预测该状态的演化。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes上，1–3 s的IoU较最佳相机世界模型提升19点，最长至8 s提升18–24点，同时推理速度提高两倍以上。

Conclusion: 显式维护持久化场景状态是提升4D占据预测一致性与效率的有效范式，未来工作可围绕变化的动态区域建模与更长时序泛化展开。

Abstract: 4D occupancy forecasting predicts the 3D semantic occupancy of a driving scene seconds ahead. Over a few seconds, the scene changes little while the vehicle moves, so most of the space ahead has already been seen. Sorting the future into unchanged, changed, and unseen space, we find that current camera-based world models fall short on the unchanged part: they fail to hold on to what they have already seen, rather than to forecast what will change. We therefore make what the model has seen its state. PersistWorld streams camera frames into a persistent 3D world state anchored in the scene, writing each frame once instead of rebuilding the state from past frames. The state is learned end to end with the forecast, and an evolver predicts it at any queried time along the ego trajectory. On Occ3D-nuScenes, PersistWorld raises the IoU of the best camera-based world model by 19 points at 1-3 s and by 18 to 24 points up to 8 s, while running more than twice as fast. Code and models: https://github.com/wangzian828/PersistWorld. Project page: https://wangzian828.github.io/PersistWorld

</details>


### [327] [A Search Worth a Billion Pixels: Explaining Gigapixel Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30578)
*Danilo Danese,Angela Lombardi,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WaFR（小波粗找到精化），一种模型无关的归因方法，融合自适应层次搜索与稀疏多尺度回归，在全分辨率至8192像素及吉像素病理图像上实现高精度、低开销的扰动归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扰动的归因方法无需访问模型内部表示，但扩展至高分辨率图像时面临扰动粒度与计算成本的权衡：细粒度扰动需大量前向评估，而粗掩码易遗漏小而关键的证据。作者希望使成本取决于证据的空间分布而非图像分辨率。

Method: WaFR为模型无关方法，将归因建模为稀疏多尺度回归，并以小波式层次分解进行自适应的“查找—细化”搜索：仅对影响预测的区域逐级细化，从而在保持细粒度定位的同时控制前向次数。

Result: 在受控定位实验中，边长增至8,192像素时Pointing Game分数仍≥0.995，且在同等前向预算下成立；在吉像素全切片图像上中位仅需176次前向，平均patch级AUROC达0.949，为所评估扰动类方法中最高。

Conclusion: WaFR以与证据空间分布相关的计算成本，解决了扰动归因在高分辨率下的粒度—成本矛盾，兼具细粒度定位与高计算效率，适用于吉像素级医学影像等超大规模场景。

Abstract: Perturbation-based attribution methods explain model predictions without accessing their internal representations, but scaling them to high-resolution images requires balancing perturbation granularity and computational cost. Fine-grained perturbations require many model evaluations, whereas coarse masks may overlook small but relevant evidence. We introduce Wavelet Find-and-Refine (WaFR), a model-agnostic attribution method combining adaptive hierarchical search with sparse multiscale regression. By selectively refining regions that influence the prediction, WaFR maintains fine-grained localisation with a computational cost that depends on the spatial distribution of the evidence. As image resolution increases to 8,192 pixels per side, WaFR maintains a Pointing Game score of at least .995 in controlled localisation experiments, including at matched forward-pass budgets. On gigapixel whole-slide images, it requires a median of 176 forward passes and achieves the highest mean patch-level AUROC among the evaluated perturbation-based methods (.949).

</details>


### [328] [Foundation Model and Radiomics Distance Scores for Post-Hoc Segmentation Failure Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30582)
*Simon Johannes Joham,Gianluca Guglielmo,Mateusz Kozinski,Martin Urschler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两种基于马氏距离的后验失败检测分数：掩码形状影像组学特征与冻结 DINOv3 基础模型特征（掩码感知池化），在无需配准与模型内部访问的前提下，前列腺与海马 MRI 上与时序/集成基线竞争；并指出全局合并排序会偏向"按站点难度排序"，主张同时报告站点内评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像分割模型在临床数据分布偏移时会静默失败，部署时缺乏质量控制且模型不透明。已有后验失败检测方法仍依赖额外的回归/分割模型、配准或模型内部信息（如集成预测），这些依赖本身也可能静默失效。

Method: 提出两种失败分数，均以到有标签参考集的马氏距离为基础：(1) 预测掩码的影像组学形状特征；(2) 冻结基础模型特征——将掩码叠加到图像上使其"掩码感知"，用冻结的 DINOv3 编码，并对 patch 特征加权池化（掩码区域权重更高）。在多中心前列腺与海马 MRI 上，测试成像协议偏移与人群偏移两种场景。

Result: 在每个站点内部按预测失败风险排序时，两种分数均与基于配准（Atlas-RCA）和基于集成（pairwise Dice）的基线具有竞争力，且无需可能静默失败的配准、也无需访问个体预测。同时发现：将所有测试病例合并为一个全局排序（既有工作做法）会奖励"按站点难度排序"，检测器即使遗漏站点内失败也能表现良好。

Conclusion: 站点内评估衡量真实的逐病例失败，全局排序与站点内排序奖励不同行为，建议两种评估方式同时报告。代码已开源。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation models can fail silently when clinical data differs from training data. Deployed models rarely ship with quality control, and are often black boxes. Prior methods that work in this post-hoc failure detection setting still need a separate regression or segmentation model, registration, or access to model internals. We instead propose two failure scores based on the Mahalanobis distance to a labeled reference set. The first uses radiomic shape features of the predicted mask. The second uses frozen foundation model (FM) features, made mask-aware by overlaying the mask on the image, encoding it with a frozen DINOv3, and pooling patch features with more weight on the mask region. We test on multi-site prostate and hippocampus MRI under imaging-protocol and population shift. Ranking cases by predicted failure risk within each site, both scores are competitive with the registration-based (Atlas-RCA) and ensemble-based (pairwise Dice) baselines, without registration that can fail silently or access to individual predictions. Our results show that pooling all test cases into one ranking, as prior work does, rewards ordering sites by difficulty, so a detector can rank sites well, miss failures within them, and still look strong. Within-site evaluation measures true per-case failure. The two evaluation schemes reward different behavior, so we recommend reporting both. The code is available at https://github.com/imigraz/fm-radiomics-failure-detection.

</details>


### [329] [DARE: Dual-Level Adversarial Learning with Domain-Aware Regularization for Whole Slide Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30589)
*Yuxuan Jiang,Haichuan Dong,Manning Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DARE——一种即插即用的病理全切片图像（WSI）无监督域适应框架，通过教师-学生架构下的自适应伪标签、补丁级与切片级双层对抗对齐，以及域感知注意力掩码正则化，缓解跨医院域偏移带来的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理WSI因扫描设备、染色协议与组织制备差异存在显著域偏移，跨源数据应用时模型性能大幅下降。UDA在自然图像中表现良好，但在计算病理中研究不足；WSI的超高分辨率及切片间/切片内异质性使现有UDA方法不稳定、易发生负迁移。

Method: 提出DARE框架：1）在教师-学生架构中引入自适应伪标签，为目标域提供稳定监督；2）对补丁级与切片级嵌入进行对抗对齐，实现双层语义表示的联合优化；3）引入域感知注意力掩码以正则化注意力、提升跨域泛化能力。

Result: 在四个公开WSI数据集上的大量实验表明，DARE在多种域偏移场景下持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DARE作为即插即用UDA框架，可有效应对WSI的域偏移与异质性挑战，实现稳定的跨域对齐与泛化，为计算病理中的无监督域适应提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Pathological whole slide images (WSIs) exhibit substantial domain shifts across hospitals due to variations in scanning devices, staining protocols, and tissue preparation procedures, leading to significant performance degradation when models are applied to data from different sources. Although unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) has shown strong performance in transferring models from a labeled source domain to an unlabeled target domain in natural images, it remains relatively underexplored in computational pathology. The ultra-high resolution of WSIs and their pronounced inter-slide and intra-slide heterogeneity pose unique challenges for domain adaptation, often rendering existing UDA methods unstable and susceptible to negative transfer. To address these challenges, we propose Dual-level Adversarial learning with domain-aware REgularization (DARE), a plug-and-play UDA framework for WSI classification. DARE incorporates adaptive pseudo-labeling within a teacher-student architecture to provide stable supervision for the target domain, alongside adversarial alignment of both patch-level and slide-level embeddings. Together, these components enable the joint optimization of semantic representations at both levels for cross-domain alignment. We further introduce domain-aware attention masking to regularize attention and improve cross-domain generalization. Extensive experiments on four public WSI datasets demonstrate that DARE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches across various domain shifts.

</details>


### [330] [HOSE: Hierarchical Orthogonal Subspace Erasure for Concept Unlearning in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30592)
*Xiaoqi Wei,Li Li,Kejiang Chen,Hanzhou Wu,Xinpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出层次化正交子空间擦除方法HOSE，通过将参数更新投影到与通用知识正交的子空间，并按U-Net层级构建差异化子空间，实现有害概念擦除与图像质量的更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型可能生成色情、暴力等有害或敏感内容，带来严重安全隐患。模型编辑可在最小参数更新下修改特定概念并保留整体能力，但扩散模型中不同概念表征相互纠缠，直接修改必然破坏非目标概念；同时U-Net的层级异质性使统一策略难以兼顾擦除与质量。

Method: 提出层次化正交子空间擦除（HOSE）：将参数更新投影到与通用知识正交的子空间以消除对非目标概念的干扰；并针对U-Net架构的层级异质性，设计层次自适应机制，为不同层构建差异化子空间投影，精确适配模型层次结构。

Result: 相比未编辑模型，有害内容生成降低80%；虽有一定图像质量下降，但在质量保持上优于其他基于编辑的基线方法，并在有害内容抑制与质量损失的权衡上显著优于基线；对抗鲁棒性高于现有SOTA方法，表明目标概念被更彻底地擦除。

Conclusion: HOSE通过正交子空间投影与层次自适应机制，解决了概念纠缠干扰与U-Net层级异质性导致擦除—质量权衡的问题，在有害内容抑制、图像质量保持及对抗鲁棒性方面均取得更优表现，代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can generate harmful or sensitive content, such as pornographic or violent imagery, raising serious security concerns. Model editing has recently emerged as an efficient approach to mitigate this issue. It modifies pretrained models with minimal parameter updates to alter specific concepts while preserving overall model capabilities. However, due to the entangled representations of different concepts in diffusion models, direct modifications inevitably disrupt non-target concepts. Meanwhile, U-Net's hierarchical heterogeneity hinders unified strategies from balancing erasure and quality. To address these issues, we propose Hierarchical Orthogonal Subspace Erasure (HOSE). This method projects parameter updates into the subspace orthogonal to general knowledge, thereby eliminating interference with non-target concepts. Considering the hierarchical heterogeneity of the U-Net architecture, we further propose a hierarchical adaptive mechanism, constructing differentiated subspace projections for different layers to precisely adapt to the model's hierarchical structure, thereby resolving the inherent trade-off between erasure and image quality in unified constraints. Experimental results demonstrate the proposed method reduces harmful content generation by 80% compared with the unedited model. While it inevitably incurs some image quality degradation, it still outperforms other editing-based baselines in terms of quality preservation, and achieves a significantly better trade-off between harmful content suppression and quality loss than these baselines. Notably, our approach demonstrates higher adversarial robustness than existing SOTA method, indicating that the target concepts have been more thoroughly erased. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sevenqiiu/HOSE.

</details>


### [331] [Advancements in Diabetic Retinopathy Classification Using Novel Multi-Stacked U-Net Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30593)
*Ameya Uppina,S Navaneetha Krishnan,Talluri Krishna Sai Teja,Joe Dhanith P R*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对比UNET及其堆叠(Stacked)变体在多数据集上对糖尿病视网膜病变(DR)五级分类的性能，发现堆叠3层时最佳，更多层会导致性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病患者大规模筛查需要全自动计算机辅助DR诊断；CNN在眼底图像DR分类上有前景，但高质量标注数据有限、图像质量差异大、类别不平衡等问题带来挑战。

Method: 采用UNET及堆叠架构变体，在多个数据集上对眼底图像进行五分类(0=无DR，4=增殖性DR)，比较不同堆叠层数下的性能。

Result: 堆叠数为3时性能最佳；进一步增加堆叠层数反而导致性能下降（软结论）。

Conclusion: UNET堆叠架构在3栈配置下最优，可为DR自动筛查模型设计提供层数选择的参考。

Abstract: Diabetes leads to several health complications throughout the body, with the complication in the eyes being Diabetic Retinopathy. It leads to damaging or abnormal changes in the blood vessels' structure, causing vision loss. The need for massive screening of a large population of diabetic patients has created a necessity for computer-aided, fully automatic diagnosis of DR. CNNs have shown great promise in the classification of DR based on the Fundus images. Building a reliable model faces considerable challenges due to the limited availability of high-quality annotated datasets and issues with varying image quality and class imbalances. This paper aims to demonstrate the UNET, and its Stacked Architecture variant and their performance across several datasets. This paper has compared and studied the UNET architecture's performance across multiple stacks and has come to a soft conclusion that the architecture performs its best at three stacks, and any more leads to deterioration of its performance. The model aims at classifying the images into five categories, 0 to 4, where 0 is no DR, and 4 is proliferative DR.

</details>


### [332] [ViDiHand2: Unifying Video Diffusion Priors and Hand Geometry for 3D Hand Motion Reconstruction in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30597)
*Yuxi Wang,Yufei Liu,Chengkai Jin,Qingyue Chen,Wenqi Ouyang,Tianyi Wei,Kerui Ren,Siyuan Huang,Zhiqi Shen,Hongsheng Li,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViDiHand2将基于视频扩散模型的手部深度估计从直接回归重构为细粒度几何约束求解，在七个数据集上超越18个基线，野外绝对关节误差降低42%、检测失败减少8.82倍，速度57 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从第一人称人类视频学习机器人操作需要精确的度量3D手部运动。现有方法利用预训练视频扩散模型（VDM）先验从全帧视频直接恢复被遮挡的手，但其基于外观的深度回归对未见相机泛化性差。

Method: 提出几何驱动框架ViDiHand2：(1) Fine-Grained Geometric Lifting与Clip-Level Ray Fitting，通过约束像素对齐2D关节与MANO手部度量结构之间的几何一致性并利用片段内时序观测来恢复度量深度；(2) Confidence-Gated Fusion，依据定位置信度自适应融合几何抬升关节与MANO预测，提升遮挡与图像截断下的鲁棒性；(3) 增强VDM主干以更好捕捉时空表征，输出上述步骤依赖的逐帧2D关节与MANO预测。

Result: 在七个公开数据集上一致优于18个基线；相比各指标最佳基线，野外绝对关节误差降低42%，检测失败率降低8.82倍，运行速度57 FPS。

Conclusion: 以几何约束求解替代外观深度回归可显著提升跨相机泛化与遮挡鲁棒性，为从第一人称视频获取度量手部运动提供了高效（57 FPS）且可实用的方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation from egocentric human videos requires accurate metric 3D hand motion. Recent methods leverage pretrained video diffusion model (VDM) priors to recover occluded hands directly from full-frame videos, but their appearance-based depth regression generalizes poorly to unseen cameras. In this work, we propose ViDiHand2, a geometry-driven framework that reformulates VDM-based hand depth estimation from direct regression into fine-grained geometric constraint solving. Specifically, we introduce Fine-Grained Geometric Lifting and Clip-Level Ray Fitting, which recover metric depth by enforcing geometric consistency between pixel-aligned 2D joints and the metric structure of MANO hands, while leveraging temporal observations across the clip. Since the lifting relies on these 2D joints, to improve robustness under occlusion and image truncation, we further develop Confidence-Gated Fusion to adaptively combine geometrically lifted joints with MANO predictions according to localization confidence. Finally, we enhance the VDM backbone to better capture spatial-temporal representations and produce the per-frame 2D joint and MANO predictions that both steps rely on. Extensive experiments on seven public datasets demonstrate that ViDiHand2 consistently outperforms 18 baselines, reducing in-the-wild absolute joint error by 42% and detection failures by 8.82x compared with the best baseline on each metric, while running at 57 FPS. The code is available at https://github.com/NTUYWANG103/ViDiHand.

</details>


### [333] [GSDet: Gaussian Splatting for Oriented Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30602)
*Zeyu Ding,Jiaqi Zhao,Yong Zhou,Wen-liang Du,Hancheng Zhu,Rui Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GSDet 将定向目标检测表述为高斯泼溅，在三维特征空间中用3D高斯表示定向目标，投影为2D高斯后转换为定向框，避免了对预定义候选（锚框/查询）的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CNN与Transformer定向检测器依赖预定义目标候选（CNN的锚框、Transformer的查询），难以有效捕捉空间信息。

Method: 从图像特征构建3D特征空间，用3D高斯表示定向目标；将3D高斯投影至图像平面得到2D高斯并转换为定向框；通过含3D高斯采样的解码器优化随机初始化高斯的均值、各向异性协方差与置信度；推理时支持自适应控制与动态高斯数量。

Result: 在3个数据集上，采用自适应控制时AP₅₀较基线提升：DIOR-R +0.7%、DOTA-v1.0 +0.3%、DOTA-v1.5 +0.55%，优于主流检测器。

Conclusion: 以高斯泼溅替代预定义候选的建模范式在定向目标检测中有效，且具备推理阶段灵活控制高斯数量的能力。

Abstract: Oriented object detection has advanced with the development of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and transformers. However, modern detectors still rely on predefined object candidates, such as anchors in CNN-based methods or queries in transformer-based methods, which struggle to capture spatial information effectively. To address the limitations, we propose GSDet, a novel framework that formulates oriented object detection as Gaussian splatting. Specifically, our approach performs detection within a 3D feature space constructed from image features, where 3D Gaussians are employed to represent oriented objects. These 3D Gaussians are projected onto the image plane to form 2D Gaussians, which are then transformed into oriented boxes. Furthermore, we optimize the mean, anisotropic covariance, and confidence scores of these randomly initialized 3D Gaussians, using a decoder that incorporates 3D Gaussian sampling. Moreover, our method exhibits flexibility, enabling adaptive control and a dynamic number of Gaussians during inference. Experiments on 3 datasets indicate that GSDet achieves AP$_{50}$ gains of 0.7\% on DIOR-R, 0.3\% on DOTA-v1.0, and 0.55\% on DOTA-v1.5 when evaluated with adaptive control and outperforms mainstream detectors.

</details>


### [334] [Towards an Armenian Epigraphic Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30603)
*Chahan Vidal-Gor{è}ne,Arpine Avetisyan,Edita Matevosyan,Seda Kirakosyan,Victoria Khurshudyan,Pollet Samvelian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了亚美尼亚铭文电子语料库（CIE），将十卷《Divan hay vimagrut'yan》（近7600条记录）转化为开放数字语料，采用版面检测、OCR与视觉语言模型生成初步结构化释读，并由专家在校对平台复核，为亚美尼亚铭文研究与古典/中古亚美尼亚语语言模型提供"白银数据"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚美尼亚铭文语料《Divan hay vimagrut'yan》（1960-2017）收录近7600条记录，其中不少铭文近数十年已受损或被毁，仅存于该印刷卷册中；而亚美尼亚铭文研究缺乏希腊、拉丁、格鲁吉亚等语言已有的数字资源。

Method: 将十卷Divan数字化：先用版面检测、OCR与视觉语言模型（VLM）对每条记录生成初步结构化释读，再由专家在校对平台（proofreading platform）上核验校正。

Result: 首批校正显示字母识别可靠，残余错误集中在编辑符号（editorial signs）上。

Conclusion: 该语料库定位为记录亚美尼亚铭文的长期平台，并可作为古典亚美尼亚语与中古亚美尼亚语的"白银数据"来源，服务于语言模型与计算语言学研究。

Abstract: The Divan hay vimagrut'yan (1960-2017), the printed corpus of Armenian inscriptions, comprises nearly 7,600 records; a growing number of the inscriptions they describe, damaged or destroyed in recent decades, survive only in its pages. Armenian epigraphy nevertheless lacks a digital resource of the kind available for Greek, Latin or Georgian. This paper presents the Corpus Inscriptionum Armenicarum Electronicum, which turns the ten volumes of the Divan into an open digital corpus. Layout detection, OCR and a vision-language model produce a first structured reading of each record, which specialists then check on a proofreading platform. First corrections show that letters are read reliably and that residual errors concentrate in the editorial signs. The corpus is designed as a lasting platform for documenting Armenian inscriptions and as a source of silver data in Classical and Middle Armenian for language models and computational linguistics.

</details>


### [335] [Tri-DNO: Trilateral Diffusion Noise Optimization for 3D Human Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30607)
*Paul Hinzer,Niklas Gard,Anna Hilsmann,Peter Eisert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tri-DNO 是一种扩散噪声优化策略，通过迭代优化预训练多模态HOI扩散模型的初始噪声来提升手-物体接触质量，无需重训模型即可生成更真实的三维人际物体交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成逼真的3D人-物交互（HOI）在AR/VR与角色动画中至关重要，而手部抓握的几何一致性（接触不足或互相穿插）是核心难点；已有扩散模型虽在HOI合成上有进展，但难以保证物理合理的手-物交互。

Method: 提出Tri-DNO，一种扩散噪声优化策略：不修改也不重训生成模型，而是针对预训练的多模态HOI扩散模型的初始噪声进行迭代优化，以采样出紧密的手-物接触，同时抑制穿插并保持手部位置的合理性。

Result: 在GRAB数据集上的实验表明，相比基线在抓握质量上有提升，且无需额外模型训练，计算开销与现有方法相比未增加；并进一步验证了该方法在实际媒体制作场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: 通过优化扩散初始噪声即可有效改善手-物接触的物理合理性，是一种无需重训、低成本且具备实用价值的HOI生成增强方案。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D human object interactions (HOI) is essential for applications, such as augmented or virtual reality (AR/VR) or character animation of interactive entertainment. A particular challenge lies in the faithful generation of hand grasps in human object interaction scenes, where even minor geometric inconsistencies, such as insufficient contact or interpenetration, can significantly compromise realism. While diffusion-based generative models have shown promising results in HOI synthesis, ensuring physically plausible hand-object interactions remains challenging.
  We present Tri-DNO, a diffusion noise optimization strategy to sample from a pretrained multimodal HOI diffusion model, improving hand-object contact. Rather than modifying or retraining the generative model, our approach iteratively optimizes the initial noise of the diffusion generator, thereby generating firm hand object contact, while mitigating penetration and keeping the hand position realistic. Experiments on the GRAB dataset demonstrate improvements over the baseline in grasp quality without additional model training or increased computational cost compared to existing methods. We further demonstrate the applicability of our approach to practical media production scenarios.

</details>


### [336] [CineAct: Interaction-State Agents for Playable, Branching Film Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30613)
*Junyan Lin,Jianqi Ma,Shujie Duan,Hanxin Zhu,Zhihao Hu,Jinming Liu,Shuai Li,Jun Zhang,Xin Jin,Chang Wen Chen,Weidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出状态优先的智能体框架CineAct，用语言模型智能体做推理、冻结的图像/视频生成器仅作渲染器，实现目标锚定、可选择且跨片段持续一致的交互式视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放视频生成器单次仅能生成数秒至十几秒短片；延长序列的方法要么依赖遵循预设脚本的智能体系统，要么从上一片段末帧自回归续写，导致离屏状态丢失并逐轮累积幻觉。用户无法提出显式目标、自主选择实现路径，并让该选择在后续片段中保持。

Method: 研究"目标锚定交互式视频生成"任务（一个目标对应多种可行路径，用户所选路径需在画面中执行且后果跨片段延续）。CineAct：交互前由Architect将世界初始化为含相机的空间布局、资产库与场景图；每轮推理计划的状态变化，用图像生成器产出目标末帧并严格校验，再由FL2V模型合成中间运动。另构建多空间基准，含长程步骤执行与开放意图分支可容许性两套评测。

Result: 显式世界状态建模相比纯像素基线，多步rollout一致性提升+11.4（自动指标）/ +23.1（人工评分）分；面向同一目标的成对分支成功率达+30.8分。

Conclusion: 将推理与渲染解耦、以显式世界状态驱动的状态优先范式，可有效维持长时程一致性与分支可控性，但结论基于作者自建基准，泛化性有待验证。

Abstract: Open video generators such as MiniMax-H3 produce short clips of only several to a dozen seconds per run. To synthesize longer sequences, existing methods either rely on agentic systems following preset scripts, or roll out autoregressively from the previous clip's last frame, losing off-screen states and accumulating hallucinations across turns. Consequently, users still cannot take an explicit goal, choose how to accomplish it, and see that choice persist across subsequent clips. We study goal-grounded interactive video generation, where one goal admits multiple valid approaches, the user's chosen approach must be executed on screen, and its consequences must persist across clips. To this end, we propose CineAct, a state-first agentic framework in which language-model agents handle reasoning while frozen image and video generators serve purely as renderers. Before interaction, the Architect initializes the world as a spatial layout with cameras, an asset library, and a scene graph; at each turn, the system deduces the planned state change, generates a target end frame with an image generator and rigorously verifies it, and synthesizes intermediate motion with an FL2V model. We further introduce a multi-space benchmark covering two suites: long-horizon step execution and open-intent branching admissibility. Experiments show that explicit world-state modeling boosts multi-step rollout consistency over the pixel-only baseline (+11.4 automated, +23.1 human points) and raises paired branching success toward a shared goal by +30.8 points.

</details>


### [337] [Auxiliary Clues Aware's Geometry Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30617)
*Qigong Lei,Qingsong Wang,Wang Lin,Qianyi Yang,Chang Yao,Jingyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AuxCA强化学习框架，通过辅助线索依赖度（ACD）加权优势与辅助激励奖励，使多模态大模型在不生成显式图形的情况下学习可迁移的辅助线构造策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何题求解是检验MLLMs复杂推理能力的严格测试，尤其是需要输入图中不存在的辅助构造时。显式作图方法受图像生成质量不稳定与级联误差困扰；现有隐式注意力机制主要关注可见元素，难以支撑需要额外几何构造的推理。

Method: 提出AuxCA（Auxiliary-Clues-Aware GRPO）：1）定义辅助线索依赖度ACD，量化辅助视觉线索对token生成的影响，并通过细粒度优势加权融入GRPO；2）引入辅助激励奖励，鼓励在正确解中有效利用辅助几何线索；推理阶段无需显式生成图形。

Result: AuxCA-8B在依赖辅助线的平面几何问题上优于显式作图基线Bagel-Canvas，并与领先闭源模型表现相当；在解析几何、立体几何及外部基准上的提升表明所学策略可跨训练域迁移。

Conclusion: 以隐式、奖励驱动的方式注入辅助构造线索，可在避免作图误差的同时获得可迁移的几何推理能力；开源代码已提供。

Abstract: Geometry Problem Solving (GPS) serves as a rigorous test of the complex reasoning capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), particularly when solutions require auxiliary constructions absent from the input diagram. However, explicit diagramming methods suffer from unstable image generation quality and cascading errors, while existing implicit attention mechanisms primarily focus on visible elements, limiting their ability to support reasoning that requires additional geometric constructions. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{AuxCA} (Auxiliary-Clues-Aware GRPO), a reinforcement learning framework that helps models learn transferable auxiliary-construction strategies that can be applied beyond the training domain without explicit diagram generation at inference time. Specifically, we introduce Auxiliary Clues Dependency (\textbf{ACD}) to quantify the influence of auxiliary visual clues on token generation and integrate it into GRPO through fine-grained advantage weighting. Furthermore, we incorporate an auxiliary incentive reward to encourage the effective use of auxiliary geometric clues in correct solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AuxCA-8B outperforms the explicit-plotting baseline Bagel-Canvas and achieves competitive performance with leading proprietary models on auxiliary-dependent plane geometry problems. Improvements on analytic geometry, solid geometry, and external benchmarks further suggest that the learned strategies can transfer beyond the training domain. Code is available at https://github.com/fukua23/AuxCA

</details>


### [338] [Motion on Demand: Adaptive Optical Excitation for Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30641)
*Yingtao Li,Chaoran Feng,Haoran Jiang,Zhenyu Tang,Wangbo Yu,Zhankuo Xu,Jianbin Zhao,Li Yuan,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvGaze是一种事件相机系统，通过仿生多模态学习控制器自适应调节棱镜旋转速度，以最优事件代价显露出图像边缘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机仅响应亮度变化，边缘可见性依赖垂直于边缘的图像运动，而自然运动随相机与场景动态不断变化；固定转速的旋转楔形棱镜无法适应变化条件——当自然运动已充足时会生成冗余事件并引入不必要的对齐误差。

Method: 受果蝇平衡棒前馈与视觉反馈分工的启发，设计学习式多模态控制器，融合亮度、事件和惯性观测，其中快速惯性通路在两次视觉更新之间细化速度决策；并利用实测编码器角度在每个事件时间戳上补偿棱镜诱导的图像运动，以保持变速过程中的几何一致性。

Result: 在EvMotion上，相比全速旋转恢复更多边缘且事件数减少39%；在DSEC（模拟光学调制）上达到0.73的特征跟踪成功率（原始事件0.55，180 RPM时为0.67），同时比360 RPM事件少26%；真机实验验证了在线闭环自适应，平均事件率相对全速旋转降低17%。

Conclusion: 自适应棱镜转速调控可在保持或提升边缘/特征跟踪性能的同时显著降低事件冗余，仿生前馈-反馈控制架构在事件相机主动视觉中有效且可实机部署。

Abstract: Event cameras respond only to brightness changes, so whether an edge is visible depends on image motion perpendicular to it---a motion that constantly shifts with camera and scene dynamics. Rotating a wedge prism in front of the lens introduces controlled image motion to reveal static or slow edges. However, a fixed rotation speed cannot adapt to changing motion conditions: when natural motion is already sufficient, additional optical excitation generates redundant events and introduces unnecessary alignment errors. Inspired by the division of labor between fast haltere feedforward and slower visual feedback in fly gaze control, we present EvGaze, an event camera system that adaptively regulates prism rotation speed. EvGaze employs a learned multimodal controller that integrates luminance, event, and inertial observations, with a fast inertial pathway that refines speed decisions between visual updates. To preserve geometric consistency during speed changes, we compensate for prism-induced image motion at individual event timestamps using measured encoder angles. On EvMotion, EvGaze recovers more edges than full-speed prism rotation with 39% fewer events. On DSEC with simulated optical modulation, it reaches 0.73 feature-tracking success (compared with 0.55 on native events and 0.67 at 180 RPM) while generating 26% fewer events than 360 RPM. Real-device experiments further demonstrate online closed-loop adaptation, reducing average event rates by 17% relative to full-speed rotation.

</details>


### [339] [Beyond Naming Accuracy: Template Composition and Species-Name Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30642)
*Junle li,Bochang zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在相同模板数量下，模型"更稳定"的结论会随具体模板措辞的不同而发生反转，说明视觉-语言分类器的稳定性比较高度依赖提示词的具体选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前发现 BioCLIP2 在四个模板上比 BioCLIP 2.5 Huge 更换预测更少，但在80个模板的大池中排名反转。该比较无法判断反转源于模板数量还是模板措辞。作者希望固定模板数量，检验措辞本身能否决定排名。

Method: 将模板数固定为4个，在80模板库和24个经审核的摄影模板池中枚举所有四模板子集，保持图像与候选物种名不变，比较两个分类器的预测一致性；随后在5,487帧未使用过的 iWildCam 序列上，对4,356个"两照片+两摄影"子集构建同时95%置信区间，检验排名是否稳健。

Result: 整体上 Huge 在经审核的模板池中更一致；但在 iWildCam 上，19.09%的四模板子集支持 BioCLIP2 更一致，Rare Species 上为5.10%。在新帧上，1,526个子集支持 Huge 不一致度更低，43个支持 BioCLIP2；即便模板数量与名词构成相同，排名仍出现分化。

Conclusion: 分类器的稳定性排名并非由模板数量决定，而是由具体句子措辞决定；单一或少量模板下的比较结论不可靠，需报告模板子集层面的分布而非单点结论。

Abstract: Vision-language classifiers identify species by matching images to text prompts containing species names. BioCLIP2 changes its predicted species less often than BioCLIP 2.5 Huge across four photographic templates, but the ranking reverses across a bank of 80 templates. This comparison leaves open whether the reversal depends on the number of templates or their wording. We hold the count at four and compare the classifiers on every four-template subset of the 80-template bank and of a second pool of 24 reviewed photographic templates, keeping images and candidate names fixed. Huge is more consistent across the reviewed pool as a whole, yet 19.09% of its four-template subsets favor Bio-CLIP2 on iWildCam and 5.10% do so on Rare Species. We then test whether both rankings hold on 5,487 frames from previously unused iWildCam sequences. Among 4,356 subsets containing two photo and two photograph templates, simultaneous 95% intervals support lower disagreement for Huge in 1,526 subsets and for BioCLIP2 in 43. Even with the same prompt count and noun mixture, the choice of sentences can determine which classifier appears more consistent.

</details>


### [340] [Part-wise Certified State Estimation of Articulated Objects from Passive Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30646)
*Orion Desbonnet,Kei Higuchi,Alex Sykes,Ayush Deshmukh,Victor Leve,Joao Moura,Sethu Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从被动双目图像中对铰接物体（如折叠纸盒）进行部件级状态估计与认证的方法，在不使用深度、掩码和接近真值的初始估计的前提下，达到与最佳模型拟合方法相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造场景中机器人常操作CAD模型中已知但在任务中会改变形状的薄壁铰接物体，需要监测其铰接状态、位姿及半变形部件形状。学习型估计器泛化好但处理薄部件精度低；模型驱动拟合精度高却依赖准确深度、接近真值的初始化与干净掩码。

Method: 在双目图像中检测CAD模型定义的角点并拟合铰接模型；将原本针对刚性物体的几何认证扩展到铰接物体，对每个部件给出是否可信的判定，从而处理拟合对隐藏部件也会输出状态的问题；用未标注视频上的认证估计作为标签，提升仿真到现实的迁移。

Result: 在7个仿真物体中的5个上，认证部件的定位精度比同输入（含深度和真值掩码）的强学习基线高3–5倍；真实纸盒上端板中位角点误差为纸盒尺寸的2.1%，比所有学习基线至少低40%，也低于以接近真值状态初始化的深度拟合。

Conclusion: 认证式的部件级双目拟合可在无深度、无掩码、无良好初始化的条件下实现高精度铰接状态估计，并可自监督生成标签改善sim-to-real迁移。

Abstract: Robots in manufacturing often handle thin, hinged objects known from CAD models that change shape during the task, like folding cartons. Monitoring such tasks requires estimating the object's articulation state, its pose and the shape of any semi-deformable part. Learning-based estimators generalise across objects but lose precision while handling thin parts. Model-based fitting is precise but often needs accurate depth, a close initial estimate and a clean object mask.
