We present part-wise certification of an articulated object's state, estimated from passive stereo. Our estimator rivals the precision of the best model-based fitting method without using depth, a close initial estimate or a mask. We detect the corners that the CAD model defines in both stereo images and fit the articulated model to them. Geometric certificates, which we extend from rigid to articulated objects, then tell whether to trust each part, because the fit returns a state even for hidden parts. Certified estimates on unlabelled video become labels that improve sim-to-real transfer. On five of seven simulated objects, we locate the parts we certify three to five times more precisely than the strongest learned baseline on the same readings, given depth and true masks. On a real folding carton, our median corner error on the end panels is 2.1% of the carton's size, at least 40% lower than every learned baseline and also lower than a depth-based fit initialised near the true state.

</details>


### [341] [Zhengrong: World Models with Pole-Based Action-Response Factorization for Direct Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30666)
*Yu Zhang,Jingrui Li,Congyu Wang,Yuhang Cheng,Wenbo Chen,Xiaobo Zhang,Jing Zou,Yuxi Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Zhengrong-PoleWM，一种将潜轨迹分解为初始条件锚点、自由响应与动作创新延迟响应的直接视觉世界模型，在四个图像目标控制基准上达到90.83%宏平均成功率，优于最佳基线6.08个百分点，且推理延迟低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接视觉世界模型无需递归潜空间展开即可预测候选动作序列的效果，但未明确刻画动作效应的时间结构（如时间演化、初始状态锚定、因果性等）。

Method: 将潜轨迹分解为初始条件锚点、自由响应和针对动作创新的延迟响应；用非线性网络把视觉历史与因果动作前缀映射为响应系数；由共享的稳定共轭极点库决定各响应随时间的演化；并推导出精确重锚定恒等式，识别表示平移轨迹尾部所需的额外基方向Ψ_κ(s)。

Result: 在Cube、TwoRoom、PushT、Reacher四个离线图像目标控制基准上，两训练种子、第20轮宏平均成功率90.83%±0.47%，超最强基线QQWorld 6.08个百分点；按验证损失选检查点达91.21%±1.00%；首个epoch的Cube对照实验中，神经与极点响应头分别比通用直接预测器高20.67和23.34个百分点；A100上30次迭代CEM搜索中位延迟74–87 ms，均值77.5 ms，较Fast-LeWM降低15.8%延迟，较自回归LeWM加速7.1倍。

Conclusion: 该极点分解式世界模型在保持动作间非线性交互、精确锚定初始状态、保证因果性与有界响应的同时，兼顾高成功率与低推理延迟，优于现有直接预测与自回归方法。

Abstract: Direct visual world models predict the effects of candidate action sequences without recursive latent rollout, yet they leave the temporal structure of action effects unspecified. We introduce Zhengrong (Zhengrong-PoleWM), a world model that decomposes a latent trajectory into an initial-condition anchor, a free response, and delayed responses to action innovations. Nonlinear networks map visual history and causal action prefixes to response coefficients. A shared bank of stable conjugate poles determines how each response evolves with elapsed time. This factorization preserves nonlinear interactions across actions, anchors predictions exactly at the initial state, prevents future actions from affecting earlier predictions, and yields bounded responses to finite control plans. We derive an exact re-anchoring identity that identifies the additional basis direction $Ψ_κ(s)$ needed to represent shifted trajectory tails. Across four offline image-goal control benchmarks (Cube, TwoRoom, PushT, and Reacher), Zhengrong-PoleWM achieves $90.83\% \pm 0.47\%$ macro-average success over two training seeds at epoch 20, exceeding the strongest baseline (QQWorld) by 6.08 percentage points. Selecting checkpoints by validation loss yields $91.21\% \pm 1.00\%$. In a controlled first-epoch Cube study without restart regularization, neural and pole response heads outperform a generic direct predictor by 20.67 and 23.34 percentage points, respectively. On an NVIDIA A100 GPU, median latency for a full 30-iteration cross-entropy method search ranges from 74 to 87 ms across the four tasks. The mean of these medians is 77.5 ms, a 15.8% latency reduction relative to Fast-LeWM and a $7.1\times$ speedup over autoregressive LeWM.

</details>


### [342] [DriveDreamer-RSI: Recursive Self-Improvement of Driving Perception and Agentic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30671)
*Dingyi Yao,Tingdong Yu,Guosheng Zhao,Chaojun Ni,Xiaofeng Wang,Jiming Mao,Guan Huang,Lihui Peng,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DriveDreamer-RSI 框架，通过感知学习与数据构建之间的反馈实现驾驶感知的递归自我提升（recursive self-improvement）与智能体化数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控生成可扩增驾驶感知训练数据，但仅追求视觉真实感并不能保证学习增益；有效生成需随感知模型演化，同时适配“生成什么数据”和“如何构建数据”。

Method: 双层循环框架：外环为 Gap-Guided Target Adaptation，依据当前感知缺陷生成带标注视频并在训练后更新目标；内环用视觉-语言智能体通过观测反馈与执行反馈迭代编辑场景；跨轮次由 Learning-Feedback-Driven Strategy Refinement 从执行经验与下游学习结果中提炼可复用的数据构建策略。

Result: 在 nuScenes 验证集上，仅用约一半合成帧，即较数据集回放基线提升 4.09 mAP 与 4.13 NDS。

Conclusion: 以感知反馈闭环驱动数据生成与策略进化，可在更低数据开销下显著提升驾驶感知性能，验证了“面向学习增益的生成”范式的有效性。

Abstract: Controllable generation can expand training data for driving perception, but visual realism alone does not ensure learning gains. Effective generation must adapt both what data to generate and how to construct them as the perception model evolves. In this paper, we present DriveDreamer-RSI, a framework for recursive self-improvement of driving perception and agentic data generation through feedback between perception learning and data construction. Its outer loop implements Gap-Guided Target Adaptation, generating labeled videos from current perception gaps and updating targets after training. Its inner loop uses a vision-language agent to iteratively edit scenes through observation and execution feedback. Across rounds, Learning-Feedback-Driven Strategy Refinement derives reusable construction advice from execution experience and downstream learning outcomes. On the nuScenes validation set, DriveDreamer-RSI surpasses the dataset-replay baseline by 4.09 mAP and 4.13 NDS points using approximately half the synthetic frames.

</details>


### [343] [Optimising VLM Harnesses to Localise Errors in Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30673)
*Ankit Yadav,Nikhil Cherian Kurian,Arpit Garg,Lingqiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Harness Search and Merge (HSM)，通过搜索并合并可执行的 VLM 评估程序（harness）来自动构造视频错误定位流程，在 Spotlight 与 IPV-Bench 上优于现有策略并具备跨生成器泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成视频中细微错误的定位依赖 VLM 的使用流程（选帧、工具调用、预测融合），不同 harness 差异显著；人工设计 harness 成本高且次优，需自动构建。

Method: 将 harness 搜索形式化为在可执行评估程序空间上的搜索；由 LLM 编排器在随机训练子集上独立优化候选 harness，将发现记录于 memory，并以构造性方式合并互补候选。

Result: Spotlight 上 F1 0.234±0.012，优于最强基线 0.173±0.016；在未见生成器上 0.213±0.008 对比 0.140±0.003；IPV-Bench 上定位性能强且虚警率低于现有优化基线。

Conclusion: 对完整 VLM harness 进行搜索可超越既有评估策略，并能迁移到未见视频生成器、降低真实视频虚警。

Abstract: Identifying subtle errors in generated videos is an important step toward automated video generation, where errors must be detected to assess and refine generated clips. Recent work shows that pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) can localise such errors, but their effectiveness depends strongly on the workflow used: choices about which frames to inspect, which tools and model calls to make, and how to combine predictions can lead to substantially different results. We study VLM harnesses for video error localisation. Instead of relying on manually designed harnesses, we propose Harness Search and Merge (HSM), an algorithm that automatically constructs them by searching over executable evaluation programs. An LLM orchestrator refines candidate harnesses independently on random training subsets, records findings in a memory, and constructively merges complementary candidates. We evaluate HSM on Spotlight, a recently proposed benchmark for localising errors in generated videos. HSM achieves 0.234 +- 0.012 F1, exceeding the strongest benchmark strategy (0.173 +- 0.016), and retains its lead on an unseen video generator (0.213 +- 0.008 versus 0.140 +- 0.003). On IPV-Bench, it achieves strong localisation performance with lower false-alarm rates than existing optimisation baselines. Together, these results show that searching over complete VLM harnesses can outperform existing evaluation strategies, transfer to an unseen video generator, and reduce false alarms on real videos.

</details>


### [344] [Speculate in the Right Order: Efficient Self-Speculative Decoding for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30704)
*Yihan Chen,Jintao Zhang,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Speculation-Aware Ordering（SAO），首个将"生成顺序"作为自推测解码（self-speculative decoding）效率优化新维度的免训练框架，通过离线构建推测友好的生成顺序，提升自回归图像生成的解码效率与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自推测解码研究主要聚焦于改进起草（drafting）与验证（verification）策略，而随机顺序自回归建模引入了"生成顺序"这一额外自由度，其与推测解码效率的关系尚未被研究。作者希望厘清生成顺序如何影响效率并加以利用。

Method: 理论上分析生成顺序对自推测解码效率的影响，识别两个关键因素：决定token被拒绝概率的draft-target mismatch，以及反映推测轮次中越早拒绝成本越高的early-rejection cost。据此提出SAO：免训练、离线优化生成顺序的框架，用简单的成对条件TV（total variation）统计构建"拒绝成本感知"的目标函数，高效构造推测友好的生成顺序，而无需通过昂贵的推测解码来评估候选顺序。

Result: 在不同骨干网络与推测宽度下的大量实验表明，SAO提升了解码效率，并额外带来生成质量的提升；与现有自推测解码方法结合时可提供互补的效率增益。

Conclusion: 生成顺序是自回归图像生成中高效自推测解码的一个新优化维度；SAO以理论驱动的离线顺序优化实现效率与质量双收益，且与既有方法正交兼容。

Abstract: Self-speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive image generation by leveraging the same model for parallel drafting and sequential verification, without requiring an additional draft model. While existing methods primarily focus on improving drafting and verification strategies, recent advances in random-order autoregressive modeling introduce an additional degree of freedom: the generation order. In this work, we theoretically analyze how generation order affects self-speculative decoding efficiency, identifying two key factors: draft-target mismatch, which determines token rejection probabilities, and early-rejection cost, which reflects the higher cost of rejecting tokens earlier in a speculative round. Motivated by these insights, we propose Speculation-Aware Ordering (SAO), a training-free framework for offline optimization of generation orders. Instead of evaluating candidate orders through costly speculative decoding, SAO uses simple pairwise conditional-TV statistics to formulate a rejection-cost-aware objective and efficiently construct speculation-friendly generation orders offline. Extensive experiments across different backbones and speculative widths demonstrate that SAO improves decoding efficiency, with the additional benefit of improved generation quality. Furthermore, SAO provides complementary efficiency gains when combined with existing self-speculative decoding methods. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to identify generation order as a new optimization dimension for efficient self-speculative decoding in autoregressive image generation.

</details>


### [345] [Intra-Class Diversity Loss for Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30707)
*Wei Zhou,BaXian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出类内多样性损失ICD，在类权重正交方向上最大化类内差异，作为正则项提升行人重识别的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人ReID中训练与测试身份不重叠，且类内变化大，需要可泛化表征。现有ReID损失主要关注类内紧凑与类间分离，忽视了有价值的类内差异信息。

Method: 基于表征与对应类权重向量的几何关系，将每个表征分解为沿类权重方向的分量及其正交分量，以此构建可解释的几何基础；ICD在该正交方向上鼓励同一身份样本间差异更大，同时由分类损失维持身份判别性。该损失无需修改特征提取网络，可作为附加正则项嵌入现有ReID框架。

Result: 在三个常用ReID数据集上的大量实验验证了ICD的有效性，并证明其与现有度量学习损失具有互补性。

Conclusion: ICD首次将类权重正交的多样性学习目标引入行人ReID，通过补充类内差异建模提升表征泛化能力，且具良好兼容性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Person re-identification (ReID) aims to retrieve images of the same pedestrian across different cameras. However, the non-overlapping identities between training and testing sets, together with large intra-class variation, make it challenging to learn generalizable pedestrian representations. Most existing ReID loss functions mainly focus on intra-class compactness and inter-class separation, which may overlook useful intra-class differences. To address these issues, we propose a novel Intra-Class Diversity (ICD) loss that aims to maximize intra-class differences in classification-irrelevant directions. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to introduce this class-weight-orthogonal diversity learning objective for person ReID. Specifically, based on the geometric relationship between the learned representation and its corresponding class-weight vector, we decompose each representation into the component along the class-weight direction and its orthogonal component. This decomposition provides a clear and interpretable geometric basis for modeling intra-class diversity. ICD then encour- ages larger differences among the orthogonal components of samples belonging to the same identity, while the classification loss maintains identity discrimination. The proposed ICD loss can be incorporated into existing ReID frameworks as an additional regularization term without modifying the feature extraction network. Extensive experiments on three widely used ReID datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of ICD and its complementarity with existing metric learning losses.

</details>


### [346] [Co-speech Gesture Attribute Annotation with 3D Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30725)
*Frances Yung,Muhammad Naufilr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两种基于人体网格恢复(HMR)的自动共语手势属性标注方法——几何规则法与SL-GCN图卷积网络，在GESRes数据集2020个手势上，物理定义类属性达到接近人际一致性的水平，高层多模态语境属性仍较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共语手势的物理属性标注需人工逐帧处理视频，成本高、耗时长，亟需自动化手段以支撑大规模手势研究。

Method: 以SAM 3D Body从单帧视频估计完整三维人体骨架，提取姿态用于标注十种手势属性：(1) AI辅助的几何规则方法；(2) 图卷积网络SL-GCN。两者在GESRes数据集的2,020个手势上与专家标注对比评估。

Result: 在高度、手臂旋转幅度、运动速度等物理定义明确的属性上，两种方法与专家标注的一致性接近人类标注者之间的一致性；而对于需高层多模态语境推理的属性，性能明显下降。规则法所需标注数据显著更少，完全可解释，且可适配不同标注体系。

Conclusion: 规则法可作为数据驱动方法实用且可解释的补充；HMR驱动的自动标注在物理属性上已具备替代人工的潜力，而语境依赖型属性仍是未来挑战。

Abstract: Co-speech gestures convey meaning alongside speech, but annotating their physical attributes in video is labour-intensive. We propose two approaches to automate annotation of ten gesture attributes using human mesh recovery (HMR), which estimates a full 3D body skeleton from a single video frame: AI-assisted geometrical rules and a graph convolutional network (SL-GCN). Both use pose estimates from SAM 3D Body, evaluated on 2,020 gestures from the GESRes dataset. For physically defined attributes such as height, arm swivel and movement speed, both methods achieve agreement with expert annotation comparable to that between human annotators. For attributes whose annotation requires higher-level multimodal contextual inference, performance remains lower. The rule-based approach requires substantially less annotated data, is fully transparent, and can be adapted to different annotation schemes, offering a practical and interpretable complement to data-driven methods

</details>


### [347] [Same Seed, Different Picture? Deciding Early at Low Resolution for Seed-Faithful Few-Step Flow Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30728)
*Huiran Duan,Weilun Feng,Yuqi Li,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无训练采样器 Decide-then-Render (DtR)：用低分辨率少步确定构图，再用种子自身噪声重建全分辨率状态并渲染细节，在 4 个大模型上提速 11–25× 且比所有对比方法更接近 50 步参考采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步采样器虽快，但从同一噪声出发已无法复现标准 50 步采样的图像，主体、属性与布局都会改变，而非仅细节差异，即"种子保真度"丧失。作者将其归因于采样初期：在噪声水平仍高于 0.9 时，初始噪声的粗粒度分量率先决定粗结构，而少步采样器在最初几步就跨越该区间，这段过程占其与 50 步参考距离的 25–52%。

Method: 关键观察是该决策是粗粒度的，故用同一粗噪声在半分辨率下运行几乎可做出相同决策，成本仅约三分之一。据此 DtR 分两阶段：先在低分辨率下用少数步确定构图（decide），再用种子自身的噪声重建全分辨率隐状态并渲染细节（render）；整体无需训练。

Result: 在 4 个大模型上，DtR 比参考采样器快 11–25×；在各加速比下均比所有对比方法（含按预算校准的少步调度、已发表的多分辨率采样器）更接近参考且 ImageReward 评分更高。在 FLUX.1-dev 上，直至约 8× 加速仍是最保真的采样器，优于缓存类加速器；用户研究中 86% 的成对比较认为 DtR 样本更接近参考。方法可迁移到留出模型与视频，蒸馏模型可作为其渲染器。

Conclusion: 少步采样的保真度损失主要源于采样起始阶段对粗结构的过早、粗粒度决策，而非细节层面；将"构图决策"与"细节渲染"解耦、并在低分辨率下廉价完成决策，是一种无需训练即可显著提速且高度保真的通用采样策略。

Abstract: Few-step samplers make large text-to-image flow models fast, but from the same noise they no longer generate the image of the standard 50-step sampler. Subjects, attributes and layout change, not just fine detail. We trace this loss of seed fidelity to the start of sampling. The coarse part of the initial noise decides coarse structure first, while the noise level is still above 0.9. Few-step samplers cross this range in their first steps, which account for 25-52% of their distance to the 50-step reference. Because this decision is coarse, a half-resolution run from the same coarse noise can make nearly the same decision at a third of the cost. We therefore propose Decide-then-Render (DtR), a training-free sampler that decides the composition in a few low-resolution steps, rebuilds the full-resolution state with the seed's own noise and renders the details. On 4 large models, DtR runs 11-25$\times$ faster than the reference sampler. At each speed-up, it is closer to the reference and rated higher by ImageReward than every compared sampler, including few-step schedules calibrated per budget and published multi-resolution samplers. On FLUX.1-dev, it remains the most faithful sampler down to about $8\times$, ahead of caching accelerators. A user study on this model found DtR's samples closer to the reference in 86% of pairwise trials. DtR transfers to a held-out model and to video, and distilled models can serve as its renderers. Project link: https://duanhuiran.github.io/DtR

</details>


### [348] [EgoDepth-Bench: Benchmarking Metric Depth for Egocentric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30734)
*Yuan Gao,Dongchen Wei,Xinyang Lin,Shuhua Cao,Xueliang Guo,Zhuohan Li,Yihao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoDepth-Bench：含941,233个同步样本、覆盖37个物理环境的第一人称操作深度基准，融合六路标定RGB与原生鱼眼视角的米制深度，实测毫米级几何精度，并提供环境留出划分以评估泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称操作场景缺乏兼具大规规模、米制真值与泛化评估的深度数据集，现有基准难以严格评测深度精度与覆盖度（尤其在手与物体区域）。

Method: 构建941,233个同步样本，覆盖37个独立物理环境；每个样本含六路标定RGB视图与由五个主动红外立体模块重建的原生鱼眼视角米制深度；用标定球杆验证几何精度（已知球间距中位误差1.27 mm）；设置环境留出划分，并在单目、双目、多视图模型上评测深度精度与预测覆盖率。

Result: 在未见环境上，微调MapAnything后米制AbsRel由0.708降至0.091，尺度对齐AbsRel由0.327降至0.072。

Conclusion: EgoDepth-Bench可提供有效监督，并支持对第一人称操作中米制与相对深度估计的严格评测；项目页提供评测代码与交互式RGB-深度序列。

Abstract: We introduce EgoDepth-Bench, comprising 941,233 synchronized samples collected across 37 distinct physical environments. Each sample combines six calibrated RGB views with metric depth in the native fisheye view, reconstructed from five active-infrared stereo modules. A calibrated ball-bar confirms millimeter-level geometric accuracy, with a median error of 1.27 mm on a known sphere spacing. Environment-held-out splits enable evaluation of generalization to unseen environments. Across monocular, stereo, and multi-view models, the benchmark measures both depth accuracy and prediction coverage, including on hands and objects. Fine-tuning MapAnything on EgoDepth-Bench reduces metric AbsRel from 0.708 to 0.091 and scale-aligned AbsRel from 0.327 to 0.072 on unseen environments. These results demonstrate that EgoDepth-Bench provides effective supervision and enables rigorous evaluation of metric and relative depth estimation in egocentric manipulation. The project page provides the evaluation code and interactive RGB-depth sequences.

</details>


### [349] [Learning Dense Head-Object Contact Estimation with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30751)
*Daniel Sungho Jung,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HECO框架，利用合成数据实现稠密头部-物体接触估计，借助文本语义上下文应对多样化交互，并用遮挡感知接触学习（基于amodal inpainting推断可见信息之外的接触区域）解决头发遮挡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头部在认知与感知中至关重要且易受外部冲击，理解头-物接触对人身安全意义重大；但稠密头-物接触估计研究稀少，核心原因是缺乏头-物接触数据。合成数据在有限数据场景下已显示训练潜力，因此有望用于该任务。

Method: 基于合成数据训练HECO。针对两大挑战：(1) 头-物交互高度多样、接触区域与模式变化大——引入文本特征中的语义上下文，编码头部区域与交互物体间的语义关系；(2) 实际接触区域常被头发严重遮挡、难以直接视觉观测——提出遮挡感知接触学习，通过amodal inpainting学习从图像可见信息之外推断头部接触。

Result: HECO经合成数据集训练后可实现头-物接触估计，克服了接触数据缺失的问题；摘要未给出具体定量指标，代码将开源。

Conclusion: 合成数据可替代真实接触标注支撑稠密头-物接触估计；语义文本上下文与遮挡感知amodal学习是应对交互多样性与头发遮挡的有效设计，为人体安全相关应用提供新思路。

Abstract: Head serves a critical role in human cognitive and perceptual functions while remaining vulnerable to external impacts, making the understanding of head contact essential for human safety. Despite its importance, dense head-object contact estimation remains largely unexplored, primarily due to the absence of head-object contact data. Recently, it has been shown that synthetic data can serve as powerful training data in limited data scenario, implying the potential of synthetic data in head-object contact estimation. There are two core challenges for dense head-object contact estimation. First, head-object interactions are highly diverse, resulting in substantial variations in contact regions and patterns across different objects and interaction types. Second, head contact regions are often heavily occluded by hair, making direct visual observation of the actual contact regions difficult. To address these issues, we introduce HEad COntact estimation (HECO) with synthetic data. To handle diverse head-object interactions, we leverage semantic context from text features that encode semantic relationships between head regions and interacting objects. To resolve hair occlusion issue of actual head contact regions, we introduce occlusion-aware contact learning that learns to infer head contact beyond visible information from images via amodal inpainting. As a result, we enable head-object contact estimation with HECO trained with our synthetic dataset, overcoming the issue of absence of contact data. The codes will be released.

</details>


### [350] [Screening for nonrigidity in structure from motion using projected reprojection residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30769)
*Íñigo Cirauqui-Viloria*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于重投影误差空间–时间排列模式的SfM诊断方法，用于在合理重建中筛查是否存在形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏SfM假设场景静止，但形变仍可能得到貌似合理（低重投影误差）的重建；现有重投影误差量级汇总（如均值、中位数）忽略了误差在各点和各视图间的分布结构，因而漏检。

Method: 构造诊断统计量：先从残差中剔除可由小的点调整与相机调整解释的分量，以及随时间变化的简单空间趋势；再度量剩余残差与固定的空间模式和时间模式的对齐程度。方法仅使用单一已估计的SfM重建（相机、三维点、图像观测），给筛查证据而非物理形变估计。

Result: 在四类合成管状场景上，诊断对全部8次预设运动采集均检出，而误差量级汇总仅检出5–6次；每类阈值取其余三类的最高静态得分，故得分最高的静态采集也被判阳性。相同阈值下，在覆盖外观与重建流程变化的20个重建变体中保留19个阳性检出，另1个为数值性弃权（未给出判断）。

Conclusion: 重投影误差在点与视图间的排列方式可揭示量级无法反映的不一致性；在四类场景已知的开发设定下，该方法可仅凭估计相机、三维点与观测标记需进一步复核的"合理"重建，但受场景族已知、变体复用两类场景等限制，结论外推需谨慎。

Abstract: Sparse structure from motion (SfM) assumes a static scene, yet deformation may still yield plausible reconstructions. Reprojection-error summaries discard how errors are arranged across points and views. We propose a diagnostic that first removes residual components explainable by small point and camera adjustments and simple spatial trends that change over time. It then measures alignment with fixed spatial and temporal patterns. The diagnostic uses one estimated SfM reconstruction and provides screening evidence rather than a physical deformation estimate. Across four synthetic tubular families, the diagnostic detected all eight acquisitions with prescribed motion, compared with five or six for summaries of reprojection-error magnitude. Each family's cutoff was the highest static score among the other three families, so the static acquisition with the highest score also screened positive. With the same cutoffs, the diagnostic retained positive detections in 19 of 20 reconstruction variants spanning changes in appearance and reconstruction pipeline, with one numerical abstention. These findings show that the arrangement of reprojection errors across points and views can reveal inconsistencies that their magnitudes alone miss. The four families were known during development, and the transfer variants reused two of them. Within this setting, the diagnostic offers a practical way to flag a plausible reconstruction for closer inspection using only its estimated cameras, 3D points and image observations.

</details>


### [351] [GRAIN: Shared-Budget Complementary Re-observation for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30770)
*Chengxuan Zhou,Shaoyang Zhang,Rui Qian,Juntao Cheng,Feng Liu,Wanru Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAIN 将跨图像的裁剪调用分配与有界输出准入统一为单一优化问题：以边界安全裁剪内部的"分辨率闭合增益"（resolution-closure gain）为跨图像可比的度量，在线性开销下于 CPU 端完成集合级计划，再用 Positive-Region Fusion 对齐推理后的空间准入。在 17 个冻结检测器、3 个数据集上，GRAIN 在 102 个 AP@100 设置中的 100 个优于等调用量的 tiling。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放大裁剪上重跑冻结检测器可在不重训练的情况下恢复小目标与密集目标，但每次裁剪都带来额外检测器调用。现有的 slicing 与自适应裁剪流水线均按单张图像孤立地规划裁剪，而图像集合内部的检测需求差异很大，导致调用预算无法在图像间合理分配；同时缺乏跨图像可比的目标函数来指导分配与最终结果准入。

Method: 1) 以"分辨率闭合增益"作为跨图像可比目标：粗假设带有分辨率赤字，对位于裁剪内部正区域的假设，裁剪按几何比例闭合其赤字；2) 用乘性残差对已覆盖证据进行折扣，构造单调次模替代目标（monotone submodular surrogate）；3) 借助前缀和与全局最大堆，在裁剪推理前于 CPU 上完成集合级计划；4) Positive-Region Fusion 在推理后将空间准入与计划中的内部支撑对齐，随后进行类平衡 top-k 选择。

Result: 在 COCO、CrowdHuman、VisDrone-DET 上，跨五个家族共 17 个冻结检测器，GRAIN 在 102 个 AP@100 设置中的 100 个优于调用量匹配的 tiling，并可迁移至 AI-TOD-v2。RF-DETR-S 在 CrowdHuman 上仅用 1/8 的裁剪调用即接近双裁剪 tiling 的效果。固定预算下，替代目标对候选方案排序的 Spearman 相关系数为 0.95–0.99，而所提扫描策略至少达到精确替代最优的 98%。

Conclusion: 跨图像统一分配裁剪调用并配合有界输出准入，可在不重训练、不增加总调用量的前提下显著提升小目标与密集目标检测性能；轻量次模替代目标在 CPU 上即可高效求得近最优计划，具备良好的精度—调用效率折中与跨数据集迁移能力。

Abstract: Re-running a frozen detector on enlarged image crops recovers small and crowded objects without retraining, yet every crop costs an extra detector call. Existing slicing and adaptive-cropping pipelines plan crops per image in isolation, even though inspection demand varies widely across an image collection. We present GRAIN, which unifies cross-image call allocation and bounded-output admission around cross-image comparable resolution-closure gains over boundary-safe crop interiors. Coarse hypotheses carry resolution deficits, crops close a geometric share of those deficits for hypotheses enclosed in their interior positive region, and multiplicative residuals discount covered evidence within a monotone submodular surrogate. Prefix sums and a global max-heap execute this collection-wide plan on CPU before crop inference, and Positive-Region Fusion aligns post-inference spatial admission with the planned interior support before class-balanced top-
  selection. Across 17 frozen detectors from five families on COCO, CrowdHuman, and VisDrone-DET, GRAIN beats call-matched tiling in 100 of 102 AP@100 settings and transfers to AI-TOD-v2. With RF-DETR-S on CrowdHuman, it nearly matches two-crop tiling using one eighth of the crop calls. At fixed budgets, the surrogate orders candidate plans with Spearman correlations of 0.95 to 0.99 while the sweep attains at least 98% of the exact surrogate optimum. Code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [352] [Reconstructing 3D+t Cardiovascular Flow From Sparse Planar Velocity Fields With Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30775)
*Viraj Patel,Deblina Bhattacharjee,Katharine Fraser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出平面感知神经场框架，从稀疏平面测量重建3D+t心血管流场，并检验物理约束的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每个测量平面仅观测两个面内速度分量且只覆盖小部分体积，从稀疏平面测量重建3D+t心血管血流困难。

Method: 平面感知神经场：以空间与时间坐标为输入学习连续3D速度场，通过逐平面投影与稀疏平面数据一致；比较普通MLP、RBF插值及物理约束SIREN（PC-SIREN），并在主动脉、动脉瘤计算数据及真实4D Flow MRI重采样平面上评估。

Result: 两套计算数据上MLP优于经典RBF；主动脉上PC-SIREN将rRMSE降至0.3756（MLP 0.4273，RBF 0.7256），且在MRI数据各解剖段均最准确；动脉瘤上MLP最佳，rRMSE 0.07–0.16，PC-SIREN为0.55–0.71。进一步考察了扫描配置、扫描数量与空间分辨率。

Conclusion: 连续神经场可由稀疏平面测量重建体内心血管流场，物理约束须与具体流场特性相匹配。

Abstract: Reconstructing time-resolved three-dimensional (3D+t) cardiovascular flow from sparse planar measurements is challenging because each plane observes only the two in-plane velocity components, on a small part of the volume. We propose a plane-aware neural-field framework that learns a continuous 3D velocity field from space and time coordinates through a per-plane projection, and then investigate the potential benefits of physics constraints. We evaluate the framework on computational aortic and aneurysm datasets, and on planes resampled from a real cardiac 4D Flow MRI scan. A plain multi-layer perceptron (MLP) was more accurate than classical radial basis function (RBF) interpolation on both computational datasets. For the aorta, a physics-constrained SIREN (PC-SIREN) reduced the rRMSE further to $0.3756$, compared with $0.4273$ for the plain MLP and $0.7256$ for RBF, and it was the most accurate model in every anatomical segment of the MRI data. For the aneurysms the plain MLP remained the most accurate model, with an rRMSE of $0.07$ to $0.16$ against $0.55$ to $0.71$ for PC-SIREN. Further experiments investigated scanning configuration, number of scans, and spatial resolution. These findings show that continuous neural fields can reconstruct volumetric cardiovascular flow from sparse planar measurements, and that physics constraints have to be matched to the flow.

</details>


### [353] [AvatarAnyone: Agentic Reconstruction of Mesh-Based Layered Avatars from YouTube Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30777)
*Aymen Mir,Peter Wonka,Stefanos Zafeiriou,Rıza Alp Güler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AvatarAnyone提出用AI智能体为每个视频中的个体规划定制化分层网格重建流程，从普通YouTube视频重建出身体+各服装/鞋履独立网格的分层avatar，支持虚拟试衣、布料仿真与物理引擎导入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目avatar多为单一融合曲面或一组高斯，无法分离服装层，难以支持虚拟试衣、布料仿真、接触建模；而野外的YouTube视频在服装类别、款式、层数上差异极大，单一固定的分层重建流水线无法适应所有组合。

Method: 用AI智能体根据输入预测服装类别、服装款式与分层结构，据此为每个人选择初始化方式并对优化过程进行分阶段编排（staged optimization），从而自动生成"身体网格+每件衣物/鞋履独立分层网格"的avatar表示。

Result: 在约150个YouTube视频上验证；在四个数据集上，AvatarAnyone在可动画单目网格重建任务上优于现有基于网格的方法；重建结果可直接导入Blender、Unreal Engine与MuJoCo，并实现替换服装层重渲染、用标准布料模拟器对服装层做布料仿真、以及与其他物体的接触建模。

Conclusion: 以智能体驱动的自适应分层网格表示，突破了固定流水线的泛化瓶颈，产出可编辑、可仿真的分层avatar，拓展了单目avatar在试衣、动画与机器人仿真等下游应用中的可用性。

Abstract: We introduce AvatarAnyone, a method for reconstructing avatars from casual YouTube videos as layered meshes, with each garment and footwear as a separate mesh layered on top of the body mesh. In such videos, people differ in garment classes, clothing types, and layers, and each combination needs a different reconstruction. A single fixed layered reconstruction pipeline therefore fails. AvatarAnyone solves this problem with AI agents that plan a custom optimization for each person's reconstruction. The agents predict the garment classes, the clothing type, and the layering. They use these predictions to choose an initialization and stage the optimization. Because the avatars are layered meshes, they support applications such as virtual try-on and cloth simulation. We change the clothing layers of an avatar and render it in different garments and poses. We can also simulate a clothing layer as cloth over the animated body with a standard cloth simulator. Most existing avatars are one fused surface or a set of Gaussians and cannot do this. We load the avatars directly into Blender, Unreal Engine, and robotics simulators such as MuJoCo. As the avatars are meshes, they support contact modeling with other objects. We show results from around 150 YouTube videos. On four datasets, AvatarAnyone outperforms existing mesh-based methods for animatable monocular reconstruction. Our project page is available at https://miraymen.github.io/avataranyone.

</details>


### [354] [BridgeMVS: Bridging Multi-View Stereo and Monodepth via Bidirectional Dynamic Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30787)
*Jianfei Jiang,Haochen Yu,Qiankun Liu,Rui Yang,Hongyuan Liu,Xianglong Meng,Jiansheng Chen,Huimin Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BridgeMVS 提出一种级联多阶段 MVS 框架，通过双向动态融合（BDF）实现单目深度特征与多视图代价体的互相增强，兼顾度量尺度精度与结构先验鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MVS 依赖显式几何获得度量深度，但在特征匹配不可靠区域（弱纹理、反光、重复纹理）表现差；MDE 提供稳健结构先验但存在尺度歧义。现有混合方法多为单向利用单目线索，未充分挖掘 MDE 与 MVS 的双向互增强潜力。

Method: 提出统一级联多阶段框架，核心为可即插即用的 Bidirectional Dynamic Fusion (BDF) 模块：在每个阶段执行 mono-to-cost 与 cost-to-mono 的动态更新——单目结构特征引导代价体细化以增强 MVS 鲁棒性，多视图几何证据反过来校准单目表示以提升度量尺度一致性；融合表示由粗到细逐级传播，并分别施加阶段级相对尺度 MDE 监督与度量尺度 MVS 监督。

Result: 在 DTU、Tanks-and-Temples、ETH3D 上取得 SOTA，同时提升单目深度估计精度；在 Tanks-and-Temples Intermediate 与 Advanced 榜单均排名第一。

Conclusion: 双向动态融合能有效桥接单目先验与多视图几何，形成互补闭环，且 BDF 可便捷嵌入现有级联 MVS 框架，为稠密三维重建提供通用增强方案。

Abstract: Multi-View Stereo (MVS) and Monocular Depth Estimation (MDE) provide complementary cues for dense 3D reconstruction. While MVS leverages explicit geometry for metric depth, it struggles in challenging regions where feature matching is unreliable. Conversely, MDE provides robust structural priors but typically yields scale-ambiguous results. Existing hybrid methods mainly exploit monocular cues in a unidirectional manner, leaving the mutual enhancement between MDE and MVS insufficiently explored. To address this limitation, we propose BridgeMVS, a unified cascaded multi-stage MVS framework that bridges monocular depth features and multi-view cost volume representations through Bidirectional Dynamic Fusion (BDF). At each stage, BDF performs dynamic mono-to-cost and cost-to-mono updates, where monocular structural features guide cost volume refinement for more robust MVS depth estimation, while multi-view geometric evidence recalibrates monocular representations to improve their metric-scale consistency. Furthermore, the fused monocular and multi-view representations are progressively propagated from coarse to fine stages and constrained by stage-wise relative-scale MDE supervision and metric-scale MVS supervision, enabling cross-branch interactions to benefit subsequent high-resolution depth estimation. In addition, BDF is designed as a plug-and-play module that can be conveniently integrated into existing cascaded MVS frameworks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BridgeMVS achieves state-of-the-art performance on the DTU, Tanks-and-Temples, and ETH3D benchmarks, while simultaneously boosting the accuracy of monocular depth estimation. Notably, BridgeMVS ranks first on both the Tanks-and-Temples Intermediate and Advanced benchmarks. The source code is available at \href{https://github.com/JianfeiJ/BridgeMVS}{https://github.com/JianfeiJ/BridgeMVS}.

</details>


### [355] [PestWeave: Linking Semantic Modeling and Evidence-Guided Text Modulation through Visual Prototypes for Fine-Grained Pest Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30796)
*Xueheng Li,Yu Wang,Yuxiang Shen,Wen Xue,Rui Li,Jie Zhang,Chengjun Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态害虫识别框架PestWeave，通过视觉原型将内容自适应的视觉组织与文本融合相连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业害虫外观多样，识别困难，促使引入文本互补知识的模态方法；但复杂背景会遮蔽判别性视觉线索，文本描述也可能包含当前图像不支持的信息，从而限制跨模态融合效果。

Method: 视觉通路在RWKV架构上引入语义token分组与三类辅助token（聚类原型token、全局token、寄存器token），将特征相似的token重排至相邻扫描位置以实现内容自适应扫描与上下文建模，并保留空间结构；这些原型同时用于指导文本调制，评估视觉对语义成分的支持程度，调节其相对贡献与整体融合强度。

Result: 在多个害虫识别数据集上的大量实验验证了PestWeave的有效性及其在实际害虫管理中的潜力。

Conclusion: PestWeave通过视觉原型连接内容自适应视觉组织与文本融合，可减少不受支持描述的干扰，是一种有效的害虫识别多模态方案。

Abstract: Agricultural pests threaten crop yield and quality, making accurate pest recognition essential for targeted management and sustainable agriculture. Their diverse appearances complicate recognition and motivate multimodal approaches incorporating complementary species knowledge from text. However, complex backgrounds can obscure discriminative visual cues, while textual descriptions may contain information unsupported by the current image, limiting effective cross-modal fusion. To address these challenges, we propose PestWeave, a multimodal framework connecting content-adaptive visual organization with text fusion through visual prototypes. Its visual pathway augments a Receptance Weighted Key Value (RWKV) architecture with semantic token grouping and three types of auxiliary tokens, enabling content-adaptive scanning and contextual modeling while preserving spatial structure. Feature-similar tokens are reordered into adjacent scanning positions, while cluster prototype, global, and register tokens provide group-level summaries, global context, and additional interaction capacity, respectively. These prototypes also guide text modulation by assessing visual support for semantic components and regulating their relative contributions and overall fusion strength, reducing interference from unsupported descriptions. Extensive experiments on multiple pest recognition datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of PestWeave and its potential for practical pest management.

</details>


### [356] [Go Far, Look Less: Active Multi-Room Reconstruction with Persistent Goals and Selective Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30802)
*Jiawei Jiang,Haonan Zhou,Wendi Hu,Jiabei Hu,Yina Xiang,Kaiyue Zhou,Gaoang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出无需训练的主动多房间三维重建框架 ROAM-GS，通过持久目标与选择性视图耦合，显著提升覆盖率和渲染质量，并以极少视角匹敌密集建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动3D重建方法在多房间场景下严重退化：短视的"下一个最优视图"打分无法为前往远处房间的移动赋予价值，并对附近不可消除残差过度优化；而稠密建图方法需流式处理数千帧才能到达远处房间。

Method: ROAM-GS 为无需训练的主动多房间重建框架，将持久目标与选择性视图耦合：每次决策选择一个目标及其变视野前缀，仅在终点处采集；引入表面重建记忆（Surface Reconstruction Memory），通过投影更新后缺陷与观察方向对位姿打分；持久目标以其剩余价值为移动赋予收益，并在到达后改善停滞时释放。

Result: 无需逐场景调参，ROAM-GS 在多房间重建上达到SOTA，同时保持单房间Replica保真度；HSSD平均覆盖率从74%提升至93%，HM3D从47%提升至96%（PSNR提升11.7 dB）；以173倍更少的视角匹敌90分钟稠密建图。

Conclusion: 通过持久目标与选择性视图的结合，ROAM-GS 有效解决了多房间主动重建中的短视与效率问题，在覆盖率、渲染保真度与采样效率上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Active 3D reconstruction aims to allocate a sparse view budget to recover complete geometry and high-fidelity renderings. However, existing methods degrade sharply in multi-room homes: myopic next-best-view scoring gives transit no credit for distant rooms and over-polishes irreducible nearby residuals, whereas dense mappers reach far rooms only by streaming thousands of frames. We propose ROAM-GS, a training-free active multi-room reconstruction framework that couples persistent goals with selective views: each decision selects a goal and a variable-horizon prefix toward it, capturing only at its endpoint. A Surface Reconstruction Memory scores poses by projecting post-update defects and viewing directions. Persistent goals credit transit with their remaining value and are released once arrivals stop measurably improving them. Without per-scene tuning, ROAM-GS achieves state-of-the-art multi-room reconstruction while preserving single-room Replica fidelity, raising mean coverage from 74% to 93% on HSSD and from 47% to 96% (+11.7 dB PSNR) on HM3D, and matching 90-minute dense mapping using $173\times$ fewer views.

</details>


### [357] [Can Multimodal Fusion MakeSense for Embodied Vision in Degraded Sensing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30836)
*Cheng-Ru Chou,Shih-Yu Lai,Pei-Lin Chiang,Shou-Po Tsai,Jing-Zhe Huang,Fukuharu Tanaka,Hirozumi Yamaguchi,Bing-Yu Chen,Yu-Lun Liu,Hsun-Ping Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MakeSense多模态融合框架，通过私有流保留传感器特有证据、共享bottleneck token融合，并训练惩罚"传感器越多误差越大"的负融合现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉系统依赖互补传感器，但即使接受任意传感器子集的模型也会出现负融合（negative fusion）：增加传感器反而增大预测误差，缺乏对有用/有害传感器组合的显式约束。

Method: 框架由各传感器私有流与共享bottleneck token组成：私有流保留传感器专属证据，bottleneck token进行信息交换。训练时对同一退化观测配对完整视图与子集视图，并以更高误差伴随更多传感器作为惩罚项；在几何允许时，距离读数仅使用测距测量，其余预测利用全部输入；通过轻量adapter与逐数据集训练适配下游任务。

Result: 在五个数据集上的配对测试揭示有益与有害的传感器增补。MM-Fi上减少负融合；轻量adapter下，同一设计改进MMAUD无人机定位、V2X-Real协同3D检测、RELI11D少样本姿态迁移、Humanoid-Everyday指令预测；基于冻结核心输出的世界模型在更长测试时域上比线性基线更准确地预测未来融合状态。

Conclusion: MakeSense可抑制负融合并支持人类活动感知、空中系统、机器人与自动驾驶等具身感知场景，具备跨任务与跨模态的可迁移性。

Abstract: Embodied vision systems rely on complementary sensors to perceive and act as conditions change. Even models that accept arbitrary sensor subsets can exhibit negative fusion: adding a sensor increases prediction error. We propose MakeSense, a multimodal fusion framework that preserves sensor-specific evidence in private streams and combines information through shared bottleneck tokens. Training pairs full and subset views of the same degraded observations, penalizing higher error with more sensors. Where geometry permits, the distance readout uses range measurements alone, while the rest of the prediction draws on all available inputs. Paired tests across five datasets reveal helpful and harmful sensor additions. MakeSense reduces negative fusion on MM-Fi. With lightweight adapters and per-dataset training, the same design improves UAV localization on MMAUD, cooperative 3D detection on V2X-Real, few-shot pose transfer to RELI11D, and command prediction on Humanoid-Everyday, where a world model trained on the frozen core's outputs forecasts future fused states more accurately than a linear baseline at longer tested horizons. MakeSense supports embodied sensing across human activity, aerial systems, robotics, and autonomous driving.

</details>


### [358] [Certified Defense against Adversarial Patches on Traffic Signs via Mask-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30840)
*Rudra Saxena,Vishal Dev Verma,Rohan Sharma,Palani Thanaraj K*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对PatchCleanser在交通标志识别中认证率极低的问题，提出掩码感知训练，将分类器暴露于来自防御自身掩码集的随机掩码对遮挡，并偏向中心采样，在不改变认证流程与保证的前提下将认证准确率从4.8%提升至60.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗贴片对交通标志识别构成实际威胁。PatchCleanser理论上可对任意落入单个掩码内的贴片提供可证明的鲁棒性，但实验显示其与常规训练分类器搭配时，在GTSRB上认证准确率仅1.7%–4.8%，几乎无法认证。根本原因是单个掩码即可遮挡用于区分类别的中央小符号，导致分类器在遮挡条件下失效。

Method: 提出掩码感知训练（mask-aware training）：在训练阶段用从防御自身掩码集中随机抽取的掩码对图像进行遮挡，并以偏向标志中心的采样策略生成遮挡样本，使分类器适应被遮挡输入。防御机制、掩码集与认证流程完全不变，因此理论保证得以保留。

Result: 以ImageNet预训练的ResNet-50为骨干，认证准确率由4.8%提升至60.0%（GTSRB）。在真实逐图像对抗贴片攻击下，防御后的ResNet-50达到85.7%准确率，而使用常规训练ResNet-50的原始PatchCleanser仅为74.6%。

Conclusion: 分类器与认证防御的掩码机制需协同设计；仅通过掩码感知训练即可在不牺牲可证明保证的情况下大幅提升认证与实证鲁棒性，且实现代价低、与现有认证流程兼容。

Abstract: Adversarial patches are a practical threat to traffic-sign recognition: a small sticker on a sign can change a classifier's decision. PatchCleanser is a certified defense that applies two rounds of masking and, for every image it certifies, provably returns the correct label for any patch that fits inside one mask. We show that on traffic signs the defense certifies almost nothing when paired with a normally trained classifier (1.7% to 4.8% certified accuracy on GTSRB), because a single mask can hide the small central symbol that distinguishes sign classes. We propose mask-aware training, which trains the classifier on images occluded by random pairs drawn from the defense's own mask set, with a sampling bias toward masks over the sign's centre. The defense, mask set and certification procedure are unchanged, so the guarantee is preserved. For an ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-50, mask-aware training raises certified accuracy from 4.8% to 60.0%. Under real per-image adversarial patches, the defended ResNet-50 reaches 85.7% accuracy, versus 74.6% for the original PatchCleanser with a normally trained ResNet-50.

</details>


### [359] [DyLoMo-DiT: Dynamic Looped Mixture-of-Experts Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30844)
*Lernie Li,Yuanliu Liu,Pengfei Xiong,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DyLoMo-DiT：以权重共享循环复用中间层加深网络而不增参数，并结合稀疏专家与随噪声级动态变化的循环次数分配计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiTs靠增加参数与计算扩展容量；MoE变体虽固定每token计算量扩大参数容量，但仍需大量参数存储。作者希望在参数与计算预算受限下更高效地利用两者。

Method: 权重共享循环：重复施加中间层以加深网络而不新增参数，释放参数预算用于更多专家或更多独立层；稀疏MoE专家按token分配参数容量；动态计算分配：按噪声级改变循环遍数，低噪声步分配更多遍数。

Result: ImageNet 256×256上，在匹配参数与计算预算下，FID较DiT降低11–24%，较DiT-MoE降低36–56%；文生图方面，缩放版DyLoMo-DiT的GenEval分数高于FLUX.1（0.714 vs 0.673），激活参数不足其十分之一（1.05B vs 12B）。

Conclusion: 权重共享循环加深与稀疏专家容量分配相结合，可更有效地利用参数与计算预算；向低噪声步分配更多循环遍数带来额外增益。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) scale model capacity by increasing both parameter count and computation. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) variants expand parameter capacity while keeping per-token computation roughly fixed, but still require substantial parameter storage. In this paper, we propose Dynamic Looped MoE DiT (DyLoMo-DiT), which reuses parameters rather than adding them: a weight-shared loop repeatedly applies the middle layers, while sparse experts allocate parameter capacity across tokens. This design deepens the network without adding parameters, freeing the parameter budget for other uses, such as more experts or more distinct layers. It also allocates compute dynamically, varying the number of passes across noise levels. Experiments show that DyLoMo-DiT makes more effective use of parameter and compute budgets, with further gains from assigning more passes to low-noise steps. On ImageNet $256\times256$, DyLoMo-DiT reduces FID by 11-24% relative to DiT and 36-56% relative to DiT-MoE at matched parameter and compute budgets. For text-to-image generation, a scaled DyLoMo-DiT achieves a higher GenEval score than FLUX.1 (0.714 vs. 0.673), with less than one tenth of its active parameters (1.05B vs. 12B).

</details>


### [360] [Age and Gender Estimation from Face and Ocular Smartphone Images via Identity Transfer and Ordinal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30850)
*Fernando Alonso-Fernandez,Kevin Hernandez-Diaz,Jose Maria Buades,Josef Bigun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究人脸/眼部身份识别预训练表征迁移到性别与年龄估计的可迁移性，并提出利用年龄组排序的序数损失提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究身份识别（人脸与眼部）预训练特征能否迁移到性别与年龄估计，并弥补眼部区域相比全脸在年龄估计上的性能差距。

Method: 采用三种不同复杂度的CNN，分别以人脸或眼部身份识别预训练，在Adience数据集11,299张正面智能手机图像上，按预设五折与主体不重叠协议评估；比较固定特征+SVM与端到端微调两种方式，并引入利用年龄组有序性的序数损失（ordinal loss）。

Result: 域匹配的身份预训练优于ImageNet与随机初始化；融合双眼区域缩小了与全脸的差距（年龄尤甚）；序数损失在各架构与输入区域上稳定提升1-off精度。最佳性别准确率：全脸88.1%、双眼部区域82.9%；年龄：眼部59.0% exact、93.1% 1-off，全脸57.6%与95.2%。眼部年龄精度为Adience上已报告的最高水平。

Conclusion: 身份识别表征可有效迁移至软生物特征估计，域匹配预训练与序数损失是关键增益来源；眼部区域在年龄估计上接近甚至部分超越全脸水平。

Abstract: We investigate the transferability of identity-recognition representations to gender and age estimation from face and ocular images. Three CNNs of varying complexity, pre-trained for face or ocular identity recognition, are evaluated using fixed features with SVMs and end-to-end adaptation. Experiments use 11,299 frontal smartphone images from Adience, following its predefined five-fold protocol and subject-disjoint validation. Results show that domain-matched identity pre-training outperforms direct ImageNet and scratch initialisation in end-to-end training, while combining both ocular regions narrows the gap with full-face estimation, especially for age. We additionally introduce an ordinal loss that exploits the ordering of age groups and consistently improves 1-off accuracy across architectures and input regions. Across the evaluated configurations, best gender accuracies reach 88.1% for the face and 82.9% for combined ocular regions, while age estimation reaches 59.0% exact and 93.1% 1-off accuracy for ocular images, versus 57.6% and 95.2% for the full face. To our knowledge, these are the highest ocular age accuracies reported on Adience.

</details>


### [361] [CableMoE: Harm-Regularized Mean-Residual Expert Fusion for Transmission-Corridor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30859)
*Shima Salehi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CableMoE：以四个冻结分割专家的可用性感知概率平均为基线，叠加校准残差与harm惩罚的即插即用式（post-hoc）混合，在输电走廊语义分割上取得84.906% mIoU、98.140% OA，较SPT提升10.000点、较概率平均提升0.263点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输电走廊语义制图受益于异质LiDAR与图像引导的预测器，但用学习型路由器直接替换强集成（概率平均）会使罕见电气资产（如绝缘子等）受损，因此需要在不破坏强集成鲁棒性的前提下引入学习式组合。

Method: 提出CableMoE：对四个冻结分割专家做post-hoc混合；可部署预测为围绕“可用性感知概率平均”的校准残差；以harm penalty抑制对均值已正确分类点的额外损失；并做匹配消融（无约束学习混合、harm正则、去除逐点门控）以验证各设计选择。

Result: 消融：无约束学习混合较概率平均损失1.032 mIoU点；harm正则在同容量下恢复0.359点；移除不必要的逐点门控在精度与稳定性上略有提升。三区拟合、两个不相交区选残差强度、router未接触的t5b5报告：84.906% mIoU、98.140% OA（较SPT +10.000，较概率平均 +0.263，三种子均优于后者）；pylon/conductor/structural-cable/insulator四类macro-IoU由SPT 84.517%、概率平均97.153%提升至98.144%。

Conclusion: 结果支持“保守残差组合”这一结论，而非提出新的分割骨干网络或普适SOTA；CableMoE的增益主要来自对强集成的稳健后处理式修正。

Abstract: Semantic mapping of transmission corridors benefits from heterogeneous LiDAR and image-guided predictors, but replacing a strong ensemble with a learned router can damage rare electrical assets. We introduce CableMoE, a post-hoc mixture of four frozen segmentation experts. Its deployable prediction is a calibrated residual around availability-aware probability averaging, while a harm penalty discourages extra loss on points that the mean already classifies correctly. A matched component study shows why each design choice is needed: an unconstrained learned mixture loses 1.032 mIoU points relative to probability mean, harm regularization restores 0.359 points at fixed capacity, and removing an unnecessary point-wise gate slightly improves accuracy and stability. With three zones for fitting, two disjoint zones for residual-strength selection, and router-untouched t5b5 for final reporting, CableMoE obtains 84.906% mIoU and 98.140 % overall accuracy. This is +10.000 points over SPT and +0.263 over probability mean; all three seeds improve the latter. Across pylon, conductor, structural-cable, and insulator classes, macro-IoU rises from 84.517% for SPT and 97.153% for probability mean to 98.144 %. The results support conservative residual composition, not a claim of a new segmentation backbone or universal state of the art

</details>


### [362] [XFreq: Geometry-Aligned Cross-View Residual Learning for Sparse 3D Ultrasound Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30869)
*Yuhuan Lu,P. Ellen Grant,Davood Karimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出XFreq——一个几何对齐的稀疏三维超声重建框架，通过低频解码器预测平滑结构场、高频解码器结合查询到的2D特征预测残差细节，并由学习门控调节残差贡献，在胎儿脑与CETUS心脏数据集上取得最高PSNR/SSIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维超声对解剖评估有价值，但仅有广泛可得的二维切片；稀疏切片导致大部分体素未被观测（即使采集位姿已知）。现有方法或插值观测强度、或引入学习到的2D图像特征，但二者在“预测平滑结构”与“补充残差细节”上的互补作用尚未被充分挖掘。

Method: 利用已知切片位姿与有限脚印(footprint)，在3D位置查询2D图像特征，并与splat强度表示和采样几何互补；低频解码器从splat表示预测平滑3D场，高频解码器额外融合查询到的2D特征预测残差细节；学习到的门控模块依据几何与跨切片特征变化调节残差贡献。在自建胎儿脑数据集与公开CETUS心脏数据集上，用从3D体数据重采样的切片、五折交叉验证评测。

Result: 多数设置下PSNR与SSIM均为最高；CETUS在32张输入切片下达到26.82 dB PSNR。消融实验验证局部特征查询、几何线索与残差预测的作用，且增益随输入切片数增加总体上升。

Conclusion: 几何对齐的跨视角特征整合对从稀疏2D切片重建3D超声体数据具有重要价值。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) ultrasound provides valuable volumetric context for anatomical assessment, motivating reconstruction from widely available two-dimensional (2D) slices. Sparse slices leave much of the volume unobserved, even when their acquisition poses are known. Existing methods interpolate observed intensities or incorporate learned 2D image features into 3D reconstruction, but their complementary roles in predicting smooth structure and residual details remain less explored. We introduce XFreq, a geometry-aligned framework for sparse 3D ultrasound reconstruction. It uses known slice poses and finite footprints to query 2D image features at 3D locations, complementing splatted intensities and sampling geometry. A low-frequency decoder predicts a smooth 3D field from the splat representation, while a high-frequency decoder additionally incorporates the queried 2D features to predict residual details. A learned gating module regulates the residual contribution based on geometry and cross-slice feature variation. We evaluate XFreq on an in-house fetal brain dataset and the public CETUS cardiac dataset, using slices resampled from 3D ultrasound volumes and fivefold cross-validation. Among the compared methods, XFreq achieves the highest PSNR and SSIM in most settings across both datasets, reaching 26.82 dB PSNR on CETUS with 32 input slices. Ablation studies further show the contributions of local feature querying, geometric cues, and residual prediction, with gains generally increasing as more input slices are used. Our findings highlight the value of geometry-aligned cross-view feature integration for reconstructing 3D ultrasound volumes from sparse 2D slices.

</details>


### [363] [A High-Resolution UAV Benchmark Dataset and Baseline Evaluation for Teak Crown Segmentation in Western Ghats Tropical Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30888)
*Anand S Sahadevan,Sunita Arya,Anup Kumar Das,Bimal Kumar Bhattacharya,S Manthira Moorthi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TeakTreeNet——首个原生2cm分辨率柚木树二分类分割数据集与评测协议（6391张224×224专家标注无人机影像块），并对U-Net、PSPNet、DeepLabV3、SegFormer进行统一基准测试；结果显示SegFormer（从零训练）达到新SOTA：F1 0.938±0.002、IoU 0.968±0.001、Acc 0.986±0.001，验证了Transformer在密集林地边界刻画上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率无人机影像的精准柚木树分割对种植园可持续管理至关重要，但该任务缺乏专门的基准数据集与可复现评测协议，导致方法间无法直接比较、难以评估各架构在原生高分辨率与密集林分场景下的真实性能。

Method: 构建TeakTreeNet数据集（6391个224×224专家标注无人机影像块，原生2cm分辨率）及二分类分割评测协议；在从零训练（scratch）与ImageNet预训练两种设置下，对U-Net、PSPNet、DeepLabV3、SegFormer四种SOTA架构进行三随机种子可复现基准测试。

Result: SegFormer取得新SOTA：F1 0.938±0.002（Teak）、IoU 0.968±0.001、Accuracy 0.986±0.001，超过全部预训练CNN；从零训练的SegFormer即可优于ImageNet预训练的CNN基线，说明域自适应训练足够，可省去大规模预训练开销；分析显示Transformer在密集种植园的边界刻画上更优。

Conclusion: TeakTreeNet为研究者提供专家标注数据与可复现评测协议，支持未来方法直接对比；基准表明SegFormer在精度、效率与可部署性之间取得最优平衡，适用于柚木种植园的作业化林业监测。

Abstract: Accurate teak-tree segmentation from high-resolution drone imagery is critical for sustainable plantation management, yet lacks dedicated benchmarks. We introduce TeakTreeNet, a comprehensive dataset and evaluation protocol for binary teak-tree segmentation at native 2cm resolution. Four state-of-the-art architectures: U-Net, PSPNet, DeepLabV3, and SegFormer were rigorously benchmarked across scratch and ImageNet-pretrained settings with three-seed reproducibility. SegFormer establishes new SOTA performance with F1 0.938 +/- 0.002 (Teak), IoU 0.968 +/- 0.001 (Teak), and Accuracy 0.986 +/- 0.001, outperforming all pretrained CNNs. Domain-adaptive training suffices, as the scratch SegFormer outperforms ImageNet pretrained baselines, avoiding large-scale pretraining overhead. Comprehensive analysis reveals Transformer's superior boundary delineation for dense plantation scenarios. TeakTreeNet provides researchers with 6391 expertly annotated drone patches (224x224) and establishes reproducible evaluation protocols, enabling direct comparison of future methods. Our benchmark demonstrates SegFormer's optimal balance of accuracy, efficiency, and deployability for operational forestry monitoring of teak plantations for India's western tropical forests.

</details>


### [364] [Speak When You See It: Proactive Task Assistants Should Wait for Evidence, Not the Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30895)
*Bohe Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出步骤完成/错误判断基准存在"视频盲"漏洞，提出Evidence-Contrastive Training (ECT)使模型真正依赖视频证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动助手需每几秒判断步骤是否完成、是否出错，但现有基准可能让模型靠"知道何时"而非"看到什么"取胜。作者用不看视频、仅按耗时报警的计时器在Ego-MC-Bench上取得0.373错误F1，超过所有已发表结果（≤0.27），说明基准存在可被时间先验利用的漏洞。

Method: 形式化"video-blind frontier"：仅用耗时、指令与训练统计所能达到的完成准确率—错误F1最优权衡，并验证已发表结果及QICD上12个零样本模型均落在其内。提出ECT：在基准自身问题上微调，将正样本与同一录像、同一耗时的负样本配对，使耗时无法区分正负。用209个QICD视频、约15 GPU小时训练2B模型。

Result: ECT在四个随机种子下于两个基准上均越过frontier；零适配迁移至Ego-MC-Bench，及时确认59%无错步骤（同骨干、约25k合成反事实+真实录像训练的已发表模型仅30%）；等耗时下区分完成/未完成步骤的测试对准确率90–92%（指令典型时长基线65%）；重缩放时间戳几乎无影响；替换为同配方另一录像使完成准确率下降三分之二。

Conclusion: 现有基准存在被时间先验"刷分"的风险，ECT通过同耗时对比负样本迫使模型依赖视频证据，显著超越video-blind frontier，并证明其决策确由视觉内容驱动。代码与权重将公开。

Abstract: Proactive assistants that guide people through physical tasks must decide, every few seconds, whether a step is complete and whether a mistake has occurred. We find that two benchmarks for this task largely reward knowing when rather than seeing what: a timer that never looks at the video, raising an alarm at 25 s and confirming each step at 30 s, reaches a mistake F1 of 0.373 on Ego-MC-Bench, above every published result (at most 0.27). We formalize this as the video-blind frontier, the best trade-off between completion accuracy and mistake F1 that policies reach from the elapsed time, the instruction and training statistics alone: every published per-step result on Ego-MC-Bench lies inside it, and so do all twelve zero-shot models we run on QICD. To move beyond it, we introduce Evidence-Contrastive Training (ECT): fine-tune on the benchmark's own questions, and pair its positives with negatives from the same recording at the same elapsed time, so that elapsed time cannot separate the two sides of a pair. Trained on 209 QICD videos in about 15 GPU-hours, a 2B model with ECT crosses the frontier on both benchmarks in all four seeds; transferred to Ego-MC-Bench without adaptation, it confirms 59% of mistake-free steps in time, against 30% for a published model with the same backbone trained on about 25k synthetic counterfactuals and real recordings. Its decisions follow the video beyond what timing explains: at equal elapsed time it tells a finished step from an unfinished one in 90-92% of test pairs, where the instruction's typical duration reaches 65%, rescaling the time stamps between the frames leaves it essentially unchanged, and swapping in another recording, of the same recipe where one exists, cuts its completion accuracy by two thirds. The code and model weights will be publicly released.

</details>


### [365] [World Models as Native Latent Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30898)
*Qinyue Li,Weijie Qiao,Qingyi Tao,Zerui Gong,Zhe Yang,Quanzhou Li,Taolin Zhang,Jinglin He,Zhonghua Wu,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Native LAM：将预训练视频扩散Transformer同时用作逆动态模型(IDM)和正/前向动态模型(FDM)，仅以可学习的"动力学寄存器"作为二者之间的因果桥梁，并在仅运动不同的反事实时序对上训练，从而将视频动力学蒸馏为紧凑的隐式动作；进一步提出PAWlicy预训练范式，用Native LAM作为大规模视频上的动力学教师来预训练VLA策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模多样化视频源（第一视角人类视频、异构机器人录制）蕴含丰富动力学，但要么缺少动作标注，要么缺乏共享动作空间。现有隐式动作模型(LAM)从零训练IDM/FDM，需在像素重建、隐空间容量与精度之间取舍；而预训练视频世界模型虽能生成高保真动力学，却以密集、跨层纠缠的方式表示。

Method: (1) 将预训练视频世界模型视为LAM，仅做最小改动即可提取其动力学；(2) Native LAM：单一预训练视频扩散Transformer兼任IDM与FDM，唯一因果桥梁是可学习的dynamics registers，并在仅运动差异的反事实时序对上训练，使寄存器将视频动力学蒸馏为紧凑的native latent action；(3) PAWlicy：以Native LAM作为大规模视频上的动力学教师预训练VLA策略，且不生成视频（区别于world action models）。

Result: 在LARY基准上，native latent actions在动作分类与控制回归两项均超过现有LAM；无需任何迁移目标即可在新机器人、新场景中复现相同运动，并实现人手到机器人的迁移。3.8B模型PAWlicy在RoboTwin 2.0上使用域随机化演示训练取得93.6%平均成功率，为VLA中最高；在RoboDojo各评估维度具竞争力，其Precision与榜单领先条目相当；真实机器人两任务上均优于pi_0.5基线。两阶段仅使用公开数据和公开世界模型。

Conclusion: 预训练视频世界模型可被高效地"改造"为隐式动作模型，通过动力学寄存器与反事实时序对实现紧凑、可迁移的隐式动作提取；以此作为动力学教师预训练VLA（PAWlicy）在仿真与真实机器人上均取得领先或具竞争力的表现，且全流程基于公开资源，具备良好的可复现性。

Abstract: Large and diverse video sources, such as egocentric human video and heterogeneous robot recordings, carry rich dynamics but come either without actions or without a shared action space. Latent action models (LAMs) can recover these dynamics, but they train their inverse- and forward-dynamics models (IDM, FDM) from scratch, trading off pixel reconstruction against latent capacity and accuracy. Pretrained video world models natively generate high-fidelity dynamics, yet represent them densely, entangled across all layers. Viewing the world model as a LAM, we show that these dynamics can be extracted with minimal changes to the model. Native LAM operates one pretrained video diffusion transformer as both IDM and FDM, with learnable dynamics registers as the only causal bridge between the two, and trains on counterfactual temporal pairs that differ in motion alone, so that the registers distill a video's dynamics into a compact native latent action. We further propose a pretraining paradigm for vision-language-action (VLA) policies, Pretraining Actions from World models (PAWlicy), in which Native LAM serves as a dynamics teacher on large-scale video; unlike world action models, it never generates video. Native latent actions outperform existing LAMs on the LARY benchmark in both action classification and control regression, and replay the same motion on new robots, in new scenes, and from human hands to robots, although no transfer objective is used in training. With a 3.8B model, PAWlicy reaches a 93.6% average success rate on RoboTwin 2.0 trained with domain-randomized demonstrations, the highest among VLAs. On RoboDojo, it is competitive on every dimension we evaluate, and its Precision, evaluated by us, is on par with the leading leaderboard entries. On real robots, it improves over the pi_0.5 baseline on both tasks. Both stages use only public data and a publicly released world model.

</details>


### [366] [GlanceNav: Efficient Vision-and-Language Navigation with Streaming Memory and Counterfactual Rewind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30900)
*Anh Dao,Quan-Dung Pham,Danh Vinh Le,Tuyen Pham Le,Quan Nguyen,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GlanceNav流式导航器：每帧仅编码一次、恒定每步开销，用门控DeltaNet递推状态+短窗口注意力+关键帧库构成常量记忆；并配套Counterfactual Rewind强化学习阶段，在R2R-CE/RxR-CE val-unseen上达71.9%/73.6%成功率，单步104 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型VLN策略在每个决策步重复编码历史帧、按孤立决策训练，导致计算冗余、可用历史短；且仅用整条轨迹的单一标量结果奖励学习纠错，信用分配粗、样本效率低。作者追问：能否每帧只编码一次、每步恒定开销，同时保留关键历史并高效从错误中学习。

Method: 1) 混合MLLM架构：Gated DeltaNet线性注意力层以递推状态贯穿整条episode；注意力层维护近期观测的短窗口与一个关键帧库，关键帧按"该帧对递推状态的改变量"选取。2) 常量大小记忆使每步计算固定，且训练与推理内存一致，支持整条episode训练。3) Counterfactual Rewind (CoRe)：因记忆可常数时间复制，将漂移的rollout回退到距目标最近处，由专家纠正少量动作，再以结果变化对决策进行信用分配的反事实奖励。

Result: 仅用R2R-CE与RxR-CE训练集（强化学习仅用1,000条指令），4B参数GlanceNav在R2R-CE与RxR-CE val-unseen上成功率分别为71.9%和73.6%；在R2R-CE上较最强的不使用外部数据的单RGB方法提升9.1个点，单步运行104 ms。

Conclusion: 结果表明，以恒定成本的流式常量记忆替代逐帧重编码是可行且高效的：既保持长程记忆，又使训练与推理对齐；配合反事实回退式RL可显著提升成功率与推理速度，为实时VLN提供了可扩展范式。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have become strong policies for vision-and-language navigation (VLN). However, most of them encode a sample of past frames again at every decision and are trained on isolated decisions, so computation is redundant and the usable history is short. They also learn from their own mistakes through outcome rewards that give one scalar to a whole episode. We ask whether a navigator can encode each frame only once, at a constant cost per step, and still remember what matters and learn efficiently from its mistakes. We present GlanceNav, a streaming navigator built on a hybrid MLLM. Its Gated DeltaNet layers carry a recurrent state through the whole episode, while its attention layers keep a short window of recent observations and a small bank of keyframes, selected by how much each frame changes the recurrent state. This constant-size memory keeps the cost per step fixed and allows training on whole episodes with the same memory as at inference. Because the entire memory can also be copied in constant time, we further propose Counterfactual Rewind (CoRe), a reinforcement learning stage that rewinds a drifting rollout to its closest approach to the goal, lets an expert correct a few actions, and credits decisions by the resulting change in outcome. Trained only on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE training splits, with 1,000 instructions for reinforcement learning, a 4B GlanceNav reaches success rates of 71.9% and 73.6% on R2R-CE and RxR-CE val-unseen. On R2R-CE, it outperforms the strongest previous single-RGB method trained without external data by 9.1 points, while running at 104 ms per step.

</details>


### [367] [Replay Before You Reply: Second-Look Prompting for Unified Multimodal Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30909)
*Yicheng Zhang,Xun Zhang,Jingyuan Ma,Tianyu Yu,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法 Second-Look Prompting (SLP)：在输入中重复图文对，使第二批 token 能因果地看到完整上下文，从而获得统一的多模态编码，在多个基准和模型上普遍涨点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLM 的因果注意力使先出现的模态在编码时只能看到部分输入（图像先出现则不知问题，反之亦然），难以形成图像与问题的统一理解。

Method: 将图像-问题对在输入中再重复一次：重复副本中的每个视觉与文本 token 都能注意到完整输入，形成统一的多模态编码；同时新增的视觉 token 本身也带来额外计算，充当 prefill 草稿纸。方法无需训练。

Result: 在 12 个基准上提升了所有被评估模型的平均分，覆盖 9 个开源模型（3B–72B）与 3 个专有前沿模型，Gemini 3 Flash 在 MATH-Vision 上提升达 5.6 分；分析表明机制有二：问题引导第二张图像的注意力落点，新增视觉 token 作为草稿纸其隐状态保留与答案相关的信息（即使换成空白图像也能提升推理）。

Conclusion: 输入呈现方式（input presentation）是提升 VLM 性能的简单有效手段，SLP 以极低成本实现跨模型、跨任务的稳定增益。

Abstract: Solving multimodal tasks requires Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to form a unified understanding of the image and the question text. However, under causal attention in current VLMs, a token can attend only to what precedes it. Whichever modality comes first is therefore encoded under partial observation of the input, uninformed by the rest of the multimodal context. We introduce Second-Look Prompting (SLP), a training-free method that repeats the image-question pair in the input. In the repeated copy, every visual and textual token can attend to the complete input, yielding a unified multimodal encoding of the pair. Moreover, the added visual tokens contribute computation beyond re-exposing the image: even a blank image in their place can improve reasoning. Across 12 benchmarks, SLP improves the overall average score of every evaluated model, spanning nine open-source models (3B-72B) and three proprietary frontier models, with gains reaching 5.6 points for Gemini 3 Flash on MATH-Vision. Our analysis reveals two complementary mechanisms: the question guides where the second image attends, and the added visual tokens act as a prefill scratchpad whose hidden states retain answer-relevant information from the image and question. These findings highlight input presentation as a simple yet effective way to improve VLM performance.

</details>


### [368] [Do Waste Classifiers Recognise Items They Have Never Seen? Object-Disjoint Evaluation on Household and Medical Waste](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30911)
*Nusrat Jahan,Mst. Eshrat Jahan Esha,Masuma Sadia,Saad Ahmed,Md Zahim Hassan,S M Jahangir Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建BanglaWaste-23数据集（4,160张图像、172个实物、23类），采用物体不重叠五折交叉验证，揭示随机划分严重高估分类精度，预训练模型在未见物体上macro-F1达98.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有垃圾分类研究多在随机图像划分上报告近完美精度，但同一实物的多张照片会同时出现在训练集与测试集，导致精度被高估；需要衡量分类器对从未见过物体的识别能力，并关注医疗废弃物误分类风险与跨数据集泛化能力。

Method: 构建BanglaWaste-23数据集（4,160张图像、172个实物、23类家庭与医疗废弃物，来自孟加拉国），每张图像标注对应实物身份；评估11个ImageNet预训练模型及2个从头训练模型（紧凑自定义网络与MobileNetV3-L），采用物体不重叠五折交叉验证与随机划分对比；并在达卡第二个数据集上测试泛化。

Result: 未见物体上最佳预训练模型macro-F1为98.8%，六个模型统计上无显著差异；随机划分使从头训练模型精度高估15–19个百分点；从头训练模型漏检11–13%医疗物品，预训练模型低于1%；达卡数据集上预训练精度下降15–22个百分点。

Conclusion: 分类器应在未见物体上评估，随机划分结果仅可视为性能上界；预训练模型在精度与医疗废弃物安全性上均优于从头训练模型，但跨域泛化仍显著下降。数据已发布于Zenodo，代码见github.com/saadbaust/BanglaWaste-23。

Abstract: Automatic waste classification supports source separation, which enables recycling and keeps hazardous medical items out of household waste. Waste classification studies often report near-perfect accuracy on random image splits, which put photographs of one item in both training and test sets. To measure how well classifiers recognise items they have never seen, we built BanglaWaste-23, with 4,160 images of 172 objects in 23 household and medical waste classes from Bangladesh. Every image also records which physical item it shows. We evaluated 11 ImageNet-pretrained models and two models trained from scratch (a compact custom network and MobileNetV3-L) using object-disjoint five-fold cross-validation and a random split. On unseen objects, the best pretrained model reached a macro-averaged F1 score of 98.8%, and six models were statistically tied. The random split overstated from-scratch accuracy by 15 to 19 percentage points. The from-scratch models also missed 11 to 13% of medical items, against under 1% for pretrained models. On a second dataset from Dhaka, pretrained accuracy fell by 15 to 22 points. Classifiers should therefore be evaluated on unseen objects, with random-split results read as an upper bound. The data are on Zenodo and the code at https://github.com/saadbaust/BanglaWaste-23.

</details>


### [369] [FGSCalib: Targetless Camera-LiDAR Extrinsic Calibration from Foreground Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30935)
*Marcell Kégl,Csaba Benedek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FGSCalib：利用场景中任意运动目标实现相机-激光雷达无标靶、无需初始外参的外参自标定，仿真中位误差5.3 cm/0.19°，真实数据中位误差9.5 cm/0.9°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定基础设施中的相机-LiDAR对需定期重标定，但使用标靶会使设备停运。现有方法多依赖标靶或已知初始外参，故需要一种无标靶、免初始值的标定方法。

Method: 假设场景中存在任意运动目标（不依赖类别）。两传感器各自以运动前景/静态背景分离得到前景掩膜；正确外参下LiDAR前景点应投影至相机前景。将相机掩膜转为边界邻近场，并在选定帧对上用两阶段模拟退火优化外参；无需测量初始外参，支持旋转式与非重复扫描LiDAR。

Result: 在21个精确真值仿真场景中中位误差5.3 cm/0.19°；72个真实场景（8个场景中9对传感器，参考值为43次人工标定的平均）中，取5次运行最低代价，51/72满足18 cm/1.8°容差（Livox 20/24，最强基线仅11；旋转式LiDAR 31/48，仅一个基线估计达标）。中位误差9.5 cm/0.9°，单次人工标定为7.3 cm/0.7°。

Conclusion: FGSCalib证明了利用无类别运动前景即可在无标靶、无初始外参条件下完成相机-LiDAR标定，精度接近单次人工标定，并在非重复扫描LiDAR上优势明显；代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Camera-LiDAR pairs installed as fixed infrastructure need periodic recalibration, but a calibration target takes the installation out of service. We present FGSCalib, a targetless method that calibrates a stationary camera-LiDAR pair from the objects moving in its view. We only assume that arbitrary moving objects are present in the scene: each sensor separates the moving foreground from the static background, without relying on object classes. With the correct extrinsic, the LiDAR foreground points project onto the camera foreground. FGSCalib turns the camera mask into a boundary-proximity field and finds the extrinsic by two-stage simulated annealing over selected frame pairs. It needs no measured initial extrinsic (random starts give similar results) and works with both rotating and non-repetitive LiDARs. We evaluate it on 21 simulated scenarios with exact ground truth and on 72 real ones (nine camera-LiDAR pairs in eight scenes), with a reference averaged from 43 manual calibrations. In simulation the median error is 5.3 cm and 0.19°. On real data we use a tolerance of 18 cm and 1.8°, met by 93% of single manual calibrations. Keeping the lowest-cost of five runs, FGSCalib meets it in 51 of the 72 scenarios: in 20 of 24 with the Livox, where the strongest of five baselines reaches 11, and in 31 of 48 with the two rotating LiDARs, where only one baseline estimate does. Its median error is 9.5 cm and 0.9°, against 7.3 cm and 0.7° for a single manual calibration. The code and the dataset are available online.

</details>


### [370] [SIAMBA: A Pseudo-Siamese Mamba-based Deep Learning Network for Multimodal Flood Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30938)
*Muhammad Fasih Ishfaq,Giulio Weikmann,Lorenzo Bruzzone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出伪孪生Mamba网络SIAMBA，融合Sentinel-1/2双时相影像进行洪水变化检测，在OMBRIA基准独立测试集上取得81.98% IoU与93.57% OA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双时相多光谱与SAR联合洪水变化检测对应急响应至关重要：Sentinel-2提供判别性光谱信息，Sentinel-1提供全天候结构信息。但云污染、光照变化、成像几何与斑点噪声以及地表覆盖响应异质性使跨模态异质性建模困难。

Method: 采用模态专属编码器的伪孪生Mamba架构：双池化SE重校准与选择性状态空间处理建模长程空间上下文；门控跨模态变化模块在多个语义层级提取绝对、有符号与相对对数比差异；注意力优化跳跃连接支撑渐进式多尺度解码。

Result: 在OMBRIA基准（独立测试集及法国、阿尔巴尼亚、圭亚那、东帝汶地理多样子集）上，独立测试集达81.98% IoU、93.57% OA，为对比方法最优；四个空间不相交区域子集上的平均IoU与OA亦最高。

Conclusion: 将选择性状态空间上下文建模与门控多尺度差异学习相结合，可有效提升多地理场景下的洪水变化制图性能。

Abstract: Flood change detection from bi-temporal multispectral and synthetic aperture radar (SAR) images is essential for rapid disaster response. In this context, Sentinel-2 multispectral imagery provides discriminative spectral observations, whereas Sentinel-1 SAR provides weather-independent structural information. Using these modalities jointly requires modelling temporal evidence while accounting for cross-modal heterogeneity. In particular, change detection is complicated by cloud contamination, illumination variability, acquisition geometry and speckle, and heterogeneous land-cover responses. To address these challenges, we propose SIAMBA, a pseudo-siamese Mamba-based network for bi-temporal flood change detection from paired Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 observations. SIAMBA uses dedicated modality-specific encoders with dual-pooling squeeze-and-excitation recalibration and selective state-space processing for long-range spatial context. A gated cross-modal change module derives absolute, signed, and relative log-ratio differences at multiple semantic levels, and attention-refined skip connections support progressive multi-scale decoding. Experiments are carried out on OMBRIA benchmark, which includes an independent test set and geographically diverse subsets in France, Albania, Guyana, and Timor. SIAMBA achieves 81.98% IoU, and 93.57% OA on the independent test set, reaching the highest values in the considered comparison. It also provides the highest unweighted average IoU and OA across four regional subsets spatially disjuncted. These results indicate that combining selective state-space contextual modelling with gated multiscale discrepancy learning effectively improves flood change mapping across the evaluated geographic settings.

</details>


### [371] [Diffutoon-2: Toon Shading with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30945)
*Yuze Sun,Zhongjie Duan,Yingda Chen,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Diffutoon-2 是一个视频扩散框架，通过"动画重建"学习卡通着色，无需跨域训练对或外部风格参考，在两个冻结骨干的互补模块（轮廓重建与模糊颜色重建）联合引导下，将写实视频转为动漫风格，并全面超越通用与领域基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡通着色需呈现扁平化、风格化外观。扩散模型为"写实视频转动漫"提供新路径，但现有方法依赖人工筛选的源-风格训练对或额外视觉参考；Diffutoon 虽通过结构引导与上色规避了这些需求，却受限于图像扩散骨干、短时窗运动模块与重复重叠窗口去噪，视频质量与效率受限。

Method: 提出 Diffutoon-2：以动画重建作为学习任务，在视频扩散骨干冻结的前提下，训练两个互补模块分别从轮廓与模糊颜色重建动画片段；推理时，用从写实素材中提取的同类条件（轮廓、模糊颜色）联合引导生成，从而避免跨域配对数据与外部风格参考，并继承视频骨干的长时序建模能力。

Result: 大量实验表明，Diffutoon-2 在多项定量指标与人工评测上均优于通用视频扩散基线及领域专用卡通着色基线。

Conclusion: 通过"动画重建 + 双条件联合引导"的自监督式范式，可在不依赖跨域训练对与风格参考的情况下实现高质量视频卡通着色，且优于现有方法，为视频风格化提供了可扩展的新思路。

Abstract: Toon shading renders objects with a flat, stylized appearance. Diffusion models open a new route to this task by transforming photorealistic videos into an anime style. However, existing approaches often rely on curated source and style training pairs or additional visual references. Diffutoon avoids these requirements through structure guidance and colorization, but builds on an image diffusion backbone with short-window motion modules and repeated overlapping-window denoising. We present Diffutoon-2, a video diffusion framework that learns toon shading through animation reconstruction. Two complementary modules independently reconstruct animation clips from outlines and blurred colors, with the video backbone frozen. At inference, the modules jointly guide generation using the same types of conditions extracted from photorealistic footage, requiring neither cross-domain training pairs nor external style references. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Diffutoon-2 outperforms both general-purpose and domain-specific baselines, across quantitative metrics and human evaluation.

</details>


### [372] [Graph2Scene: Relation-Aware Text-to-Image Generation with Graph-Guided Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30964)
*Peizheng Li,Xin Ai,Guanghui Cheng,Zikang Dong,Qiange Wang,Yanfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Graph2Scene：一种无需训练、以场景图为核心控制信号的扩散生成框架，通过将节点-关系依赖转化为选择性注意力引导，并自适应控制时长，显著提升复杂提示的组合式生成与关系准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型难以处理含多元素及其交互的复杂提示；现有注意力控制方法主要关注元素出现位置，忽视对象间的交互方式，导致描述交互的关系token把注意力分配到无关区域，造成交互缺失或错误。

Method: Graph2Scene为training-free框架：将场景图中的元素表示为节点、关系表示为边，把元素-关系依赖转化为选择性注意力引导；将每个元素定位到对应区域，并引导相关元素之间的关系交互；为在关系控制与避免过度约束之间取得平衡，根据场景复杂度自适应调整控制时长，并在去噪过程中逐步松弛图引导。

Result: 在T2I-CompBench与ConceptMix上组合式生成明显提升，尤其对象关系方面；ConceptMix中关系准确率相较最强的基于FLUX.1的区域控制基线从0.59提升至0.84；在LongBench-T2I上对长而复杂的提示亦表现出优势。

Conclusion: 以场景图作为核心控制信号、并自适应调节控制强度与时长，是改善复杂提示下关系交互生成的可行且有效的方向，且无需额外训练即可集成。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models still struggle with complex prompts containing many elements and interactions. Existing attention-control methods mainly focus on controlling where elements appear, but often ignore how objects interact with each other. As a result, relation tokens describing interactions between elements may assign high attention to irrelevant regions, leading to missing or incorrect interactions. In this paper, we propose Graph2Scene, a training-free framework that treats scene graphs as a core control signal for diffusion generation. It represents elements as nodes and their relations as edges, and translates these element-relation dependencies into selective attention guidance. It grounds each element to its corresponding region and guides relational interactions among the involved elements. To achieve appropriate relation control without over-constraining the generation process, Graph2Scene further adapts the control duration to scene complexity and progressively relaxes graph guidance during denoising. Experiments on T2I-CompBench and ConceptMix show clear improvements in compositional generation, especially for object relations. On ConceptMix, Graph2Scene improves relationship accuracy from 0.59 to 0.84 over the strongest FLUX.1-based regional-control baseline. Results on LongBench-T2I further show its advantage on long and complex prompts.

</details>


### [373] [Parsing Visual Latents for Multimodal Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31021)
*Zhuoying Yang,Yichen Guo,Kathy Jia,Zichang Su,Ayesha Kashif,Nange Hai,Jeffrey Ming Han Li,Zhewen He,Silvio Kempf,Ni Jiachen,Junjie Yang,Muhammad Hamza,Zakaria El Ouahidi,Gabriel Azpitarte,Manling Li,Yi Fang,Shuhong Liu,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LatentParse是一个免训练框架，用"分析师VLM"通过预定义工具解析CLIP/DINOv3/VGGT三类视觉编码器的隐特征，将其蒸馏为简洁的文本关系证据，再与图像共同输入VLM以提升多视图空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图空间推理需从2D观测推断物体与相机间的3D关系，依赖跨视图对应与场景几何，而VLM难以可靠提取。预训练视觉编码器（CLIP关联物体与图像区域、DINOv3跨视图匹配语义、VGGT编码多视图几何）的隐特征已包含这些信息，但以灰度特征图形式直接放入上下文时，VLM无法有效利用。

Method: 提出免训练的LatentParse：由一个analyst VLM使用预定义工具探测三类编码器特征——定位物体、跨视图跟踪、测量视图间运动；再将结果蒸馏成简短的文本报告，与原始图像一同提供给目标VLM作答。

Result: 在MMSI-Bench、MV-RoboBench和SPAR-Bench上评测，五个VLM相对仅图像基线平均提升0.7–2.4个百分点；在MMSI-Bench上将Qwen3-27B从34.4%提升至39.5%，其中相机相关问题上提升最大，达19.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验表明，隐特征一旦被解析为关系证据，无需训练即可对空间推理产生实际增益；关键在于特征的"解析"形式而非简单呈现。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning across multiple views requires inferring the 3D relations between objects and the camera from 2D observations, which depends on cross-view correspondence and scene geometry that vision-language models (VLMs) do not reliably extract. Pretrained vision encoders already capture this information in their latent features, with CLIP linking objects to image regions, DINOv3 matching semantics across views, and VGGT encoding multi-view geometry. However, VLMs do not benefit from these features when we present them directly in context as grayscale feature images. We therefore propose LatentParse, a training-free framework that parses latent features into explicit spatial evidence for VLMs. In LatentParse, an analyst VLM probes the features of all three encoders with predefined tools that locate objects, track them across views, and measure motion between views. It then distills the results into a concise text report, which is provided together with the images to the VLM to answer the question. We evaluate LatentParse on MMSI-Bench, MV-RoboBench and SPAR-Bench, where it improves all five VLMs over the photo-only baseline by 0.7-2.4 percentage points on average. On MMSI-Bench, it raises Qwen3.8-27B from 34.4% to 39.5%, with the largest improvements of 19.2 percentage points on the camera-related questions. These results suggest that latent features become useful for spatial reasoning once they are parsed into relation evidence, without training. Code is available at https://github.com/Harvard-AI-and-Robotics-Lab/LatentParse.

</details>


### [374] [OmniV-Bench: Can MLLMs Comprehend Immersive Spatio-temporal Scenes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31022)
*Liu Yang,Huiyu Duan,Yixuan Gao,Sirui Liu,Lu Liu,Zhaolin Cai,Ke Gu,Xiongkuo Min,Xiaoping Zhang,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出OmniV-Bench基准（4,123段全向视频、8,923个问答对、11项任务、三类理解能力）用于评测MLLMs的360°全向视频时空理解，并针对评测暴露的能力不足提出OmniV-Harness——基于视角引导证据初始化与任务相关证据获取的智能体框架，可稳定提升MLLMs的沉浸式时空推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全向视频（ODV）具有完整360°视场，能提供丰富空间上下文与时序动态，对VR/AR与具身智能意义重大；而现有多模态大模型虽在时空理解上进展显著，但其对ODV所承载的沉浸式360°时空信息的理解能力尚缺乏系统探究与评测手段。

Method: 构建OmniV-Bench基准：收录多场景4,123段高质量ODV，配套8,923个人工标注问答对，覆盖通用、空间、时空理解三大类的11项细粒度任务；在此基础上开展大规模基准实验，并提出OmniV-Harness智能体式框架，通过视角引导的证据初始化与自适应工具调用的任务相关证据获取来增强ODV理解。

Result: 基准实验表明，ODV的时空复杂性对当前MLLMs构成显著挑战；引入OmniV-Harness后，MLLMs的沉浸式时空推理能力得到一致且稳定的提升。

Conclusion: OmniV-Bench填补了全向视频时空理解评测的空白，OmniV-Harness提供了有效的智能体增强方案；二者将在论文发表后开源，以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Omnidirectional videos (ODVs) capture dynamic environments with a full $360^\circ$ field of view, providing rich spatial context and temporal dynamics for applications such as virtual reality (VR), augmented reality (AR), and embodied intelligence. Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made great progress in spatial and temporal understanding, yet their ability to comprehend the immersive 360 spatio-temporal information conveyed by ODVs remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we first introduce OmniV-Bench, a Benchmark designed to comprehensively evaluate the capabilities of MLLMs on spatio-temporal Omnidirectional Video understanding. OmniV-Bench contains 4,123 high-quality ODVs collected from diverse scenes, along with 8,923 human-annotated question-answer pairs spanning 11 fine-grained tasks across three categories, i.e., general, spatial, and spatio-temporal understanding. Extensive benchmark experiments reveal that the spatio-temporal complexity of ODVs poses significant challenges for current MLLMs. To address these limitations, we further propose OmniV-Harness, an agentic harness that enhances ODV understanding through viewpoint-guided evidence initialization and task-relevant evidence acquisition with adaptive tool use.
